# ProbOS Progress

**BF-671 shipped (2026-07-15) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — the composer now owns the sole context-aware output-audio control across ordinary chat and active 1:1/group calls (#1038).** Ordinary chat reads and writes the existing persisted per-agent `hxi_chat_tts_${agentId}` preference; an active call reads and writes only session-scoped `callAudioEnabled`, with no state copying or reset on transitions, and the duplicate header control and ownership are removed. Greeting, `sendText`, and current controller replies gate future output at delivery time against the live authoritative target agent and thread, preventing mounted-profile drift. The singleton controller now uses generation-safe ownership across acquisition, preemption, VAD, async fetch/JSON/group-submit, callbacks, state, and timers; stale operations are dropped, queued stale leases self-release, and React retains each arm's owner-bound disposer so delayed cleanup cannot disarm a replacement. Response parsing now accepts `response` first, then `reply`, then `message`, and enters `agent_speaking` before callback so muted synchronous completion remains valid. Group non-meeting silence, meeting voice versus progressive reveal, call-audio/open-mic coupling, existing microphone behavior, and future-output-only/no-cancellation semantics are preserved. The sole control has action labels, `aria-pressed`, title, a stable test ID, and stroke-SVG/no-emoji states. **Validation (UI-only): correction 100 passed; focused 162 passed; full Vitest 2,123 passed / 1 skipped; production build 2,615 modules transformed; Playwright 1 focused / 7 full passed**; final Architect review APPROVED. **No new AD; BF-671 is the new BF ceiling; BF-670 remains reserved/open for #1037.** closes #1038.

**AD-1122 shipped (2026-07-15) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — Sensorium budget telemetry v2 now reports the truthful merged chain-sensorium character footprint, not the full request/model window, and attributes sustained overages without changing prompts (#1036).** The existing post-merge chain seam counts surviving non-empty strings separately in the cognitive and situation buckets. Transition payloads expose only deterministic top-N contributor metadata (`bucket`, `output_key`, path-resolved `layer`, `chars`, and per-entry `estimated_tokens`), with aggregate estimates computed before truncation; no value, snippet, hash, digest, or content-derived identifier is retained. `SensoriumConfig.warning_chars` is canonical, while canonical-first Pydantic `AliasChoices` plus a read-only legacy property preserve `token_budget_warning` input compatibility; the tracked operator `config/system.yaml` remains untouched. Bounded per-agent scalar state emits an immediate strict-threshold `crossed`, one ratio-triggered `escalated`, and cooldown summaries as `sustained`, with strict hysteresis rearm and reset on disable, threshold change, stop, or completed rearm—no task, timer, persistence, history, or global map. The existing typed `SENSORIUM_BUDGET_EXCEEDED` event is extended additively; transition state commits before logging/event delivery, and logging or event-sink failures remain contained without replaying the transition. The change is observe-only: sensorium content, ordering, wrappers, context selection, attention, model tier, token caps, and LLM call count remain invariant. **Validation:** **628 passed / 2 expected local-embedding skips across 5 serial isolated gates**; final Architect review APPROVED. **AD-1122 is the new top-level; BF ceiling remains BF-669.** closes #1036.

**BF-669 shipped (2026-07-14) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — deterministic AD-599 reflection replays are now idempotent without weakening write-once episode authority (#1035).** Typed `EpisodeDuplicatePolicy` and `EpisodeStoreOutcome` contracts distinguish expected same-reflection replay from unexpected collisions and distinguish `STORED`, `DUPLICATE`, and `SKIPPED`; the real and mock stores make the primary write-once decision under runtime-local serialization, preserving the first authoritative row under concurrent calls. Expected replays require a timestamp-neutral full AD-599 exact-content proof and log at DEBUG, while conflicting or default-policy duplicates retain a WARNING with the full ID and first-write authority. The canonical mock and protocol match the production contract. Step 15 opts in explicitly and counts only `STORED`, so duplicate-only runs emit no Created INFO and do not prime working memory as newly created reflections. Ordinary store errors still honest-degrade per candidate, cancellation still propagates, and canonical hash/version, metadata/schema, sidecar behavior, and shutdown ordering/write-free consolidation remain unchanged. **CI correction (test-only):** CI exposed a timing-dependent attribution-conflict hash oracle that reconstructed the incoming episode with the old timestamp; the follow-up captures the actual second store attempt. **Validation:** **50 independent node passes + 6 file tests + 385 focused + 50 adjacency + 616 passed / 1 skipped blast**; final Architect review APPROVED. **No new AD; BF-669 is the new BF ceiling.** closes #1035.

**BF-668 shipped (2026-07-14) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — IntentBus broadcast-handler latency observability is now class-aware instead of applying one 100 ms budget to deterministic, network, and cognitive work (#1034).** Typed `HandlerLatencyClass` metadata defaults to deterministic on `BaseAgent`, is inherited as cognitive by `CognitiveAgent` and network by `HttpFetchAgent`, and can be supplied explicitly per subscription; validated positive thresholds are exactly **100 / 10,000 / 30,000 ms**. All eight production subscription seams — common onboarding, patched-agent hot replacement, Yeoman proactive scan, VisionAggregator, VisionConsumer, group-chat coordination, device service, and device-consensus dispatch — now carry the inherited or explicit class. `_subscribers` remains the callable map while a separate class sidecar is snapshotted together with each callable before fan-out. Completed handlers feed bounded telemetry with at most 200 samples per key and 1,000 access/insertion-LRU keys, nearest-rank p95, mean/max, and responded/declined/error outcomes. Class-aware warnings fire only for normally completed handlers above their own budget; errors retain exactly one handler-error WARNING plus one error sample and no latency warning, while cancellation/timeout produces no handler sample, warning, or result. Broadcast filtering, concurrency, task count, timeout cancellation, completion-order results, and federation remain unchanged, as do targeted send, NATS/JetStream, `dispatch_async`, and cognitive-queue semantics. **Tests:** **246 focused + 394 blast passed**; final Architect review APPROVED. **No new AD; BF-668 is the new BF ceiling.** closes #1034.

**BF-667 shipped (2026-07-14) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — referent grounding now distinguishes strongly asserted identifiers from implicit conceptual node-noun phrases before collaboration (#1033).** Source syntax classifies quoted identifiers, explicit `node id` forms, machine-shaped IDs, hexadecimal IDs, and valid service forms as strong while bare alphabetic locator forms remain implicit; every candidate still traverses the existing resolver chain first, but an unresolved implicit candidate is recorded as ambiguous with no cue, WARNING, central selection, injection, or confab-probe side effect. Same-width non-whitespace barriers preserve source offsets while preventing locator matches from bridging fenced or inline code spans. Exact-token dedupe, deterministic first-position ordering, evidence-priority in-place promotion, and the 20-unique-token cap preserve stronger later evidence without reordering candidates. The router's redundant stopword policy is removed: source grammar is the single assertion authority, actionable unresolved warnings are truthful, and one central-token computation is reused for warning disposition, probe scheduling, and cue return. The default-OFF gate remains before construction, and the shared AD-970 kickoff path is preserved. **Tests:** **15 correction + 181 focused + 266 blast passed**; final Architect review APPROVED. **No new AD; BF-667 is the new BF ceiling.** closes #1033.

**BF-666 shipped (2026-07-14) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — force-describe now evicts expired or reaped latest-frame references once instead of repeatedly retrying missing perception blobs (#1032).** The upload mirror and delayed consumer path now share finite, monotonic complete-tuple `(sha, captured_at)` cache writes, updating session and global slots independently without regressing newer frames; complete-tuple compare-and-clear atomically removes every exact session/global alias while preserving different-SHA and same-SHA/newer-capture replacements. A bounded, cancellation-safe force-call guard collapses concurrent requests before storage selection. Selection rejects candidates beyond the prompt-freshness/retention bound (retention when prompt freshness is disabled) before storage or model work, then treats public `exists()` only as a cheap preflight: authoritative missing `read()` handling and the post-process existence check close both retention TOCTOU windows. Force results are now tied to the invocation-specific produced observation, preventing an empty, busy, or failed describe from leaking a historical working-memory description with the same SHA. Attachment-store resolver and unexpected backend failures honest-degrade with the candidate retained; cancellation propagates and releases owned guards. Low-novelty frames still cache before supervisor admission, synchronous upload mirroring remains intact, BF-304 describe singleflight is unchanged, and existing working-memory persistence, freshness sentinel, observer, and episode behavior is preserved. **Tests:** **51 focused + 179 blast-radius passed**; final Architect review APPROVED. **No new AD; BF-666 is the new BF ceiling.** closes #1032.

**BF-665 shipped (2026-07-13) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — pooled LLM refresh is now generation-safe across concurrent completions, CRITICAL bypass, health probes, and shutdown (#1031).** Every pooled transport borrower now holds an endpoint-keyed generation lease; refresh is endpoint-singleflight and generation-conditional, so only the caller observing the current generation can publish its replacement while stale observers reuse the installed generation. Superseded clients retire without interrupting in-flight peers and close only after their final borrower releases, with cancellation-deferred ownership cleanup. A persistently empty post-refresh response now records exactly one failed tier attempt, resets recovery dwell, advances `last_failure` without advancing `last_success` or caching the empty result, and continues through the existing fallback/error path. Health probes force endpoint governance, hold both the endpoint permit and generation lease, and accept HTTP 200 only when usable assistant content or reasoning is present; empty or malformed probes cannot advance recovery or trigger refresh. Generation-aware close stops health admission, rejects new leases, drains active and retired generations, closes each distinct client once, and remains concurrent-call, cancellation, and idempotency safe. **Tests:** **150 focused + 358 blast-radius passed**; final Architect review APPROVED. **No new AD; BF-665 is the new BF ceiling.** closes #1031.

**BF-664 shipped (2026-07-13) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — ProfileChatTab auto-scroll now follows bounded-transcript appends after the 200-message active-thread or 100-message cold-chat display window saturates (#1030).** The prior effect depended only on `messages.length`; once `appendThreadMessage` dropped the oldest row and appended a new tail at the cap, length stayed constant, so neither Captain-always-follow nor pinned-agent-follow ran. The scroll policy and component now observe tail message identity plus immediate-predecessor continuity: ordinary and capped appends remain incremental, same-size unrelated replacements stay bulk jumps, Captain appends always follow, pinned agent appends follow, and unpinned agent appends never yank the reader from an earlier turn. Context switches, initial history, and hidden-to-visible meeting transcript remounts still jump instantly to the latest message. Hidden revisions advance without DOM work. Smooth follows retain post-layout `requestAnimationFrame()` measurement and cancel stale frames on rerun, hide, replacement, context switch, and unmount. BF-293's agent-reply reset now keys on tail revision, so capped agent replies still clear the stale PTT empty-transcript counter while Captain messages do not. **Tests:** **43 focused behavioral tests**, **2,044 full UI tests passed / 1 skipped**, and the production TypeScript/Vite build completed with 2,615 modules transformed; Architect review APPROVED. closes #1030.

**BF-662 shipped (2026-07-11) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — embedding backend transitions are now reconstructable, backend-aware, and lossless across episodic memory, procedures, and EvolutionStore lessons (#1028).** `LocalHashEmbeddingFunction` is registered through ChromaDB's supported registry and exposes a deterministic name/config backend identity, so a fresh process can reopen and text-query a persisted local-EF collection without an explicit EF or network access. AD-584 schema currency now includes that backend identity; `EpisodicMemory.embedding_migration_required(model, backend)` owns metadata inspection, and an EF conflict sentinel forces migration despite a current sidecar while failures/timeouts never record success. `ProcedureStore.start()` now rebuilds its semantic collection asynchronously from authoritative SQLite `content_snapshot` rows before returning, preserves valid rows while skipping malformed ones, closes partial resources on cancellation/error, and retries interrupted rebuilds. EvolutionStore—whose Chroma collection is its sole persistence—now performs bounded verified shadow-copy transitions, count/content proof, collision-safe rename swaps, pair-preserving rollback, and strict restart recovery. Transaction ownership requires exact owner/name, 16-lowerhex transaction ID, native non-boolean count, and valid role/state; any malformed, ambiguous, mismatched, or unrelated temporary state is preserved untouched and startup degrades rather than selecting a false authority. The canonical collection is never deleted before a verified replacement; only parser-proven disposable temporary collections can be removed. Runtime wiring owns the persistent Evolution client and shutdown closes it before episodic Chroma; previously unclosed runtime-owned SQLite sidecars and the mock participant index now stop idempotently. **Tests:** **236 focused + 459 blast-radius passed**, warning-strict and natural-exit; final consolidated BF-659→BF-663 gate **830 passed / 1 Windows symlink-privilege skip**. Fresh-process reconstruction, A↔B transitions, empty/idempotent paths, malformed rows, cancellation cleanup, every forward/rollback rename boundary, corrupt candidate/backup, count mismatch, unowned/partial/mismatched/multiple leftovers, repeated recovery, and repeated shutdown are covered. Architect review APPROVED. closes #1028.

**BF-661 shipped (2026-07-11) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — warm boot now preserves and quarantines recoverable designed-skill source instead of deleting every source that lacks exactly `handle_<filename>` (#1027).** Persisted skill names are validated at every source/descriptor/quarantine/history boundary; canonical path checks fail closed on traversal, symlink, and Windows-junction escapes. Quarantine markers use bounded exact-schema reads, redacted fields, source hashes, unique sibling temp files, `fsync` + atomic replace, and per-skill compare-and-act locking. Store/quarantine/clear/remove run as complete cancellation-deferred transactions: both private-runner and outer-caller cancellation retain the lock until executor I/O and commit scheduling finish, then cancellation re-raises. `store_skill` removes only stale different-hash markers; same-hash markers survive. Warm boot uses the initial candidate plus at most two public source rereads, performs one atomic final source+marker read, validates with `SkillValidator` before import, verifies the loaded handler is async and bind-compatible, attaches with `persist=False`, and deletes only AST-proven inert stubs; malformed, mismatched, load-failing, or runtime-contract-failing source remains recoverable behind quarantine. Credential redaction now covers quoted JSON/Python values and complete quoted/bare Bearer credentials. **Tests:** **410 passed / 1 platform-specific symlink test skipped** focused + **230 blast-radius passed**, including deterministic source/marker races, repeated caller/private-runner cancellation, task cleanup, stale-hash behavior, oversized/malformed markers, quoted secret redaction, path/junction containment, Git debounce recovery, two-reread bounds, and real `SkillBasedAgent` dispatch; Architect review APPROVED. closes #1027.

**BF-663 shipped (2026-07-09) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — the AD-1121 confab probe now uses independent uncached samples, conservative structured verdicts, graceful runtime-owned task admission/drain, and record-only evidence persistence (#1029).** Each draw now gets a fresh request ID and a unique semantically inert nonce while the function signature remains token-only (seed/transcript isolation preserved). Responses must begin with structured `YES`/`NO`/`UNKNOWN`; only unambiguous YES/NO are usable, while malformed, hedged, modal, contradictory, equivocal, and double-negated explanations abstain—there is no default affirmation. Runtime-owned `schedule_confab_probe()` / `close_confab_probe_scheduling()` close admission synchronously at shutdown entry; one shielded drain cancels and awaits every accepted probe before `llm_client.close()`, survives outer shutdown cancellation without second-cancelling child cleanup, clears the registry, then re-raises. EvidenceCollector wiring now supports classification-only, record-only, and combined modes with one instance and at most one Ward Room listener; probe evidence persists even when emergence classification is disabled or its LLM/Ward Room dependencies are unavailable. **Tests:** 100 parser/probe tests + **233 combined focused/blast tests with warnings promoted to errors** passed; Architect review APPROVED. closes #1029.

**BF-660 shipped (2026-07-09) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — referent grounding now works under ProbOS's Windows selector-loop policy, cleans up Git children on timeout/cancellation, and rejects verb-shaped prose false positives (#1026).** Replaced `asyncio.create_subprocess_exec` (unsupported by `WindowsSelectorEventLoopPolicy`) with argv-only, `shell=False` `subprocess.Popen` in an executor worker. The resolver uses cancellation signaling plus shielded worker completion so timeout, parent cancellation, and whole-loop cancel-all each kill and reap exactly once before return/re-raise. Git invocation keeps explicit `cwd` and option termination. Entity extraction now applies a focused grammar stop-set after the locator regex: ordinary continuations such as `node is/seems/appears/not`, `record shows/indicates/exists/of`, and `entity was/may/to` are rejected while alphabetic identifiers (`oracle`, `alpha`, `atlas`), `oracle_probe`, `alpha_1`, hyphenated IDs, explicit `node id`, and hex IDs remain valid. Default-OFF still creates no resolver/subprocess. **Tests:** 24 AD-1119 focused + 21 AD-1120/1121 blast = **45 passed**, including a real temporary Git repo under an installed Windows selector policy, off-loop thread assertion, cancel-all lifecycle, timeout/cancellation cleanup, and extraction matrices; Architect review APPROVED. closes #1026.

**BF-659 shipped (2026-07-09) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — BF-654 endpoint caps are now hard, cancellation-safe, and keyed to the endpoint actually used (#1025).** Removed the endpoint timeout fail-open that recreated the synchronized overload herd (cap=1 previously reached peak=5); background transport now waits cancellably for the actual `attempt_tier` permit. Priority-lane cleanup begins immediately after acquisition, so cancellation at endpoint wait, transport, BF-612 jitter/refresh/retry, or AD-617 429 backoff restores every held permit. A task-local `ContextVar` preserves CRITICAL bypass without changing `_complete_inner(request)` or leaking state across nested/concurrent calls. Endpoint permits span the complete same-tier retry lifecycle and release before fallback; client lookup occurs after permit acquisition so a queued caller cannot retain a client closed by the preceding holder's BF-612 refresh. The `max_inflight_per_endpoint <= 0` escape hatch and AD-636 lane-timeout policy remain unchanged. **Tests:** 23 focused BF-654/BF-659 + 164 blast-radius priority/rate/retry/health/per-tier/client tests = **187 passed**; Architect implementation review APPROVED. closes #1025.

**AD-1121 shipped (2026-07-09) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — Live cascade-confab divergence probe (detection): a default-OFF, best-effort, NON-BLOCKING context-free self-consistency probe (SelfCheckGPT arXiv 2303.08896 / CoVe arXiv 2309.11495) that fires ONLY on an AD-1119-UNRESOLVED central room referent, flags a probable `CASCADE_CONFAB` in near-real-time, records it via the AD-454 taxonomy pipeline, and NOTIFIES the Captain — COMPLETING the cascade-confabulation prevention epic (AD-1119 guard / AD-1120 behavioral / AD-1121 detection). Additive + DEFAULT-OFF → BYTE-IDENTICAL when `config.grounding.confab_probe_enabled=False` (#1024).** STEP-0 empirical descope: the epic sketch bundled "persist room transcripts (in-RAM only)" but that forensic note was about `ward_room.db`, NOT the group-chat store — verified against HEAD, `ChatThreadStore` (`threads/__init__.py`) opens `sqlite3.connect(chat_threads.db, isolation_level=None)` = AUTOCOMMIT, so every `append_message` is durably on-disk (the Captain empirically recovered a month-old thread via `GET /api/threads/{id}/messages`). Rebuilding persistence would duplicate a shipped capability (DRY) → **transcript persistence REMOVED from AD-1121 scope; AD-1121 = probe + detection only.** **(1)** NEW `src/probos/cognitive/confab_probe.py` (cognitive layer, `runtime`-free — DIP: `llm_client` INJECTED, never imported): frozen `ProbeResult` (`token`/`usable`/`affirm`/`is_divergent`/`samples` + `affirm_rate` property), pure `_classify_existence(text) -> "AFFIRM"|"NOT_AFFIRM"` (denial-marker regex `_CONFAB_DENIAL_RE`: `no record`/`does not exist`/`no such`/`not aware`/`cannot find`/`no … named`/`not a real`/`fictional`/`unknown`/`no information`/`i (have|find) no`/`unable to (find|locate)`/`not (a )?(standard|known|valid)`), and `async probe_referent(llm_client, token, ...)` — **CONTEXT-FREE BY SIGNATURE**: it takes ONLY the referent token, so the seed/transcript has no parameter through which to reach the LLM (enforced by construction, not discipline). N=3 independent draws (`tier="fast"`, `temperature=1.0`, `max_tokens=160`) via `asyncio.gather(..., return_exceptions=True)`; divergence = existence-affirmation consistency — FLAG iff `affirm_rate < 0.5` AND `usable >= 2`; `<2` usable → ABSTAIN (no flag). Honest-degrade at every boundary (None client / per-sample raise / empty batch / any raise → non-divergent; NEVER a false flag). **(2)** `evidence_collector.py` — additive public `record_observation(*, behavior_code, thread_id, author_id, author_callsign="", reasoning="", confidence=1.0)` persisting a PRE-CLASSIFIED `EvidenceObservation(behavior_codes=(CASCADE_CONFAB,), …)` reusing the existing dedup window + gapless `OBS-NNNN.yaml` numbering (`_is_duplicate`/`_persist`/`_record_dedup`), `author_id=thread_id` → one OBS per room per window; Tier-2 (None on dedup/persist fail). CALLED ONLY by AD-1121 → AD-454 byte-identical. **(3)** `runtime.py` — public `self.confab_probe_tasks: set[asyncio.Task]` (mirrors `_nats_publish_tasks`; the seam registers the best-effort probe task without reaching a private attr). **(4)** `routers/thread_fanout.py` — the AD-1120 `_select_central_cue` is refactored into a token-selector `_select_central_referent(verdict, seed_text) -> str | None` (same kind/stop-word filter + the ONE git-HEAD availability probe, now returning the TOKEN); `_observe_referent_grounding`'s tail is restructured to `if not (confab_probe_enabled or ground_before_collaborate_enabled): return None` (so the 3 AD-1119 call-site tests still hit `None` with no second git-HEAD probe, and the AD-1120 B2 cue path is byte-identical), computes the central token ONCE, and — when `confab_probe_enabled` — schedules NEW `_probe_cascade_confab(runtime, thread, token)` as a best-effort `asyncio.create_task` + `add_done_callback(discard)` (NEVER blocks the crew reply). On a divergence flag `_probe_cascade_confab` records the OBS (if `evidence_collector` wired — else honest-degrade) and ALWAYS posts `runtime.notification_queue.notify(..., notification_type="action_required", suggested_action=None)` — SURFACE, don't act (no auto-terminate / room close; the verdict is a signal for human adjudication). **(5)** `config.py` — NEW `GroundingConfig.confab_probe_enabled: bool = False` (third flag; no effect unless `referent_gate_enabled` is also on). **Precision guard:** the probe runs ONLY on a referent AD-1119's deterministic resolvers ALREADY marked UNRESOLVED — a real referent they know is RESOLVED → never central → never probed. **Tests** `tests/test_ad1121_confab_probe.py` (10, BF-287 real fixtures — real `SystemConfig`/`NotificationQueue`/`EvidenceCollector(tmp)` + a scripted `_ScriptedLLM` recording every request, NO MagicMock; git avoided via an `entity`-kind central token `node oracle_probe`): divergent (3 denials) → OBS + `action_required` notification naming the token (`suggested_action=None`); consistent (3 affirmations) → neither; **context-free** — a `SECRET_CANARY_TOKEN` seed NEVER appears in ANY recorded prompt/`system_prompt` (only the token does); failure/abstain (all-raise, `None` client, 1-usable) → non-divergent, no OBS/notify; `_classify_existence` unit; **default-OFF golden** — `confab_probe_enabled=False` → observe returns None, ZERO tasks scheduled, ZERO probe requests; `record_observation` dedup; **non-blocking** — a slow LLM returns the AD-1120 cue immediately with the probe still pending; `affirm_rate`; config default OFF. Focused `-q -n 0` → **10 passed**; MANDATORY regression AD-1119 (15) + AD-1120 (11) `-q -n 0` → **26 passed, UNCHANGED**; blast radius (9 group-chat/`thread_fanout` files + `test_notifications` + `test_ad1053` + `test_ad454_evidence_collector` + `test_ad454_taxonomy` + `test_config`) → **168 passed, 0 regressions**. `get_errors` clean on all 6 created/modified files. Highest landed top-level was **AD-1120**; **AD-1121 is the new top-level**. Left UNCOMMITTED (LOCAL — the Captain decides the push).

**AD-1120 shipped (2026-07-09) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — Ground-Before-Collaborate: turn AD-1119's OBSERVED honest-absence cue into an INJECTED one (the behavioral guard half of the cascade-confabulation defense) + an always-on constitutional standing order that makes "no such referent" a REWARDED terminal finding. Two-flag dependency; the flag-gated injection path is DEFAULT-OFF → BYTE-IDENTICAL when `config.grounding.ground_before_collaborate_enabled=False` (#1023).** Builds ONLY on AD-1119's shipped verdict/resolver API (no `referent_gate.py` edit). Live-runtime forensics (2026-07-08) traced a crew "Oracle Health Check" that reasoned at length about fabricated node `e77acec7`; AD-1119 shipped the deterministic *observe-only* guard (logs a gap-clean honest-absence cue, changes nothing). AD-1120 closes the behavioral half: when enabled, STEER the crew to the "structurally unresolvable" close instead of confabulating on a fabricated referent. **(1) `config.py`** — NEW `GroundingConfig.ground_before_collaborate_enabled: bool = False` (default OFF; no effect unless `referent_gate_enabled` is ALSO on — the two-flag dependency). **(2) `routers/thread_fanout.py`** — the AD-1119 verdict is computed ONCE at the fan-out choke point: `_observe_referent_grounding` keeps its name + the AD-1119 first-line early-return + the observe loop VERBATIM (the 3 landed AD-1119 call-site tests stay green), changes its return `None → str | None`, and — only when BOTH flags are on — RETURNS the CENTRAL unresolved referent's cue via NEW `_select_central_cue(verdict, seed_text)`. Central = the FIRST `verdict.unresolved` token whose kind ∈ `_GROUNDING_INJECT_KINDS={hex,entity}`, `token.lower() not in _GROUNDING_STOPWORDS`, and (for `hex`) git is available. **Central-referent selection** re-calls `extract_referents` (pure — NOT a re-resolve) for the `{token:kind}` map the frozen `GroundingVerdict` doesn't carry, DROPS the `service` kind entirely (kills the AD-1119 DD-5 `"The node" → "The"` determiner false-positive WITHOUT touching the AD-1119 regex), and the stop-word set is defense-in-depth for a determiner `entity` capture (`"node the" → "the"`). **Git-availability gate:** probe ONCE via `GitObjectResolver().resolve("HEAD")` (reuses the shipped resolver as a positive control) ONLY when a hex candidate exists; probe `False` (git-less deploy / non-repo) → DROP hex candidates (fail safe, so a git-less deploy does not falsely flag every hex as fabricated); `entity` needs no git. The cue is threaded `group_chat_fanout` (captured, not discarded) → `_fan_one_round(grounding_cue=…)` (both round-0 + cascade call sites) → `_send_one` sets `params["grounding_cue"]` BESIDE the AD-967 `params["room_roster"]` (rides the exact same tested param seam; omitted when None → byte-identical). **(3) `cognitive/cognitive_agent.py`** — NEW overridable hook `_conversational_grounding_cue_block(observation)` (next to `_conversational_room_outputs_block`): returns `""` unless `params["is_group_chat"]` AND a non-blank `grounding_cue` is attached, else `"\n\n"+cue`; composed in the conversational branch immediately AFTER the BF-651 `_outputs_proto` block. The cue is the AD-1119 string VERBATIM — already `is_capability_gap`-clean. **(4) `config/standing_orders/ship.md`** — NEW always-on `<!-- category: core_directives --> ## Ground Before You Collaborate` section (Tier-2 `ship.md` is loaded ship-wide for every crew agent — a new `grounding.md` would be DEAD since `compose_instructions` loads only named tiers; the AD-729a precedent): no multi-agent investigation proceeds past framing until its central referent is grounded; "there is no such referent; the question is structurally unresolvable" is the correct, VALUED finding → close the room. Gap-clean (`is_capability_gap==False`, verified). **Byte-identity scope:** the always-on `ship.md` prose DOES change the composed prompt vs HEAD by design (the one judgment-call constitutional addition, AD-729a precedent); the DEFAULT-OFF byte-identity guarantee is scoped to the FLAG-GATED CUE PATH (no `grounding_cue` param, hook renders `""` when off) — NOT a "full agent prompt == HEAD" golden. **NOT built (AD-1121 boundary):** no divergence probe, no transcript persistence, no auto-close/delete of threads ("the room closes" is LLM guidance via the cue + standing order, not a code path), no trust-scoring change, no new consensus path, no `referent_gate.py` edit, no `standing_orders.py` change, no second wiring seam, no HTTP. **Tests:** NEW `tests/test_ad1120_ground_before_collaborate.py` (11, BF-287 real fixtures — real `AgentRegistry()`, a real `tmp_path` git repo (`_init_git_repo`), real `SystemConfig()`, `_RealAgent(BaseAgent)`, `SimpleNamespace` runtimes, `@_requires_git` where git is exercised; NO MagicMock at the registry/git/ward-room boundary): headline hex cue injected (both flags on, real tmp repo, `e77acec7` absent → non-None gap-clean cue containing `e77acec7`); determiner/service token NOT injected (seed `"The membership is degraded."` → `service "The"` dropped → None; PLUS the stop-word `entity` path `"check node the now"` → `"the"` dropped → None); no hex cue when git unavailable (patched probe → None); no cue when the central resolves (real registered `beef1234` → None); used `"The membership is degraded."` (service `"The"` only) — faithful to the pinned `_select_central_cue`, only the example seed changed. **Gates:** focused `-q -n 0` → **11 passed**; AD-1119 regression gate `tests/test_ad1119_referent_gate.py -q -n 0` → **15 passed** (unchanged — the `_observe_referent_grounding` name/None-contract preserved); **AD-1119 shipped (2026-07-08) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — Referent-Grounding Gate (guard G1): a default-OFF, observe-only cognitive gate that resolves each candidate referent in a group-chat room seed against ship ground truth (git object / agent / ward-room channel) BEFORE the crew reasons on it, logging a gap-regex-safe honest-absence cue for the unresolved ones — the highest-leverage prevention for the cascade-confabulation class (the live 2026-07-08 `e77acec7` fabricated-node case, #1022). Additive + DEFAULT-OFF → BYTE-IDENTICAL when `config.grounding.referent_gate_enabled=False`.** Live-runtime forensics traced a crew "Oracle Health Check" investigation that reasoned at length about node `e77acec7` — a fabricated id (not a git object, an agent, a channel, or in any source/DB). Root mechanism: no agent verifies an identifier exists before reasoning about it. AD-1119 is the deterministic *guard* half of a defense-in-depth pair (AD-1120 behavioral / AD-1121 detection follow, NOT built here). **(1)** NEW `src/probos/cognitive/referent_gate.py` (cognitive layer, `runtime`-free — DIP): `extract_referents(text)` (pure; strips fenced ```` ``` ````/inline `` `code` `` spans first, then 3 regex kinds — hex `\b(?=[0-9a-fA-F]*[a-fA-F])[0-9a-fA-F]{7,40}\b` excludes plain decimals, `node|record|entity <tok>`, and a conservative Capitalized/`*_service` + `service|membership|telemetry|cluster|node` span — ordered by first appearance, deduped, capped 20); frozen `Referent`/`GroundingVerdict` (with `has_unresolved`); a `ReferentResolver` `typing.Protocol`; three honest-degrading resolvers — `GitObjectResolver` (`asyncio.create_subprocess_exec("git","cat-file","-e", f"{token}^{{object}}", cwd=repo_root)`; missing git / non-repo / timeout → False, never raises, never false-RESOLVED; `repo_root` constructor-injected, default `parents[3]`), `AgentResolver` (registry id / pool / callsign, liveness-independent), `WardRoomResolver` (guards `ward_room is None`); `ReferentGroundingGate.evaluate(text)` (try-all-resolvers, first-True → RESOLVED, all-False → UNRESOLVED + cue; NEVER raises — extraction guarded + each resolver individually wrapped); a `@staticmethod _honest_absence_cue` (gap-regex-safe — `is_capability_gap(cue) is False`); and a narrow-dep `build_default_resolvers(*, registry, callsign_registry, ward_room, repo_root=None)` factory. **(2)** `routers/thread_fanout.py` — NEW `async def _observe_referent_grounding(runtime, thread, seed_text)` mirroring `_observe_conversation_corrections` (first line = the flag early-return → byte-identical when off; builds the gate from `runtime.registry`/`callsign_registry`/`getattr(runtime,"ward_room",None)`; emits one `AD-1119[observe]` WARNING per unresolved token; Tier-2 honest-degrade), wired as ONE awaited call at `group_chat_fanout` immediately after `agent_ids = crew_agent_participants(...)`. OBSERVE-ONLY: the cue is computed + logged, NEVER injected into any agent's context (that is AD-1120), `all_replies` untouched. **(3)** `config.py` — NEW `class GroundingConfig(BaseModel)` (`referent_gate_enabled: bool = False`) mounted on `SystemConfig` beside `os_activity`/`device` (default OFF; no `config/system.yaml` edit — model default). **Tests:** NEW `tests/test_ad1119_referent_gate.py` (15, BF-287 real fixtures — real `AgentRegistry()`, a real `tmp_path` git repo, real `SystemConfig()`, `ward_room=None`; the only stubs are a real Protocol-implementing `_RaisingResolver` + a real minimal `BaseAgent` subclass, NO MagicMock at the registry/git/ward-room boundary): extraction happy/negatives/dedupe; RESOLVED via a real git object (full + 8-char abbreviation); RESOLVED via a real registered agent (id + pool); the headline `e77acec7` → UNRESOLVED + a non-empty cue with `is_capability_gap(cue) is False`; resolver honest-degrade (a raising resolver does not bubble); git-missing/non-repo honest-degrade; the DEFAULT-OFF golden `test_observe_off_is_noop` (a spy proves `build_default_resolvers` is called 0 times + zero `AD-1119` log records — the byte-identity guarantee); flag-ON observe emits exactly one WARNING and mutates nothing; and `SystemConfig().grounding.referent_gate_enabled is False`. Gate: focused `-q -n 0` → **15 passed**; blast radius (28 `thread_fanout`-importing test files + `tests/test_config.py`) `-q -n 0` → **297 passed, 0 regressions**. `get_errors` clean on all four files. Highest landed top-level was AD-1118; **AD-1119 is the new top-level**. Left UNCOMMITTED (LOCAL — the Captain decides the push).

**BF-658 shipped (2026-07-08) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — benign concurrent-deletion race in the KnowledgeStore glob-then-async-read loaders logged a misleading `Failed to load episode … [Errno 2] No such file or directory` WARNING (#1021).** `load_episodes` globs `episodes/*.json` (a snapshot) then reads each with `await self._read_json(fp)`, which runs `path.read_text` in an executor — so the `await` yields to the event loop. A concurrent `store_episode` → `_evict_episodes` (`fp.unlink()`) deletes an over-capacity episode that was in the glob snapshot before its read → `FileNotFoundError` → the broad `except Exception` reported it as a load failure. **Benign** (the episode was being evicted anyway; the git-backed store is secondary persistence, live memory is ChromaDB) but the WARNING misleadingly implied data loss. Same latent pattern in the two sibling glob-loop loaders (`load_agents` ↔ `remove_agent`, `load_skills` ↔ `remove_skill`). **Fix:** a `except FileNotFoundError:` branch (DEBUG, "vanished between listing and read (concurrent eviction/removal)") BEFORE the broad `except Exception:` in all three loaders — the raced-away case is skipped quietly while genuine parse/schema errors still WARN. The three single-file loaders (trust/workflow/manifest) are untouched (a failure there is real). Idiomatic "handle the race at the point of use" — no lock/exists()-precheck. **Tests:** NEW `tests/test_knowledge_store.py::…::test_load_episodes_concurrent_deletion_skips_quietly` (raced file → surviving episode loads, no WARNING, one DEBUG) + `test_load_episodes_corrupt_file_still_warns` (a genuinely unparseable file STILL WARNs — the carve-out doesn't swallow real errors). Gate: `-k "load_episodes or eviction or concurrent_deletion or corrupt_file or store_episode or load_agents or load_skills"` **18 passed**. closes #1021.

**BF-657 shipped (2026-07-07) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — CI `python-tests` red for weeks: ChromaDB collections were created with `embedding_function=None`, which does NOT disable embeddings — Chroma substitutes its OWN network-downloaded default ONNX EF (all-MiniLM), which lazily fetches `~/.cache/chroma/onnx_models/.../onnx.tar.gz` at the first `.add()`/`.query()`. CI blocks that download (and wiped the cache every run), so ~14 embedding-dependent tests died with `FileNotFoundError: onnx.tar.gz` (#1020).** The AD-170 "no embeddings → keyword fallback" intent was defeated at collection creation: `get_embedding_function()` returns `None` when both real-model downloads fail, and all 5 collection sites passed that `None` straight into `get_or_create_collection`. Local passed only because the model was cached; CI cannot download it. **Fix (all in `knowledge/embeddings.py` + mechanical swaps):** **(A)** NEW `LocalHashEmbeddingFunction` — a fully chromadb-1.5.8-protocol-compliant (`__init__`/`__call__`/`name`/`get_config`/`build_from_config`, 0 `DeprecationWarning`s) network-free deterministic bag-of-hashed-tokens EF: reuses `_tokenize` (stop-words stripped → correct lexical ranking), 384-dim L2-normalized, and a STABLE `_stable_bucket` (blake2b digest, NOT builtin `hash()` which is `PYTHONHASHSEED`-salted → would break persisted vectors after a reboot). Proven `PersistentClient` create→add→query→REOPEN with count preserved, no network. **(B)** NEW `get_collection_embedding_function()` = `get_embedding_function() or LocalHashEmbeddingFunction()` — NEVER `None`; swapped at the 5 EF sites (`episodic.py:685`/`:1299`, `procedure_store.py`, `evolution_store.py`, `semantic.py`). **(C)** NEW `get_active_embedding_model_name()` = local name when `get_embedding_function() is None` else the real name — honest offline labeling at 4 metadata sites (`episodic.py:1338`, `cognitive_services.py:418`, `procedure_store.py`, `semantic.py`) so an offline→online boot re-embeds cleanly; Chroma's EF-config conflict detection remains the PRIMARY migration guard (`except ValueError "Embedding function conflict"` recovery untouched). **(D)** NEW `PROBOS_EMBEDDINGS=local` short-circuit at the top of `get_embedding_function()` (skips the slow HF/Chroma-S3 download probes, returns `None`; `embed_text`/`compute_similarity` keyword fallback + all other behavior byte-identical for any other/unset value). **(E)** `.github/workflows/ci.yml` `python-tests` now sets job-level `env: PROBOS_EMBEDDINGS: local` and the dead `Clear stale chroma onnx cache` step is removed. `embed_text`/`compute_similarity`/`_keyword_embedding`/`get_embedding_model_name`/`migrate_embedding_model` param write + `config.py`/`section_registry.py` `embedding_model` all UNTOUCHED. **Tests:** NEW `tests/test_bf657_local_embedding_fallback.py` (10 — protocol compliance/no-warning, deterministic+384-dim+empty-zero, lexical ranking, never-None + passthrough + active-name, real `PersistentClient` add/query/reopen network-free, and the trap: episodic-seed + semantic-index re-run under forced-local with the memo reset). Gates: forced-local (CI-equivalent) **70 passed**; real-model (toggle unset) **20 passed**; blast radius (embeddings/episodic/semantic/procedure_store consumers) **376 passed**. No existing test obsoleted. closes #1020.

**BF-656 shipped (2026-07-07) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — boot-log hygiene: eliminate two benign-but-recurring boot WARNINGs that fired on every boot for PERMANENT (not transient) conditions, eroding the signal value of the WARNING level (#1019). Two disjoint, surgical, root-cause fixes.** **(A)** The AD-423a ontology seed registered a no-op `codebase_query` `DirectServiceAdapter` placeholder that the real AD-544 native `CodebaseQueryTool` then legitimately replaced → one `probos.tools.registry — Replacing existing tool registration: codebase_query` every boot. `codebase_query` has since GRADUATED to a real runtime tool, so the taxonomy placeholder is redundant AND colliding. Fix = remove the `codebase_query` entry from `config/ontology/resources.yaml` `tool_capabilities` (replaced by a BF-656 explanatory comment); the seed now iterates 6 capabilities instead of 7, so there is no placeholder to collide with. ZERO production code: `ToolRegistry.register`'s replace-warning guard is 100% intact for genuine dupes, the AD-423a seed loop and `_noop_handler` are untouched, and the real `CodebaseQueryTool` (tool_id `codebase_query`, `ensign→read` perms) is unchanged → all crew keep read access. **(B)** Two stale 6/22 test-artifact skills (`new_capability`, `test_skill` — 12-byte `# test skill` stubs that exec cleanly but have no `handle_<name>` function) could NEVER restore, so warm boot re-warned `probos.warm_boot — Warm boot: no handler function for skill …` per skill, forever. Fix = self-cleaning prune: a new `KnowledgeStore.remove_skill(intent_name)` (mirrors `remove_agent` — unlink `.py`+`.json` + `_schedule_commit`), called on the warm-boot `handler is None` branch ONLY, replacing the WARNING with an INFO "pruning from knowledge store" line, wrapped in log-and-degrade so a prune failure never aborts the restore loop. Scoped STRICTLY to the permanent no-handler case: the outer transient `except` (SyntaxError/ImportError/top-level raise) is UNTOUCHED → those still log "restore failed" and are NEVER pruned (may be environment-recoverable). Mutates only gitignored `data/knowledge/` state (its own git repo), never the OSS tree. **Tests:** NEW `tests/test_bf656_boot_log_hygiene.py` (8): Part A — ontology no longer exposes `codebase_query` (len 6), native tool still owns the id (access preserved), replace-guard still warns for a real dupe, and (HEADLINE) seeding the ontology then registering `codebase_query` emits NO replace warning (the direct regression guard); Part B — `remove_skill` deletes both files + schedules the commit, (HEADLINE) a no-handler skill is pruned + INFO-logged with the old WARNING gone, a valid skill still restores and is NOT pruned, and (SAFETY) a transient exec-error skill is NOT pruned and still logs "restore failed". One obsolete-contract assertion updated (`test_ontology_ops_comms_resources.py::test_all_capabilities` `>= 7` → `>= 6`; the filtered `>=4`/`>=2` + partition-invariant asserts stay green: all_crew 5→4, 4+2==6). Gates: `test_bf656_boot_log_hygiene`+`test_ontology_ops_comms_resources`+`test_ad544_native_tools` **49 passed**; `test_ad423a_tool_foundation`+`test_ontology` **61 passed** (both `-n 0`, isolated `PROBOS_DATA_DIR`). One shared backend BF sequence (no new AD); roadmap Bug Tracker row skipped (BF rows stopped at BF-624, per BF-652/654/655 precedent).

**BF-655 shipped (2026-07-07) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — balanced TTS `start`/`end` producer so a 1:1 call's "speaking" state (voice-modulation icon, avatar head-bob, PTT mic lock) never latches when a terminal `end` is missed (#1018). Producer-first fix in `ui/src/audio/voice.ts` + a bounded PTT self-heal watchdog.** All three symptoms are downstream of the SAME `onSpeechEvent` `start`/`end` pair: each consumer latches "speaking" on `start` and clears ONLY on a matching `end`, with NO self-heal → one missed `end` strands the state until remount. Two terminal paths fired `start` without `end`: the server supersede/pause (`_synthesizeAndPlay` `'pause'` → `_finish(false)`, when a newer `speakResponse` pauses the prior audio — the AD-1062 call-open greeting overlapping a PTT reply is the call-specific trigger) and the browser cancel/interrupt (`_speakBrowserFallback` had no `onerror`, so `speechSynthesis.cancel()` fires `onerror`, not `onend`). **Fix (producer-first, two localized edits):** **(A)** server `'pause'` listener now `_finish(true)` — a superseded/paused utterance emits its terminal `end` (same `agent_id`, `source:'server'`); the existing `_settled` guard prevents any double-`end` under `ended`/`error`+`pause`; the AD-1071 queue is untouched (it stops on the `_speakGeneration` token, not `end`, and `_finish` still `resolve()`s). `fireEnd=false` is now used ONLY on the `play()`-reject path (no `start` fired → balanced). **(B)** `_speakBrowserFallback` wires `onerror` and a single-settle closure bool so whichever of `onend`/`onerror` fires first emits exactly one `end` (`source:'browser'`), the other a no-op. Together, `start`/`end` are balanced on every terminal path → the icon, head-bob, PTT gate, AND the hot-mic conversation-controller completion clear with NO consumer change. **Defense-in-depth:** the BF-300 PTT gate (`ProfileChatTab.tsx`, the only FUNCTIONAL symptom — icon/head-bob are cosmetic) gained a bounded `PTT_TTS_WATCHDOG_MS=45000` self-heal — a `useRef` timer cleared-then-armed on `start` (reset-on-fresh-start), cleared on `end`, and cleared on effect cleanup (no dangling timer, required by the `bf300` real-timer suite). Scoped to the PTT consumer only (ModulationIndicator/CrewVRM stay cosmetic-self-clear; extending is a FORWARD option). **Tests:** NEW `ui/src/audio/__tests__/voice.balancedEnd.test.tsx` (10: server single-end, supersede-pause fires `end` for the older utterance [HEADLINE stuck-latch regression], no-double-end on ended+pause, browser cancel via `onerror`, browser `onend` single end, AD-1071 flag-OFF single POST, + watchdog mirror ceiling-self-heal / reset-on-fresh-start / cleared-on-end and 2 `?raw` source-wiring assertions). Gate: the 8 required suites **48 passed**; full UI **2024 passed / 1 skipped**; `npm run build` (tsc -b) clean. `speakResponse` signature, AD-1071 `_speakGeneration` queue, `runSentenceQueue`/`voiceChunking.ts`, BF-300 gate semantics + mic early-return, BF-621 meeting reveal (`meetingVoice.ts`/`useMeetingVoice.ts`) all UNTOUCHED. `config/system.yaml` UNTOUCHED. UNCOMMITTED (the Captain validates first).

**BF-654 shipped (2026-07-06) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — per-endpoint in-flight concurrency cap for the LLM client (#1017). Additive over AD-636 lanes + BF-612 recycle; DEFAULT-ENABLED (cap 8) with a `<=0` escape hatch that is BYTE-IDENTICAL to pre-BF-654.** On `probos serve` boot, ~80 concurrent background LLM calls all hit the 30s AD-636 lane-acquire timeout together and FAIL OPEN (`sem=None`) at once → flood the shared Copilot proxy (`127.0.0.1:8080`) into an empty-content storm. The one shared client made the lanes global, but the fail-open converted the lane cap into unbounded concurrency in exactly the boot-burst condition. **Fix:** a hard per-endpoint in-flight `asyncio.Semaphore` keyed by `_client_key` (`base_url|api_format` — the httpx-pool key), acquired in `complete()` AROUND `_complete_inner`, BACKGROUND-ONLY (CRITICAL/interactive bypasses so Captain DMs are never queued behind boot work). Text tiers (`fast/standard/deep`) share ONE cap (the proxy); vision/ollama (distinct `_client_key`) gets its own → never throttled by the text cap. **Deadlock-free:** single global lock order L(lane)→E(endpoint), release E before L. Longer endpoint acquire timeout (`_endpoint_acquire_timeout=120.0`, < the 300s deep httpx timeout) with a jittered last-resort fail-open (`_endpoint_failopen_jitter_seconds=0.5`) so a saturated herd de-syncs instead of re-flooding. **(1)** `config.py` — NEW `LLMRateConfig.max_inflight_per_endpoint: int = 8` (`<=0` disables → no semaphores → acquire block skipped → byte-identical). **(2)** `cognitive/llm_client.py` — `import random`; construct `self._endpoint_semaphores` per distinct `_client_key` in `__init__` (mirrors the AD-636 semaphores; no running loop needed on 3.10+); acquire/release in `complete()`; optional cap-gated jitter before the BF-612 `_refresh_client` (no-op when disabled). AD-636 lanes, BF-612 recycle, `_complete_inner` signature, RPM limiter all UNTOUCHED. **Tests:** NEW `tests/test_bf654_endpoint_concurrency_cap.py` (10, BF-287 real fixtures + a fake slow transport counting concurrent entries) — HEADLINE: peak concurrent in-flight to one endpoint `<=8` under a 30-call burst AND a counterfactual proving the SAME burst floods to `>8` when the cap is disabled (the cap is load-bearing); ollama endpoint independent; CRITICAL bypass; no-deadlock mixed priorities; fail-open on timeout; `<=0` byte-identical; per-`_client_key` keying; BF-612 jitter only when enabled. Gate: `pytest tests/test_bf654_endpoint_concurrency_cap.py tests/test_ad636_llm_priority_scheduling.py tests/test_ad637f_priority.py tests/test_bf612_empty_content_retry.py tests/test_llm_client.py -q -n 0` → **100 passed** (existing AD-636/AD-637f/BF-612/llm_client suites UNCHANGED). `get_errors` clean on all edited files. `config/system.yaml` UNTOUCHED. UNCOMMITTED (the Captain validates first).

**AD-1095 shipped (2026-07-01) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — ARD detached-signature verification primitive: the OSS plumbing for the commercial AD-C-027 attestation issuance (boundary: verify=OSS, sign/issue=commercial). Additive + DEFAULT-INERT → BYTE-IDENTICAL to the AD-1047 honest-degrade when no issuer key is supplied.** Completes the JWS-verify AD-1047 explicitly deferred (`signature_verified` was hardcoded `False`). **(1)** `federation/ard/trust_verifier.py` — NEW pure `verify_manifest_signature(manifest, *, issuer_public_key_b64=None) -> bool`: canonicalizes `manifest.to_dict()` MINUS the `signature` field with `sort_keys=True, separators=(",",":")` (the interop contract a commercial issuer MUST reproduce) and verifies the detached signature via the AD-843b substrate Ed25519 `device_pairing.verify_signature` (NO new crypto dep — `cryptography>=42` is core). Honest-degrade: no signature / no key / any error → `False`. **(2)** `verify_entry(...)` gained an optional keyword `issuer_public_key_b64: str|None=None`; the hardcoded `signature_verified=False` is now conditional on a supplied key, so the `+_W_SIGNATURE` evidence weight fires ONLY on a cryptographically-verified signature. **DEFAULT-INERT:** with no key (every existing caller), `signature_verified` stays `False` → byte-identical (AD-1047's `test_signature_present_but_always_unverified_v1` passes UNCHANGED). **Tests:** NEW `tests/test_ad1095_manifest_signature_verify.py` (14, REAL Ed25519 via `generate_keypair`/`sign_challenge` — NO mocks): valid-sig verified + alpha 3.0; no-key backward-compat (alpha 2.0); wrong-key / tampered-sig fail; the canonicalization interop contract (documented form verifies, a non-sorted serialization does NOT). Gate: `test_ad1095` + `test_ad1047` **23 passed** (AD-1047 unchanged). Current highest top-level was AD-1094; **AD-1095 is the new top-level** (the OSS half of commercial #42/AD-C-027).

**AD-1071 shipped (2026-06-30) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — sentence-chunked TTS pipelining (voice edge) v1 (epic #1006; AD-1071 is the reserved epic sub-number, NOT a new top-level). Additive + DEFAULT-OFF → BYTE-IDENTICAL when `config.tts.sentence_pipelining_enabled=False`.** The SAFE slice of "streaming reply → TTS": full LLM streaming is explicitly OUT of scope (a separate multi-week AD). v1 keeps the non-streaming LLM reply, then — when the operator opts in AND the backend is Piper AND the reply has >1 sentence — splits the finished reply into sentences and synthesizes + plays them SEQUENTIALLY so the first audio starts after synthesizing only the FIRST sentence (cutting time-to-first-audio) instead of the whole reply. When OFF (default) OR browser backend OR a single sentence, one TTS POST per full reply runs UNCHANGED. **(1)** `config.py` — NEW `TTSConfig.sentence_pipelining_enabled: bool = False` (default-OFF; no `config/system.yaml` edit — model default). **(2)** `routers/avatars.py` — `GET /api/avatars/tts/status` now surfaces `sentence_pipelining_enabled` (both the `tts`-present and `cfg is None` branches), mirroring how `enabled`/`backend` are surfaced, so the browser reads it via its existing one-time status probe. **(3)** NEW `ui/src/audio/voiceChunking.ts` — PURE, jsdom-testable-without-Audio: `splitIntoSentences(text, maxChunks=40)` (splits on `[.!?]`+whitespace, trims, drops empties, keeps a trailing fragment, a small abbreviation guard so `Dr.`/`Mr.` don't split, caps at 40 merging overflow into the last chunk, `[text]` for 0-1 sentences, `[]` for empty) + `runSentenceQueue(items, processOne, shouldContinue?)` (strictly-ordered async queue: item[i+1] starts only after item[i]'s Promise resolves; honest-degrade swallow-and-advance; `shouldContinue` early-stop for cancellation). **(4)** `ui/src/audio/voice.ts` — `TtsStatus` gained `sentence_pipelining_enabled` (parsed at all 4 object-literal sites, default-OFF when absent/older-runtime); extracted the per-utterance POST+Audio+visemes side-effect into `_synthesizeAndPlay(text, profile, agent_id, emotion)` (now resolves on the audio's `ended` so the queue can advance; adds a `pause` listener that resolves WITHOUT a spurious `end` — matching the pre-AD-1071 single-call path — so a superseded queue stops cleanly); NEW monotonic `_speakGeneration` token bumped at the top of every `speakResponse` so a newer reply cancels an in-flight queue; the piper branch of the `speakResponse` IIFE now pipelines when `status.sentence_pipelining_enabled && splitIntoSentences(text).length > 1`, else calls the single `_synthesizeAndPlay(text, …)` path. Call sites (`IntentSurface.tsx`) UNCHANGED — the flag is read inside `speakResponse`. Each sentence still gets its own visemes for lip-sync; a failed sentence synth logs + falls back to browser for THAT sentence and continues. **Default-OFF byte-identical guarantee (load-bearing):** flag OFF ⇒ the piper path takes the single-call `_synthesizeAndPlay(text)` branch (one POST of the FULL reply) — proven by the new `voice.pipelining.test.tsx` "multi-sentence reply makes exactly ONE POST of the FULL text" test AND the unchanged 9-test `voice.serverTts.test.tsx` suite. **Gates:** backend `pytest tests/test_ad1071_tts_pipelining.py tests/test_ad738_piper_tts.py -q -n 0` → **31 passed** (5 new + 26 existing, incl. 3 status-shape contract tests updated for the new field); UI `vitest run voiceChunking.test.ts voice.pipelining.test.tsx voice.serverTts.test.tsx` → **27 passed** (13 pure + 5 pipelining + 9 unchanged serverTts); `npm run build` clean; `get_errors` clean on all edited files. `config/system.yaml` UNTOUCHED. NEW `tests/test_ad843c1_device_actuation.py::test_device_missing_after_authorize_fails_closed` (BF-287 real fixtures: a `DeviceNodeRegistry` subclass whose `authorize()`→True but `get_device()`→None drives the race; a counting `_CountingAdapter` + a recording `_RecordingTrustNetwork` assert actuate-never-called + record_outcome-never-called + the audit episode). **AD-1072 shipped (2026-06-29) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — tool-search + sub-agent delegation tools for the AD-1065 conversational `AgenticLoop` (epic #1006, the last of AD-1070/1071/1072). Two keystone tools, both default-OFF + additive → `WorkItemAgenticExecutor.run` byte-identical when off.** (1) NEW `src/probos/tools/search_capabilities_tool.py` `SearchCapabilitiesTool` (Tool protocol, `tool_id="search_capabilities"`, read-only): `invoke({query, kind?})` → `await list_capability_catalog(runtime)` (routers/tools.py) unifying tools/skills/mesh_intents, keyword-ranks by case-insensitive token overlap (name×3 + description×1), drops score-0, caps top-10, returns `{results:[{name,kind,description,held_by?}],count}`; empty query → `{results:[],count:0,message:"query required"}`; any exception → `error="search_failed: …"` (never raises). Lets an agent DISCOVER a capability before acting instead of confabulating a verb (BF-651/AD-1064). (2) NEW `src/probos/tools/delegate_task_tool.py` `DelegateTaskTool` (`tool_id="delegate_task"`): `invoke({task, to})` hands a bounded subtask to another crew agent BY CALLSIGN through the SAME governed `WorkItemAgenticExecutor` (delegate's tool perms / mesh restrictions / consensus gates / tool-trace all apply — no bypass). Depth guard FIRST (`_delegation_depth >= max_depth` → refuse, NO nested executor constructed — recursion/fan-out bound); callsign via `runtime.callsign_registry.resolve` with the AD-1076 liveness-independent `registry.get_by_pool(agent_type)` fallback (a RESTING crew member still resolves); self-delegation → `target_not_found`; runs the nested executor with `extra_context={_delegation_depth: depth+1}`. (3) `cognitive/agentic_dispatch.py` — `WorkItemAgenticExecutor.run` gained an additive `extra_context: dict|None=None` (default-None → byte-identical) merged into the 4-key loop context, plus `search_ids` + `delegate_ids` registration blocks after `skill_ids` (gated on `config.agentic_tools.tool_search_enabled` / `delegation_enabled`, idempotent, log-and-degrade) extended into the `tool_ids` dedup; the delegate tool reuses the executor's own `self._llm`. (4) `config.py` — NEW `AgenticToolsConfig(tool_search_enabled=False, delegation_enabled=False, delegation_max_depth=1[0..3], delegation_max_iterations=5[1..25], delegation_tier="standard")` mounted on `SystemConfig` beside `dm_agentic` (NO `config/system.yaml` edit). Tests `tests/test_ad1072_agentic_tools.py` (16, BF-287 real `ToolRegistry`/`CallsignRegistry`/`WorkItemAgenticExecutor` + scripted-LLM): search ranking/kind-filter/empty/honest-degrade; delegation depth-guard (counting-fake executor NEVER constructed at max depth) / happy-path (nested run carries `_delegation_depth==depth+1` + runs AS the target) / resting-agent resolves / unknown+non-crew+self → `target_not_found` / missing-input degrade; registration gating (both flags off → neither registered + offered, byte-identical; flags on → each registered exactly once, idempotent on a 2nd run); and the end-to-end headline (a scripted parent loop emits a `delegate_task` call → a nested governed executor runs the target → its result returns into the parent transcript). **Gates:** focused `tests/test_ad1072_agentic_tools.py -n 0` → **16 passed**; blast-radius `test_ad1066_code_execution_tool.py + test_ad1068_use_skill_tool.py + test_config.py -n 0` → **25 passed** (0 regressions); `get_errors` clean on all 4 source files + the test. **AD-1054 shipped (2026-06-26) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — OS-activity sensor: a default-OFF, consent-gated, local-only desktop foreground-window watcher → an `os.activity` runtime event (OSS plumbing). Pure sensor — no intelligence. Additive + default-OFF → byte-identical when off.** A desktop (AD-759, Electron) watcher POSTs active-window METADATA ONLY to a new runtime ingestion endpoint, which (when consent is on) emits a new `OS_ACTIVITY` event. Nothing in OSS consumes it (it gates a commercial overlay consumer, out of scope here). **Two privacy gates (defense in depth):** the runtime endpoint is the authoritative boundary (refuses + emits nothing when off) AND the watcher self-gates (does not call `activeWindow()` unless consent reads ON — the sensor does not even run without consent). **(1)** `config.py` — NEW `OSActivityConfig(enabled: bool = False, poll_interval_seconds: int = 5 [ge=1, le=60])` mounted on `SystemConfig` after `device` (default OFF; no `config/system.yaml` edit — model default). **(2)** `events.py` — `EventType.OS_ACTIVITY = "os_activity"` + `OSActivityEvent(BaseEvent)` (`active_app`, `window_title`, `app_path`, `url`, `ts`); serializes per the `BaseEvent.to_dict()` contract; metadata only. **(3)** `routers/system.py` — NEW `POST /api/os-activity` (consent-gated: OFF (default) → `{ingested:False, reason:"disabled"}` + NO event; ON → emit one `OSActivityEvent` via `runtime.emit_event`; bad payload → 422; emit-raises → honest-degrade HTTP-200 `{ingested:False, reason:"emit_error"}`, never 500) + NEW `GET /api/os-activity` (public consent read returning ONLY `{enabled, poll_interval_seconds}` — no secrets — so the watcher self-gates without crew-scope auth; the crew-scoped `/api/config` is deliberately NOT used). **(4)** `desktop/package.json` — `get-windows` (MIT, sindresorhus) added to a NEW `dependencies` block (NOT vendored; `externalizeDepsPlugin()` auto-externalizes it; `koffi` MIT transitive). **(5)** NEW `desktop/src/main/osActivityWatcher.ts` — pure `mapActiveWindowToPayload(Result)→payload` + pure `shouldEmit(prev,next)` change-detector + a self-gating heartbeat (reads `GET /api/os-activity`; only when enabled polls `activeWindow()` every `poll_interval_seconds`; POSTs ONLY on a window CHANGE). `get-windows` is a LAZY dynamic `import()` (vitest/CJS never loads the native module); `fetch`/`activeWindow` are injectable (DI); honest-degrade (probe undefined / Wayland / permission-denied → skip silently). **(6)** `desktop/src/main/index.ts` — armed (gated) in `app.whenReady()` after `createMainWindow()`; stopped on a NEW `before-quit` handler (held timer ref cleared on stop). **Privacy guarantees:** (a) default-OFF two-gate; (b) consent required to start; (c) local-only (emitted in-process; NOT persisted/exported/forwarded by this AD); (d) active-window metadata only — explicitly NO keystroke / screen / clipboard / content capture; (e) change-detection (POST on change, not a continuous stream); (f) additive / byte-identical when off. ActivityWatch (MPL-2.0) is pattern-absorbed only (heartbeat + opt-in) — NO MPL code copied. **Gates:** backend isolated `pytest tests/test_ad1054_os_activity_sensor.py -q -n 0` → **13 passed** (BF-287 real `SystemConfig` + real `OSActivityEvent` + a captured emit sink + `TestClient`); backend regression `test_config.py` + `test_events.py` + `test_ad1053_actionable_notifications.py` + `test_ad637d_system_events_nats.py` → **56 passed, 0 regressions**; desktop focused watcher vitest **9 passed** (incl. the "`get-windows` never loaded when off" lazy-import-gate assertion via a hoisted import spy); full desktop vitest **85 passed** (was 76, +9, 0 regressions); `npm install` succeeded (resolved `get-windows`/`koffi`); `npm run build` (electron-vite) **clean** (main bundle 41.22 kB; `get-windows` externalized, not bundled); `get_errors` clean on all 8 touched files. Current highest landed top-level was **AD-1053** → this is the next sequential top-level **AD-1054**. `config/system.yaml` UNTOUCHED. UNCOMMITTED. Verify all changes comply with the Engineering Principles in `.github/copilot-instructions.md`.

**AD-1053 shipped (2026-06-25) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — actionable accept→dispatch affordance on the notification surface (OSS plumbing). Additive + default-None → byte-identical when unused.** A thin, generally-useful extension of the AD-323 Bridge notification surface: a notification can carry an optional producer-authored `suggested_action` (`{label, intent, params, target_agent_id?}`), the HXI renders an **Accept** button for it, and accepting it broadcasts the carried intent into the mesh then acks the notification. Any agent/producer can use it — generic OSS infrastructure (enables a commercial overlay consumer, out of scope here). **(1)** `notifications.py` — `AgentNotification.suggested_action: dict|None=None` (serialized in `to_dict()`), `notify(...)` threads it through (default-None → every call site byte-identical), NEW `NotificationQueue.get(id)` accessor. **(2)** `routers/system.py` — NEW `POST /api/notifications/{id}/accept`: reads the PRODUCER-authored action, builds `IntentMessage(intent, params, target_agent_id)`, `await intent_bus.broadcast(...)` (delegates to `send()` when targeted; passes the AD-698 pre-auth seam + consensus), then acks. Honest-degrade HTTP-200 (never 500): `not_found` / `no_action` (ack-only) / `no_bus` / `dispatch_error` (left unacked for retry); success → `{dispatched:True, intent, responders}`. **(3/4)** `store/types.ts` `NotificationView.suggested_action?` + `BridgeNotifications.tsx` `NotificationCard` Accept button (stroke-SVG check, `strokeWidth 1.5`, amber `#f0b060`, NO emoji, `stopPropagation`, POST accept) — byte-identical when absent. **Security:** the client supplies ONLY the notification `id`; the intent/params are producer-authored (no client intent injection); the dispatch still passes the AD-698 pre-auth seam + consensus. **Gates:** backend isolated `pytest tests/test_ad1053_actionable_notifications.py -q -n 0` → **8 passed** (BF-287 real `NotificationQueue` + real `IntentBus` capture + real `IntentMessage`); focused notification/system-router regression **89 passed, 0 regressions**; focused vitest **3 passed**; full UI **288 files, 1918 passed / 1 skipped** (was 1915/1 — +3, 0 regressions); `npm run build` (tsc -b + vite) clean; `get_errors` clean on all 6 touched files. Current highest landed top-level was **AD-1052** → this is the next sequential top-level **AD-1053**. `config/system.yaml` UNTOUCHED. UNCOMMITTED. **BF-640 shipped (2026-06-25) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — CloudPicker OAuth `postMessage` handler verifies `event.origin` (#1005, security hygiene).** The OAuth-completion `message` listener in `CloudPicker.tsx` validated `ev.data.type`/`ev.data.provider` but NOT `ev.origin` — any script/iframe able to `postMessage` to the window could forge an `oauth_complete` and drive `setAuthorized(true)` + `loadFiles('')` (a defense-in-depth gap; impact low because the backend holds the real OAuth token, so a forged message can't itself exfiltrate — the `/files` fetch fails if OAuth never completed). Pre-existing since AD-720c (2026-05-17), found in the 2026-06-24 weekly review. Fix: `if (ev.origin !== window.location.origin) return;` at the top of the handler — only a same-origin message is trusted. Tests: the 3 existing posts switched from `window.postMessage(…, '*')` (jsdom gives no usable `ev.origin`) to a `dispatchOauthComplete()` helper emitting an explicit same-origin `MessageEvent` (+ a `waitFor(window.open)` so the synchronous dispatch lands after the listener attaches); NEW `ignores a forged cross-origin oauth_complete message` test (wrong-origin → stays unauthorized; a same-origin message after still drives the flow). CloudPicker 8 passed; full UI **1915 passed / 1 skipped** (+1, 0 regressions); `npm run build` clean; `get_errors` clean. `config/system.yaml` UNTOUCHED.

**BF-639 shipped (2026-06-25) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — `_emit_event_local` holds its coroutine-listener task references (#1003).** The AD-637d in-memory event fallback dispatched coroutine listeners with `asyncio.create_task(fn(event))` WITHOUT retaining the reference — exceptions inside async listeners were swallowed and the task could be GC'd before completing (the async-discipline standing-order violation; the NATS path ~10 lines above already did it right via `_nats_publish_tasks`). Fix: store the task in a NEW `self._event_listener_tasks` set + `add_done_callback(discard)` (mirrors `_nats_publish_tasks`; deliberately NOT `_background_tasks`, the AD-824 shutdown-cancel registry for long-lived loops), and promote the listener-failure log `debug → warning` with context. Found in the 2026-06-24 weekly review. Tests: the faithful-port `_FakeRuntime` updated to match + NEW `test_emit_event_local_retains_then_discards_coroutine_task` (behavioral) + `test_real_runtime_emit_event_local_holds_task_reference` (source-contract on the REAL method — the BF-287 'tested a copy' guard); `test_ad637d` **12 passed**; `get_errors` clean. `config/system.yaml` UNTOUCHED.

**BF-638 shipped (2026-06-25) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — `AttentionFaculty._coerce_exogenous_bid` no longer aliases a caller-supplied `AttentionBid` (#1002).** A ready `AttentionBid` passed into the exogenous path was returned BY REFERENCE, then `_drain_pending_exogenous` repriced its `zone_floor`/`salience` IN PLACE — silently corrupting the caller's reusable bid (latent: no production caller passes a shared bid today, the live path builds fresh bids via `_text_bid`/`_build_event_bid`). Fix: `_coerce_exogenous_bid` returns `dataclasses.replace(payload)` (a copy), so the faculty never owns the caller's reference. Found in the 2026-06-24 weekly review (AD-1032 cluster). Tests: NEW `test_coerce_exogenous_bid_copies_a_ready_attention_bid` + `test_delivering_an_attention_bid_does_not_mutate_the_caller_object` (`test_ad1032_arousal.py`); attention faculty suites (1032+1029+1030+1036) **86 passed**, 0 regressions; `get_errors` clean. `config/system.yaml` UNTOUCHED.

**BF-637 shipped (2026-06-25) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — A2UI + AD-797 artifact cards never rendered in the live HXI; the render gate fed the unresolved `threadId` prop (#735).** `ProfileChatTab` built each message body via `renderMessageBodyWithArtifacts(msg.text, threadId, …)`, but both card gates require a truthy thread id (`if (a2ui && threadId)` / `if (stub && threadId)`) and the only live mount (`AgentProfilePanel`'s `<ProfileChatTab agentId={agentId} />`) passes NO `threadId` prop → `undefined` → every A2UI (AD-811a/b/b-1) and artifact (AD-797) card degraded to literal `[A2UI: …]` / `[Artifact: …]` stub text, in BOTH 1:1 and group threads. **AD-843c-2 shipped (2026-06-24) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — consensus gate for the sensitive device intents (#818): the LAST #818 sub-piece makes `device.location`/`device.camera`/`device.screen` REACHABLE AND consensus-gated, mirroring the AD-1019c propose-only-pool + commit-on-APPROVED pattern; completes #818 (the architect closes the issue after merge). Default-OFF.** NEW `src/probos/agents/device_consensus_proposer.py` — `DeviceConsensusProposer` (a faithful propose-only mirror of `McpConsensusProposer`: `tier="utility"`, validates the `device_actuate` request shape, returns `requires_consensus=True`, NEVER actuates; imports ONLY `probos.substrate.agent` + `probos.types` + stdlib — clean layer discipline, NO consensus/mesh/cognitive/runtime import). EDIT `src/probos/runtime.py` — THREE new methods inserted after `_store_mcp_invoke_episode`: (1) `submit_device_actuate_with_consensus(device_id, intent_name, params, *, timeout, policy)` — authorize FIRST (c-1 parity: unpaired/ungranted → episode `authorized=False`, NO trust, NO votes, early return), then broadcast the NEW INTERNAL `device_actuate` proposal intent (params carry `device_id`+`intent_name`+`params`) through `submit_intent_with_consensus`, committing `device_node_adapter.actuate` ONLY on `ConsensusOutcome.APPROVED` AND `not [v for v in result["verifications"] if not v.verified]` (the EXACT predicate from `submit_mcp_invoke_with_consensus` — NO `QuorumEngine.evaluate` hand-roll; the era-4/AD-362 guard, a rejected/insufficient vote performs ZERO actuate); (2) `_store_device_consensus_episode` (mirrors `_store_mcp_invoke_episode`, `anchors=AnchorFrame(channel="device", trigger_type=intent_name)`, honest-degrade when episodic None); (3) `_dispatch_device_consensus_intent` (the runtime-owned bus handler routing the 3 sensitive intents → the consensus method). **Trust ONLY on an ATTEMPTED actuation (architect override of the spec's "trust on both"):** `trust_network.record_outcome(device.trust_record_id, success=committed, source="device")` fires ONLY inside the APPROVED + no-failed-verification branch, AFTER the actuate try/except — a REJECTED/INSUFFICIENT/failed-verify/unauthorized path performs NO actuation → NO trust write (avoids the consensus-weighting lock-out death-spiral + the INSUFFICIENT-penalizes-device bug; trust reflects the actuation OUTCOME, not the mesh's governance decision). A genuine APPROVED-but-actuate-fails STILL records `success=False` (an actuation WAS attempted). **Episodes store on ALL paths** (approved-actuated, blocked, rejected, insufficient, unauthorized) for audit/learning completeness. `__init__` adapter extraction: the inline `NoOpDeviceNodeAdapter()` is named `self.device_node_adapter: DeviceNodeAdapter` (byte-identical — same instance — shared by the c-1 `device.notify` service AND the c-2 consensus commit). EDIT `src/probos/startup/finalize.py` — NEW `async def _wire_device_consensus(*, runtime, config)` (mirrors `_wire_desktop_ux`), called from `finalize_startup`: gated on `config.device.enabled` (default False) → when OFF it creates NO `device_consensus` proposer pool + adds NO `device_consensus_dispatch` subscription → the 3 sensitive intents stay UNREACHABLE = **byte-identical to AD-843c-1, no governance bypass**; when ON it `register_template` + `create_pool("device_consensus", "device_consensus_proposer", target_size=3)` and subscribes `device_consensus_dispatch` (≠ `DEVICE_NODE_SERVICE_ID` — distinct id, no clobber) to the `requires_consensus` names from `DEVICE_INTENT_DESCRIPTORS`. The internal `device_actuate` intent is declared ONLY on the proposer (NOT in `DEVICE_INTENT_DESCRIPTORS`) — distinct from the 3 external intents so the dispatch handler never re-broadcast-loops. **Gate:** `pytest tests/test_ad843c2_device_consensus.py tests/test_ad843c1_device_actuation.py tests/test_ad1019c_consensus.py tests/test_ad1019c_proposer.py -q -n 0` (PROBOS_DATA_DIR-isolated) → **34 passed** (13 new c-2 + 843c-1 8 + 1019c consensus 7 + 1019c proposer 6, 0 regressions); blast radius `-k "device or consensus or quorum"` → **146 passed**; `get_errors` clean on all 4 touched files; proposer imports confirmed substrate.agent+types+stdlib only. NEW `tests/test_ad843c2_device_consensus.py` — 13 real-fixture cases (BF-287: real `ProbOSRuntime`+`start()`+`DeviceConsensusProposer` pool + real Ed25519 pairing + real delegating `_TrustSpy`/`_StoreSpy`/`_CountingAdapter`/`_FailingAdapter`): 5 proposer-unit (propose valid / reject missing device_id / reject missing intent_name / ignore unhandled / descriptor consensus+utility-tier); approved-commits-single-actuate (trust success=True, score rose, episode channel="device"); rejected (`approval_threshold=1.1`) → 0 actuate, episode `reason="not_committed"`, **NO trust** (death-spiral guard, α/β unchanged); insufficient (no pool) → 0 actuate, **NO trust**; unauthorized (`ghost-1`) → refused pre-consensus, `authorized=False`, 0 actuate, no trust record; episodic-None honest-degrade; **approved-but-actuate-fails (`_FailingAdapter`) → committed=False but 1 actuate attempt + trust success=False recorded** (the override penalizes real actuation failures, not governance rejections); wire-OFF no-op (no pool, no dispatch sub — byte-identical); device.notify c-1 regression (still routes through `device_node_service`, NOT `device_actuate`). NO OS-native backend (NoOp only), NO AD-844 mobile client, NO fleet pairing, NO decomposer `device.*` teaching, NO HXI device events, NO `QuorumEngine.evaluate` direct-vote. `device_service.py`/`device_node.py`/`config.py`/`config/system.yaml` UNTOUCHED. AD-843c-2 is a PRE-RESERVED #818 sub-number (current highest landed top-level = **AD-1052**) — NO new top-level AD; this is the LAST #818 sub-piece, completing #818 (the architect closes the issue after merge).
