**AD-843b shipped (2026-06-24) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — Ed25519 device pairing + per-device capability grant + probationary Beta(1,3) trust prior (#818): the 2nd #818 sub-piece (cryptographic pairing layered on the AD-843a substrate primitives). A device enrolls by presenting an Ed25519 public key + signing a pairing challenge; the registry verifies the signature, stores the `public_key`, sets the capability grant, and seeds a probationary Beta(1,3)=0.25 trust prior on FIRST pairing only (mirrors `FederationPeerRegistry`). Pure crypto + trust-prior wire — NO actuation governance routing (AD-843c).** NEW `src/probos/substrate/device_pairing.py` (imports `cryptography` + stdlib `base64` ONLY — clean substrate layer discipline, NO consensus/mesh/cognitive/federation/runtime import; Ed25519 was MISSING at HEAD — net-new build on `cryptography>=42`, NOT a reuse of the SHA256 string-id in `identity.py`). FIVE functions: `generate_keypair() -> (Ed25519PrivateKey, public_key_b64)`; `encode_public_key(pk) -> str` (base64 of the 32-byte raw form); `decode_public_key(b64) -> Ed25519PublicKey` (FAIL-FAST boundary — raises `ValueError` on malformed base64 / wrong length); `sign_challenge(sk, challenge) -> str` (base64 of the 64-byte sig); `verify_signature(pubkey_b64, challenge, sig_b64) -> bool` (HONEST-DEGRADE — never raises on a bad key/sig, returns `False`, the security gate callers branch on). Wire format = base64 of the raw key/sig; two-tier exception handling (decode fail-fast / verify honest-degrade). EDIT `src/probos/substrate/device_node.py` — imports `device_pairing.verify_signature` (substrate→substrate, NOT consensus) + a module logger; `DeviceNodeRegistry.__init__` gains KEYWORD-ONLY `trust_network: Any|None=None` (the CONCRETE `TrustNetwork` is INJECTED as `Any` — the Protocol lacks `create_with_prior` — never imported, preserving substrate discipline), `probationary_alpha=1.0`, `probationary_beta=3.0` (the no-arg AD-843a path stays intact); NEW `pair_device(device_id, public_key, capabilities, *, challenge, signature) -> DeviceNode|None` — `verify_signature` gate (bad sig → `logger.warning` + `None`), then on FIRST pairing builds the `DeviceNode` (`public_key` + grant + `trust_record_id=f"device:{id}"`) and seeds the probationary Beta(α,β) prior via the injected `TrustNetwork.create_with_prior` (mirrors `register_peer`); IDEMPOTENT — re-pairing a known id returns the existing record and does NOT reset the prior; honest-degrade when no `TrustNetwork` injected (pairs, no prior). EDIT `src/probos/config.py` — NEW `DeviceConfig(BaseModel)` (mirrors `FederationPeerTrustConfig`: `probationary_alpha=1.0 gt=0`, `probationary_beta=3.0 gt=0` → E[trust]=0.25) mounted on `SystemConfig.device` (inert until AD-843c constructs the registry with a real `TrustNetwork`). Raw α/β stored, never derived means. **Gate:** `pytest tests/test_ad843b_device_pairing.py tests/test_ad843a_device_node.py tests/test_config.py -q -n 0` → **26 passed** (12 new 843b + AD-843a still **8 passed** / 0 regressions + test_config green); `get_errors` clean on all 4 files; import smoke confirms `device_pairing` imports ONLY `cryptography`+stdlib and `device_node` imports `device_pairing` (both substrate), NOT consensus. NEW `tests/test_ad843b_device_pairing.py` — 12 real-fixture cases (BF-287: real standalone `TrustNetwork()` in-memory `_records` — no `start()`, no `PROBOS_DATA_DIR` — real Ed25519 crypto, all sync): keypair sign/verify round-trip; tampered-challenge → `False`; wrong-key → `False`; malformed-sig → `False` (no raise); encode/decode round-trip; bad-length → `ValueError`; pair stores pubkey+grant+`device:phone-1`+is_paired; pair seeds Beta(1,3) (raw α==1.0/β==3.0/score≈0.25); bad-sig → `None`+not-paired+no-record; idempotent re-pair (α unchanged after a `record_outcome` shift); none-`TrustNetwork` honest-degrade pairs; the AD-843a no-arg API intact (register_device + authorize granted-ok). NO actuation governance routing / `record_outcome`-on-actuation / episodic / IntentBus / runtime wiring / registry-with-real-`TrustNetwork` construction (all AD-843c), NO OS backend, NO AD-844 mobile client, NO fleet pairing / pubkey rotation / asyncio.Lock. `config/system.yaml` UNTOUCHED. AD-843b is a PRE-RESERVED #818 sub-number (current highest landed top-level = **AD-1052**) — NO new top-level AD; AD-843c (consensus/episodic actuation routing + runtime wiring) remains. UNCOMMITTED. Verify all changes comply with the Engineering Principles in `.github/copilot-instructions.md`.

**AD-843a shipped (2026-06-24) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — DeviceNodeAdapter Protocol + `device.*` intents + pure `DeviceNodeRegistry` capability-grant gate + `NoOpDeviceNodeAdapter` (#818): the foundational, STANDALONE, UNWIRED substrate primitives for the brain→limb device tier (the local cognitive mesh is the brain; a paired phone/tablet/kiosk is a limb it can actuate). NO trust/quorum/episodic/Ed25519/runtime wiring — all deferred to AD-843b/c.** NEW `src/probos/substrate/device_node.py` (imports ONLY `probos.types` + stdlib `time`/`dataclasses`/`typing` — clean substrate layer discipline, NO consensus/mesh/cognitive/federation/runtime import). FIVE exports: (1) `DEVICE_INTENT_DESCRIPTORS` — the four `device.*` `IntentDescriptor`s with correct governance metadata: `device.location`/`device.camera`/`device.screen` are `requires_consensus=True` (they exfiltrate private sensor data — physical location, camera frames, on-screen secrets), `device.notify` is `False` (low-sensitivity outbound push); each `tier="domain"`. (2) `DeviceNode` `@dataclass` (mirrors `FederationPeer`): `device_id`, `capabilities: frozenset[str]=frozenset()` (the per-device grant = the raw immutable set of granted `device.*` names), `trust_record_id=""` (AD-843b Beta-prior handle, stored-only here), `public_key=""` (Ed25519 placeholder — `""` = not crypto-paired, AD-843b populates), `metadata`, `paired_at`. (4) `NoOpDeviceNodeAdapter` — echo adapter, NO OS backend (`result={"backend":"noop","intent":…,"echo":dict(params)}`, `agent_id=f"device:{id}"`, `intent_id=msg.id`); satisfies the Protocol structurally. (5) `DeviceNodeRegistry` — pure in-memory pairing store + capability-grant gate: `register_device` (False if known, mirrors `register_peer`), `get_device`/`list_devices`/`is_paired`/`unregister_device`, and `authorize(device_id, intent_name) -> tuple[bool,str]` — `(False,"unpaired device: <id>")` if not paired, `(False,"capability not granted: <intent>")` if ungranted, `(True,"")` otherwise. `authorize` is the PURE governance-metadata gate — it does NOT call the adapter/TrustNetwork/QuorumEngine (AD-843c). `__init__` takes NO `trust_network` param in 843a (AD-843b adds it). **Ed25519 is MISSING at HEAD → AD-843b builds it on `cryptography` (a new build, not a reuse).** NEW `tests/test_ad843a_device_node.py` — 8 real-fixture cases (BF-287: no MagicMock; real `DeviceNode`/`DeviceNodeRegistry`/`NoOpDeviceNodeAdapter`/`IntentMessage`): consensus flags + tier + all-four-present; NoOp satisfies the Protocol; NoOp async happy-path echo; authorize unpaired-reject; authorize ungranted-reject; authorize granted-ok; register idempotent; record stores the raw frozenset grant + `public_key==""`. **Gate:** `pytest tests/test_ad843a_device_node.py -q -n 0` → **8 passed** (pure module, no runtime boot); `get_errors` clean on both files; import smoke confirms ONLY `probos.types` + stdlib. **AD-708d shipped (2026-06-24) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — swipe gestures chat↔mesh in `MobileShell` (#484/AD-708): a reusable, dep-free + three-free `useSwipe` pointer-event hook wired into the PADD `MobileShell` body — a left-swipe → mesh, a right-swipe → chat. The c-3 header CHAT | MESH tap toggle is PRESERVED as the accessible primary; swipe is a touch-only enhancement. Discrete switch (no live drag), horizontal-dominant + threshold so chat vertical scroll is NEVER hijacked (no preventDefault). Handler param type `SwipeCoords {clientX, clientY}` (a supertype of `React.PointerEvent` → JSX-assignable to `onPointerDown`/`onPointerUp` AND unit-test-callable with a plain obj). The desktop formulas were READ and RE-DERIVED as pure TS, NOT imported. **Re-derived formulas (verified against `scene.ts` at HEAD — match the spec exactly):** `trustToCss` 4 bands with the same stops + thresholds (`#e8963c`→`#f0b060` ≥0.7 · `#6690b8`→`#88a4c8` ≥0.35 · `#5848a0`→`#7060a8` >0 · `#a0a8b8` silver at 0) via a private componentwise-sRGB `lerpHex`, trust clamped to [0,1] so out-of-range never yields an invalid hex; `confidenceToOpacity` = `clamp((0.4 + c*1.8)/2.2, 0.25, 1)` (normalizes the desktop `confidenceToIntensity` 0.4..2.2; 0.25 floor keeps low-confidence faintly visible); `nodeRadius` = `(TIER_BASE[tier] + c*0.15) * 28` (the desktop `agentNodeSize`; `TIER_BASE` core .22/utility .28/domain .35, unknown→.28 fallback → ~6..14px); `selectMeshAgents(agents, limit=24)` progressive-disclosure curated subset (crew-first → trust-desc → callsign-asc tiebreak, cap 24, input not mutated); `computeMeshLayout(agents, viewport)` concentric-ring-by-tier (mirrors the desktop 3.5/5.5/7.5 ordering as ring fractions core .30/utility .55/domain .80 of `min(w,h)/2`, even angular spacing, viewport-centered, deterministic, one `MeshNode` per agent with color/opacity/radius derived; a single node sits ON its ring, not center). **AD-708e shipped (2026-06-23) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — LAN mDNS service advertisement for PADD discovery (#484), BACKEND: a default-OFF, opt-in advertiser that publishes a stable `_probos._tcp.local.` service + a `<hostname>.local` A record + the live server port so a phone on the LAN can reach the HXI at `http://probos.local:<port>` without chasing a DHCP IP. HONEST SCOPE — mDNS gives a stable `.local` ADDRESS, NOT zero-input discovery (browsers have no DNS-SD API; that's the deferred native-app AD-708-1). INERT until the operator binds the LAN (`probos serve --host 0.0.0.0`); the default `127.0.0.1` loopback is unreachable from a phone, so the advertiser SKIPS + WARNS on loopback.** NEW `DiscoveryConfig` (config.py, default-OFF `enabled=False`; `service_type="_probos._tcp.local."` with a `.local.`-suffix `@field_validator`; `hostname="probos"` with a bare-DNS-label validator; `instance_name="ProbOS"`; `txt_path="/"`) mounted on `SystemConfig` next to `desktop` via `Field(default_factory=DiscoveryConfig)` — **NO `config/system.yaml` edit** (a default install boots BYTE-IDENTICAL). NEW `src/probos/discovery/` package: `protocol.py` (`ServiceAdvertiser` `@runtime_checkable` Protocol — `async start()->bool`, `async stop()->None` — the DIP seam the runtime depends on); `advertiser.py` (`NoOpAdvertiser` advertises nothing / `start()→False`; `resolve_lan_ipv4()` = the no-packet UDP-connect trick, honest-degrade to `None`; `ZeroconfAdvertiser(cfg, host, port, *, zc_factory=None)` with a DI seam — the `zeroconf` import is **LAZY inside `start()`**, never at module top, so the server boots fine with the lib ABSENT; explicit host semantics — specific non-loopback → advertise as-is, bind-all (`0.0.0.0`/`""`) → resolve LAN IPv4 (`None` → warn+skip), loopback (`127.0.0.1`/`localhost`/`::1`) → warn+skip; Tier-2 honest-degrade — a register failure logs + stops + returns `False`, NEVER blocks the server; idempotent `stop()` unregister+close that never raises; `_zeroconf_available()` = `find_spec("zeroconf")`; `build_advertiser(cfg, host, port)` → `NoOpAdvertiser` when cfg is `None` / flag off / lib absent, else `ZeroconfAdvertiser`); `__init__.py` re-exports. WIRED in `__main__._serve` (NOT `startup/finalize` — host/port live only in `_serve`, and finalize can't see a `--host` override): `_advertiser = build_advertiser(getattr(config,"discovery",None), host, port)` + `await _advertiser.start()` after the listening banner (prints `PADD discovery: http://<hostname>.local:<port>` ONLY when actually advertising), `_advertiser` bound before the server body + `await _advertiser.stop()` guarded in the cleanup `finally`. NEW `pyproject.toml` `[project.optional-dependencies] discovery = ["zeroconf>=0.130"]` — `python-zeroconf` is LGPL-2.1, kept an OPTIONAL extra (`pip install probos[discovery]`) so the Apache-2.0 core install stays LGPL-free; NEVER vendored/modified. SECURITY: the advertised `ServiceInfo` carries ONLY non-sensitive fields (service type, instance name, LAN IPv4, port, static TXT path) — never a token/identity/path/username (test-enforced); auth is OFF by default (`auth.crew_scope_token=""`), so advertising a default install makes it LAN-discoverable AND LAN-accessible — the config docstring + DECISIONS state "set a token and bind `--host 0.0.0.0` deliberately before enabling". NEW `tests/test_ad708e_mdns_discovery.py` (10, BF-287 real `_FakeAsyncZeroconf` with ASYNC register/unregister/close recorders — a sync fake would hide a missing `await`: factory flag-off / lib-absent → NoOp; flag-on+lib → Zeroconf; NoOp idempotent; register+unregister+close exactly-once; loopback → skip+warn (caplog); bind-all → resolved LAN IP packed into the `ServiceInfo` address; secrets excluded; register-error → honest-degrade `False`; `inspect.getsource(_serve)` contains `build_advertiser(`+`.stop()`) + `tests/test_config.py` +3 (DiscoveryConfig default-off on leaf + root model; service_type/hostname validators reject bad). **Gate (PROBOS_DATA_DIR-isolated, the live instance is running): `pytest tests/test_ad708e_mdns_discovery.py tests/test_config.py -q -n 0` → 16 passed; import smoke `SystemConfig().discovery.enabled` == `False` AND `zeroconf` NOT in `sys.modules` after `import probos.discovery` (LAZY proven — server boots without the lib); `get_errors` clean; `config/system.yaml` untouched.** Do NOT build: native zero-input DNS-SD (AD-708-1), federation/multi-mesh, the HXI QR/`.local` affordance (separate UI AD), auth-model change, vendoring zeroconf. AD-708e = a PADD-discovery sub-number under #484 (top-level stays AD-1052). UNCOMMITTED.

**AD-708a shipped (2026-06-23) — LOCAL (Captain decides push) — `usePadDevice` device-class primitive (responsive-HXI foundation, #484/AD-708): the missing predicate "is this client a handheld PADD?" = a COARSE primary pointer (touch) AND the AD-392 `'mobile'` viewport breakpoint. The later mobile shell (AD-708b) + gesture layer (AD-708d) route on this; it MUST distinguish a real phone from a NARROW DESKTOP WINDOW — routing on width alone would dump a desktop user who snaps a window <768px into the chat-only mobile projection (an untested-UI break the HXI has suffered before). Wired NOWHERE — the pure primitive only.** TWO NEW FILES, ZERO edits to any existing file (the desktop HXI is provably byte-identical). NEW `ui/src/hooks/usePadDevice.ts`: `hasCoarsePointer(): boolean` (pure — `window.matchMedia('(pointer: coarse)').matches`, honest-degrades to `false` when matchMedia is absent/throws, mirroring `prefersReducedMotion`'s guard); `isPadDevice(): boolean` (pure — `hasCoarsePointer() && getBreakpoint() === 'mobile'`, composing AD-392's exported `getBreakpoint` — DRY, no re-implemented width logic); `usePadDevice(): boolean` (subscribing hook — seeds from `isPadDevice()`, re-evaluates on BOTH the `window` `resize` event (width axis) AND the `(pointer: coarse)` `MediaQueryList` `change` event (pointer axis) via the `addEventListener`-with-`addListener`-fallback + try/catch teardown idiom from `usePrefersReducedMotion`; honest-degrades to a stable `false` when matchMedia is unavailable; cleans up all listeners on unmount). The narrow-desktop-window guard IS the pointer gate: a fine-pointer client at `<= 768px` is NOT a PADD, so the byte-identical-desktop guarantee holds. This is the progressive-disclosure (HXI Design Principle #5) DEVICE gate, intentionally distinct from width-only `useBreakpoint`. NO emoji (HXI #3); full type annotations (explicit return types) on all three exports. NEW `ui/src/hooks/__tests__/usePadDevice.test.ts` (vitest, mirrors `usePrefersReducedMotion.test.ts` + the AD-392 GlassAdaptive `Object.defineProperty(window,'innerWidth',...)` convention — `vi.stubGlobal('matchMedia', ...)` pointer stub + restore in `afterEach`): 9 cases — `hasCoarsePointer` absent/coarse/fine; `isPadDevice` false-at-desktop-width (width gate), false-at-mobile-width-no-pointer (narrow desktop stays OUT — the byte-identical guard), true-coarse+mobile; `usePadDevice` false-absent, true-coarse+mobile, and resize-subscription updates after an `innerWidth` change + `act(() => window.dispatchEvent(new Event('resize')))`. NOT wired into `main.tsx`/`App.tsx`/`CompactApp.tsx`/`useBreakpoint.ts`/any component or store (that is AD-708b); NO mobile shell (AD-708b), 2D mesh (AD-708c), swipe gestures (AD-708d), mDNS, or native app (AD-708-1). `config/system.yaml` UNTOUCHED. **AD-731a-1 shipped (2026-06-22) — cross-host attachment distribution v1: a default-OFF, fail-closed serving endpoint that returns content-addressed attachment bytes to an authenticated federation peer, PLUS a content-verifying client helper (issue #638).** NEW `src/probos/routers/federation_attachments.py` — `GET /api/federation/attachments/{content_hash}` behind `Depends(require_crew_scope)`, with a Defense-in-Depth body in order: (1) feature-flag off → 404 `attachments_remote_serving_disabled` (byte-identical to "absent"; does not leak token state); (2) **fail-closed token hardening** → 403 `remote_serving_requires_token` when `auth.crew_scope_token` is empty (because `require_crew_scope` is a pass-through with an empty token — never serve bytes through an open gate); (3) 64-hex format → 400 `invalid_content_hash` (no store touch); (4) absent → 404 `attachment_not_found`; (5) `size > max_attachment_bytes` → 413 `attachment_too_large`; (6) serve via the **Protocol-safe** `store.get_path()` + `ext_to_mime(path.suffix)` + `FileResponse` (mirrors the proven `routers/chat.py` GET serve pattern, adding the auth dependency). NEW config `AttachmentsConfig.serve_remote_enabled=False` (default-OFF; **NO `config/system.yaml` edit**). NEW client `src/probos/federation/attachment_fetch.py` — `fetch_remote_attachment(peer_url, content_hash, *, auth_token, store, http=None, max_bytes=10MiB) -> bool`: validates 64-hex BEFORE any network call (`ValueError`), GETs with `Authorization: Bearer`, 404→False / non-404 ≥400→raise, size-cap→False, **INTEGRITY `sha256(blob)==content_hash` or reject (tampered/corrupt bytes NEVER stored)**, derive mime from `content-type` (store is the SSOT on acceptability), then `store.write(..., origin="chat_attachment")`. DI: `store`/`http` injectable. `get_path` confirmed ON the `AttachmentStore` Protocol → used `FileResponse(get_path)` (Protocol-safe), NOT `read()`-bytes. Mounted in `api.py` (import tuple + `for r in (...)` loop, matching the existing pattern). NEW `tests/test_ad731a_remote_attachment.py` (BF-287: real `FilesystemAttachmentStore` on `tmp_path` + real `AttachmentsConfig`/`AuthConfig` + real ASGI app — NO MagicMock at the substrate boundary): 7 serving (200 known-sha / 404 unknown / 404 flag-off / **403 token-unset fail-closed** / 401 wrong+missing Bearer / 400 bad-sha / 413 over-cap) + 6 client (roundtrip-verified / **tamper-detected, write NOT called** / 404→False / bad-mime-rejected / malformed-hash-raises-before-network / oversize-rejected). Pre-reserved AD-731 sub-number — NO new top-level AD; AttachmentStore Protocol, AD-731 ref shape, the existing chat attachments route, and `require_crew_scope` all UNTOUCHED. NATS transport, a mime-fastpath, and auto-resolution are DEFERRED (AD-731 follow-ups). **Gates:** `test_ad731a_remote_attachment.py test_config.py -q -n 0` 16 passed; app-creation smoke `test_distribution.py` green (new router mounts, 0 regressions). honest-degrade to offline/empty. **DD-2 governed suggestions (human-in-control):** an agent posts a full-content suggested edit for a file; the Captain sees Preview/Accept/Dismiss — **Accept routes through the AD-1021b governed write** (`saveWorkspaceFile`→`submit_write_with_consensus`, i.e. consensus), Dismiss drops it; agents NEVER silently write (no new accept endpoint, no raw write). **DD-3 store/gate:** a thin in-memory `WorkspaceSuggestionStore` attached EXPLICITLY on the runtime (not getattr-lazy — avoids the MagicMock trap), bounded per (owner,path) oldest-evict; 3 additive endpoints (`GET/POST /{id}/workspace/suggestions`, `POST …/{sid}/dismiss`) gated by the EXISTING `ExecutionConfig.workspace_write_enabled` (single switch: list honest-degrades `[]` when off, post/dismiss `503`); path-confined via `resolve_file` BEFORE the store (traversal→400 never enters the store — asserted); 1 MiB cap (413). **UI:** `CodeWorkstation` renders the injectable `CoEditPanel` ONLY when `agentId` present → BYTE-IDENTICAL to AD-1021/1021b when absent; full UI 261 files / 1699 passed / 0 failed (+1 file, +12); `npm run build` clean; full pytest `tests/ -n 4` = 17292 passed / 1 failed / 23 skipped — the lone failure is `test_dreaming.py::test_nl_to_dream_cycle_changes_weights`, a pre-existing non-deterministic dreaming/Hebbian integration test with ZERO code overlap with this AD (unrelated flake). **Epic #965 (HXI Workspaces & Workstations) is now fully built** (Monaco + browser AD-1052/a/b/c + chat/MCP-app AD-1024 + AD-1022/1023 container + AD-1021b write-through + AD-1021c presence/co-edit).

**AD-1021b staged (2026-06-20) — Monaco workstation governed write-through (epic #965): the AD-1021 code/text Workstation gains Load/Save against an agent's AD-997 workspace folder; sub-AD of AD-1021 (no new top-level — current highest landed = AD-1052).** Read `GET /api/agent/{id}/workspace/file?path=` (AD-998 enabled∧persistent∧root gate, honest-degrade `found:false`, 400 on traversal, UTF-8 `errors=replace`, 1 MiB read cap) + governed write `POST /api/agent/{id}/workspace/file` ({path,content}) routed ONLY through `runtime.submit_write_with_consensus` (FileWriterAgent proposes → quorum + red-team gate the commit — never written in the router). **Default-OFF** new master switch `ExecutionConfig.workspace_write_enabled=False` (separate from `enabled`) → write **503s** when off/ephemeral/unrooted; **413** over the 1 MiB `_MAX_WRITE_BYTES` cap. **Dual-layer path confinement (DD-3):** `WorkspaceManager.resolve_file` (resolve()+is_relative_to, symlink-escape-safe; `None` on empty/absolute/NUL/traversal/folder-itself) AND a **400 at the API endpoint BEFORE governance** (traversal → 400 **and** `submit_write` NOT called — asserted). **Consensus is synchronous + terminal — NO PENDING:** response is `committed` or `refused{consensus_outcome,approval_ratio}` (governance exception → refused/`error`, never 500); the UI "pending" is only the in-flight await. **UI:** `CodeWorkstation` gains OPTIONAL `agentId`/`loadFile`/`saveFile` (stays assignable to `ComponentType<NativeWorkstationProps>`); path input + Load/Save (stroke-SVG) + `workstation-save-status` banner render ONLY when `agentId` present — scratch/prop-doc **BYTE-IDENTICAL** when absent. **AD-1018a staged (2026-06-20) — MCP OAuth device-code HXI modal: the headless device-code (RFC 8628) flow gets its operator surface, completing epic #955 (now closeable); sub-AD of AD-1018 (no new top-level), UI-only, default-OFF.** AD-1017a shipped the device-code backend (start/poll endpoints) but the AD-1018 credential modal only offered static-token + OAuth auth-code (the browser-redirect flow that can't complete headless). This adds the third credential `kind`. **UI-only — no `src/probos/**`, no `config/system.yaml`, no new npm dep.** **`McpServersPanel.tsx`:** the `CredentialModal` `kind` radio widens `'static'|'oauth'` → `+'device'` (static + auth-code byte-identical; the `kind` ternary becomes an explicit 3-way); new `DeviceStartInput`/`DeviceStartResult`/`DevicePollResult` types + `startDeviceAuthApi`/`pollDeviceAuthApi` same-origin fetchers (terminal `unknown_flow`/`access_denied`/expired throw; 200 carries `{status:pending|authenticated}`) + an **injectable `schedulePoll`** on `McpDeps`; a `connectDevice` (start → render `user_code`/verification link) + a poll `useEffect`. **DD-2 (poll discipline):** schedules on the server `interval` via the injectable `schedulePoll` (default `setTimeout`→cancel fn); exactly one live timer, cleanup cancels the latest under a `stopped` guard (no leaked timers / no setState-after-unmount), `expires_in` deadline stops a stale grant. **DD-3 (write-only secret):** `client_secret` sent once to `/device/start` then `setClientSecret('')` — never retained, never rendered. **DD:** the `device_code` never reaches the browser (the modal holds only the opaque `flow_id` + display fields; the poll secret stays server-side). **Honest-degrade:** panel already 404→degrades when `management_enabled` is off; a terminal poll error stops polling + re-enables Connect as **Retry**. **HXI:** stroke-SVG (no emoji), amber/dim, `data-testid` on every interactive element, reusing the existing modal helpers. **Tests:** `McpServersPanel.test.tsx` +6 via `makeDeps` + a `makeSchedule` capture-scheduler (no real timers / no real network). **Gates:** focused 22→28 green; full UI 1674→**1680 passed** (1 skipped, 0 fail); `npm run build` (tsc -b + vite) clean. **AD-1017a staged (2026-06-20) — MCP OAuth device-code (RFC 8628): headless OAuth for MCP servers — the last non-UI child of epic #955; sub-AD of AD-1017 (no new top-level), backend-only, default-OFF.** AD-1017 shipped static-token + OAuth auth-code (browser-redirect) and named device-code "not built — AD-1017a"; the auth-code popup opens a browser ON the ProbOS host (unusable headless), so RFC 8628 (authorize on a second device via a `user_code`) is the only OAuth flow that works on a server deployment. Reuses `McpOAuthProvider`'s httpx plumbing + the vault-by-ref persistence + `_reregister` + `OAuthTokenBundle` — two provider methods + two endpoints over the SAME path. **`McpOAuthProvider`:** + `device_authorization_url` kwarg; `start_device_authorization()` (POST `client_id`/`scope` [+secret when set] → `{device_code,user_code,verification_uri,...}`; raises on transport/non-200/missing-field; never logs the body); `poll_device_token()` (→ `OAuthTokenBundle` on success, **`None` while pending** — RFC 8628 returns HTTP 400 for `authorization_pending`/`slow_down`, NON-terminal, so it does NOT reuse `_token_post`; raises `McpOAuthError(status=400)` only on terminal `access_denied`/`expired_token`/`invalid_grant`/transport/unparsable; never logs the body). **`routers/mcp_servers.py`:** `_DEVICE_FLOWS` (keyed by `id(runtime)`, lazy-expiry prune) + `DeviceStartBody`/`DevicePollBody` + `_merge_device_oauth_config` + `device_authorization_url` into `_build_oauth_provider`; `POST /{id}/auth/device/start` + `POST /{id}/auth/device/poll`. **DD-2 (security):** the `device_code` is a poll secret held SERVER-SIDE in `_DEVICE_FLOWS`; the client gets only an opaque `flow_id` — `device_code` is NEVER in any response (asserted); `client_secret` vaulted by ref (`mcp:{id}:oauth_secret`); tokens never in a response/store-row/log; `device/poll` validates flow ownership before polling. **DD-1:** device URLs live in the per-server NON-secret `oauth_json` → **no `config/system.yaml`**, no `MCPConfig` field. **DD-4:** poll-success persistence is identical to the auth-code callback (vault `mcp:{id}:oauth` + `auth_kind="oauth"` + `_reregister`) → AD-1017 Bearer resolution + `/auth/refresh` work on a device-obtained bundle unchanged. **DD-5:** default-OFF — both endpoints 404 when `management_enabled` off (byte-identical), 503 no-vault. **Tests:** `tests/test_ad1017a_mcp_device_code.py` (**18**, BF-287 real `McpServerStore`/`EncryptedFileCredentialVault`/`MCPBridge`/`SystemConfig`/`TestClient`; only the external httpx mocked via `httpx.MockTransport` — 9 provider unit + 9 router incl. the `device_code`/token never-in-response-or-logs security assertions); existing `test_ad1017_mcp_auth.py` (38) still green. **Gates:** focused pytest **56 passed** (18 new + 38 existing); full Python `tests/ -q -n 4` **17269 passed, 23 skipped, 0 failed** (981s; +18, 0 regressions). Backend-only — **no `ui/` change** (vitest/build untouched). `config/system.yaml` untouched. **Remaining #955: AD-1018a** (the device-code HXI modal, UI-only over these endpoints) — then #955 is closeable.

**AD-1052c staged (2026-06-20) — Browser Workstation INPUT-FORWARDING: the human clicks/types/scrolls on the watch canvas and those events forward to the live Playwright `page`, making the shared browser BIDIRECTIONAL (agent drives + human takes the wheel) — the second half of the Captain's "embedded and shared like VS Code browser sharing"; sub-AD of AD-1052 (no new top-level), default-OFF, epic #965.** Works for BOTH a headless watch session and an AD-1052b bridged external browser; the AD-706 engine + AD-706a stream are REUSED. **DD-0 (reuse-vs-add):** ADD `BrowserSession.forward_input(event)` reusing the SAME Playwright primitives the agent path uses (`getattr(page,"mouse"/"keyboard",None).click/move/wheel/type/press`), NOT the `compute_use_click` LLM-vision flow (wrong shape — the human already knows where they clicked). **DD-1 (coord protocol — no viewport leak):** the UI sends NORMALIZED `(nx,ny)∈[0,1]`; the backend de-normalizes via `_resolve_viewport()` = `page.viewport_size` (Page property, dict|None) → new `BrowserToolConfig.viewport_width`/`height` fallback (defaults 1280/720, **read-only — NOT wired into `new_context()`** so `start()` is byte-identical). **DD-4 (governance):** default-OFF `input_forwarding_enabled` flag + an explicit UI **Drive** toggle (`aria-pressed`, rendered only when the flag is on — watch stays read-only by default) + `BROWSER_INPUT_FORWARDED` once per drive-episode (`_driven_sessions`) / `BROWSER_INPUT_REFUSED` on refusal (NOT per keystroke). **DD-6 (security):** coords clamped `[0,1]` backend-side; `key` checked against `_FORWARD_KEY_ALLOWLIST` (no modifier combos); `type` text capped 4096; Playwright treats input as literal (no eval); endpoint `require_crew_scope`, UI POSTs same-origin no-token. **Additive (5 Python, 2 UI, 0 config):** `events.py` 2 EventTypes; `config.py` `input_forwarding_enabled` + `viewport_width`/`height`; `session.py` helpers + `_resolve_viewport` + `forward_input` (click/type/key/scroll, honest-degrade, never raises); `tool.py` `_driven_sessions` + `input_forwarding_enabled` property + `forward_input` gate; `browser_stream.py` `InputForwardRequest` + `POST /api/browser/sessions/{id}/input` + `GET /sessions` flag; `BrowserStreamPanel.tsx` `_normalizePointer` + drive props (no-prop ⇒ byte-identical); `BrowserWorkstation.tsx` Drive toggle + injectable `forwardInput` on both mounts. **Slice boundary:** AD-1052d (cross-origin reverse proxy) remains. **Gates:** focused Python (`test_ad1052c+1052b+1052a`) **37 passed**; **AD-1052b staged (2026-06-20) — Browser Workstation BRIDGE mode: `connect_over_cdp` to an EXTERNAL Captain-launched Chrome so an agent can drive the user's REAL logged-in browser; sub-AD of AD-1052 (no new top-level), HIGH-RISK, default-OFF, epic #965.** The Captain launches Chrome with `--remote-debugging-port=9222`; the already-shipped AD-706 `BrowserTool` attaches over CDP. **DD-1 (disconnect-not-close — headline safety):** `BrowserSession.connect()` reuses the browser's EXISTING `contexts[0]`/`pages[0]` (the user's real logged-in session — `new_context()` would be cookie-less); the connected `stop()` branch calls `browser.close()` (which over a `connect_over_cdp` browser DISCONNECTS per Playwright docs — does NOT quit the user's Chrome) + `playwright.stop()`, and NEVER closes the user's `page`/`context`. **DD-2 (consent — SUPERSEDES the slice prose's "HookBus/consensus consent gate"):** verify-first rejected MCP-consensus quorum (wrong authority — the Captain connecting to the Captain's OWN browser is not an agent vote), the HookBus `PRE_TOOL_USE` gate (wrong layer — the connect doesn't flow through agent dispatch), and the tier-3 `_pending_confirmations` ACK (wrong scope — per-action, not per-session); the right-sized primitive is the **explicit-confirm endpoint gate** = default-OFF `bridge_enabled` flag + `confirm:true` body param + `BROWSER_BRIDGE_CONNECTED`/`REFUSED` audit events (reuses AD-706 governance). **DD-3 (SSRF allowlist):** `_validate_cdp_endpoint` exact-matches `urlparse().hostname` against `bridge_allowed_hosts` (localhost-only default) in the BACKEND, refusing remote endpoints AND the `http://127.0.0.1.evil.com:9222` host-suffix bypass. **Additive (5 Python, 1 UI, 0 config):** `events.py` 3 new `BROWSER_BRIDGE_*` EventTypes; `tools/browser/session.py` `_connected` + sibling `connect()` + `is_connected` + connected `stop()` branch (LAUNCHED `start()`/`stop()` byte-identical — DD-5); `config.py` `bridge_enabled`/`bridge_allowed_hosts`; `tools/browser/tool.py` `_validate_cdp_endpoint()` + `connect_bridge_session()`; `routers/browser_stream.py` `POST /api/browser/bridge/connect` (`require_crew_scope`, honest-degrades when `browser_tool` unset); `workstation/BrowserWorkstation.tsx` flips `bridge` enabled + a `renderBridge()` (endpoint input + consent note + Connect → reuses `BrowserStreamPanel` on connect, no token — DD-1/DD-6). **DD-6 (reuse the watch surface free):** the AD-706a stream is `page.screenshot()`-based → page-bound → works over a `connect_over_cdp` page → zero new streaming code. **Deviation (flagged):** `_validate_cdp_endpoint` bracket-normalizes the IPv6 host (`urlparse("http://[::1]:9222").hostname=="::1"` vs the config's `"[::1]"`) so the localhost default works + test-9 passes, while preserving the exact-host SSRF guard; config kept verbatim. **Gates:** focused Python (`test_ad1052b + test_ad1052a + test_ad706 + test_ad706a`) **53 passed, 1 skipped**; full Python `tests/ -q -n 4` **17234 passed, 23 skipped, 0 failed** (902s; 0 regressions vs clean HEAD `63edf0e2`, where commit `ef6376d2` had already resolved the 16 stale failures); focused vitest **22 passed**; full UI suite **258 files, 1658 passed, 1 skipped, 0 failed** (was 1652 — +6); `npm run build` (tsc -b + vite) **clean**. **`config/system.yaml` untouched**; no new npm dependency; no token in browser JS. **AD-1052 staged (2026-06-20) — Browser/Web-App Workstation (v1: embedded-iframe + the unifying mode model): the third & last workstation type; pure-UI, default-OFF, ZERO backend (#965 epic).** The backend `browser` type is ALREADY registered (`finalize._wire_workstation_types` baselines monaco/browser/chat native, gated `WorkstationsConfig.enabled`), so — exactly as in AD-1021's situation — the AD's job is the `browser` React component + its `nativeWorkstations` registration. **Research synthesis (one type, one contract, three modes):** a single `browser` type with `mode ∈ {embedded, watch, bridge}` over ONE observation/action contract (the AD-706 vocabulary: screenshot+state+url+title / navigate·click·type·scroll·key·back/forward); the modes are different transports over the same contract — *embedded* = a native sandboxed iframe (the live page IS the observation; the human drives it natively), *watch* = MJPEG of an AD-706 headless session, *bridge* = the vocabulary aimed at an attached external Chrome. **v1 ships `embedded` + names `watch`/`bridge` as visible-but-disabled selector segments; the AD-706 engine is REUSED (never rebuilt) by the later slices.** **Fix (8 UI files, additive — mirrors AD-1021):** NEW `workstation/BrowserWorkstation.tsx` (`{ typeId }: NativeWorkstationProps`, ignores `doc`; a 3-segment mode selector [Embedded active; Watch/Bridge `disabled` titled "AD-1052a"/"…1052b"], a URL textbox + Go, a sandboxed `<iframe data-testid="browser-frame" sandbox="allow-scripts allow-same-origin allow-forms allow-popups" referrerPolicy="no-referrer">` shown only after a URL commits, else a static honest-degrade `browser-empty` banner noting X-Frame-Options/CSP block embedding; an exported pure `_normalizeUrl` — http(s) only, prepend `https://` when scheme-less, reject `javascript:`/`data:`/`file:`/`blob:`/`about:`/`vbscript:` → `browser-url-error`); NEW `workstation/BrowserWorkstationPanel.tsx` (mirrors `WorkstationPanel`: store-flag `browserWorkstationOpen` gated, fixed inset0 zIndex30 overlay, header X + Escape close, body `<BrowserWorkstation typeId="browser"/>`); `nativeWorkstations.ts` += `browser` (auto-composes into the AD-1023 WorkspacePanel + is what the AD-1022 launcher seam resolves); `store/useStore.ts` += `browserWorkstationOpen:false` (no action, mirrors `mcpAppsOpen`); `App.tsx` mounts `<BrowserWorkstationPanel/>`; `bridge/stations.tsx` += Engineering `browser-workstation-toggle`. **DD-2** reuse-not-rebuild (v1 = a UI surface, NO browser engine; the AD-706 Playwright engine is reused only by 1052a/b/c). **DD-3** embedded = iframe, not screencast (true shared-screencast of arbitrary cross-origin = 1052a watch + 1052c input-forward, because X-Frame-Options/CSP block iframing). **DD-6** security: cross-origin `allow-same-origin` grants only the framed site's OWN origin storage (SOP still blocks the parent HXI origin), the `http(s)` allowlist blocks scheme injection, `no-referrer`; bridge/input-forward are destructive-capable → HookBus/consensus consent in 1052b/c, never v1. **DD-7** default-OFF: type reg gated `WorkstationsConfig.enabled` (already), overlay gated `browserWorkstationOpen=false`; AD-1052b bridge-external `connectOverCDP` + consent gate; AD-1052c bidirectional input-forwarding; AD-1052d cross-origin reverse proxy; AD-1052e doc-driven initial URL. **AD-1019e staged (2026-06-20) — MCP authoring endpoints (risk tiers + department lockers): the HTTP authoring surface over the pre-existing AD-1019b stores; backend-only, NO migration, gated `management_enabled`; unblocks #964/AD-1019d (epic #955).** **Scope-inversion finding:** #964 (AD-1019d HXI) declared *"No backend change"* — false. AD-1019b shipped the authorization *substrate* (`McpToolRiskStore`, `DepartmentToolGrantStore`, 3-source `resolve_mcp_access`, the `default_risk` column) but its review **explicitly deferred the authoring endpoints**, so there was no surface to *set* a risk tier or *stock* a department locker. AD-1019e adds that surface (the stores are consumed, NOT modified). **(1) `routers/mcp_servers.py`:** `GET /{id}/tools` augments each tool with `risk`/`risk_source` (`resolve_tool_risk` over `record.default_risk` + the per-tool override; fields OMITTED when no risk store → byte-identical); `PUT /{id}/tools/{tool}/risk` (`RiskBody{risk}`; 404 unknown server, 400 `invalid_risk`, 503 no store, else `set_risk`) + `DELETE /{id}/tools/{tool}/risk` (`clear_risk` → `{cleared}`), mirroring the AD-1019 per-agent grant endpoints; `McpServerUpdateBody += default_risk:str|None` (the existing `model_dump→replace→validate_record` merge + `store.update` persist it — DD-2). **(2) NEW `routers/mcp_departments.py`** (`/api/mcp/departments`): `GET /grants`, `POST /{department}/tools` (`{server_id, tool?, enabled}` → composite `mcp:{name}[:{tool}]` id → `issue_grant`), `DELETE /grants/{id}`; included in `api.py`'s flat loop. **DD-1 (key consistency, VERIFIED):** the risk store keys on the server **RECORD ID** — `mcp_workbench.py` builds `_McpTool(server_id=record.id)` and both dispatch readers call `get_risk_sync(record.id, tool)`; the authoring `{server_id}` path param IS that record id, so an authored override is the one the invoke path resolves (a `set→dispatch-read` parity test asserts it). The 2/3 Pydantic `output_dir` defaults are KEPT (test-locked by `test_ad477_naval`); only their consumption changed — `config.py` untouched, as are `shell.py`/`runtime.py`/`finalize.py`/`config/system.yaml`. **AD-1037 staged (2026-06-20) — affect-salience capture: populate `Episode.affect_salience` at encoding via a deterministic store-time lexicon scorer; default-OFF, byte-identical when OFF (#986, Oracle recall epic #902).** AD-979f shipped the *slot* (`Episode.affect_salience`) AND a *dormant consumer* (the AD-873 reranker's affect term, weight `0.0` → skipped) but **deferred the populator**, so every episode stored `0.0` and the affect axis was inert. **Source triage:** (c) AD-1032 arousal **BLOCKED** (not wired); (a) AD-737 `intent_emotion` **REJECTED** (never reaches any of the 18 `Episode(` store sites + valence-not-arousal); (b) **CHOSEN** a pure store-time lexicon mirroring the shipped AD-598 `importance_scorer` (no LLM/network — `store()` is hot). **Fix (additive, four source files):** NEW `cognitive/affect_scorer.py` with one pure `score_affect(episode) -> float` (disjoint `_HIGH`/`_MODERATE` arousal frozensets + `_TAU=2.0`; `raw = 1.0*high + 0.5*moderate + min(0.5,0.1*"!"-count) + min(0.5,0.25*ALLCAPS≥3-count)`; `affect_salience = clamp01(1 - exp(-raw/TAU))`; `raw==0 ⇒ 0.0`; whole body `try/except → 0.0`); a **one-line** capture hook in `store()` immediately after the AD-598 importance block, gated `if getattr(self, "_affect_capture_enabled", False) and episode.affect_salience == 0.0:` → `dataclasses.replace(episode, affect_salience=…)` (only when `>0`); `MemoryConfig.affect_capture_enabled=False` (after `recall_rerank_weights`); the flag threaded through `EpisodicMemory.__init__` + the PRIMARY `__main__.py` boot ctor (the rebuild-CLI ctor left default-False). **DD-2** affect is orthogonal to `importance` (importance = retention priority via trigger/channel/outcome; affect = emotional charge via arousal vocabulary). **DD-3** MAGNITUDE not valence (both "thrilled" and "devastated" raise salience). **The 18-sites → 1-chokepoint rationale:** `Episode(` is built at ~18 sites across 13 files but ALL funnel through the single `store()` chokepoint, so the capture hooks ONE place — NO constructor signature change (the field already exists from AD-979f), NO frozen-dataclass contract change. **Default-OFF / byte-identical:** OFF ⇒ scorer never called ⇒ slot stays `0.0` ⇒ the AD-979f rerank term (weight `0.0`) is already skipped ⇒ recall ordering byte-identical; `config/system.yaml` untouched. **Flip path:** set `affect_capture_enabled: true` AND `recall_rerank_weights["affect"] > 0` under `memory:` and restart (store-time only → new episodes; backfill is AD-1037a). **+16 pytest** (`tests/test_ad1037_affect_capture.py`, BF-287 — a REAL `_FakeCollection` stub, NOT MagicMock, + the `__new__` pattern from `test_ad598` Test 9; **AD-1038 staged (2026-06-20) — live remember/know render: surface the agent's OWN AD-979f Tulving remember/know type into its response as a gap-safe metacognitive cue; render/gate-only, default-OFF, byte-identical when OFF (#987, Oracle recall epic #902).** AD-979f shipped the `RecallConfidence.recall_type` classifier + the `remember_know_typing_enabled` gate but **never rendered the type into a live prompt** (AD-981b's DD-7 explicitly earmarked #987/AD-1038 as out of scope), so an agent that *remembers* (strong + episodically grounded) and one that merely *knows* (familiarity without grounded specifics) spoke identically. **Fix (ONE file, `cognitive/cognitive_agent.py`; NO `episodic.py`/ranking/classifier/consensus change — it REUSES the AD-981b rail):** AD-981b's `_recall_confidence_band` probe is refactored into a thin band-only delegator over a new shared `async _recall_confidence_probe(...) -> RecallConfidence | None` (the single `recall_for_agent_with_confidence` round-trip that already produces BOTH the AD-979a band AND the AD-979f `recall_type`).
