**AD-981b staged (2026-06-19) — surface the agent's OWN Feeling-of-Knowing band into its response: an honest-absence cue for a weak/none name-cued recall (the "Heidi" misinformation case); default-OFF, byte-identical when OFF (#988, Oracle recall epic #902).** AD-981a made the AD-979a FoK band **observable** on the live sovereign path but it still only reached the AD-979d cross-agent escalation — **never the agent's own answer**. The "Heidi" case: the Captain asks a name-cued question (*"do you remember Heidi?"*) for which **nothing is recorded** (only "Grim" is in the shard); the BF-027 relaxed agent-return threshold still surfaces the topically-distant "Grim" episode, so `recent_memories` is **non-empty** ⇒ the empty-memories honest-absence flag never fires ⇒ the agent affirms the named memory and **invents provenance** (a misinformation-effect confabulation). The band for that query is **weak**/**none** — the signal that should drive "I have nothing recorded," thrown away on the own-response path. **Fix (two files, `config.py` + `cognitive/cognitive_agent.py`; NO `episodic.py` change, NO ranking change, NO new recall mechanism — the band is REUSED, already computed unconditionally inside `recall_for_agent_with_confidence`):** (1) `MemoryConfig.recall_confidence_gating_enabled=False` (between `cross_agent_recall_enabled` and AD-979e). (2) Two **pure** helpers by `_format_memory_section`: `@staticmethod _recall_confidence_note(band)` → a **gap-regex-safe** honest-absence cue for weak/none (`""` for strong/empty; verified `is_capability_gap(note) is False` — "lacks"→"does not contain" so it doesn't trip `_CAPABILITY_GAP_RE`), and `_recall_confidence_segment(observation)` → `[note, ""]` or `None` (the testable render-decision unit). (3) An async `_recall_confidence_band(self, *, query, mem_id, k=5)` reading ONLY `self._runtime`, **Tier-2** (any failure → `""`), `k=5` literal. (4) A flag-gated probe call site in `_recall_relevant_memories` (after the AD-979d slice-2 block, `query`/`_mem_id` in scope) that stashes `observation["_recall_fok_band"]`. (5) A render injection at **both** the DM and WR episodic sites in `_build_user_message` (`if _fok_seg: _emit("recall_confidence", _fok_seg)`). **Byte-identical-OFF (NON-NEGOTIABLE):** OFF ⇒ no probe ⇒ no `_recall_fok_band` key ⇒ segment `None` ⇒ no emit ⇒ prompt unchanged; `config/system.yaml` untouched (default-OFF ships in `config.py`). **Flip path:** set `recall_confidence_gating_enabled: true` under `memory:` in `config/system.yaml` and restart. **+13 pytest** (`tests/test_ad981b_recall_confidence_gating.py`, BF-287 real `EpisodicMemory` + `_Holder`/`SimpleNamespace`; the Heidi regression yields band **weak**, best_sim≈0.061, count=1); blast-radius (AD-981a / AD-979d slice-2 + cross-agent / AD-979a / AD-979f / distribution / HXI chat — **103 passed**) green; `import probos.runtime` clean. STAGED (not committed).

**AD-979e slice 1 staged (2026-06-19) — self-healing reconsolidation: capture/persist an additive, capped, provenance-tagged recall access path; default-OFF, byte-identical, mechanism-only, NO recall effect (#907, Oracle recall epic #902).** The **highest-risk** capability in the recall frontier — the only one that can *edit* memory — so slice 1 is deliberately conservative: **capture/persist ONLY**, additive episode metadata, DIRECT-only, capped, provenance-tagged, default-OFF, with **NO recall READ** of what it stores and **NO canonical-content mutation**. New `async def record_recall_access_path(episode_id, *, query, bridge, source=MemorySource.DIRECT.value) -> bool` (next to `update_episode_metadata`) whose poison-resistance is **structural — every gate returns `False`**: (1) default-OFF (returns before any read/write → byte-identical); (5) DIRECT-only (refuse + log a non-DIRECT source — never re-encode from a SECONDHAND/peer/reflection fragment); empty-terms; honest-degrade on a metadata read failure / unknown id / malformed envelope; (3) de-dup of an identical normalized `sorted(terms)` (repeat-poison resistance); append `{"q", "bridge", "ts", "src"}`; (2) FIFO cap to the last `_RECONSOL_MAX_PATHS=8`; persist via `update_episode_metadata` (metadata-only — **no `documents=` → no re-embed**, so the **AD-541b write-once invariant** on document/embedding/user_input/reflection is preserved). Module-level `_RECONSOL_MAX_PATHS=8` + pure `_normalize_access_terms` (lowercase, `[a-z0-9]{2,}`, order-stable dedup), `MemoryConfig.reconsolidation_enabled=False`, a defaulted `EpisodicMemory.__init__` param, and the canonical `__main__.py` boot site (the rebuild-CLI ctor threads no AD-979x flag → left unchanged). **Three issue-text corrections heeded** (verified against HEAD): `recall_with_control`'s 3rd return is `actions` not terms; the AD-958d Hebbian access-path graph **does not exist** (metadata is the only additive surface); there is **no callable AD-592 guard** (poison-resistance is structural, not a call). `MemorySource` was not imported in `episodic.py` (the verify-first claim was wrong) — added to the existing `from probos.types import …` line. **Byte-identical-OFF:** flag default `False` → `record_recall_access_path` returns `False` on gate 1 (no read/write/key), and there is **no live caller** this slice; `_metadata_to_episode` reads only known `.get()` keys so the new key is ignored on read (round-trip safe). **+10 pytest** (`tests/test_ad979e_reconsolidation.py`, BF-287 real `EpisodicMemory`/MiniLM/ChromaDB): default-OFF byte-identical, ON additive write, additive-only invariant (canonical content + document byte-identical), AD-541b intact, FIFO cap, de-dup, DIRECT-only (SECONDHAND+REFLECTION refused), provenance, unknown-id + empty-query honest-degrade. Blast-radius (AD-979a / AD-979d slice 1+2 / AD-981a / dreaming / distribution = 108 passed) green; `import probos.runtime` clean. **Deferred:** slice 2 = capture at the live gap-bridge moment; slice 3 = the recall READ that heals (where an integrity/consensus gate is decided); slice 4 = offline dreaming bulk pass. Staged for review, not committed.

**AD-979d slice 2 staged (2026-06-19) — live-wire the `CrossAgentRecallService` into the sovereign recall path; #906 is now LIVE, default-OFF / byte-identical when OFF (#906, Oracle recall epic #902).** Slice 1 shipped the service but with **no live caller**. Slice 2 wires it into `CognitiveAgent._recall_relevant_memories` via ONE new helper `_maybe_cross_agent_recall(self, *, query, mem_id, k=3)` (reads ONLY `self.id` + `self._runtime`, Tier-2 throughout — every gate/failure ⇒ `[]`) + one flag-gated call-site block. On a **weak** own Feeling-of-Knowing band (the AD-979a/c slow-gap), it escalates to the single most-associated crew peer (Hebbian `REL_SOCIAL` top-1, weight `> 0`) and appends the peer's **confident** recall to `observation["recent_memories"]` as `[secondhand]`. **ID-space resolution (load-bearing):** `REL_SOCIAL` edges key on the **live** `agent.id` but episodic shards key on `sovereign_id or id` (they diverge for onboarded crew) — resolved at the call site WITHOUT changing the service: rank peers in live-id space, pass a **singleton** shard id (`peer_candidates=[top.sovereign_id or top.id]`) so the service's `get_preferred_targets` over a 1-element list is an identity; `requesting_agent_id = mem_id` (shard) drives self-exclusion/governance. **Byte-identical-OFF:** the call-site `if mem_cfg is not None and getattr(mem_cfg, "cross_agent_recall_enabled", False):` (the existing slice-1 flag, default `False`) skips the block entirely when OFF — no helper call, no extra query, `observation` untouched; `mem_cfg` re-fetched defensively before the guard (it is assigned inside the BF-138 `try`, which can leave it unbound on an early exception). **Render (Py3.12):** `"source": MemorySource.SECONDHAND.value` (the string) — `Episode.source` is a `str` field, so this is byte-consistent with the existing recall path; the bare enum would render as its repr under Python 3.12's enum `__format__` and break the `[secondhand]` marker. **+9 pytest** (`tests/test_ad979d_slice2_live_wiring.py`, BF-287 — real `EpisodicMemory`/`HebbianRouter`/`TrustNetwork`/`CrossAgentRecallService`, requester a tiny `_Holder` stub; deterministic weak band via `relevance_threshold=0.99` + `weak_floor=0.0`; includes a **structural** byte-identical-OFF test). Slice-1 (+10) + blast-radius (100 passed) green; `import probos.runtime` clean. Still deferred (#907): multi-peer fusion + trust-weighting, IntentBus + rate limiter, reconsolidation into the requester's own shard.

**AD-979d staged (2026-06-19) — distributed cross-agent associative recall, slice 1 (the `CrossAgentRecallService`); default-OFF, byte-identical when OFF (#906, Oracle recall epic #902).** When an agent's own sovereign recall returns a **weak** Feeling-of-Knowing band (the AD-979a/c *slow-gap* — relevant-but-under-the-bar, a vocabulary-mismatch miss; **not** strong recall, **not** genuine absence), a human asks a colleague who was there. This ships the **mechanism half only**. Honest decomposition (the load-bearing finding): `SocialMemoryService` (AD-462d) is Ward-Room **BROADCAST only** (no directed single-peer path → not reusable), and every agent's episodes live in **ONE shared collection** sharded by `agent_ids`, so a "peer query" is just `recall_for_agent_with_confidence(peer_id, …)` on that same store — **no IntentBus transport and no rate limiter needed** (those are only for out-of-process peers). New standalone `cognitive/cross_agent_recall.py`: `CrossAgentRecallService` (constructor-injected — episodic store + Hebbian router + optional trust network; peers passed in per call; never imports the registry) with one 9-gate method `escalate_recall(...)` where **every guard returns `[]`** (the no-op path): **(1)** OFF gate (`enabled=False` default → returns before touching any dependency → **byte-identical**); **(2)** governance refuse under `OWN_SHARD_ONLY` (AD-607e); **(3)** FOK gate — escalate **only** on a `weak` own band (`own_band` is the requester's already-computed band, supplied directly); **(4)** exclude self from candidates; **(5)** Hebbian **top-1** (`get_preferred_targets(…, rel_type=REL_SOCIAL)[0]`, the single most-associated peer); **(6)** Tier-2 around the peer recall → log-and-degrade to `[]`; **(7)** surface **only** the peer's `strong` band (no confabulated corroboration); **(8)** attach the peer's **raw** Beta `(alpha, beta)` (never a derived mean, never weighted); **(9)** bounded `PeerRecall` items carrying `MemorySource.SECONDHAND`. `MemoryConfig.cross_agent_recall_enabled=False` (default OFF) + a construction-only runtime late-bind mirroring the AD-462d block (`self._cross_agent_recall_service`, **no hot-path call**). The three consumed subsystems (`EpisodicMemory`/`HebbianRouter`/`TrustNetwork`) and `RecallConfidence` are consumed **UNCHANGED**. **Byte-identical-OFF proof:** flag OFF ⇒ service never enabled, and even constructed `escalate_recall` returns `[]` at gate 1 before any dependency is touched; AD-979a/b/c + routing + config suites stay green (58 passed). **+10 pytest** (`tests/test_ad979d_cross_agent_recall.py`, BF-287 discipline — real `EpisodicMemory` on `tmp_path` + real MiniLM, real in-memory `HebbianRouter` (weights via `record_interaction(…, rel_type=REL_SOCIAL)`), real `TrustNetwork` (via `record_outcome`); NO MagicMock; two sovereign shards seeded via the real store-with-`agent_ids` path). **Slice 1 = service/mechanism ONLY. Deferred:** live `cognitive_agent.py` wiring (the live recall site is multi-tier and doesn't surface the FOK band → needs a restructure), IntentBus transport + per-domain rate limiter (out-of-process peers), multi-peer fan-out + trust-**weighted** fusion (raw `alpha`/`beta` only here), reconsolidation / storing into the requester's shard (#907).

**AD-979f staged (2026-06-19) — remember/know recall typing (A) + affective-salience rerank slot/axis (B3); default-OFF, byte-identical when OFF (#908, Oracle recall epic #902).** Two cognitive-science recall refinements, contained to the episodic tier, both OFF by default. **(A) Remember/Know typing** (Tulving 1985): `RecallConfidence` gains `recall_type: str = ""` + two pure module-level helpers next to `classify_recall_confidence` — `classify_remember_know(band, top_episode)` (`"remember"` iff `band=="strong"` AND the top has episodic grounding [a populated `AnchorFrame.channel`/`participants`/`trigger_agent`/`source_timestamp`] AND `source != "reflection"`; `"know"` for strong/weak-without-grounding or a reflection synthesis; `"none"` for empty band/episode) + `remember_know_phrase` (pure formatter). Gated by `MemoryConfig.remember_know_typing_enabled=False`, threaded into `EpisodicMemory.__init__` and attached at BOTH confidence sites (`recall_with_confidence`, `recall_for_agent_with_confidence`) via `dataclasses.replace` — OFF ⇒ no replace ⇒ `recall_type` stays `""` ⇒ byte-identical (the early-return empty-store/empty-query paths untouched). **(B3) Affective salience** — the honest finding is that **affect is NOT stored on `Episode` today** (no capture pipeline), so this ships the **mechanism + storage SLOT/axis ONLY**: `Episode` gains a LAST field `affect_salience: float = 0.0` (frozen-safe; round-trips through ChromaDB with a `(TypeError, ValueError)`→0.0 honest-degrade, pre-979f rows → 0.0); `_composite_recall_score` gains `w_a = float(weights.get("affect", 0.0)); if w_a: score *= _clamp01(episode.affect_salience) ** w_a` after the confidence term; `recall_rerank_weights` gains `"affect": 0.0`. **Default weight 0.0 ⇒ term skipped (`x**0==1`) ⇒ byte-identical.** Wired at the primary `__main__.py` boot site (the rebuild CLI ctor untouched). **Byte-identical-OFF proof:** the AD-873 (composite, affect absent/0.0) + AD-979a (`RecallConfidence` default `recall_type==""`) suites stay green. **+23 pytest** (`tests/test_ad979f_remember_know_affect.py`, BF-287 real `Episode`/`RecallConfidence`/`MemoryConfig`/`EpisodicMemory` on `tmp_path` — no MagicMock). Gate: ad979f + ad873 + ad979a = 61 passed; blast (ad979b/c + ad988) = 52 passed; `import probos.runtime` ok. **DEFERRED follow-ons:** the affect-capture pipeline (what writes `affect_salience`) + the live agent-wording render of `recall_type` (only the pure `remember_know_phrase` ships). Staged for review, not committed.

**AD-988 staged (2026-06-19) — retrieval-reason transparency: surface *why* an episodic fragment was retrieved; default-OFF, byte-identical when OFF (#932, Oracle recall epic #902).** The episodic tier computes a full `RecallScore` breakdown and the Oracle **collapses it to one scalar** (`composite_score`) at the `OracleResult` projection — so the agent saw a score but not the dominant signal (the Counselor's 2026-06-13 gap: *"I can tell it's reaching, but I can't tell why"*). **Fix (contained: oracle_service.py + types.py + a config flag):** (1) `OracleResult` gains `match_reason: str = ""` (additive/defaulted — the other ~8 construction sites keep `""`, no regression). (2) New **pure** `dominant_match_reason(rs) -> str` in `types.py` (deterministic, unit-testable): `keyword_hits>0` ⇒ `"keyword match (<n> hit[s])"` (lexical/FTS5 = strongest explicit signal, wins outright); else the MAX of the normalized `[0,1]` graded signals named with its value (`"semantic similarity (0.83)"`/`"Hebbian co-activation"`/`"anchored context"`/`"recency"`/`"temporal context"`, ties → first); `trust_weight` **excluded** (a weighting, not a reason); all-zero ⇒ `"weak/ambiguous match"`. (3) Gate: default-OFF `MemoryConfig.oracle_match_reason_enabled=False`, read at the OracleService construction site (`startup/cognitive_services.py`, defensive `getattr`) into `self._match_reason_enabled` (threaded via a defaulted `__init__` param — every existing `OracleService(...)` unchanged). (4) The episodic `_query_episodic` projection (the only site where `rs` is in scope) sets `match_reason` when ON, `""` when OFF; the `recall()` fallback (no `RecallScore`) stays `""`. (5) `query_formatted` appends `, why: {reason}` only when ON and a reason is present — OFF (default) ⇒ rendering **byte-identical** to pre-AD-988 (asserted against an exact expected string; ON-minus-the-why-clause == OFF). Closes the **3rd instance** of the *rich scoring computed then discarded at the prompt boundary* pattern (after AD-979a, BF-625) — candidate architecture-review flag. **Deferred (per #932 design):** the other 6 tiers' per-tier reason strings; "match terms" extraction (`keyword_hits` is a count). Tests: `test_ad988_retrieval_reason.py` (**21**, BF-287 real `RecallScore`/`OracleResult`/`MemoryConfig`/`OracleService`/`Episode`); regression `test_ad462f_memory_refs` + oracle tiers (686/686b/688/695/696) + `test_distribution` = **105 passed**; `import probos.runtime` ok. NO existing-field/signature change; NO scoring/ranking change; `config/system.yaml` untouched. Staged for review, not committed.

**AD-1032 staged (2026-06-19) — exogenous interrupts & arousal/zone reconfiguration; faculty-local, default-OFF, byte-identical when OFF (#980, sixth & final build of the attention pilot, epic #975/#983).** Gives the `AttentionFaculty` (AD-1029) a real **arousal model** — the cognitive-layer mirror of HXI Design Principle #9 (LCARS Red-Alert). An exogenous event (an @mention, a bridge alert, a materially-changed scene, a safety/consensus event, urgent peer gossip) raises a **faculty-local** cognitive zone (REUSES the AD-588 `CognitiveZone`; **never** touches the agent's circuit-breaker zone) GREEN→AMBER→RED by severity; arousal **narrows** the bid competition (Yerkes–Dodson): **RED** suppresses ambient sources (`camera_scene_ambient`/`camera_scene`/`telemetry`/`gossip`) + multiplies the budget, keeping threat-relevant + **pinned** only; **AMBER** suppresses only the ambient camera bid; low-severity events **queue** without reconfiguring (a single `scene_change`/`gossip` stays GREEN; the same type repeating within the window → AMBER); the zone **decays** one level per quiet `arbitrate` and fully resets to GREEN after `arousal_full_decay_seconds`. **Severity table** (Captain-approved 2026-06-19): alert/consensus/safety→RED, mention→AMBER, scene_change/gossip→GREEN. `AttentionConfig` gains `arousal_enabled=False` + `arousal_red_budget_multiplier=0.5` + `arousal_full_decay_seconds=300.0` + `arousal_repeat_window_seconds=60.0` (all defaulted); `events.py` gains the `EXOGENOUS_{MENTION,ALERT,SCENE_CHANGE,CONSENSUS,SAFETY,GOSSIP}` taxonomy (definitions only). The single **governed boundary** `CognitiveAgent.on_exogenous_event(event_type, *, severity=None, **payload)` forwards through the AD-397 `spine.deliver_exogenous` inlet when ON (faculty reads `arousal_enabled` from its parent's live config); a no-op when OFF. **Double-gated:** the faculty is only composed when `attention.enabled`, so arousal is doubly inert in production. **DEFERRED (scope cut):** the live mesh emission-site wiring (real router @mentions / bridge alerts / consensus events calling the boundary) — those sources live in separate subsystems with no clean per-agent inbound hook; every acceptance criterion is met through the governed boundary, so this AD did NOT touch runtime/router/bridge/consensus. **Byte-identical (multi-level):** `enabled=False`⇒no faculty; `enabled=True`+`arousal_enabled=False`⇒faculty active but arousal bypassed⇒`arbitrate`==AD-1029; ON+GREEN/no-event⇒no narrowing — proven by AD-1029 suite + AD-1028 goldens UNCHANGED (gate ad1032+ad1029+ad1028+ad1031 = **82 passed**). Tests: `test_ad1032_arousal.py` (25, BF-287 real objects); blast-radius `test_distribution`+`test_hxi_chat_integration` = 38 passed; `import probos.runtime` smoke ok. NO circuit-breaker zone write; NO live emission wiring; NO HXI; NO contract change; `config/system.yaml` untouched. Staged for review, not committed.

**AD-1031 staged (2026-06-19) — camera / visual scene as a salience-gated bid; default-OFF, byte-identical when OFF (#979, closes #973, fixes the BF-632 class, epic #975/#983).** Turns the always-on AD-733a visual-scene prepend into a **salience-gated camera bid**: the full scene wins prompt space only when SALIENT — the Captain REFERENCED vision (`see/look/screen/camera/"what do you see"…`), the frame MATERIALLY CHANGED (`novelty ≥ camera_novelty_minimum`, default **0.3**), or it's a VISUAL TASK (image attachment) — otherwise a RECESSIVE one-line `[Live camera] {summary}` (present-but-quiet). Stops agents over-narrating an unchanged scene (#973) and removes the visual block's prompt dominance (the BF-632 class). **Two-site, one-flag:** `AttentionConfig` gains `camera_scene_bid_enabled=False` + `camera_novelty_minimum=0.3`; new pure `visual_reference_score(text)->float` in `salience.py` (keyword/phrase, word-boundary tokenized so `"seem"`/`"overlook"` don't false-match; fully unit-tested); the router (`routers/agents.py`) gates the AD-733a prepend — ON ⇒ pass the rendered scene + `novelty`/`summary` via `params["_visual_scene"]` (no prepend), OFF (default) ⇒ prepend EXACTLY as today (byte-identical), `force_describe`+`note_dm_activity` run in both, `captain_message` stays RAW (BF-632); the agent DM branch (`_build_user_message`) emits the gated bid with **PRIMACY** (`zone_floor=-1e6` + high salience, leads) when prominent / the empty-WM BF-294 sentinel, **RECESSIVE** (`zone_floor=+1e6` + salience `-1.0`, trails + drops first under scarcity) otherwise, and NOTHING when OFF/no scene. Bid flows through `ContextAssembler` (attention OFF — the production path) or `AttentionFaculty` (ON), **NOT** the `deliver_exogenous` inlet (AD-1032). **Byte-identical proof:** AD-1028 DM golden + AD-733a router-prepend tests green UNCHANGED. NO new vision LLM call (reuse `novelty_score`); NO engagement-adaptive cadence (follow-up); WR branch + `config/system.yaml` untouched. Tests: `test_ad1031_camera_bid.py` (14, BF-287 real objects); gate (ad1031+ad1028+ad733a)=**59 passed**, blast-radius (distribution+hxi_chat_integration)=**38 passed**, `import probos.runtime` ok. Staged for review, not committed.

**AD-1030 staged (2026-06-19) — salience scoring (relevance × recency × importance) for episodic + working-memory bids (#978, epic #975/#983).** Makes the `AttentionFaculty` (AD-1029) actually **adaptive**: the fixed insertion priority for the two biggest, most-variable context sources — **episodic recall** + **working memory** — is replaced by a transparent **linear salience** so memories are selected/ordered by how much they matter to the current goal (the raw Captain message). New pure module `src/probos/cognitive/salience.py`: `compute_salience(*, relevance, recency, importance, weights)` = weight-normalized `(w_rel·rel + w_rec·rec + w_imp·imp_norm)/Σw` over `[0,1]` terms; helpers `recency_decay(age, half_life)=exp(-age/half_life)`, `importance_norm=(imp-1)/9` (AD-598 1–10), and a pure `cosine_similarity` — **no I/O, no embedding model, no `await`; fully unit-testable**. `AttentionConfig` gains `salience_scoring=False` + `w_rel=1.0`/`w_rec=0.5`/`w_imp=0.5` + `recency_half_life_seconds=86400.0` (all defaulted; **STRICTLY default-OFF** ⇒ AD-1029 fixed-priority byte-identical, and `attention.enabled` also default-OFF ⇒ doubly inert). Gated behind the flag: the recall write-site enriches each memory with internal `_embedding`/`_timestamp`/`_importance` keys (underscore ⇒ never rendered); `_build_user_message` (DM + WR) embeds the goal ONCE, scores + **reorders the episodic block by salience DESC** (a high-relevance low-recency memory can outrank a low-relevance recent one) and weights the bid by its best member; the **working-memory** bid gets a scored salience (relevance + recency, **importance omitted** — WM has none). **Spreading activation (AD-604) DEFERRED** — async + dormant, a recall-layer concern, would force an `await` onto the synchronous faculty hot path; AD-1030 ships the pure three-term scorer only. **HARD-STOP guard (c): WM ships bid-salience-only** — `render_context` is a category-grouped/priority-sorted/budget-evicted render with no flat ordered path, so no WM internal reorder (its content is unchanged; only its competition weight is scored). `AttentionManager.score_bid` stays the identity over the (now scored) bid salience; the task-scoring `_compute_single` is untouched. Byte-identical proven: AD-1028 goldens + AD-1029 suite pass UNCHANGED (gate AD-1030+1029+1028 = **70 passed**); blast `test_distribution` + `test_hxi_chat_integration` = **38 passed**; `import probos.runtime` ok. Tests: `test_ad1030_salience.py` (27, BF-287 real embeddings — the rank-flip acceptance, recency/importance/cosine, ON-reorders/OFF-preserves, render-safety, WM bid-salience-only). Scope: NO spreading activation, NO camera gating (AD-1031), NO arousal/zone (AD-1032), NO learned scorer, NO WM reorder, NO `config/system.yaml` change. Staged for review, not committed.

**AD-1029 staged (2026-06-19) — the `AttentionFaculty`: per-agent deterministic paired attention organ (#977, epic #975/#983).** The pilot **Cognitive Organ** of the Composable-Cognition / Attention epic (`docs/development/attention-architecture.md` §3, §4), on AD-1033's organ contract + AD-1034's spine + AD-1028's `AttentionBid`/`ContextAssembler` — the answer to "could the controller be a deterministic agent each cognitive agent is paired with?": **yes, as a paired cognitive organ, not a mesh peer.** New `src/probos/cognitive/attention_faculty.py`: `AttentionFaculty(BaseCognitiveOrgan)`, `default_name="attention"` — **deterministic, synchronous, NO LLM, NO bus round-trip on the hot path** — that DRIVES the AD-1028 assembler. `perceive` drains pending **exogenous** bids + merges them with the per-turn bids; `decide` delegates to `ContextAssembler.assemble` under the global token budget (v1 salience = the bid's **fixed insertion priority** — NO salience scoring, that's AD-1030); `act` writes a **bid-competition audit trace** (a pure SIDE-EFFECT — never alters the prompt). The synchronous entry `arbitrate(bids, *, token_budget) -> list[str]` runs `perceive→decide→act` and, **with no pending exogenous + v1 fixed salience, returns EXACTLY `ContextAssembler.assemble(bids, token_budget=…)`** — the byte-identical "faithful driver" guarantee. Exogenous intake: `on_signal(EXOGENOUS_SIGNAL_KIND)` coerces a between-turn signal (delivered through the agent-owned `spine.deliver_exogenous` inlet — NOT the real intent bus, that's AD-1032) into a pending bid visible to the NEXT turn (the spine's generic `drive_cycle` is a deterministic no-op for the faculty — drain happens ONCE at `arbitrate`). Audit sink: the AD-431 journal `record` is async + LLM-shaped, so per the AD-1033 organ-audit decoupling the trace routes through the sync `OrganAuditEmit` seam (the agent wires `_emit_attention_audit` debug-log — **NOT a new persistence layer**). **Minimal `CognitiveAgent` wiring:** `_compose_organs` composes the faculty **only when `memory.attention.enabled`** (same resolution `_resolve_attention_budget` uses) — default-OFF ⇒ NO organ ⇒ `has_organs` False ⇒ `drive_cycle` no-op ⇒ `_build_user_message` keeps the **textually-unchanged** AD-1028 inline `ContextAssembler` path ⇒ **byte-identical**; DM + WR branches call `faculty.arbitrate(…)` only when a faculty is composed; tiny `_active_attention_faculty()` accessor (`spine.get_organ("attention")`, guard None). **Paired-faculty classification** (new): 1:1, intrinsic, deterministic, meta-cognitive **child component** (born-with/dies-with the parent, identity namespaced under it) — **not** a mesh peer (no registry/address/trust/vote; structurally not a `BaseAgent`). Deferred so v1 is provably behavior-preserving: salience math (AD-1030), camera gating (AD-1031), arousal/zone + real-bus wiring (AD-1032). Tests: `test_ad1029_attention_faculty.py` (24, BF-287 real faculty + real spine) — default-OFF no-faculty, `arbitrate`==`assemble` (unbounded + tight), `_build_user_message` ON==OFF byte-identical (DM+WR), enabled-gated attach/subscribe/idempotent, exogenous visible-then-drained-once, `drive_cycle` no-op, audit recorded + prompt unchanged, AST proof of no `await`/no bus, organ_id namespaced + not a mesh agent. **AD-1028 goldens pass UNCHANGED** (default-OFF byte-identical proof); gate AD-1029+AD-1028 = **43 passed**; blast `test_distribution` + `test_hxi_chat_integration` = **38 passed**; `import probos.runtime` clean. Staged for review, not committed.

**AD-1028 staged (2026-06-18) — ContextAssembler seam + `AttentionBid` + global token budget, behavior-preserving (#976, epic #975/#983).** Third and final build of the Composable-Cognition / Attention foundation (`docs/development/attention-architecture.md` §4, §6), on top of AD-1033's organ contract and AD-1034's spine. The DM and Ward-Room prompts were assembled **push-style** — every source prepended into a fixed ~14-step chain with no arbiter and **no global token budget** (a busy DM could silently overflow the model window). This AD introduces the **pull-style seam**: every block becomes an `AttentionBid` and a pure `ContextAssembler` selects/orders the winners under a budget. **The Captain's non-disruptive mandate is the paramount constraint: with `attention.enabled=False` (default) the DM + WR prompt is BYTE-IDENTICAL to today.** New in `cognitive/attention.py`: `AttentionBid{source, render (LAZY zero-arg renderer), modality, salience, token_cost, zone_floor, pin}` + `estimate_tokens` + a pure `ContextAssembler.assemble(bids, *, token_budget) -> list[str]` (pinned always kept; non-pinned admitted highest-salience-first while the running total stays in budget — scarcity drops the LOWEST-salience unpinned bid, never blind truncation; survivors ordered by `(zone_floor, insertion)` for primacy/recency; `render()` called ONLY on survivors so a dropped bid's renderer is never invoked). `AttentionManager` gains a `score_bid`/`score_bids` seam (v1 = fixed salience; the task-scoring API is untouched). New `AttentionConfig{enabled=False, token_budget=120_000}` nested at `MemoryConfig.attention` (defaulted → runs out-of-the-box). `CognitiveAgent._build_user_message` DM + WR branches refactored so each block becomes a bid whose LAZY renderer returns EXACTLY the segment(s) it previously appended (the DM trailing-`""` and WR leading-`""` separator conventions reproduced inside each bid); budget resolves via new `_resolve_attention_budget()` (flag-OFF → unbounded `1_000_000_000` so nothing drops; flag-ON → configured `token_budget`, the first global overflow guard). All async/side-effecting blocks (telemetry caching, sensorium dispatch, DM self-monitoring) keep their awaits + side effects at collect time. **Byte-identity proven by two golden snapshots** captured from the unmodified method (`tests/fixtures/ad1028_golden/{dm,wr}_golden.txt`) that the refactored method reproduces with the flag off. The `_build_user_message` signature + the subclass override contract (`architect.py`/`builder.py` `super()` delegation) are unchanged; chain/`proactive_think`/fallback paths untouched. Defers salience scoring (AD-1030), camera gating (AD-1031), exogenous interrupts (AD-1032), the `AttentionFaculty` organ (AD-1029). Tests: new `test_ad1028_context_assembler.py` (19: 2 golden + 6 assembler + 3 config + 3 manager-seam + 2 budget-gating + 3 estimate-tokens) = 19 passed; blast `test_distribution + test_hxi_chat_integration` 38 passed unchanged; sensorium/comm regression 53 passed; `import probos.runtime` OK. Staged for review, not committed.

**AD-1034 staged (2026-06-18) — the `CognitiveSpine` (thin): an agent's synchronous central nervous system, behavior-preserving (#982, epic #975/#983).** Second build of the Composable-Cognition foundation (Design Principle #12; `docs/development/composable-cognition.md` §2.2–§2.5, §9), on top of AD-1033's organ contract. New `src/probos/cognitive/spine.py`: a `CognitiveSpine` owned by each `CognitiveAgent` (`self._spine`) that (1) **composes** organs (ordered `attach_organ`/`detach_organ`/`detach_all` registry; organs born-with/die-with the parent), (2) **drives** the `perceive→decide→act` cycle across organs synchronously in attach order (`drive_cycle` — **zero organs returns immediately**), (3) carries a synchronous in-process **signal** channel (`subscribe`/`emit_signal`, opt-in `on_signal` convention — NOT the intent bus), and (4) exposes the single governed mesh-boundary **inlet** `deliver_exogenous` (STUB: stores + forwards in-process; real-bus wiring is AD-1031/1032). `CognitiveAgent` wiring is minimal: construct `self._spine` in `__init__`, a `_compose_organs()` birth hook + `stop()`→`detach_all()` teardown, and a **GUARDED hook** in `_run_cognitive_lifecycle` (between recall and decide) that drives the cycle **only if `has_organs`**. **Byte-identical (the hard requirement):** with zero organs (the default for every existing agent) the hook never fires — proven by the existing real-agent lifecycle/`handle_intent`/`direct_message`/`stop` suites staying green unchanged (`test_distribution` + `test_hxi_chat_integration` + `test_ad398_crew_identity` + `test_ad492_cognitive_correlation_ids` + `test_ad568e_faithfulness_verification` + `test_ad585_tiered_knowledge` = 153 passed). PERF/§9: the cycle path is synchronous — no `await` on a bus/network call (AST-asserted); sovereignty (AD-397): the spine never holds/touches the intent bus (source-asserted). Scope: NO concrete organ (AD-1029), NO real-bus wiring (AD-1031/1032), NO mesh-peer registration, NO async spine, NO `config/system.yaml` change. Tests: `test_ad1034_cognitive_spine.py` (29, BF-287 real objects); gate 29 passed + `import probos.runtime` smoke ok. Staged for review, not committed.

**AD-1033 shipped (2026-06-18) — the `CognitiveOrgan` contract: a child component of an agent, not a mesh agent (#981, epic #975/#983).** First build of the Composable-Cognition foundation (Design Principle #12; `docs/development/composable-cognition.md` §2.1, §7) — the *contract* an organ must satisfy, before any organ (AD-1029) or spine (AD-1034) exists. New standalone `src/probos/cognitive/organ.py`: a `@runtime_checkable` `CognitiveOrgan` Protocol (the **synchronous** `perceive→decide→act` cycle, idempotent parent-bound `attach`/`detach`, `name`/`organ_id`/`parent_id`/`attached`, `set_audit_emit`); `make_organ_id(parent_id, name) -> "{parent_id}.{name}"`; and a minimal `BaseCognitiveOrgan` (stores `parent_id`+`name`, exposes `organ_id`, idempotent attach/detach + `on_attach`/`on_detach` hooks, deterministic no-op cycle, `_emit_audit_trace`). Non-membership is load-bearing: an organ is NOT in the registry, NOT mesh-addressable, has NO trust/vote/consensus standing, and is born-with/dies-with its parent — proven structurally (`BaseCognitiveOrgan` is not a `BaseAgent`). The audit hook is decoupled from the cognitive journal (AD-431): a sync `OrganAuditEmit` no-op default, since the journal's `record` is async and the organ cycle must not `await` (§9). **Byte-identical:** the module imports nothing from `probos` and nothing imports it. Scope: NO spine (AD-1034), NO concrete organ (AD-1029), NO registry/mesh/`CognitiveAgent` change. Tests: `test_ad1033_cognitive_organ_protocol.py` (27, BF-287 real objects); gate 27 passed + `import probos.runtime` smoke `ok`. Staged for review, not committed.

**BF-632 shipped (2026-06-18) — THE root cause: drive 1:1 DM recall off the RAW Captain message, not the router-assembled prompt text (#974).** The BF-631 DM-recall diagnostic on the live runtime was decisive: it logged the actual recall query as `q='--- Current Visual Context ---\nMost recent observation (14h ...'` for EVERY 1:1 DM. The HXI router prepends the visual-context block (AD-733a), project preamble (AD-793), and targeted-recall block (AD-725) onto `message_text`, and `_recall_relevant_memories` used `params["text"][:200]` as the recall query — so the query was the VISUAL SCENE, not what the Captain asked. Every 1:1 recall searched for the room and returned the agent's own visual reflections; the dogs (and everything the Captain actually said) never surfaced. This is why BF-630/BF-631 (both genuine recall fixes) didn't move the needle, and why the offline repros — which used the clean query — surfaced the dogs but live never did. Fix: thread raw `req.message` as `params["captain_message"]`; new pure `_dm_recall_query(params)` returns `(captain_message or text)[:200].strip()`; `_recall_relevant_memories` uses it for the direct_message query. `text` (what the LLM sees) is unchanged. Offline proof against the live store: contaminated visual query → ANSWER_PRESENT=False (scene reflections); raw query → ANSWER_PRESENT=True (dogs surface). The three fixes compose (BF-632 query → BF-630 keyword axis → BF-631 echo filter). Tests: `test_bf632_dm_recall_query.py` (6); gate (bf632+bf631+bf630+ad567b+ad981c) = 59 passed. Activates on next reboot. Follow-up: audit the ward_room/group recall query for the same prepend contamination (likely #900). PUSHED.

**BF-631 shipped (2026-06-18) — filter query-echo recalls from per-message DM recall + recall observability (#974).** After BF-630, a fresh-boot test STILL failed. An offline reproduction against a COPY of the live store (Yeo's real LIEUTENANT→ENHANCED tier + exact `recall_weights`) proved `recall_weighted` NOW returns the dog answer "My dog, Grim, is a giant snouser" — so recall PLUMBING works — but it ranks 4th of 5 because the top 3 slots are the Captain's OWN prior identical askings of "What do you know about my dogs?" (query-echoes: cosine 0.527 vs the answer's 0.363, zero information). The answer reached the prompt but was buried under 3 copies of the question, so the agent denied. Fix: new pure `_filter_query_echoes(episodes, query)` drops episodes whose normalized text contains the normalized query verbatim (substantive-query gate, never-strip-to-empty), called in `_recall_relevant_memories` before rendering. End-to-end offline against live data: rendered set goes `[echo,echo,echo,ANSWER,baseline]` → `[ANSWER,baseline]` (answer becomes TOP memory). Plus a concise INFO `BF-631 DM-recall` observability line (the investigation flew blind across 3 diagnoses). Tests: `test_bf631_query_echo_filter.py` (7); gate (bf631+bf630+ad567b+ad979c+ad981c) = 69 passed. Activates on next reboot. PUSHED.

**BF-630 shipped (2026-06-18) — keyword_search OR-expands natural-language queries before FTS5 MATCH (#974).** A fresh-boot gold-standard test proved AD-981c was necessary but NOT sufficient: with the runtime confirmed on AD-981c code, Yeo still couldn't recall the Captain's giant schnauzers. Live-log + read-only `episode_fts.db` forensics found the true root cause: the live 1:1 DM per-message recall runs `_recall_relevant_memories` → **`recall_weighted`** (ENHANCED tier), NOT the `recall_for_agent_with_confidence` AD-981c wired. `recall_weighted` has a keyword axis (AD-567b/BF-134) but called `keyword_search(query)` with the RAW Captain message, which FTS5 treats as a *phrase* (and the trailing `?` is a hard `fts5: syntax error` → caught → `[]`). Proven: `MATCH 'What do you know about my dogs?'` → syntax error; OR-expanded `"what" OR … OR "dogs"` → 6 hits incl. *"My dog, Grim, is a giant snouser"* (porter stems `dogs`→`dog`). Fix: centralize the NL→FTS5 translation INSIDE `keyword_search` (apply `fts_or_query` before MATCH, `[]` when no usable tokens) so ALL three NL callers (`recall_weighted`, the AD-603 anchor-merge, the hybrid fusers) are correct by construction; the two fusers drop their now-redundant pre-expansion and pass the raw query (byte-identical net MATCH). OR-of-keywords is a strict SUPERSET of the old phrase/AND behaviour ⇒ every previously-found episode still found. Activates on next reboot, no config change. Tests: `test_bf630_keyword_search_nl.py` (6, BF-287 real store + real MiniLM + real FTS5) incl. the end-to-end dogs case + the sovereign shard guard; gate (bf630 + ad567b/c/d + ad979c + ad981c) = 116 passed. PUSHED.

**AD-981c shipped (2026-06-18) — wire AD-979c hybrid retrieval onto the sovereign recall path, shard-aware (#974).** The crew's live per-message recall runs through the SOVEREIGN `recall_for_agent_with_confidence` (agent-scoped, AD-397), which ignored the already-enabled `hybrid_recall_enabled` flag and stayed dense-only — so a memory an agent owns but encoded under different vocabulary than the query (the proven cross-session "you told Yeo about two giant schnauzers, then after a restart Yeo couldn't recall the dogs" miss) was never surfaced, even though the FTS5 sparse axis catches it by keyword. Fix: gate the sovereign fusion on the SAME `self._hybrid_recall_enabled and self._fts_db is not None` condition the global path uses (DD-1 — **no new config flag**; `config.py` default stays `False` ⇒ byte-identical default installs; the Captain's `config/system.yaml:117 true` activates it on next reboot). New `EpisodicMemory._fuse_dense_sparse_for_agent(agent_id, query, dense_episodes, k)` mirrors `_fuse_dense_sparse` (`fts_or_query`→`keyword_search`→`reciprocal_rank_fusion`→hydrate via `get_by_ids`) but adds the **load-bearing sovereign-leak guard** (DD-2): `keyword_search`/`get_by_ids` are GLOBAL, so every fused episode is post-filtered to ones the agent owns (`agent_id in episode.agent_ids`) **before** the k-cap (filter-then-cap, never cap-then-filter). The gated tail is appended AFTER the AD-981a FoK band/logging + activation blocks, BEFORE `return`, so the band/`best_sim`/log line stay the DENSE distribution (DD-3 — fusion changes WHICH owned episodes return, not the cosine confidence). Honest-degrade: empty/failed sparse axis → dense list unchanged. The global `_fuse_dense_sparse`/`recall_with_confidence` are untouched (AD-979c byte-identical); `recall_for_agent` is still a byte-identical shim. Tests: `test_ad981c_sovereign_hybrid.py` (10, BF-287 real `EpisodicMemory` + real ONNX MiniLM + real FTS5 sidecar — no MagicMock at the store boundary): the gate (owned buried-keyword episode dense-sub-threshold ~0.19<0.25 surfaced ON / absent OFF) + the mandatory sovereign-leak guard (a counselor-owned keyword match NOT in the yeoman's hybrid recall; counselor still recalls its own) + FoK band/best_sim unchanged by fusion (returned conf + logged line) + default-off byte-identical + honest-degrade (raised/empty/no-usable-token sparse → dense). Focused gate `test_ad981c + test_ad979c + test_ad981a` 36 passed; AD-979c/AD-981a suites unchanged. Forward: AD-981b (FoK-band-gated consult on the sovereign path) still earmarked. Working tree staged for Captain review (not committed).

**AD-1025a shipped (2026-06-17) — anchor the voice-listing endpoint to the install root, not CWD (#971).** Fast-follow completing AD-1025's DD-5 audit: `GET /api/avatars/tts/voices` (`routers/avatars.py`) resolved `Path("tools/piper/voices").resolve()` (CWD-relative) and ignored the new `tts.voices_dir`, so the HXI per-agent voice picker showed **no voices** when ProbOS was launched from outside the repo root — the same launch-from-a-sibling-folder incident AD-1025 fixed for synthesis. Fix: new public `resolve_voices_dir(voices_dir) -> Path` in `piper_backend.py` (wraps AD-1025's `_anchor_path`; absolute as-is, relative under the install root, NEVER CWD), shared by the synthesize path and the endpoint as the single source of truth; the endpoint now reads `config.tts.voices_dir` and anchors it. Migrated the 4 BF-291 voice-catalog enumeration tests off the `monkeypatch.chdir` (CWD) contract to absolute/config-driven `voices_dir` + chdir-elsewhere (proving CWD-independence; `_make_runtime_with_tts` gains a `voices_dir` param), and added `test_ad1025a_voice_listing_anchors_relative_dir_to_install_root` (default relative dir + monkeypatched `_probos_root` + CWD in a sibling folder → voice still listed). Remaining DD-5 paths (`code_search` rg, `config.py` data/ defaults, `proactive`, `security`, `session_manager`) → future AD-1025b sweep. PUSHED.

**AD-1026 shipped (2026-06-17) — quiet, phased boot output with a `--verbose` flag (#970).** Presentation-only: the default `probos serve` console boot is now short and issue-focused, with `--verbose`/`-v` restoring today's full detail; **no boot LOGIC changed** — the entire diff is `src/probos/__main__.py` + a new test file. **DD-1:** new pure `_console_log_level(log_level, verbose) -> int` (WARNING when quiet, else the configured level) + a `verbose` param on `_setup_logging`; the **console** `StreamHandler` drops to WARNING+ by default (verbose restores INFO/DEBUG) while the rotating **file** handler stays at INFO+ in both modes (full diagnostics always on disk) and the root logger stays at the configured level. **DD-2:** new pure `_render_boot_summary(status, verbose) -> list[str]` replaces the inline per-pool loop — collapsed default = one `✓ {total} agents across {N} pools` line + the red-team line (NO per-pool lines), `--verbose` = the byte-identical per-pool + red-team + Total lines; a pool below its target size still surfaces its own line even collapsed (issue-on-detail). **DD-3:** `serve --verbose`/`-v` threads `args.verbose` → `_serve` → `_boot_runtime` → `_setup_logging` + the summary call, also into `_boot_and_run` (interactive) with `verbose=False` for the bare-`probos` default shell. **DD-4:** a module-level WARNING+ counting handler (`_boot_warning_count()`, reset each `_setup_logging`) drives a single dim post-`ready` pointer `⚠ {n} notice(s) during boot — full log: <data>/logs/probos.log; rerun with --verbose for detail.` (suppressed when verbose). Tests: new `test_ad1026_boot_verbosity.py` (17, BF-287 real handler objects + a logger-state snapshot/restore fixture): level mapping, console WARNING/INFO + file INFO-regardless, collapsed-vs-verbose summary + degraded-pool surfacing, counter reflect+reset, `serve --verbose`/`-v`/absence. Focused 17 passed; blast `-k "ad1026 or distribution or main or boot or logging"` 314 passed. Default (no flag) ⇒ collapsed boot. Not pushed (Captain reviews).

**AD-1025 shipped (2026-06-17) — anchor operator-asset + runtime-artifact paths to the ProbOS root, not CWD (#969).** Live incident: the Captain launched `probos serve` from a sibling folder, and the CWD-relative Piper binary/voice resolution + the AD-690 rejection-cache DB path both missed their installed assets — TTS silently degraded to the browser and Step-7i relationship inference was skipped, even though `D:\ProbOS\tools\piper\` and `D:\ProbOS\data\` were correctly populated. The fix anchors both to the install root (mirroring `__main__.py`'s bundled-tools `project_root`): new `_probos_root()` (`Path(__file__).resolve().parents[4]`, empirically verified = repo root holding `pyproject.toml` + `tools/piper`) + `_anchor_path()` (absolute as-is, relative under root, NEVER the CWD) in `piper_backend.py`; `_resolve_binary_path`/`_resolve_voice_model` rewritten to anchor (the latter now takes the already-anchored voices base); new `TTSConfig.voices_dir` (default `tools/piper/voices`, threaded `select_backend`→`PiperBackend.__init__`→both voice call sites incl. the BF-291 override) makes the hardcoded voices dir configurable; the rejection-cache wirer now reads the **absolute** `runtime.data_dir` (AD-468) instead of the relative `config.data_dir` fallback (BF-628 class); and the two degrade WARNINGs now log the **resolved** candidate path (what/why/what-next). Default behavior byte-identical when CWD == repo root. DD-5 audit (report-only, NOT fixed): `routers/avatars.py:131` (sibling hardcoded `tools/piper/voices`), `agents/code_search.py:254` (`tools/rg`), `config.py` `data/captains_log`+`data/plan_of_day`+`data/agent_probes.db` defaults, `proactive.py:275` (`data/briefing_state.json`), `routers/security.py:43` (relative `data` + private `_data_dir`), `cognitive/session_manager.py:20` (`data/sessions`). Tests: new `test_ad1025_path_anchoring.py` (13, BF-287 real `tmp_path` + `SimpleNamespace` runtime + real `SQLiteRejectionCache`; the headline regression monkeypatches `_probos_root` + `os.chdir`s away to reproduce the incident); migrated the obsolete CWD-contract resolver/synthesize cases in `test_ad738_piper_tts.py` + `test_ad738e_1_per_emotion_prosody.py` (count preserved, in place). Focused gate 39 passed; blast `-k "ad738 or ad1025 or ad690 or tts or piper or rejection or config"` 705 passed. Forward markers: AD-1025a (status-endpoint `degraded_reason` + `voice.ts`), AD-1026 (boot-log verbosity / startup Piper self-probe). Committed locally (Captain reviews + pushes).

**AD-1019a shipped (2026-06-16) — HXI per-agent + per-tool MCP enablement surface (#961, epic #955).** The operator UI for the AD-1019 substrate (UI only, no backend). New `ui/src/components/mcp/McpAgentAccess.tsx` mounted as an expandable **"Agent access"** section per server row in `McpServersPanel`: per crew agent (`GET /api/crew/roster`) a server-level enable/disable/**reset-to-default** + an expander of **per-tool** toggles, each with its resolved `{enabled, source}` badge (`tool`/`server`/`default`) so precedence is legible. Toggles `POST {enabled, tool?}`; reset `DELETE ?tool=`. Per-agent access loads lazily on expand (avoids ~100 live `list_tools()` round-trips on mount). HXI discipline: deps-injectable, stroke-only SVG amber/dim, **no emoji** (asserted), `data-testid` everywhere, honest-degrade (404 → "MCP management is disabled"). Known follow-up: `/access` returns only `server_enabled` for the server scope, so the server-level control can't *visually* split explicit-disable from default (per-tool source badge is accurate; needs a 1-field backend return). Tests: `McpAgentAccess.test.tsx` (12) + panel +1 = `vitest run src/components/mcp` 33 passed; `npm run build` clean; personnel+bridge 90 passed. PUSHED.

**AD-1019 shipped (2026-06-16) — per-agent + per-tool MCP enablement: authorization + enumeration substrate (#960, epic #955).** Adds *which agents* may use a server and *which tools* on it, **reusing the audited `ToolPermissionStore` (AD-423b/AD-894) — no new grant store** — via composite tool ids: server-level `mcp:{name}` (all tools) + tool-level `mcp:{name}:{tool}` (one tool). New pure `integrations/mcp_bridge/access.py` `resolve_mcp_access(grants, server, tool) -> (enabled, source)` with precedence **tool-level overrides server-level, restriction beats grant** (tool-restriction → tool-grant → server-restriction → server-grant → `(False,"default")`; MCP is **opt-in per agent**). `routers/mcp_servers.py` += 4 endpoints (all gated on `management_enabled`, 404 off, honest-degrade never-500): `GET /{id}/tools` (live `list_tools` enumeration, `{tools:[],error}` on failure), `GET /{id}/agents/{aid}` (per-tool resolved enablement), `POST`/`DELETE` per-(agent,tool|server) grant via `ToolPermissionStore`. Pure resolver exhaustively unit-tested. Tests: `test_ad1019_mcp_enablement.py` (24); MCP regression 370 passed; no errors. Scope: this is the substrate — the **HXI surface is AD-1019a**, **MCP-tool invocation gating into the agent tool-call path is AD-1019b** (named, not built). PUSHED.

**AD-1018 shipped (2026-06-16) — HXI MCP Servers management panel (#959, epic #955).** The operator-facing surface for the AD-1015 CRUD + AD-1017 auth endpoints (UI only, **no backend changes**). New `ui/src/components/mcp/McpServersPanel.tsx` mirrors `ShipsLockerPanel` (zustand `mcpServersOpen` open-flag, mounted **unconditionally** in `App.tsx`, deps-injectable `_fn = deps?.x ?? apiX`, honest-degrade, Escape-close) + `RolePicker` (typed, stroke-only SVG icons, amber `#f0b060`/dim, **no emoji**, `data-testid` everywhere): a server **list** (name, http/stdio badge, url/command summary, enable/disable toggle, `auth_kind` badge, Test → `OK · N tools`/`FAILED` **text**); a **create/edit form** (http url+headers / stdio command+args+env+cwd, client validation mirroring `validate_record` — kebab name, http⇒url, stdio⇒command, **no secret inputs**); a **credential modal** (static **write-only** token → `putCredential`; oauth non-secret config + **write-only** client_secret → `startOAuth` → `window.open` + `oauth_complete` postMessage listener → refresh; `Refresh token` → `refreshOAuth`; `Remove credential` → `deleteCredential`); delete-with-confirm; stdio trust note "Runs a local command on this machine". **Secrets discipline (HARD): no token/secret value is ever rendered back** — write-only inputs cleared after save, only the `auth_kind` badge + non-secret config shown thereafter (a test asserts the token is absent from the DOM after save). Honest-degrade: `GET` 404 → "MCP management is disabled"; fetch failure → error state. Launched from the Bridge → Engineering station (`MCP Servers`). 10 injectable deps each default to a real `fetch` (Vitest injects `vi.fn()`, no real network). Tests: `McpServersPanel.test.tsx` **20 green** + `paletteCommands.test.ts` (12→13 launches); `npm run build` clean; bridge+personnel regression **90 passed**. Scope: no backend, no per-agent grants (AD-1019), no device-code UI.

**AD-1017 shipped (2026-06-16) — MCP server authentication: static tokens + OAuth authorization-code (#958, epic #955).** AD-1015's inert `auth_kind`/`credential_ref` are now **live**: a server's stored credential is resolved and injected at register time, with **secrets ONLY in the credential vault by ref — never in the store row, never in any API response, never logged.** New `integrations/mcp_bridge/mcp_oauth.py` `McpOAuthProvider` (modeled on the AD-720c `CloudPickerProvider`, reuses `OAuthTokenBundle` by **import only** — cloud-picker code untouched), parameterized by the record's non-secret `oauth_json` + the vault-stored `client_secret`: `start_authorization`/`handle_callback`(`grant_type=authorization_code`)/`refresh`(`grant_type=refresh_token`) over the resident `httpx`; token values never logged. `store.py` `McpServerRecord` += four **non-secret** fields (`auth_header_name`/`auth_scheme`/`auth_env_var`/`oauth_json`) with a migration-safe `_migrate_ad1017()` (`ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN` defaults, `OperationalError` swallowed) — columns appended **after** `created_at`/`updated_at` so a fresh DB and an `ALTER`-migrated AD-1015 DB share identical positional order; `validate_record`'s secret denylist unchanged. `routers/mcp_servers.py` adds `_resolve_auth_headers`/`_resolve_auth_env` merged into a runtime-aware `_register` (`auth_kind=="none"` → `{}` ⇒ **byte-identical to AD-1015**) + endpoints (all gated on `management_enabled`, 404 off): `POST/DELETE /{id}/credential` (static token in vault `mcp:{id}`, 503 no-vault), `POST /{id}/auth/start` (vault `client_secret` under `mcp:{id}:oauth_secret`, CSRF state via lazy `_get_state_store` mirroring cloud_pickers, return `{auth_url,state}`), `GET /{id}/auth/callback` (consume state→403, exchange, store bundle `mcp:{id}:oauth`, **same popup-close HTML**), `POST /{id}/auth/refresh` (reactive re-exchange, 400 no-refresh-token). Scope: device-code AD-1017a, HXI AD-1018, per-agent grants AD-1019 (named, not built). Tests: `test_ad1017_mcp_auth.py` (38, BF-287 real store + real `EncryptedFileCredentialVault` + real `MCPBridge` + real `TestClient`; only httpx token exchange `MockTransport`-mocked) — token never in row/response/logs (asserted), OAuth start→callback→refresh, invalid-state 403, gate-off 404, provider unit tests, store migration + round-trip; parity `-k "mcp or ad449 or ad597 or ad1015 or credential or cloud_picker"` **384 passed** (346 **unchanged** + 38). `auth_kind="none"` ⇒ byte-identical.

**AD-1016 shipped (2026-06-16) — credential backend abstraction: OS keychain backend (#957, epic #955).** Secrets can now optionally live in the OS keychain (Windows DPAPI / macOS Keychain / Linux libsecret) instead of the AD-706f encrypted file vault, operator-selectable via `credential_vault.backend`. New `security/keyring_backend.py` `KeyringCredentialBackend` implements the `CredentialVault` Protocol **exactly** (5 async kw-only methods, `inspect.signature`-equal to `EncryptedFileCredentialVault` — structurally substitutable); values live only in the OS keychain via the AD-754 `CredentialEncryptor`, with a **non-secret metadata sidecar** (`{ref: {scope, created_at, last_read_at, read_count}}` — values NEVER stored) using the file-vault's atomic tmp+rename + `RLock`. Sync `keyring` calls wrapped in `asyncio.to_thread`; `store` writes keyring-first + raises a clear error on failure (no orphan metadata); `read`/`delete`/`list_refs` honest-degrade (None/[]) on any keyring error, never crash the runtime. Crew-token gating resolved: keychain backend needs `enabled=True` but **not** `crew_scope_token` (OS encrypts at rest; sidecar holds no secrets). `config.py` `CredentialVaultConfig`: `backend` `"file"(default)|"keychain"` + `@field_validator` rejecting other values + `keyring_index_path`/`keyring_service_name`. `finalize.py` switches on `backend`: file path **unchanged** (needs token); keychain built when `enabled` (token optional); both honest-degrade to None. Scope: no MCP auth/OAuth (AD-1017), no HXI (AD-1018), no per-agent grants (AD-1019); Protocol surface, file vault, `credential_encryption.py`, `cloud_pickers`, `actions` untouched. **No secret value can reach the sidecar.** Tests: `test_ad1016_keyring_backend.py` (22, BF-287 real dict-backed fake keyring, NOT MagicMock); gate `-k "credential or vault or 706f or 754 or config"` 645 passed (623 **unchanged** + 22). Default `backend="file"` ⇒ byte-identical.

**AD-1015 shipped (2026-06-16) — MCP server registration store + CRUD management API (#956, epic #955).** The foundation for the MCP management UX: MCP servers move from static-config + read-only to a persisted, runtime-mutable registry. New `integrations/mcp_bridge/store.py` `McpServerStore` (mirrors `IntentGrantStore` — `ConnectionFactory` + WAL + in-memory cache + `db_path=""` cache-only) with a frozen `McpServerRecord`/`to_public_dict` (single serialization seam) + pure `validate_record` (http⇒url, stdio⇒command∈allowlist, kebab name, **structural secret-key denylist** → no credential value ever persisted/returned; points at `auth_kind`+`credential_ref` for AD-1016). New `routers/mcp_servers.py` (`/api/mcp/servers`, gated on new `MCPConfig.management_enabled=False`, 404 when off): list/create/get/update/delete/enable/disable/test-connection. Bridge key rule `url(http)/name(stdio)`; http register sync, stdio register/unregister async; only `bridge.py` change is a new `unregister_server`. `finalize` seeds enabled rows **after** config servers (config wins — bridge dedups on key); `shutdown` mirrors `intent_grant_store`. test-connection uses a transient client closed in `finally` (no leaked subprocess), honest-degrade `{ok:false}` at 200. `credential_ref`/`auth_kind` inert (AD-1016). Scope: no HXI (AD-1018), no credentials (AD-1016), no OAuth (AD-1017), no per-agent grants (AD-1019); pack-`mcpServers`-wiring forward-refs re-pointed AD-1015→**AD-1020**. Tests: `test_ad1015_mcp_server_store.py` + `test_ad1015_mcp_servers_api.py` (56, BF-287 real store + real `MCPBridge` + real `TestClient` + AD-1014 echo fixture); parity `-k "mcp or ad449 or ad597"` 286 passed (230 **unchanged** + 56); config 574 passed. Default-OFF ⇒ byte-identical.

**AD-1014 shipped (2026-06-15) — stdio/subprocess MCP transport, marketplace launch parity (#954).** ProbOS can now launch local MCP servers as subprocesses (`uvx`/`npx`/`command`, NDJSON over stdio) the way Copilot/Claude Code/Hermes do — unlocking the MCP marketplace. Architect→Builder workflow, researched against the MCP spec + Copilot + Claude Code + Hermes. New `transport.py` (`Transport` Protocol, `HttpTransport` byte-identical lift, `StdioTransport` subprocess+NDJSON); `MCPClient(transport=None)` defaults to HTTP so AD-449/AD-597 tests stay unchanged; `MCPServerConfig.type` discriminator (http|stdio) + `MCPConfig.stdio_enabled=False` (default-OFF) + `command_allowlist`. `MCPBridge.register_stdio_server` guards **allowlist → consent (HookBus PreToolUse) → spawn**, all before any subprocess. Mechanism-only: no pack wiring (AD-1020), no OS sandbox (#936 Tier-2). Tests: `test_ad1014_stdio_mcp_transport.py` (24, BF-287 real subprocess fixture); HTTP parity 230 passed **unchanged**; config 573 passed. Default-OFF ⇒ byte-identical. PUSHED.

**AD-1013 shipped (2026-06-15) — skills-only Capability-Pack loader (#948).** First write-side loader slice + lowest-risk tier: `packs/loader.py` `load_pack_skills` loads a pack's declared **folder-skills** (markdown `SKILL.md` instructions — no code execution) via the existing AD-596d `CognitiveSkillCatalog.import_skill` path (validate + dedup + register), tagging `origin="pack:<name>"`. Returns `PackLoadResult` (loaded/skipped). Honest-degrade: duplicate/invalid → skipped-with-reason; standalone `.md` skipped; agent `.py` never touched (that's the AD-1014 self-mod-chain tier). Consent + `packs.enabled` gating live in the caller; loader is pure mechanism (mechanism-first). Tests: `test_ad1013_pack_skills_loader.py` (8, BF-287 real catalog + real packs); pack + skill regression 191 passed. PUSHED.

**AD-1012 shipped (2026-06-15) — lifecycle-hook bus PreDispatch wiring (#949, #944).** Wires the AD-1004 `HookBus` substrate as the canonical `PreDispatch` capability gate, **default-OFF** (`config.hooks.enabled`). New `hooks/handlers.py` `make_capability_gate_handler` turns the inline AD-1007 capability check into a pluggable hook (DENY only on explicit `restricted`, agent-precedence); the bus is instantiated at startup only when enabled, exposed as `runtime.hook_bus`, and `reply_pipeline.step_4h` routes through it when wired (byte-identical inline fallback otherwise). This is the shared lifecycle point where Capability-Pack hooks (#948) + a future consensus handler will attach with most-restrictive-wins aggregation. Tests: `test_ad1012_hook_bus_wiring.py` (10, BF-287 real bus + real store); AD-1004 15 + AD-1012 10 = 25 green; touched-path regression **714 passed**. #949 closed. PUSHED.

**AD-1003f shipped (2026-06-14) — Ship's Locker pack component preview, expandable (#944, #948).** UI capstone of the Capability-Pack read-side: each valid pack in the Locker's INSTALLED PACKS section is now clickable (+/−) to expand and show its declared `skills:`/`agents:` (fetched on demand from `GET /api/packs/{name}`, AD-1003e; `fetchPackDetail` deps-injectable). Stale-response guard + honest-degrade (Loading… / No declared components). Read-only — lists components, never loads/executes. Tests: ShipsLockerPanel +3 (16 total). UI build clean. **The Capability-Pack read-side is now complete end-to-end** (parse → scan → inventory API → Locker list → preview API → Locker expand); only the trust-gated loader remains on #948. PUSHED.

**AD-1003e shipped (2026-06-14) — pack content preview (#944, #948).** Fifth Capability-Pack slice: read-only enumeration of what a pack DECLARES (its skill/agent component files), so a pack can be inspected before the deferred loader (held, trust-gated). `packs/scanner.py` `preview_pack` loads the manifest + lists the actual files under its declared `skill_paths`/`agent_paths` (folder-skills via `SKILL.md`, standalone `.md` skills, agent `.md`/`.json`/`.py`); `PackContents`/`PackComponent`; `describe_pack_contents` serializable form; `GET /api/packs/{name}` (resolves by manifest name; 404 disabled/not-found/invalid). READ-ONLY — files LISTED never opened/parsed/imported/executed; `..`-traversal blocked; POSIX-normalized paths. Tests: ad1003e (10 BF-287 real packs) + ad1003c +3 (detail endpoint). Pack suite 48 passed. Read-side now complete through component preview; only the trust-gated loader remains on #948. Also closed #788 (voice-lib absorb — won't-migrate, rationale: STT already on the maintained `@huggingface/transformers`; VAD a thin wrapper over the same Silero model for the privacy/per-frame-tap/offline requirements a turnkey lib can't meet). PUSHED.

**AD-1003d shipped (2026-06-14) — Ship's Locker "Installed Packs" section (#944, #948).** Fourth Capability-Pack slice: surfaces the AD-1003c `GET /api/packs` inventory in the HXI. `ShipsLockerPanel` gains an INSTALLED PACKS section (+ `fetchPacks` helper) fetched independently of the catalog — a packs failure honest-degrades to the disabled note, never blocks the catalog. Renders the disabled note (default), empty note, or one row per pack (name + version + hooks/mcp flags + description; bad manifest → red "invalid manifest"). Read-only — viewing only, no install action. Tests: ShipsLockerPanel +3; UI 13, build clean. **The Capability-Pack read-side is now complete** (parse AD-1003a → scan AD-1003b → API AD-1003c → HXI AD-1003d); the loader behind the operator trust gate is the remaining write-side slice. PUSHED.

**AD-1003c shipped (2026-06-14) — GET /api/packs read-only installed-pack inventory endpoint (#944, #948).** Third Capability-Pack slice: surfaces the AD-1003b scanner over HTTP (the cross-tool "installed plugins" list). `PacksConfig` (default OFF, `packs_dir=data/packs`); `GET /api/packs` returns the disabled-empty shape by default, else `describe_scan` of the configured dir (resolved against the runtime data dir, BF-628 lesson) + `enabled`/`packs_dir`. Read-only — nothing installed/loaded/executed even when enabled (loader is a later slice behind the operator trust gate). Tests: ad1003c (5, BF-287 TestClient + tmp_path packs); broad config/execution/packs regression 737 passed. PUSHED.

**AD-1011 shipped (2026-06-14) — ship-wide skill coverage view (#815 CLOSED).** #815's view 2 (per-agent skill assignment) already shipped (Service tab AD-983b/c + SkillLibrary AD-898); this adds view 1 (the civilization skill-registry lens). Backend `GET /api/skills/coverage` inverts every crew agent's skill profile into a `skill→holders` map joined against the registry so unheld skills surface as gaps (`holder_count`/`holders[callsign,proficiency]`/`gap` per skill + `crew_count`/`gap_count`); profiles gathered concurrently, honest-degrade per agent. UI `SkillCoverage.tsx` — coverage bar + red GAP flag + expandable holders, mounted above SkillLibrary in the Ship's Office skills pane. Tests: ad1011 (5 BF-287 real skill registry+service) + SkillCoverage (5) + console unchanged (8). Backend 5, UI 13, build clean. PUSHED.

**AD-1003b shipped (2026-06-14) — Capability-Pack scanner: read-only installed-pack inventory (#944, #948).** Second safe slice of Capability Packs (after AD-1003a's manifest parser). `packs/scanner.py` `scan_packs(packs_dir)` walks subdirectories, treats any with a `find_manifest` hit as a pack, loads+describes each into a `PackEntry` (path + summary | error); a bad manifest becomes an error entry (honest-degrade) so one bad pack never hides the rest. `describe_scan` returns the serializable inventory (`packs[]` + `counts{total,valid,error}`) — the shape a future `GET /api/packs` / UI "installed plugins" list consumes. READ-ONLY — nothing installed/loaded/executed/wired (loader is a later slice). Tests: ad1003b (9, BF-287 real tmp_path packs) + ad1003a 21 regression. Smoke clean. #948 stays open. PUSHED.

**AD-1010 shipped (2026-06-14) — Personnel role-picker UI (Agent Customizations epic #944, #952).** Surfaces the AD-1009 role-template backend in the Ship's Office. New `RolePicker.tsx` as a 4th `'roles'` tab in `CrewPersonnelConsole`: lists every role (`GET /api/crew/roles`) with post title + department + `N skills · N tools · N caps`; expand to see skills/resolved-tools/served-capabilities; a crew-member selector + per-role **Apply** POSTs `apply-role` (additive + reversible; AD-889 guard preserves per-agent overrides). Completes the Captain's "assign a role as a template, then override per agent" loop visually. UI-only — no new endpoint; role-defaults *authoring* (a role disabling a capability by default) remains the deferred layer. Tests: RolePicker (7, deps-injected) + console unchanged (8). UI 15, build clean. PUSHED.

**AD-909a shipped (2026-06-14) — UI Restrict verb in the Tool Certifications console (#951 CLOSED, follow-on to AD-909).** AD-909 made mesh read-intents restrictable per-agent via the API; this adds the Ship's Office UI verb. `crew_grant_tool` gains optional `is_restriction` (default False — existing callers byte-identical); `ToolCertifications` gains a **Grant / Restrict mode toggle** — Restrict issues the per-agent off-switch (`is_restriction=true`, `permission=none`, caps a READ-for-all tool at NONE for that agent, overriding the role/ship default). Mirrors the AD-1008 toggle; no new consensus gate. Tests: ad894 +2 (real store + resolve_permission: agent capped NONE, others keep READ) + ToolCertifications +2 (restrict/grant POST shapes). Backend 14, UI 8, build clean. PUSHED.

**BF-629 fixed (2026-06-14) — conversational web_search/read_page now reasons over results instead of pasting raw links (#953, Copilot-parity).** Captain live test: asked Ezri the same question as GitHub Copilot — Copilot returned a synthesised briefing, Ezri returned a raw list of links and only summarised after a second prompt. Root cause: the AD-869 Tier-2 `[MESH]` do-and-report path delegates the search to a separate `WebSearchAgent` and pastes its result **verbatim** (`_render_mesh_read_result`); the originating agent never reasons over it. Copilot (and ProbOS's own `AgenticLoop`) keep the tool result in the same model's context and continue — search→reason→answer. Fix: a **synthesis pass** in `reply_pipeline.step_4h_mesh_read_parse` — after a `requires_reflect` read (`web_search`/`read_page`, `_MESH_READ_SYNTHESIZE_INTENTS`), one LLM pass via `runtime.llm_client` has the **originating** agent reason over the result in its own voice (Captain's question + rendered results as context), mirroring the AD-934 deliberate re-roll. Verbatim reads (`read_file`/`list_directory`) skip it — the raw output IS the answer. `DmMeshSynthesisConfig` default OFF in the model / **ON in system.yaml** (AD-935 pattern). Tier-2 honest-degrade: a missing client / empty / raised response keeps the verbatim render — so a degraded LLM (incl. the BF-289/612 empty-content proxy surface the Captain also hit) never drops the Captain's results, it falls back to the raw list. Tests: bf629 (9, BF-287 real pipeline+gate, fake bus/LLM at the edges): synthesise web_search/read_page, prompt carries question+results, disabled→verbatim, read_file not synthesised, empty/no-client/raise→verbatim, failed-read→no-synthesis. Regression: mesh/pipeline/capability 48+44 passed; config defaults OFF + system.yaml loads ON. PUSHED.

**AD-1009 shipped (2026-06-14) — role templates: view a role's loadout + apply it as a starting template (Agent Customizations epic #944, #952).** Captain's role-framework directive: assign a role as a template, then override per agent. The role→skills→tools engine already exists at birth (`ACM.commission`, AD-889); this adds **visibility** + an **explicit apply action**. `GET /api/crew/roles` enumerates every role (post title/department + required skills + resolved tools via AD-888 + served mesh capabilities) — read-only, derived, no new role config. `POST /api/crew/{id}/apply-role` (`ApplyRole{role_id?}`) applies a role's skill+tool template via the commission engine (own role if omitted; additive + reversible → no consensus gate). **AD-889 guard fix:** the tool-grant idempotency check now skips on ANY active per-agent decision (grant OR manual restriction), so re-applying a role never clobbers a Captain override — a manual disable survives re-apply (agent-precedence). Deferred: role-level capability/tool DISABLE authoring + Personnel role-picker UI → AD-1010. Tests: ad1009 (10 BF-287 real handlers/ontology/registry) + ad889 +1 (real stores, restriction survives re-commission); regression 85; smoke clean. PUSHED.

**AD-1007 + AD-1008 shipped (2026-06-14) — per-agent capability gate: disable blocks the agent, agent-precedence over role (Agent Customizations epic #944, #952).** Captain steer: disabling a capability must BLOCK the agent, and an explicit per-agent decision WINS over the role default in both directions (agent-disable beats role-enable; agent-grant beats role-disable) — **agent-precedence**, not most-restrictive. **AD-1007 (backend):** new `IntentGrantStore.resolve_sync` three-state resolver (granted/restricted/no_opinion, most-recent-wins, tie→fail-safe-restricted); `IntentGrantStore` now instantiated at startup (was class-only) + on `runtime.intent_grant_store` + stopped in shutdown; `SetCapability.kind` widens to include `capability`; `set_agent_capability` capability branch (validate reachable → 404, revoke-opposite, issue grant/restriction); enforcement at TWO origination points — `reply_pipeline.step_4h` (conversational `[MESH]` blocked when restricted) + `WorkItemAgenticExecutor` (restricted mesh tools filtered from the loop); `get_agent_capabilities` surfaces `granted`/`source`. **AD-1008 (UI):** CAPABILITIES — CAN REQUEST rows toggle On/Off (optimistic + revert) via `kind=capability`; SERVES rows read-only (role-governed). Role-default layer + SERVES toggling deferred to AD-1009 (#952). Tests: ad1007 (15 BF-287) + CapabilityPanel +3; regression 49; backend smoke + UI build clean. PUSHED.

**AD-1006 shipped (2026-06-14) — capability clarity: "serves" vs ship-wide "can request" (Agent Customizations epic #944).** Captain review found every crew card showed an identical 83 capabilities + 0 tools, and the Counselor couldn't tell tools from capabilities. Root cause: the capability axis is the mesh-intent surface (`_mesh_intents` unions ALL agents' `intent_descriptors`), so it's ship-wide-identical, not the agent's role. Fix (read-only, Phase 1): `get_agent_capabilities` tags each mesh intent `served` (true when the viewed agent declares it); `CapabilityPanel` splits CAPABILITIES into **SERVES** (only this agent fulfils) vs **CAN REQUEST** (ship-wide); Service-tab intro + empty-tools copy now teach Tools (callable functions in context, Copilot sense) vs Skills (role specialties) vs Capabilities (mesh requests). Presentation only — no gate/dispatch change. Held follow-ons: AD-1007 role-template framework (capabilities in `RoleTemplate` + apply-role; role→skills→tools already exists at commission via AD-889), AD-1008 per-agent capability toggle + wire AD-1005 IntentGrantStore into dispatch (needs Captain steer on enforcement + precedence). Tests: ad1006 (4 BF-287 real route handler) + CapabilityPanel +2 + ProfileServiceTab copy; backend 17, UI 24, build clean. PUSHED.

**AD-909 shipped (2026-06-14) — universal mesh read-intents in the persistent tool catalog (#872, backlog).** `web_search`/`read_page`/`http_fetch` were wrapped as first-class Tools by `register_mesh_intent_tools` (AD-856) but only lazily inside the per-dispatch executor — so they were absent from `GET /api/tools` (and the AD-885 lens / AD-899 console) until first use, and the Captain couldn't turn one off per-agent. New `_wire_mesh_intent_tools` in `startup/finalize.py` (alongside the AD-706 browser-tool wiring) seeds them into the PERSISTENT `tool_registry` at startup, `provider="mesh"`, READ-for-all (empty `default_permissions`). `register_mesh_intent_tools` gained an idempotent `provider` param (dispatch keeps `"AD-856"`; startup passes `"mesh"` and wins since it runs first). The off-switch already works: a Captain `is_restriction` (permission `none`) restricts the READ-for-all base down to NONE in `resolve_permission` — no model change, no new consensus gate (Minimal Authority). UI "Restrict" verb deferred to AD-910. Tests: ad909 (11, BF-287 real registry+permission-store+intent-bus); regression 577 passed. Smoke clean. #872 CLOSED. PUSHED.

**AD-1003a shipped (2026-06-14) — Capability-Pack manifest parser/validator (Agent Customizations epic #944, #948).** First safe slice of Capability Packs: ProbOS adopts the cross-tool agent-plugin `plugin.json` format (VS Code / Copilot CLI / Claude Code) so it can consume IDE plugins + emit portable packs. `packs/manifest.py` `PackManifest` (Pydantic v2: name/description/version/author + skills/agents/hooks/mcpServers), same kebab-case `name` rule the IDEs use (raises loudly vs their silent fail), `find_manifest` (VS Code auto-detection order), `load_manifest`/`parse_manifest`/`describe_pack`. `extra="allow"` preserves ProbOS-native additive keys (mesh-intent grants, standing-order overlays) so a ProbOS pack stays a valid base plugin elsewhere. **Parse + validate ONLY** — nothing installed/executed/wired (the install slice needs hook-bus wiring first). Tests: ad1003a (21, BF-287 real files). Smoke clean. #948 stays open. PUSHED.

**AD-1005 shipped (2026-06-14) — IntentGrantStore, per-agent mesh-intent grant substrate (Agent Customizations epic #944).** The authorization substrate for the settled write-intent gating design: a crew agent that originates a consensus-gated WRITE intent (`run_python`) must be granted it — per-agent authorization at origination, layered with (not replacing) consensus. `cognitive/intent_grants.py` `IntentGrantStore` mirrors `SkillGrantStore` (SQLite+WAL+sync cache+soft-revoke), keyed by intent_name, + `is_granted_sync` (restriction-wins) — the read a future PreDispatch hook consumes. **Mechanism only, default OFF, NOT wired** (mirrors the AD-1004 hook-bus discipline; empty store gates nothing). Flagged: 3rd ToolPermissionStore mirror → unified CapabilityGrantStore is a later refactor. Tests: ad1005 (8, BF-287 real store: cache + real-DB persistence). Smoke clean. PUSHED.

**AD-1002 shipped (2026-06-14) — Instructions + Model read tabs in the Service hub (Agent Customizations epic #944, #947).** The last two VS Code axes for the per-agent hub, read-only. Backend `GET /api/agent/{id}/instructions`: the agent's identity instructions (present/char_count/preview, no full-prompt dump), the four composing standing-order tiers (federation/ship/department/agent via `get_order_tiers` AD-893, char counts only), and the model (resolved `_resolve_tier()` + configured `CognitiveConfig` tiers + honest note that selection is global, not per-agent). UI: `InstructionsSection` in `ProfileServiceTab` — read-only tier breakdown + model tier. Honest about the architecture (no per-agent model-selection claim). Also satisfies the older AD-893 read-surface intent (#857) without auto-closing it. Tests: ad1002 (5, BF-287 real CognitiveConfig) + ProfileServiceTab (+4); backend regression 97 passed; 200 profile UI + build clean. #947 ready to close. PUSHED.

**AD-1001b shipped (2026-06-14) — Ship's Locker UI overlay (Agent Customizations epic #944, #946).** The UI half of the global capabilities catalog: `ShipsLockerPanel.tsx`, a read-only full-screen overlay (WardRoom/Settings pattern: `position:fixed`, `shipsLockerOpen` store flag, Escape-to-close) bound to `/api/tools/catalog` (AD-1001a). Four sections — Tools / Skills / Capabilities (mesh) / MCP — with origin (built-in/MCP/extension), `held_by` ("who holds what"), and a consensus badge on write intents. Opened from **Bridge → Engineering station** (ship-wide = Ship's-Computer concern). **Caught + fixed a latent palette-access gap:** adding an Engineering action dropped the "System" launch from the AD-946 command palette (the flatten surfaces actions OR the expand-fallback, never both); followed the Communications precedent (explicit mirror action) — gave Engineering a "System" mirror action alongside "Ship's Locker", zero UX loss. Read-only, additive. Tests: ShipsLockerPanel (10) + updated paletteCommands (12 launches); full UI **1507 passed / 1 skipped**; build clean. #946 ready to close. PUSHED.
