Fix (frontend only): a tiny in-Canvas `FaceFraming` helper (`useThree` → `camera.lookAt(0,1.42,0)`) aims the gallery camera at face height. **AD-948 — group-chat hygiene (#884):** agent replies leaked the AD-722a `<intent emotion=…>` self-tag into the visible group transcript — the tag is stripped on the 1:1 path (`apply_divergence_check`) but the group fan-out (`thread_fanout._send_one`) never stripped it. Fix (backend only): `_send_one` calls the single-source `strip_intent_self_tag` unconditionally before persist/return (the tag must never reach the Captain). +2 pytest (`test_ad948_group_intent_tag_strip.py`, BF-287); full UI suite **1373 passed / 1 skipped** (AD-947, zero regressions), `npm run build` clean; AD-948 blast-radius `-k "thread or chat or fanout or divergence"` **607 passed**. Epic remaining (architect→builder): AD-950 conversational proactivity, AD-951 agent next-speaker selection, AD-952 human response dynamics, AD-953 conversational memory, AD-954 first-class group/call surface. COMMITTED LOCAL ONLY — NOT pushed.

**BF-614 fixed (2026-06-09, LOCAL ONLY — the Captain decides the push) — Group chat: crew replied all-at-once and never to each other; an added member showed a "?" avatar.** Three Captain-reported group-chat problems (Ezri+Yeo room). **(a)+(b) are one linked bug:** the AD-915 facilitator's `max_speakers_per_turn` defaults to `0` (OFF = AD-914 all-reply) and `system.yaml` never set it — so round 0 fanned to BOTH agents (the simultaneous-reply wall), AND because the AD-935 cascade excludes a round's own speakers, a 2-agent room where both spoke in round 0 had nobody left to react, so agents never engaged each other. **Fix:** `group_chat.max_speakers_per_turn: 1` in `system.yaml` (Pydantic default stays 0 for zero-config) — round 0 now picks the single most-relevant agent (@-mention hard-include preserved), and the OTHER agent is free to react next round, so the cascade runs (Ezri → Yeo → Ezri turn-taking, bounded by `max_agent_rounds` + convergence). **(c) the "?" avatar:** the fan-out returns a blank `callsign` for an added member and `ChatMessageRow` used `msg.callsign ?? hostCallsign` (`??` misses `''`); now it resolves from the agents map by `authorId` (blank→map→honest '?' if truly unknown; legacy/1:1→host). **Triaged, not code bugs:** voice (the AD-945 voice-output toggle in the Engineering station was likely off), "no separate meeting window" (current AD-937/938 in-place design), "proxy no response" (BF-612/289 uptime surface, restart clears). +4 Vitest (`ChatMessageRow.test.tsx`); full UI suite **1373 passed / 1 skipped**, `npm run build` clean; focused pytest **940 passed** (yaml loads clean). COMMITTED LOCAL ONLY — NOT pushed.

**BF-613 fixed (2026-06-09, LOCAL ONLY — the Captain decides the push) — Meeting-view crew avatar + Captain camera control.** In a meeting (`MeetingView`), each crew member showed only their `AgentAvatarBadge` ("E") instead of their VRM avatar — though the SAME agent's VRM rendered fine in the separate `CrewAvatarPopout`. Root cause: `AvatarSlot` read `appearance` (which carries `vrm_url`) off a cast of the store's **base `Agent`**, which never carries it — `appearance` is a per-agent `AgentProfileData` field hydrated from `GET /api/agent/{id}/profile`. So `vrm_url` was always `undefined` and every slot fell to the badge. (Classic BF-287 mocked-contract trap: `MeetingView.test.tsx` seeded `appearance` straight onto the mock Agent, so the test was green on a contract production never met.) **Fix (frontend only, `MeetingView.tsx`):** (1) `AvatarSlot` now fetches `GET /api/agent/{id}/profile` and reads `vrm_url`/`expression_overrides`/`dsl` from the fetched `appearance` — the exact path the popout uses — honest-degrading to the badge on any failure; (2) the Captain's slot gains an in-meeting **camera toggle** (`captain-camera-toggle`, stroke-SVG, amber when on, no emoji) driving the global `startCameraStream`/`stopCameraStream`, so the Captain can share their camera with the meeting. Tests rewritten to the production contract (fetch-stub-driven VRM, async `findBy`) + 3 new toggle cases; full UI suite **1369 passed / 1 skipped**, `npm run build` clean. **Live-verified**: seeding a meeting fired the `/profile` fetch (which never fired before), `CrewVRM` mounted + attempted the load, and the camera toggle rendered. COMMITTED LOCAL ONLY — NOT pushed.

**AD-946b shipped (2026-06-09, LOCAL ONLY — the Captain decides the push) — Command-palette polish: a bare `>` now lists every station launch.** AD-946 opened the palette on a leading `>`, but `matchPaletteCommands('', …)` returned `[]`, so a bare `>` (before the Captain types a search term) showed "No matching command." instead of the full list — a discoverability gap. Now an empty/whitespace query returns ALL commands (`commands.slice()` — a fresh array), so `>` alone lists every launch (Ward Room, Chats, Crew, Personnel, Metrics, Notebooks, Records, Explorer, Work Board, System, Settings) and typing narrows it; "No matching command." shows only for a genuinely non-matching query (`>xyzzy`). One-line change in the pure `matchPaletteCommands` helper; both call sites are already gated on the leading `>`, so the NL `/api/chat` path, the `>` trigger, the registry-as-source-of-truth, and the `CommandPalette` empty-state component are all unchanged. The AD-946 "empty→`[]`" test case is rewritten to "empty→all commands" + a fresh-array (`not.toBe`) guard; full UI suite **1366 passed / 1 skipped** (unchanged), `npm run build` clean. **COMMITTED LOCAL ONLY — NOT pushed.**

**AD-946 shipped (2026-06-09, LOCAL ONLY — the Captain decides the push) — The `Ask ProbOS…` omnibox becomes the Ship's-Computer command palette — the FINAL wave of the "Bridge as Ship's Computer" epic (#873, this issue #881), which this AD CLOSES.** The keyboard can now drive Bridge station launches directly: the Captain types a leading `>` to enter command mode, the matching launches surface in a dropdown above the input, and Enter runs the highlighted one — the SAME store action the Bridge fires. **Hard guarantee:** a plain natural-language question (anything NOT starting with `>`) submits **byte-for-byte exactly as today** via `/api/chat`; the palette is an additive affordance, never a replacement for the NL path, and a `>` string is never POSTed to chat. **Trigger = a leading `>`** (VS-Code command mode) — chosen over an always-on dropdown / fuzzy-rank (a substring match under ordinary questions like "what is the **engineer**'s trust" risks an accidental Enter-run); it reuses the existing `/grant` leading-sigil interception and the Spotlight `triggerInput`/`pendingChar` seam (typing `>` while collapsed opens the shell with `>` seeded), and the placeholder stays byte-identical `"Ask ProbOS..."` (the AD-719/941 selectors depend on it). **ONE source of truth:** a new pure `ui/src/components/bridge/paletteCommands.ts` derives the command list from the SAME `buildBridgeStations(ctx)` registry the Bridge renders — `buildPaletteCommands` flattens it (a station contributes its discrete `actions` when it has any, else its primary `onExpand` launch; `config` panels EXCLUDED → forward marker AD-946a) into the 11 launches **Ward Room, Chats, Crew, Personnel, Metrics, Notebooks, Records, Explorer, Work Board, System, Settings** (Communications' onExpand is skipped because it has actions → no duplicate Ward Room), and `matchPaletteCommands` is a case-insensitive token-AND substring over `${station} ${label}` (empty query → `[]`). ONE additive field `onExpandLabel?: string` on `CommandStation` (`operations → 'Work Board'`, `engineering → 'System'`) lets the body-only launches read as the Captain names them while the registry stays the single source of their labels (rejected an alias map — a second hand-maintained list the epic forbids); `BridgePanel` never reads the field → AD-943/944/945 render is byte-unchanged. A new presentational `ui/src/components/CommandPalette.tsx` (props-only, nav state on the IntentSurface host like the @-picker) renders `role="listbox"`/`option`/`aria-selected`, a stroke-SVG `ChevronRight` glyph, amber active `#f0b060`, **NO emoji** (HXI #3), and a "No matching command." empty state. **IntentSurface wiring:** a `useMemo`'d `paletteCommands` from 3 read-only selectors; local `paletteOpen`/`paletteIndex` (no new store state/flag/localStorage key); `handleInputChange` opens command mode on a leading `>` (exclusive with the @-picker); `handleKeyDown` adds a palette nav branch (Arrow cycles / Enter/Tab runs, returns BEFORE the @-picker branch) + an Esc-closes-palette-first arm; `handleSubmit` gets a top guard so a `>` string is never POSTed; the dropdown is portal-anchored above the input like the @-picker, the click-outside + 150ms `onBlur` close allow `[data-testid="command-palette"]`, and a jsdom-guarded scroll-into-view effect mirrors the @-picker. **Scope fence:** the NL submit path, attachments, `/grant`, the @-picker, the placeholder, every Bridge station render, and the AD-943/944/945 model are byte-for-byte; no second global key listener; no `config`/voice palette entries (forward marker **AD-946a**); no backend/REST/pytest; no push. **+22 Vitest** (`paletteCommands.test.ts` / `CommandPalette.test.tsx` / `IntentSurface.commandPalette.test.tsx` — including the NL-unchanged regression guard — + 1 `stations.test.tsx` case for `onExpandLabel`); full UI suite **1366 passed / 1 skipped** (228 files, +22 over the 1344 AD-945 baseline, zero regressions); `npm run build` clean (tsc -b + vite); `npx playwright test` **4 passed** (the AD-941 specs never touch the omnibox submit). No backend change → no pytest. **COMMITTED LOCAL ONLY — NOT pushed.** This AD **CLOSES** the "Bridge as Ship's Computer" epic (#873). **AD-942 shipped (2026-06-09, LOCAL ONLY — the Captain decides the push) — Clean Teams-style chat names: the CHATS list + the open-chat header now name every chat by its PARTICIPANTS, not by message content.** Agent-initiated chats (AD-918 `create_group_chat` / AD-924 `[GROUP_CHAT title="…"]`) can land a conversational phrase in `thread.title` ("Hello Yeo", "Thanks, let me know if anything changes", "I acknowledge, and will review") — which read as noise in the list and unlike Teams/Slack/Discord, where a DM/group is named by its members and only a deliberately-renamed room keeps a custom title. **Frontend only, display convention only — the stored `thread.title` is never rewritten.** New pure `chatDisplayName(thread, agents)` in `chatFilters.ts` = the crew callsigns joined by ", " (1 crew → just the callsign, e.g. "Bones"; ≥2 → "Ezri, Yeo"), UNLESS the Captain explicitly renamed the room — detected via `metadata.title_locked` (written by the AD-917 `GroupChatHeader` rename → AD-794 `set_title(lock=True)`) — in which case the deliberate `thread.title` wins. Wired into `ChatsPanel`'s 1:1 + group row titles and the `GroupChatHeader` displayed title + rename pre-fill (the rename box opens pre-filled with the effective name). NO backend/REST/pytest, NO `thread.title` write change, NO AD-938/939/940/941 touch. **+5 Vitest** (`chatFilters.test.ts` — 1:1 by callsign / group by joined callsigns / `title_locked` wins / locked-but-empty falls back / no-crew degrade); full UI suite **1333 passed / 1 skipped** (+5 over the 1328 baseline, zero regressions); `npm run build` clean; **live-verified in the HXI** (the message-titled rows now read "Reed"/"Keiko"/"Nova"/… and groups read "Ezri, Yeo"). the dm sidebar `.map`s `/api/wardroom/dms`), whereas an abort = "no backend" drives each component down its Tier-2 honest-degrade path (safe defaults); (2) `gotoApp` pre-dismisses the first-run `WelcomeOverlay` (an `inset:0; zIndex:100` click-catcher) by setting `hxi_seen_intro` in localStorage via `addInitScript` BEFORE navigation + a `setShowIntro(false)` belt-and-suspenders; (3) `meeting-avatars` opens its group directly via the store (`openGroupChat` helper) so the still-open CHATS panel can't overlap the meeting toggle — `group-chat-open` still exercises the panel row-click open path. **Kept e2e OUT of Vitest:** `vitest.config.ts` `exclude: [...configDefaults.exclude, 'e2e/**']` (the default glob would otherwise run the `*.spec.ts`); `e2e/` is outside `tsconfig include:["src"]` so `npm run build` (tsc -b) is unaffected. `@playwright/test` devDep + `test:e2e`/`test:e2e:ui` scripts + `playwright.config.ts` (testDir `./e2e`, webServer `npm run dev`, chromium project); **AD-938 shipped (2026-06-09, LOCAL ONLY — the Captain decides the push) — Group chat now shows the real thread transcript: opening a group from CHATS renders the `GroupChatHeader` (participants + meeting toggle) AND the group's actual messages with per-author avatars + `HH:MM` timestamps (was an empty, 1:1-looking view; adding people to a 1:1 then opening the new group showed the same broken view).** **Frontend only.** TWO root causes (verified vs HEAD): **(1) the opened group thread was never hydrated into `chatThreads`** — `ChatsPanel.handleOpen` (+ the `NewChatModal` 2+ branch) called `openGroupChatThread` but never `setChatThread(thread)`, so everything that reads `chatThreads.get(activeThreadId)` broke: `GroupChatHeader` (`if (!thread) return null`), the `meetingActive` selector (`…?.metadata.meeting_active`), and `MeetingView`'s participant list; **(2) the transcript rendered the per-AGENT buffer, not the thread's messages** — `ProfileChatTab` displayed `agentConversations.get(agentId)?.messages` and the only history load was `GET /api/agent/{id}/chat/history` (the agent's 1:1 history), so a group ALWAYS showed the host's 1:1 buffer (`activeThreadId` from AD-937 was correct for the send target + meeting flag, but the DISPLAYED transcript ignored it). **Fix Part 1 (hydrate-on-open):** `ChatsPanel.handleOpen` + `NewChatModal`'s 2+ create branch now call `setChatThread(thread)` (already in the store, `useStore.ts:1311`) before `openGroupChatThread`, so the header/participants/meeting flag/transcript resolve the moment the host opens (`handleJoin` already routes through `handleOpen`). **Fix Part 2 (thread-keyed transcript):** `threadApi.listMessages(threadId, limit=200)` wraps `GET /api/threads/{id}/messages` → `{thread_id, messages:[…]}` (verified `routers/threads.py:302`; each `{id, thread_id, author_id, role, body, created_at, metadata}`), Tier-2 `[]` on failure; `useStore` gains a `threadMessages: Map<string, AgentProfileMessage[]>` display slice + `setThreadMessages` (load-on-open) / `appendThreadMessage` (send-reconcile, 200-cap); `ProfileChatTab` adds a load-on-open `useEffect([activeThreadId])` that maps the DTOs (`threadDtoToMessage`: captain→`role:'user'`/no avatar, agent→`role:'agent'`+`authorId`+callsign resolved from the agents map, else→`system`) into the slice, switches the displayed source via a pure `selectTranscriptMessages(activeThreadId, threadMsgs, conversation?.messages)` (thread context → the thread's real messages; cold 1:1 with no thread → the per-agent buffer, unchanged AD-406 UX), and reconciles the send path (the optimistic Captain message + each group `per_agent_reply`/1:1 reply are ALSO `appendThreadMessage`'d when a thread is active — no `callsign:` text prefix, the AD-936 `ChatMessageRow` header shows the author). The three pure/data-path helpers (`threadDtoToMessage`, `selectTranscriptMessages`, `loadThreadMessages`) live in a NEW audio-dep-free `profileTranscript.ts` (the AD-936 `ChatMessageRow` extraction precedent) so they're testable without rendering the audio/screen-dep-laden `ProfileChatTab`. `agentConversations`/`addAgentMessage` UNTOUCHED (still the cold-1:1 path + cross-session `/chat/history` seed). **Scope fence:** NO backend/REST/pytest (the endpoint already exists), NO WebSocket/poll live-push (**AD-935a**), NO `Glyphs.tsx`/`AgentAvatarBadge` change, NO `agentConversations` removal, NO AD-933/934/935 pipeline / facilitator / Ward Room / `IntentMessage` / MeetingView-Captain-slot (**AD-939**) / draggable-panel (**AD-940**) / Playwright (**AD-941**) touch. **+28 Vitest** (`threadApi.listMessages`, `store/threadMessages`, `ProfileChatTab.threadTranscript` — mapper/selector/two-author-avatars/loader/send-reconcile/no-emoji, `ChatsPanel.hydrate`, `NewChatModal.hydrate`; BF-287 real store via `useStore.setState`, mocked `threadApi`); full UI suite **1317 passed / 1 skipped** (222 files; +28 over the 1289 baseline, zero regressions); `npm run build` clean (tsc -b + vite). No backend change → no pytest. **COMMITTED LOCAL ONLY — NOT pushed.** Forward markers: **AD-935a** (live WebSocket/poll push so a peer's messages stream into an open thread without a reopen — this AD loads on open + reconciles on send, enough for the synchronous-cascade model), **AD-938a** (paginate/scrollback for long threads; `listMessages` currently loads the last 200).

**AD-937 shipped (2026-06-08, LOCAL ONLY — the Captain decides the push) — Non-destructive 1:1→group transition: adding a person to a 1:1 chat no longer destroys it (the Captain-reported regression where the 1:1 became unreachable — reopening the agent's profile showed the group).** **Frontend only.** TWO-layer bug (verified vs HEAD): **Layer 1 (destructive mutation)** — `GroupChatHeader.handleAdd` → `addParticipant` → backend `add_participant` did an in-place read-modify-write that rewrote the SAME 1:1 row's `participants ["a"]→["a","b"]`, repurposing the 1:1 INTO the group; **Layer 2 (addressing collision)** — group-open bound the group into the host's SINGLE `threadIdByAgent` slot (`ChatsPanel.handleOpen` + `NewChatModal` 2+ both did `setThreadForAgent(host, groupId)`), so `ProfileChatTab`'s `props.threadId ?? threadIdByAgent.get(agentId)` resolved a roster-open to the GROUP, not the 1:1. **Fix Part A (non-destructive create):** `NewChatModal` gains an optional `seedParticipantId?` that pre-populates `selected=[seed]` and renders the host as a LOCKED, non-removable chip; the 1:1 add control — `GroupChatHeader`'s ≤1-crew branch and the rewired AD-932 `EmptyChatAddPeople` (no longer materialize-then-mutate) — opens the seeded modal, so confirming with 2+ runs the existing `createThread({title, participants})` and mints a SEPARATE new group row; the 1:1 row is NEVER mutated. The ≥2-crew group path keeps the inline `AddParticipantPopover`→`addParticipant` mutate flow (correct — matches Teams). **Fix Part B (stop groups hijacking the 1:1 slot):** `useStore` gains `activeProfileThreadId: string|null` (default null) + `openGroupChatThread(hostId, threadId)` (sets the override + `activeProfileAgent`, NOT `threadIdByAgent`); `openAgentProfile` ALSO sets `activeProfileThreadId: null` (a roster/1:1 open clears any group override). A NEW pure `resolveProfileThreadId(prop, override, map, id)` (its own light, audio-dep-free module) resolves `props.threadId ?? activeProfileThreadId ?? threadIdByAgent.get(agentId)` at ALL THREE `ProfileChatTab` sites (the reactive selector + both send paths, so a send while viewing a group routes to the group); `ChatsPanel.handleOpen` and the `NewChatModal` 2+ branch open via `openGroupChatThread`. **Net:** a group is addressed by `activeProfileThreadId`; the agent's `threadIdByAgent` slot is reserved for its 1:1; reopening from the roster clears the override → the (un-mutated) 1:1 resolves. **Scope fence:** NO backend / `ChatThreadStore.add_participant` change (is-default guard = forward marker **AD-937a**), NO explicit group-name input (NewChatModal auto-titles from callsigns; **AD-937b**), NO AD-933/933a/933b/934/935 pipeline / facilitator / Ward Room / `IntentMessage` / `Glyphs.tsx` touch, NO removal of add-to-existing-group. **+11 Vitest** (new `ad937ThreadResolution.test.ts` — override-not-slot, openAgentProfile-clears, resolver priority, the HEADLINE Captain reopen-1:1 repro; plus seeded-modal/override cases across `NewChatModal`/`EmptyChatAddPeople`/`GroupChatHeader`/`ChatsPanel`; obsolete `setThreadForAgent`+materialize-then-mutate contracts updated). Full UI suite **1289 passed / 1 skipped** (217 files; +11 over the 1278 baseline, zero regressions); `npm run build` clean. No backend change → no pytest. Forward markers: **AD-937a** (backend is-default `add_participant` guard), **AD-937b** (explicit group-name input).

**AD-936 shipped (2026-06-08, LOCAL ONLY — the Captain decides the push) — Per-message chat metadata: author avatar + `HH:MM` timestamp (Teams/Slack/Discord-style). The profile chat transcript now renders the author's `AgentAvatarBadge` + callsign label (agent messages) and a dim local `HH:MM` timestamp (every message) above each bubble; group replies thread the real author's `agent_id`/`callsign` so each shows its OWN avatar instead of a shared host badge + a `callsign:` text prefix.** **Frontend only, additive/backward-compatible.** Part B (timestamp) was pure render — `AgentProfileMessage` already carried `timestamp` (`ui/src/store/types.ts:320`, set in `useStore.addAgentMessage`); Part A (avatar) needed author identity the model lacked (the AD-914 fan-out added every reply under the HOST id with the callsign jammed into the text). **Three additive changes (verified vs HEAD):** (1) `AgentProfileMessage` gains optional `authorId?`/`callsign?`; (2) `useStore.addAgentMessage` gains an optional 4th `opts?: {authorId?, callsign?}` param (interface + impl, conditional-spread so legacy messages stay byte-identical; ~10 call sites unchanged); (3) `ProfileChatTab` group-reply add threads `{authorId: r.agent_id, callsign: r.callsign}` and DROPS the inline `callsign:` prefix. The heavy bubble JSX + a pure `formatChatTime(tsSeconds)->HH:MM` helper are extracted into a NEW presentational `ChatMessageRow({msg, hostAgentId, hostCallsign, body?})` (the `groupsend`/`bf294b` precedent — ProfileChatTab's audio/screen deps make a full jsdom render impractical); the row resolves the avatar `department` via the `ChatsPanel.deptOf` runtime cast + `''` Tier-2 fallback (`Agent` has no formal `department`), falls back to the host avatar for `authorId`-less 1:1/legacy messages, and shows no avatar for `user`/`system` (dim timestamp only; dim-italic system style preserved). **Scope fence:** NO backend/REST/pytest, NO WebSocket/poll (AD-935a), NO `Glyphs.tsx`/`AgentAvatarBadge` API change, NO date-separator headers (AD-936a), NO AD-935 cascade / fan-out / `DmReplyPipeline` / Ward Room touch. **+8 Vitest** (`ProfileChatTab.metadata.test.tsx`, BF-287 real store via `useStore.setState`): agent avatar+callsign+`HH:MM`, two distinct per-author avatars, host fallback, user=timestamp/no-avatar, system unchanged, `formatChatTime` shape, `addAgentMessage` back-compat + opts land, no-emoji guard. Full UI suite **1278 passed / 1 skipped** (216 files; +8 over the ~1270 baseline, zero regressions); `npm run build` clean. No backend change → no pytest. Forward marker: **AD-936a** (date-separator / "Today" headers).

**AD-935 shipped (2026-06-08, LOCAL ONLY — the Captain decides the push) — Agent-to-agent group-chat reactivity (bounded synchronous cascade): after the Captain round, an agent reply fans to the OTHER crew for up to `max_agent_rounds` extra rounds, so agents react to each other in real time instead of only when the Captain posts.** The Captain-reported gap (verified against HEAD): the AD-914 group fan-out fires ONLY on the Captain turn (`routers/threads.py` `if body.role == "captain":`) and AD-914 deliberately bounded it to "fan once and STOP" — so if an agent asks a *peer* a question, there is **no reply until the Captain sends another message**. **Architecture decision — BOUNDED SYNCHRONOUS, NOT async.** The chat transcript has **no live-refresh** (no polling loop, no `chat_message`/`thread_message` WebSocket case in `useStore.handleEvent`; `ProfileChatTab` renders only what the send POST returns in `per_agent_replies`), so a fire-and-forget async cascade would create messages the UI never shows until a manual refresh — AND is harder to bound. AD-935 therefore runs the cascade **synchronously inside the Captain's POST** and returns ALL replies across rounds in the existing `per_agent_replies` list (the UI already renders it, in order). True streaming/async reactivity is forward marker **AD-935a** (unlocked once a live-refresh exists). **Five changes (verified vs HEAD):** (1) `src/probos/config.py` `GroupChatConfig` gains `agent_reactivity_enabled: bool = False` + `max_agent_rounds: int = 2` (transitional flag #14 — ships OFF; zero-config boot byte-identical). (2) `config/system.yaml` `group_chat:` flips `agent_reactivity_enabled: true` + `max_agent_rounds: 2` for the operator (AD-925 default-False-Pydantic/true-yaml pattern). (3) `src/probos/routers/thread_fanout.py` extracts `group_chat_fanout`'s single-round core (facilitate → `gather(_send_one)` → AD-933a episode write) into a new `_fan_one_round(...)` and rewrites `group_chat_fanout` as round 0 (Captain) + a bounded loop: each round fans the PREVIOUS round's agent messages to the OTHER crew (excluding that round's speakers), stopping on empty `speaking_order` / convergence / all-decline / the round cap. `_fan_one_round` rebuilds history from the store each call (cascade rounds include the just-persisted replies); a threaded `before` arg keeps round 0's history byte-identical (`before=captain_msg.created_at` excludes the Captain turn; cascade rounds pass `None`). The cascade is **awaited** (synchronous), Tier-2 honest-degrade (a round failure returns the replies gathered so far). (4) `_send_one` honors `[NO_RESPONSE]`: a decline (case-insensitive, brackets-stripped == `NO_RESPONSE`) or empty reply returns `{..., "_declined": True}` and `_fan_one_round` filters declines BEFORE persist, the episode write, and `per_agent_replies` — so a decline never shows in the transcript or propagates the cascade (this also fixes round 0, where a literal `[NO_RESPONSE]` would previously have been persisted). (5) `src/probos/cognitive/cognitive_agent.py` passes `params["is_group_chat"] = True` from the fan-out and adds the overridable `_conversational_group_chat_protocol(observation)` hook (next to `_conversational_deliberate_protocol`, invoked next to `_delib_proto`) that returns the group decline-teaching string when `params.get("is_group_chat")` — **gap-regex-safe** (no `can't`/`cannot`/`don't have`/`unable to`/`not able to`, verified against `_CAPABILITY_GAP_RE`). The decline mechanism is what makes the cascade meaningful in default production config (agents with nothing to add decline, leaving the asker's message to fan to the others). **+13 pytest** (`tests/test_ad935_group_reactivity.py`, BF-287 — real `ChatThreadStore` on `tmp_path`, real `IntentBus(SignalManager(reap_interval=1.0))`, real-but-fake registry/agents whose subscribed `direct_message` handlers return scripted replies keyed by per-agent call index, a real `GroupChatConfig` toggled per test, a recording `episodic_memory` stub; valid `_WARD_ROOM_CREW` agent types so `is_crew_agent(agent, None)` admits them): flag-OFF AD-914 parity, agents converse (2 rounds in order), round-cap stop, convergence stop (before the cap), `[NO_RESPONSE]` not persisted/propagated, `[NO_RESPONSE]` round-0 fix, exclude-author, all-decline stop, @-mention bypass under cap, AD-933a episode per cascade reply, `_conversational_group_chat_protocol` gap-safe, Tier-2 degrade. The existing AD-914/915/916/933/933a/933b fan-out tests stay green (**72 passed**, byte-identical — no obsolete-contract updates needed). **Scope fence:** NO async/fire-and-forget cascade (AD-935a), NO chat WebSocket/poll, NO reply-shape / `direct_message` / `DmReplyPipeline` / AD-934-step / AD-933b-ref / `IntentMessage` / Ward Room change, NO cascade from agent-initiated posts (AD-935b), NO trust/Hebbian from a2a (AD-935c). Focused gate **13 passed**; blast-radius `-k "thread or chat or fanout or facilitat or convergence or reply or pipeline or group or cognitive_agent"` **1025 passed / 1 skipped** (the 4 `test_skill_agent.py::TestSkillPipeline` fails are the pre-existing serial full-suite isolation flakes — `Event loop is closed` skill-pool cross-test pollution — green **26/26 in isolation**, unrelated to the fan-out). No UI change → no Vitest. Forward markers: **AD-935a** (async/streaming once a live-refresh exists), **AD-935b** (cascade on agent-initiated group posts), **AD-935c** (trust/Hebbian learning from a2a exchanges).

**AD-934 shipped (2026-06-08, LOCAL ONLY — the Captain decides the push) — In-chat `[THINK]`/`[DELIBERATE]` deep-tier re-roll (Option C): a flag-gated post-LLM pipeline step that, on a `[THINK]`/`[DELIBERATE]` marker in an agent's draft reply, makes ONE deep-tier LLM pass to reconsider + improve the draft, replacing the reply text. Default OFF → zero behavior change.** The Captain chose Option C over the AD-632 chain (Options A/B): the agentic loop already serves deep tool-work, so the right increment here is contained, tool-free inline deliberation — not chain re-dispatch. **Four additive changes (verified vs HEAD):** (1) `src/probos/config.py` adds `DmDeliberateConfig(BaseModel)` (`enabled=False`, `tier="deep"`, `max_tokens=800`) mounted on `SystemConfig` right after `dm_targeted_lookup` — default OFF, opt-in because the re-roll adds a full deep-tier LLM pass (latency + cost) per marker-bearing reply; (2) `src/probos/cognitive/dm_sanity_gate.py` adds `_DELIBERATE_RE`/`_DELIBERATE_STRIP_RE` + `extract_deliberate(text)->bool` / `strip_deliberate(text)->str` (mirrors the AD-845 `[CREATE_TASK]` parse/strip contract — the lax strip removes well-formed AND malformed markers so none leak); (3) `src/probos/cognitive/dm/reply_pipeline.py` adds `step_4j_deliberate_parse` — flag-gated on `config.dm_deliberate.enabled`, **always strips** the marker first (even when disabled) so it never reaches the Captain, then (enabled + marker present) makes one deep-tier `llm_client.complete(LLMRequest(prompt, system_prompt, tier, max_tokens))` pass and adopts `resp.content` or honest-degrades to the draft on a missing client / empty / raised response; **NEVER raises** (Tier-2). Registered in `_full_steps()` BETWEEN `step_4g_create_task_parse` and `step_5_episodic_store` (so the re-rolled reply is what gets stored / divergence-checked / emitted → `run()` is now **18** steps) and APPENDED to `_escalation_steps()` after 4g (the group subset is now **7**), so 1:1 AND group both gain the capability behind the flag; (4) `src/probos/cognitive/cognitive_agent.py` adds the overridable `_conversational_deliberate_protocol(observation)` hook (returns the `[THINK]` teaching string only when `config.dm_deliberate.enabled`, else `""`), invoked in the conversational branch next to `_task_proto`/`_nb_proto` — the teaching string is **gap-regex-safe** (no `can't`/`cannot`/`don't have`/`unable to`/`not able to` per the `_CAPABILITY_GAP_RE` lesson). **NOT built:** Option A/B (NO AD-632 chain wiring, NO `_pending_sub_task_chain`, NO `_build_chain_for_intent` DM branch, NO chain templates, NO two-turn re-dispatch); NO change to `DmSanityGateConfig`, the existing 17 steps' order/bodies, `DmReplyContext`, `build_response`, the AD-933a episodic write, the AD-933b ref surfacing, the facilitator, `IntentMessage`, or the Ward Room; the hook returns `""` when the flag is OFF. **Tests: +14 pytest** (`tests/test_ad934_deliberate.py`, BF-287 — a real `DmSanityGate`, a real `DmDeliberateConfig`, a `DmReplyContext` built directly, and a real-but-fake `_FakeLLMClient` (NOT `MagicMock`) whose `complete(request, *, priority=…)` records the request + returns an object with `.content`): extract/strip units; flag OFF+marker → strip only, 0 llm calls; flag ON+marker+refined → replaced, called once with `tier=="deep"`; flag ON+no-marker → no call; empty/raise/None-client → honest-degrade to draft; `_full_steps` orders 4g→4j→5 (18); `_escalation_steps` appends 4j (7); the hook is `""` off / non-empty + gap-safe on; `DmDeliberateConfig` defaults. **Two obsolete-contract step-membership tests updated** (the direct, necessary consequence of inserting 4j, NOT scope creep — the same pattern AD-933a/933b used): AD-933's `_ALL_STEPS` 17→18 + `_ESCALATION_SUBSET` 6→7 (+ renamed `test_run_invokes_all_eighteen_steps_in_order`), AD-933b's `_ALL_STEPS`/`_ESCALATION_STEPS` likewise (+ `test_run_still_eighteen_steps_in_order`, `len==18`); `test_bf296` greps `_full_steps` for 4b, which 4j does not move (stays green). Focused gate **14 passed**; blast-radius `-k "dm or reply or pipeline or sanity or deliberate or escalat or cognitive_agent or config"` **1376 passed / 1 skipped** (the 1 skip pre-existing; warnings are the pre-existing BF-326 MagicMock-dir + importlib_metadata deprecations — no skill-pool flakes). No UI change → no Vitest. **COMMITTED LOCAL ONLY — NOT pushed** (the Captain decides the push). Forward markers: **AD-934a** (per-agent opt-in / richer teaching + use the `[THINK reason]` focus hint in the re-roll prompt), **AD-934b** (surface a subtle "deliberated" indicator in the HXI).

**AD-933b shipped (2026-06-08, LOCAL ONLY — the Captain decides the push) — Group-chat image generation: add `step_4c_image_gen_parse` (AD-730-3 `[GEN_IMAGE prompt]`) to the AD-933 channel-agnostic escalation subset so a crew agent can generate an image inside a room, and surface the generated SHA refs onto the persisted group message metadata.** The gap (verified against HEAD): `reply_pipeline.step_4c_image_gen_parse` is already channel-agnostic and 1:1-safe — it reads `ctx.sanity_gate` + `ctx.runtime.config.avatars.image_gen_*`, calls `dispatch_image_gen(runtime, agent_id=…, prompt=…)`, appends the SHA to `ctx.generated_attachment_ids`, and honest-degrades (marker stripped, operator message appended) when the tier is disabled/unconfigured — but AD-933's `_escalation_steps()` subset deliberately **omitted** 4c, and the group fan-out returns `{agent_id, callsign, text}` and never calls `build_response()` (which is what surfaces `generated_attachment_ids` on the 1:1 response). Net: a group-generated image would be created + stored in `AttachmentStore` yet **invisible** — no path put the SHA ref anywhere the room could see it. **Two changes, both additive:** (1) `src/probos/cognitive/dm/reply_pipeline.py::_escalation_steps()` adds `self.step_4c_image_gen_parse` in **run()-order** (4c precedes 4e in `_full_steps`), so the subset tuple is now `(4c, 4e, 4i, 4h, 4f, 4g)` and `run_escalation_only()` runs **6** steps; `run()` and `_full_steps()` are left **byte-identical** (4c was already in `_full_steps`, so the 1:1 path is completely unaffected — only the docstring's Included/Excluded lists + the relative-order parenthetical were updated for accuracy). (2) `src/probos/routers/thread_fanout.py::_send_one` initializes `generated_ids: list[str] = []` before the AD-933 escalation block, captures `generated_ids = list(pipeline.ctx.generated_attachment_ids or [])` from the **SAME** `pipeline.ctx` the AD-933 block already built (no second pipeline), and — only when non-empty — extends the existing `store.append_message(...)` metadata `{"intent_id": intent.id, "fanout": "ad914"}` with `"generated_attachment_ids": generated_ids` (AD-916 established `metadata`-carried attachment refs on chat messages). **Tier-2:** if the escalation block is skipped (no reply / no agent) or raises, `generated_ids` stays `[]` and the metadata is **byte-identical** to the AD-914 baseline. `step_4d_follow_up` is **deliberately NOT** added (its `conversation_pacing_scheduler` re-injects a synthesized user-turn — an ambiguous target in a multi-agent room: forward marker **AD-933b-2**); UI rendering of group-generated images is forward marker **AD-933b-3**. **NO change** to `build_response`, the 1:1 path, `DmReplyContext`, `IntentMessage`, the facilitator, the AD-933a episodic write, the `{agent_id, callsign, text}` return shape, or the Ward Room. **One obsolete-contract test updated** (the direct, necessary consequence of the spec'd subset change, NOT scope creep): the AD-933 `test_run_escalation_only_invokes_only_the_subset` asserted the OLD 5-step subset — its `_ESCALATION_SUBSET` constant was re-pointed to include `step_4c_image_gen_parse` (6 steps, 4c first), preserving its named intent (escalation runs ONLY the subset, the other 11 steps never fire). +5 pytest (`tests/test_ad933b_group_image_gen.py`, BF-287 real `ChatThreadStore`+`IntentBus(SignalManager(reap_interval=1.0))`+`DmSanityGate`, `dispatch_image_gen` monkeypatched at its source module since step_4c imports it function-locally): (1) `run_escalation_only()` runs exactly `{4c,4e,4i,4h,4f,4g}` in run()-order, the other 11 NOT; (2) `run()` still 17 steps byte-identical (regression guard); (3) a group `[GEN_IMAGE a cat]` reply persists `metadata.generated_attachment_ids == ["sha-xyz"]` with the marker stripped; (4) a disabled tier honest-degrades (operator message appended, marker stripped, no ref key, no crash); (5) a plain reply leaves metadata exactly `{"intent_id","fanout"}`. Focused gate **5 passed**; blast-radius `-k "thread or chat or fanout or reply or pipeline or image or escalat"` **860 passed / 4 failed / 1 skipped** — the 4 fails are the pre-existing `test_skill_agent.py::TestSkillPipeline` serial full-suite isolation flakes (`Event loop is closed` cross-test pollution; **green 7/7 in isolation**; about skill-pool spawning, zero connection to image-gen/fan-out). No UI change → no Vitest. **COMMITTED LOCAL ONLY — NOT pushed** (the Captain decides the push). Forward markers: **AD-933b-2** (in-chat `[FOLLOW_UP]`), **AD-933b-3** (UI render of group-generated images), **AD-934** (in-chat `[THINK]`/`[DELIBERATE]`).

**AD-933a shipped (2026-06-08, LOCAL ONLY — the Captain decides the push) — Group-anchored episodic write for the fan-out: every group-chat fan-out reply now persists ONE `channel="chat"` / `session_type:"group"` episode tagged with the thread id, closing the learning-loop hole AD-933 left open.** The gap (verified against HEAD): the fan-out sends `direct_message` with `params["from"]="hxi_profile"`, which the agent's universal episodic safety-net `_store_action_episode` (`cognitive_agent.py:8034`) **skips** for exactly that case — it defers to the pipeline's `step_5_episodic_store` — and AD-933 deliberately **excluded** `step_5` from the group escalation subset because `step_5` hardcodes `session_type:"1:1"`/`channel:"dm"` and firing it on a multi-agent turn would write **mislabeled** episodes. Net result: a group-chat reply wrote **no episode at all** — agents could not remember what they said in a room (no episodic recall, no dreaming consolidation, no wellness/divergence analysis), violating "every execution path stores an episode or the learning loop breaks." **The fix mirrors the AD-719 @-mention fan-out** (`routers/chat.py` ~L215–256), but group-anchored. After `replies = await asyncio.gather(...)` at the END of `group_chat_fanout` (`src/probos/routers/thread_fanout.py`), a Tier-2 episodic-write loop: `episodic_memory = getattr(runtime, "episodic_memory", None)` (skip entirely if `None`); resolve participants once as `["captain"] + [callsign-or-agent_id per crew speaker]`; then for each reply — **skipping** the sentinel set `{"(no response)", "(delivery failed)", ""}` and any reply with no `agent_id` — build the episode (prefer `runtime.dream_adapter.build_episode(episode_input, {"response":…, "agent_ids":[…]}, t_start, t_end)` when a `dream_adapter` is present, mirroring AD-719; else a direct `Episode(timestamp=…, user_input="[group chat] Captain: …", dag_summary={}, outcomes=[{"intent":"direct_message","success":True,"response":reply["text"][:500],"session_type":"group","callsign":…,"source":"group_chat_fanout"}], agent_ids=[reply["agent_id"]], duration_ms=…, source="group_chat_fanout", anchors=AnchorFrame(channel="chat", trigger_type="group_fanout", participants=…, chat_thread_id=thread_id))`), then `await episodic_memory.store(episode)` inside a `try/except Exception` that logs `AD-933a: group episode store failed …; continuing` and **NEVER raises** — so the fan-out always returns all replies. `t_start = time.monotonic()` is captured at the TOP of the function (new module-level `import time`), `t_end` right after the gather. **NO change** to `_send_one`'s return shape, the `DmReplyPipeline`, `_store_action_episode`, `step_5`, the 1:1 path, the facilitator, `IntentMessage`, or the Ward Room — additive, group-only. **One obsolete-contract test updated** (the direct, necessary consequence of the spec'd behavior change, NOT scope creep): the AD-933 `test_group_path_writes_no_1to1_episode` asserted `recorder.stored == []` (the pre-AD-933a "no episode at all" claim AD-933a's whole purpose overturns) — re-pointed to "group episodes ARE written now, and NONE are 1:1-labelled" (`session_type != "1:1"`, `channel != "dm"`), preserving its named intent (step_5/1:1 never fires on the group path). +6 pytest (`tests/test_ad933a_group_episode.py`, BF-287 real `ChatThreadStore`+`IntentBus(SignalManager(reap_interval=1.0))`+a real-but-fake recording `episodic_memory` (NOT `MagicMock`), no `dream_adapter` so the group-anchored fallback `Episode(...)` runs); focused gate **6 passed**, blast-radius `-k "thread or chat or fanout or episod or dream"` **1285 passed / 1 skipped**. Forward markers: **AD-933b** richer escalation subset, **AD-934** in-chat `[THINK]`/`[DELIBERATE]`.

**AD-933 shipped (2026-06-08, LOCAL ONLY — the Captain decides the push) — Group-chat escalation ladder: wire the AD-726 one-shot post-LLM escalation subset into the AD-914 group-chat fan-out, so a group reply can resolve an inline mesh read, dispatch an `[ACTION]`, or open a `[CREATE_TASK]` — not just ship a Tier-1 reply.** The gap (verified against HEAD): the 1:1 chat path (`routers/agents.py:agent_chat`) and the group fan-out (`routers/thread_fanout.py:group_chat_fanout._send_one`) are **structurally identical** up through `result = await runtime.intent_bus.send(intent)` — both build `IntentMessage(intent="direct_message", …)` — but the 1:1 path then constructs a `DmReplyContext` + `DmReplyPipeline` and runs the **post-LLM escalation + cleanup ladder** (AD-726), while `_send_one` took `reply_text = str(result.result)` **RAW** and persisted it, **never building the pipeline**. So group chat was **Tier-1-only**: an agent in a room could not resolve an AD-869 inline mesh read, dispatch an AD-745 browser `[ACTION]`, or open an AD-845 `[CREATE_TASK]` — the entire escalation ladder that already exists on the 1:1 path was missing in the group path, blocking the "ask the room to do something complex → it spins up a task / task room" collaboration flow. **The fix is DRY + additive.** `DmReplyPipeline.run()`'s hardcoded 17-step tuple is extracted into `_full_steps()` (single source of truth) and the `for step in (…)` body into `_run_steps(steps)` (the verbatim AD-726 per-step Tier-2 guard); `run()` becomes `await self._run_steps(self._full_steps())` — **byte-identical** (same 17 steps, same order, same guard). A new `_escalation_steps()` returns the **channel-agnostic subset** in run()-order — `step_4e_action_dispatch` (AD-745), `step_4i_notebook_parse` (AD-911), `step_4h_mesh_read_parse` (AD-869 read-only mesh), `step_4f_extract_artifacts` (AD-797), `step_4g_create_task_parse` (AD-845) — and public `run_escalation_only()` runs exactly that subset under the same guard. In `_send_one`, `sanity_gate = getattr(runtime, "dm_sanity_gate", None)` is resolved **once** before the `asyncio.gather` (DRY; `step_4g` needs it), and **only when** a real `result.result` came back **AND** the agent resolved (no agent → can't escalate), it builds `DmReplyPipeline(DmReplyContext(…, sampling_state=None, avatar_event_bus=None, chat_thread_id=thread_id))`, `await pipeline.run_escalation_only()`, and `reply_text = pipeline.ctx.response_text or reply_text` (now tag-stripped + may carry the `(Task opened: <id>)` suffix) before the existing `store.append_message(…)`. The whole block is **Tier-2 honest-degrade** (inner `try`/`except` + the per-step guards inside the pipeline) so a failure ships the raw reply rather than silencing it. **Three execution modes now map cleanly:** 1:1 `/chat` → `run()` (full 17 steps); group fan-out → `run_escalation_only()` (5-step subset); proactive `[GROUP_CHAT]`/`[STATUS]`/`[ARTIFACT]` tags → the AD-924/927/928 proactive extractors (unchanged). **The full pipeline is rejected for group** because `step_5_episodic_store` hardcodes `session_type:"1:1"`, `channel:"dm"`, `participants:["captain", callsign]` → firing it on a multi-agent group reply writes **mislabeled** episodes (a learning-loop integrity regression); `step_6` similarly records `"Captain DM"`, and `step_1` (sanity retry), `step_2/3` (games), `step_4b` (outbound DM), `step_4c` (image-gen), `step_4d` (follow-up), `step_7` (divergence), `step_8` (mark-emitted/avatar), `step_9` (emotion) are all 1:1/avatar-scoped. Each of the 5 included steps is a **strict no-op** for any reply lacking its marker, and the markers are emitted only by specifically-taught agents (e.g. Yeo for `[CREATE_TASK]`), so the subset is inherently bounded and safe outside the 1:1 path. **NOT changed (non-goals / forward markers):** **`DmReplyPipeline.run()` behavior byte-identical**; the 1:1 `/api/agent/{id}/chat` path still calls `run()`; **no step-method body change**, no `DmReplyContext` field change, no `build_response()` change; **NO** facilitator / who-speaks-order / `IntentMessage` / vision-pipeline / persist-call change; **NO** AD-632 chain (`_CHAIN_ELIGIBLE_INTENTS`, `_should_activate_chain`, `_execute_chain_with_intent_routing`, `_pending_sub_task_chain`) — in-chat `[THINK]`/`[DELIBERATE]` via the dormant escape hatch is the deferred **AD-934**; **NO** AD-925 task-room logic (it fires downstream automatically when a created task fans to ≥2 crew); **NO** Ward Room. Group-anchored episodic write = forward marker **AD-933a**; a richer subset = **AD-933b**. **Tests: +8 pytest** (`tests/test_ad933_group_chat_escalation.py`, BF-287 — real `WorkItemStore` (in-memory SQLite, started), real `ChatThreadStore` on `tmp_path`, real `IntentBus(SignalManager(reap_interval=1.0))` with subscribed `direct_message` handlers, real `DmSanityGate` on `runtime.dm_sanity_gate`, real-but-fake registry/callsign stubs — NO MagicMock at the substrate boundary): group `[CREATE_TASK]` opens a dispatchable `yeo-delegated` work item + persists the reply tag-stripped with `(Task opened: <id>)`; plain reply is a no-op (zero work items); `dm_sanity_gate=None` honest-degrades (no crash, no work item); the group path writes NO 1:1-labelled episode (proves `step_5` excluded via a recording `episodic_memory`); an AD-869 mesh marker runs/strips without hanging (empty pool → `step_4h` fast-degrades); `run_escalation_only()` invokes ONLY the 5-step subset and `run()` still invokes all 17 in order (spy regression guards for the refactor); the fan-out return shape `{agent_id, callsign, text}` is preserved with the mutated `text`. The `test_bf296` `inspect.getsource(run)` structural guard was re-pointed at `_full_steps` (the tuple's new home). Focused gate **8 passed**; blast-radius `-k "thread or chat or fanout or dm or reply or pipeline or create_task or escalat"` **1164 passed | 1 skipped**, zero regressions. No UI change → no Vitest. **COMMITTED LOCAL ONLY — NOT pushed** (the Captain decides the push). Forward markers: **AD-933a** (group-anchored episodic write), **AD-933b** (richer subset), **AD-934** (in-chat `[THINK]`/`[DELIBERATE]`).

**AD-932 shipped (2026-06-08, LOCAL ONLY — the Captain decides the push) — Discoverable "+ Add people" on a fresh/empty 1:1 chat: a small `EmptyChatAddPeople` affordance that materializes the 1:1 thread so the AD-917 `GroupChatHeader` (+ its add-participant picker) takes over — turning the 1:1 into a group.** The gap (verified against HEAD): the AD-917 `GroupChatHeader` (with `AddParticipantPopover`) mounts in `ProfileChatTab` only as `{activeThreadId && <GroupChatHeader …/>}`, and `GroupChatHeader` itself early-returns `if (!thread) return null` — but a **brand-new 1:1 has no thread until the first message is sent** (the row is created server-side by `get_or_create_default_for_agent` on the first `agent_chat` turn), so on an empty 1:1 `activeThreadId` is `undefined` → no header → **no add-participant control at all** (the Captain's observation: undiscoverable). **The fix** is a focused new `ui/src/components/profile/EmptyChatAddPeople.tsx` mounted by `ProfileChatTab` via a single sibling line `{!activeThreadId && <EmptyChatAddPeople agentId={agentId} />}` — **mutually exclusive** with `GroupChatHeader` (one shows iff `activeThreadId`, the other iff `!activeThreadId`) and **disjoint** from the AD-929 Files rail (`showWorkspaceFiles = !!activeThreadId && …` is `false` whenever `!activeThreadId`). The component **self-gates on `agent?.isCrew`** (only a crew 1:1 can become a group; non-crew / unknown host → renders nothing) and on click **materializes** the thread: `createThread({ title: callsign, participants: [agentId] })` → `setChatThread(t)` + `setThreadForAgent(agentId, t.id)`, so `activeThreadId` resolves on the next render and the **existing** `GroupChatHeader` (+ its `AddParticipantPopover`) mounts for the actual add — a **materialize-then-hand-off** (AD-932 just gets you from "no thread" → "thread exists, header visible"). **`createThread` (not a hidden default)** is correct because there is no client-callable get-or-create-my-1:1 endpoint (`get_or_create_default_for_agent` is server-side only), and on the empty state the client genuinely has no thread for the agent; the minted `participants=[agentId]` row is also what the server's default lookup matches. **Tier-2 honest-degrade:** `createThread` → `null` keeps the button, no store write, no crash. HXI #3 — inline-SVG `UserPlus` (reused, **no `Glyphs.tsx` change**), amber `#f0b060` / dim palette, glow-on-hover, no emoji. **Independent of AD-931** (builds on AD-917/threadApi; touches only `profile/`). **NOT changed (non-goals / forward markers):** **NO `GroupChatHeader` contract change** (its `if (!thread) return null` + prop surface stay exactly as-is; auto-opening the picker on materialize is forward marker **AD-932a**); no full-`ProfileChatTab` render test (follow the `groupsend`/`bf294b` precedent — component-isolation tests only); **NO Ward Room DM convergence** / chain-pipeline / AD-574c-i; no backend / new endpoint / `get_or_create_default_for_agent` exposure; no AD-929 Files-rail / AD-925 task-room change; no AD-931 dependency; no `Glyphs.tsx` exports; no `LeftRail.tsx` wire; no eager thread creation on profile open (materialized only on the explicit click). **Tests: +8 Vitest** (`ui/src/components/profile/__tests__/EmptyChatAddPeople.test.tsx`, BF-287 real-store + mocked `threadApi.createThread`): renders for a crew agent; renders nothing for non-crew and for an unknown agentId; click calls `createThread` once with `{title: callsign, participants:[agentId]}`; success writes `setChatThread` (`chatThreads`) + `setThreadForAgent` (`threadIdByAgent`); honest-degrade on `null` (no store write, button stays); button clickable; no-emoji guard. Full Vitest **1270 passed | 1 skipped** (215 files; baseline 1262 + 8, zero regressions); `npm run build` (tsc -b + vite) clean. **COMMITTED LOCAL ONLY — NOT pushed** (the Captain decides the push). Forward marker: **AD-932a** (auto-open the picker on materialize).

**AD-931 shipped (2026-06-08, LOCAL ONLY — the Captain decides the push) — Unified CHATS surface: one Teams/Slack-style home listing BOTH 1:1 and group conversations, with a prominent "+ New chat" picker.** The Captain's two observations (verified against HEAD): (1) the AD-919 `GroupChatListPanel` was **visibility + Join only** — no create affordance anywhere in the HXI; (2) "GROUP CHATS" sat apart from 1:1 DMs, whereas Teams/Slack present **one Chats list** holding both (a 1:1 lived only inside an agent's profile chat tab, never in a list). AD-931 **repurposes** (moves + renames) the panel into `chats/ChatsPanel.tsx` rather than adding a sibling: it extracts the five pure helpers + constants verbatim into `chats/chatFilters.ts`, renames the store flag `groupChatListOpen`→`chatsOpen` (+ `openChats`/`closeChats`) and the TopNav `GROUP CHATS` (`group-chats-toggle`)→`CHATS` (`chats-toggle`), and leaves `LeftRail.tsx` the forward marker AD-719b-parent-wire. **The unified `isChat` filter** widens AD-919's `isGroupChat`: `!thread.task_id && (isAgentCreated || crewParticipantIds.length >= 1)` — **1:1s appear** (the `get_or_create_default_for_agent` default thread is a real listed `chat_threads` row, and `GET /api/threads` has no participant filter → frontend-only, no backend change), while **AD-925 task rooms are excluded** (they carry `task_id`). A **1:1 row** shows a single avatar + callsign and **no Join**; a **group row** keeps the AD-919 treatment (multi-avatar strip, agent-created badge, Join when `!captainJoined`); both open via the unchanged host pattern (`setThreadForAgent(host)` + `openAgentProfile(host)`). **"+ New chat"** opens `NewChatModal`, which **reuses** the AD-917 `AddParticipantPopover` as a multi-select (accumulate `onAdd`→`selected[]`, feed it back as `existingParticipantIds`, removable chips, "Start chat" disabled until ≥1) and **branches on selection count to avoid a duplicate/divergent 1:1**: **1 agent → `openAgentProfile(id)`** (NO `createThread` — the server owns the 1:1 default), **2+ → `createThread({title, participants})`** then open the host (`createThread` honest-degrades to `null` → modal stays open). **NOT built (non-goals / forward markers):** **no Ward Room DM convergence** — the two DM systems (Ward Room chain pipeline vs 1:1 Chats one-shot pipeline) stay a **deliberate A/B test**, convergence gated on a pipeline decision (AD-574c-i untouched); no backend/REST/pytest (every endpoint exists); no `LeftRail.tsx` wire; no `Glyphs.tsx` exports (reuse `UserPlus`/`Close` + local inline SVGs); no `createThread` for the 1-agent path; no `GroupChatHeader`/`ProfileChatTab` change; the discoverable in-chat add-people on an empty 1:1 is the sibling **AD-932**. **Tests: +25 Vitest** across three new files — `chats/__tests__/chatFilters.test.ts` (8), `chats/__tests__/ChatsPanel.test.tsx` (11, migrated from the 10 AD-919 panel tests with test #1 flipped to "lists BOTH 1:1 and group; excludes task room"), `chats/__tests__/NewChatModal.test.tsx` (6) — **superseding** the deleted 10-test `groupchat/__tests__/GroupChatListPanel.test.tsx` (net ≈ +15). Full Vitest **1262 passed | 1 skipped** (214 files; baseline 1247 + 25 − 10 superseded, zero regressions); `npm run build` clean. **COMMITTED LOCAL ONLY — NOT pushed.** Forward marker: **AD-932** (discoverable in-chat add-people).

**AD-926a shipped (2026-06-08, LOCAL ONLY — Task Workspace Rooms epic held for Captain review/push) — Task-level multi-file input upload: the WRITE/population path AD-926 deferred.** The Captain asked to "attach multiple files as context inputs for a task." AD-926 shipped the READ (`GET /api/threads/{id}/inputs`) and AD-929 the Files-rail display, but the authoritative `WorkItem.metadata["input_attachments"]` source was a **defined-but-dormant seam** — nothing wrote it. AD-926a is the missing WRITE half: a new `POST /api/work-items/{work_item_id}/inputs` (multipart `files: list[UploadFile]`) on the **existing** `/api` workforce router. Each file routes through the **SHARED** chat uploader `chat._validate_and_store_attachment` (the same AD-720a defense-in-depth chain the chat multipart endpoint uses — feature gate → MIME allowlist → size cap → sha256 → magic-byte validator → idempotent content-addressable `AttachmentStore.write`) with the DEFAULT `origin="chat_attachment"` (operator intent, never age-reaped; a new `task_input` origin would coerce to `chat_attachment` anyway, so none is added), and a ref `{content_hash, mime, filename}` (the exact shape `_collect_task_inputs` reads, `source="task"`; `filename` taken from the `UploadFile`, whose success dict has none) is appended. **The `update_work_item` REPLACE crux:** `WorkItemStore.update_work_item(id, metadata=…)` writes the `metadata` column as a WHOLE-COLUMN REPLACE (no native merge, no `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`), so the merge happens at the call site as a **single read-merge-write** — validate+store ALL N files FIRST, then ONE `get_work_item` → `dict(metadata)` → append refs (DEDUPE by `content_hash`) → `update_work_item(metadata=merged)`, preserving every other metadata key + existing inputs. Storing all files before the single write eliminates the intra-request race; the cross-request last-writer-wins is a documented bounded seam (operator-driven manual action) → an atomic store-level `append_input_attachments` (`BEGIN IMMEDIATE`, mirroring `set_title`) is the forward marker **AD-926a-2**. **Honest-degrade per file:** a rejected file (oversize / mime mismatch / disallowed) lands in `skipped` rather than 500-ing the request (good files still attach); 503 when `work_item_store` is None, 404 when the work item is missing (mirroring the sibling routes). **Operator authority, no consensus** (mirrors the other work-item mutations PATCH / transition / assign / delete; attaching a file is additive, reversible, low-risk — Safety Budget axiom). **UI:** `WorkspaceFilesRail` gains a `taskId?` prop (`ProfileChatTab` passes `workspaceThread?.task_id`); the Inputs section shows a multi-file **"+ Attach"** button (a hidden `<input type="file" multiple>` + inline stroke-SVG, HXI #3 no-emoji, amber) ONLY when `taskId` is set (a ≥2-crew room without a bound work item has no place to hold inputs); a new `inputsApi.attachTaskInputs(workItemId, files)` posts ONE multipart `files[]` request (mirroring `ProfileChatTab.uploadAttachment`) and refreshes the rail from the returned `inputs`. **Non-goals / forward markers:** no input edit/versioning (inputs are immutable context; artifacts/outputs are the editable side, AD-797/AD-927); no delete-input v1 (**AD-926a-1**); no store-level atomic merge — `update_work_item` is not refactored (**AD-926a-2**); no new attachment origin; no kanban/`WorkBoard` card attach; no change to the `AttachmentStore` Protocol, the `WorkItem` schema, the AD-926 read endpoint / `_collect_task_inputs`, the AD-925 create path, or the `source="message"` path; no consensus gate, no new `EventType`, no new config field, no new router (extend the workforce router). **Tests:** `tests/test_ad926a_task_file_upload.py` (+10, BF-287 — real `WorkItemStore` + real `FilesystemAttachmentStore` + real `ChatThreadStore` + real `SystemConfig`, `UploadFile` built from `io.BytesIO`, the chat uploader store cache seeded; two-file append + read-back, bytes land by sha256, second-upload append preserving a sentinel metadata key, within-request dedupe, mixed good/bad per-file skip, disallowed-mime skip, 404, 503, empty-files no-op, integration via `GET /api/threads/{id}/inputs` `source="task"`) + `ui/src/components/inputs/__tests__/inputsApi.attach.test.ts` (+5 — multipart `files[]` shape, URL encoding, `body.inputs` return, non-ok throw, no-emoji source guard) + `ui/src/components/workspace/__tests__/WorkspaceFilesRail.attach.test.tsx` (+5 — button shows only with `taskId`, hidden without, `attachTaskInputs` called with the picked files, rail refreshes from the response, no-emoji guard). Focused pytest **28** (10 AD-926a + 10 AD-926 + 8 AD-925); blast-radius `-k "workforce or work_item or thread or chat or attachment or input"` **797 passed**; full Vitest **1247 passed | 1 skipped** (212 files; baseline 1237 + 10, zero regressions); `npm run build` clean. **COMMITTED LOCAL ONLY — NOT pushed** (held for Captain review/push alongside the rest of the Task Workspace Rooms epic).

**AD-930 shipped (2026-06-08, LOCAL ONLY — held for Captain review/push) — Crew presence layer: the Microsoft-Teams-style per-crew presence dot (`offline | online | working | in_meeting`), the last Teams-signature gap after group chat (AD-913→919), meetings (AD-920→923), and the Task Workspace Rooms epic (AD-925→929).** The Captain asked for "a good indicator to see if the agent is actively working or in a meeting." Every signal a presence layer needs **already exists** — AD-930 **aggregates** three verified-existing signals and invents no new telemetry: **liveness** = `BaseAgent.is_alive` (`AgentState` ∈ {ACTIVE, DEGRADED}) over `registry.all()` filtered by `is_crew_agent`; **in_meeting** = the agent is a participant of a non-archived chat thread whose `metadata.meeting_active` is set (AD-920); **working** = `AgentMeta.last_active` (bumped in `BaseAgent.update_confidence()` after each completed op) within a new `CommunicationsConfig.presence_working_window_seconds` (default 90.0). **The "working" honesty:** there is **no canonical per-agent in-flight / executing-right-now flag at HEAD** (`avatars/telemetry.py:24` says so verbatim), so v1 `working` is an **honest recent-activity proxy** (last completed operation), NOT a fabricated in-flight flag — a true in-flight signal is the forward marker **AD-930a**. **Model:** a 4-state string with **liveness as the floor** (a not-alive agent is `offline` even if still a thread participant) and, among alive agents, the Captain's precedence `in_meeting > working > online`. **Endpoint:** a read-only `GET /api/crew/presence` on the **existing** `/api/crew` router (no new router) → `{"presence": {agent_id: state}, "count": N}` for crew only; Tier-2 honest-degrade (registry `None` → `{}`,`0`; store `None` → no `in_meeting`; config-missing → 90s default). **POLLED, not streamed** — the simplest, least-invasive v1 (no new WebSocket channel, no riding the fleet avatar-telemetry stream). **UI:** a pure no-emoji `PresenceDot` (green `#60c070` / amber-pulse `#f0b060` / blue `#5090d0` / dim `#666680`; `data-presence` + `data-pulse` test hooks; HXI #3 stroke/SVG + HXI #4 motion=state), a `presence: CrewPresenceMap` store slice + `fetchPresence` (mirrors the `openCrewManifest` fetch idiom) polled every 10s **while `CrewRosterPanel` is open**, a dot beside each `CrewRow` callsign, and an **optional** `presence?` prop on the reusable `AgentAvatarBadge` (omitted = byte-identical original badge, backward-compatible — no existing call site or test changes). Wiring presence into every badge surface app-wide needs a global poll → forward marker **AD-930b**. **NOT built (non-goals / forward markers):** no true in-flight signal / new telemetry (**AD-930a**); no activity feed (that is AD-928's `[STATUS]`); no global app-wide presence poll (**AD-930b**); no WebSocket/stream; no `degraded`/`away` state; no change to `AgentState`/`BaseAgent`/`AgentMeta`/`ChatThreadStore`/the `/roster` endpoint/`MeetingView`/`Glyphs.tsx`; no new `EventType`/REST router/store method beyond `fetchPresence`/config class (extend `CommunicationsConfig` only). **Tests: +9 pytest** (`tests/test_ad930_presence.py`, BF-287 — a real `ChatThreadStore` on `tmp_path` + a real-but-fake `_FakeRegistry` of `_FakeAgent` duck-stubs with controllable `is_alive`/`meta.last_active` + a real `CommunicationsConfig`, direct-`await` of `crew_presence` over a `SimpleNamespace` runtime, no MagicMock at the store/config boundary): `in_meeting`, `working` (just inside 90s), `online` (just outside 90s), `offline` (not alive), non-crew excluded, precedence (`in_meeting` beats `working`), `registry None`, `store None` degrade, count integrity. **+12 Vitest** across three new files — `PresenceDot.test.tsx` (6: each state's color/`data-presence`, working `data-pulse`, no-emoji + `?raw` source), `CrewRosterPanel.presence.test.tsx` (3: dots render from the slice + poll fires while open, absent agent → `offline`, no-emoji), `AgentAvatarBadge.presence.test.tsx` (3: `presence` prop overlays a dot, omitted = no dot/backward-compatible, no-emoji). Backend focused gate 20 (9 AD-930 + 11 AD-924); full Vitest **1237 passed | 1 skipped** (210 files; baseline 1225 + 12, zero regressions); `npm run build` (tsc -b + vite) clean. **COMMITTED LOCAL ONLY — NOT pushed** (held for Captain review/push alongside AD-926a). Forward markers: **AD-930a** (true in-flight "working" signal), **AD-930b** (global app-wide badge poll).

**AD-928 shipped (2026-06-08, LOCAL ONLY — epic held for Captain review) — Show-your-work status protocol: the Microsoft-Teams "activity feed" feel for a task workspace room (the AD-928 row of the "Task Workspace Rooms" epic AD-925 → AD-929, filled after Captain deferral).** As the crew work a task they **narrate meaningful progress** in the room and post a **clear final result**, so a human watching the room sees the work happen and a crisp completion. The mechanism mirrors AD-924/AD-927 exactly. **ONE `[STATUS]` tag family with an optional `final` flag, NOT two tags.** A new module-level `_STATUS_PATTERN` (`r'\[STATUS(\s+final)?\s*\](.*?)\[/STATUS\]'`, `DOTALL|IGNORECASE`; g1=`" final"`|None, g2=body) parses both `[STATUS]progress[/STATUS]` → `metadata={"kind":"status"}` and `[STATUS final]done[/STATUS]` → `metadata={"kind":"status","status_final":true}`. **Why one tag, not `[STATUS]` + `[RESULT]`:** DRY — a second tag doubles the regex/extractor/rank-gate/cap surface for a semantic that is just a boolean on the same message kind; the activity-feed feel = a stream of `kind="status"` messages plus one distinctly-marked completion (mirrors AD-923's deterministic `meeting_end` marker). A new async `ProactiveCognitiveLoop._extract_and_execute_statuses` (a near-clone of the AD-927 artifact extractor, minus the two-call store write) posts each block via `runtime.chat_thread_store.append_message(room.id, author_id=agent.id, role="agent", body, metadata=…)` (SYNC, returns `None` if the thread is missing = built-in honest-degrade; `to_dict` carries `metadata`, so the existing `GET /api/threads/{id}/messages` already surfaces the marker for a future UI consumer). **Binding: REUSE `_resolve_agent_task_room(agent.id)` UNCHANGED** (AD-927 participation resolver — the most-recently-active non-archived `task_id` thread that lists the agent; the agent IS a participant of its AD-925 auto-room). No resolvable room → honest-degrade (suppress, strip tag, no post, no crash). **Rank gate + anti-flood (three NEW `CommunicationsConfig` fields, all defaulted):** `status_min_rank="lieutenant"` (status narration + artifact output are the two task-room work-output channels — same tier; Ensigns cannot think proactively anyway), `status_max_per_turn=3` (at most "started / progress / done" in one turn; the 4th+ suppressed `rate_limited`), `status_max_bytes=4096` (4 KiB — status lines are short prose, smaller than the artifact 256 KiB; oversize → suppressed `too_large`). The gate is wired INLINE in `_extract_and_execute_actions` immediately after the AD-927 artifact gate, reusing the in-scope `_RANK_ORDER_DM`. The bare opening tag of a rank-gated-out status is later removed by the pre-existing BF-203 hallucinated-tag cleanup — correct, the status path simply never ran. **MESSAGE-ONLY v1:** the work-item lifecycle transition (`in_progress → done`) is the forward marker **AD-928a** (verified: `grep work_item|current_task|transition` over `proactive.py` returns ONLY the two `task_id` refs inside the AD-927 resolver — there is no clean current-work-item handle in the proactive context). The UI status-chip render is the forward marker **AD-928b** (`ChatMessage` has no `metadata`/`kind` field + the thread-message load path drops `metadata` + the render is a heavy inline loop — not cheap; but the backend persists + surfaces the marker, so AD-928b is a pure presentational follow-up with no backend dependency). **Crew awareness:** a new ASCII-only `### Show Your Work` standing order in `federation.md`'s `## Communications` (between Group Chat and Notebook — it sits inside the AD-924 content test's ASCII slice, so it MUST stay ASCII) + a `## Showing Your Work` manual section in `config/manuals/group-chat.md`, both teaching narrate-meaningful-milestones-not-micro-steps + one `[STATUS final]` for the outcome + anti-flood. **Dormancy:** unlike AD-925's auto-create wiring, `[STATUS]` is NOT gated by `agentic_dispatch.orchestrator_enabled` and does NOT depend on `CrewTaskExecutor` — it fires whenever any Lieutenant+ agent that participates in a task room emits the tag, so the capability is live the moment a task room with participants exists. +13 pytest (`tests/test_ad928_show_your_work.py`, BF-287 — real `ChatThreadStore` behind a `MagicMock(spec=ProbOSRuntime)` shell). Focused gate 35 (13 AD-928 + 11 AD-927 + 11 AD-924); blast-radius `-k "proactive or status or thread or chat or group or artifact or standing or manual"` green. **COMMITTED LOCAL ONLY — NOT pushed** (the whole epic AD-925 → AD-929 is held for Captain review). Forward markers: **AD-928a** (work-item lifecycle), **AD-928b** (UI status chip).

**AD-929 shipped (2026-06-08, LOCAL ONLY — epic held for Captain review) — Unified workspace view: the Teams-channel "Files" rail (the surface that completes the "Task Workspace Rooms" epic AD-925 → AD-929).** AD-925/926/927 delivered the substrate (auto task room) and both file folders (Inputs, Outputs), but the pieces were **not assembled into one surface** — `InputsList` was mounted nowhere and the artifact Outputs (`ArtifactDrawer`) lived only in the `CompactApp` tray, absent from the full HXI where task/group rooms actually open. AD-929 binds Conversation + Files into one workspace view, frontend-only (all endpoints already exist — **no backend, no pytest, no new REST route, no new EventType, no store slice**). **Layout = a collapsible right-hand "Files" rail INSIDE `ProfileChatTab` (chosen over a tab bar).** `ProfileChatTab` is the **only mount common to both hosts** (`CompactApp` Yeo tray *and* `AgentProfilePanel` full HXI), so the rail travels with the conversation into both with one component and no host-specific layout surgery. The chat stays the **primary column** (Teams defaults a channel to its Conversation) with a right rail of two stacked sections — **INPUTS** (top) over **OUTPUTS** (bottom). A tab bar was rejected: it hides the conversation when viewing Files, and `AgentProfilePanel`'s existing tab bar is **per-agent** (a per-room "Files" tab there is a category error) and does nothing for `CompactApp` (no tab bar). **Gate = workspace room only.** A new pure `isWorkspaceRoom(thread, agents)` (in `ui/src/components/workspace/isWorkspaceRoom.ts`) returns `true` when `thread.task_id` is set (an AD-925 auto task room) **OR** it has **≥2 crew participants** (a group room; the verbatim `GroupChatHeader:36-40` crew filter — exclude the `"captain"` sentinel, resolve each id to its agent, keep only `isCrew`) — so a 1:1 DM shows **no rail**. Both `task_id` and `participants` are on the client store view `AD791aChatThreadView`, so the gate is a pure function over store data (no fetch). **Outputs = the lighter `ArtifactList`, NOT the full `ArtifactDrawer`.** The new `WorkspaceFilesRail.tsx` composes the **presentational** AD-797 `ArtifactList` fed by a self-contained `fetchThreadArtifacts(threadId)` call, so the full `ArtifactDrawer` is **never mounted twice** — it stays exactly where it is in `CompactApp` (untouched), and the rail owns its own fetch + local state with no coupling to the global `selectedArtifactId` / `artifactsByThread` slice. Outputs rows are read-only "open" actions: selecting an artifact opens `/api/artifacts/{id}/content` in a new tab (parity with `InputsList`'s open-the-bytes model); rich inline `ArtifactViewer` preview stays the standalone drawer's job (forward marker **AD-929a**). **Inputs = the AD-926 `InputsList`** fed by `fetchThreadInputs(threadId)`. Both fetches are Tier-2 honest-degrade to `[]` so a failed endpoint shows an empty section instead of crashing the rail. **Collapse persisted** under `localStorage probos.workspaceFiles.collapsed` (the `ArtifactDrawer` `'1'`/`'0'`/`null` load/persist idiom), but **default-collapsed on first run** (a thin 28px "FILES" rail with a count badge) — a divergence from `ArtifactDrawer`'s default-expanded, justified by the primary host (`AgentProfilePanel`) being a 420px floating panel where an expanded 300px rail would crowd the chat; the Captain expands on demand (the panel is resizable). **HXI #3 — local inline stroke-SVG glyphs** (`strokeWidth 1.5`, amber `#f0b060` / dim `#666680`, no emoji); `Glyphs.tsx`/`Glyphs.test.tsx` are untouched (the AD-917/919 export-count gotcha). `ProfileChatTab` mounts the rail behind the gate via three small edits (import + 3 selectors + wrap the outer return's column in a row with the rail as a `showWorkspaceFiles && activeThreadId` sibling); the chat column behavior is byte-identical for 1:1 DMs. **Not built (forward markers / non-goals):** AD-929a (inline `ArtifactViewer` in the rail), AD-929b (gate the standalone `CompactApp` `<ArtifactDrawer />` to non-workspace threads — the benign cosmetic double-Outputs in the tray), AD-929c (a per-room "Files" tab in the full-HXI panel), AD-928 (show-your-work status protocol, deferred for Captain review), AD-930 (presence), AD-926a (task-level upload). **Tests: +14 Vitest** across two new files — `isWorkspaceRoom.test.ts` (6: `task_id` room → true; ≥2-crew group → true; 1:1 DM → false; captain-only → false; non-crew → false; undefined → false) + `WorkspaceFilesRail.test.tsx` (8: Inputs section renders; Outputs section renders; `fetchThreadInputs`/`fetchThreadArtifacts` called with the threadId; Outputs row opens `/api/artifacts/{id}/content`; collapse persists `'1'` + `data-collapsed`; expand-from-`'0'` + default-collapsed-first-run; no-emoji guard) — real fixtures, `importOriginal` partial mocks of the api modules (BF-287). Full suite **1225 passed | 1 skipped (207 files)** = baseline (1211) + 14, 0 regressions; `npm run build` (tsc -b + vite) clean. **COMMITTED LOCAL ONLY — NOT pushed** (the whole epic is held for Captain review; HEAD now 5 ahead of origin/main = AD-924).

**AD-927 shipped (2026-06-08) — Outputs folder: agent-authored `[ARTIFACT]` into the task room (3rd AD, completing the "Task Workspace Rooms" foundation AD-925 → AD-927).** AD-925 auto-creates the task room and AD-926 surfaces its Input pane; AD-927 gives a crew agent the **write path** to deposit a finished deliverable into the room — its **Output folder**. A new module-level `_ARTIFACT_PATTERN` parses `[ARTIFACT name="…"]body[/ARTIFACT]` (mirroring `_GROUP_CHAT_PATTERN`; g1=name, g2=body, `DOTALL|IGNORECASE`), and a new async `ProactiveCognitiveLoop._extract_and_execute_artifacts` performs the **verified two-call write** (no phantom one-call helper): `hashlib.sha256(body).hexdigest()` → `await runtime.attachment_store.write(hash, blob, "text/markdown", origin="agent_artifact")` (the AD-797 dedicated, never-age-reaped origin) → `runtime.artifact_store.add_version(thread_id=room.id, …)` (SYNC, auto-versions `MAX+1`, BF-324 `BEGIN IMMEDIATE`), so the artifact surfaces in the **existing** AD-797 `ArtifactDrawer` when the room thread is active (`activeThreadId` → `GET /api/artifacts/thread/{id}`) — **no new UI, no new REST route, no new EventType**. **The binding crux (participation-based, honest v1):** a proactive turn carries no inherent thread, so a new `_resolve_agent_task_room(agent_id)` resolves the most-recently-active non-archived thread that has a `task_id` AND lists the agent as a participant (`chat_thread_store.list_threads(include_archived=False)` is already `last_active_at DESC`; the agent IS a participant of its AD-925 auto-room). No resolvable room → honest-degrade (suppressed, tag stripped, no crash, no artifact). A richer current-work-item binding is the forward marker **AD-927a**. **Rank gate + anti-flood (NEW `CommunicationsConfig` fields, all defaulted):** `artifact_min_rank="lieutenant"` (a work artifact is a normal deliverable — a lower bar than *convening* a room at Commander+), gated INLINE in `_extract_and_execute_actions` right after the AD-924 group-chat gate via the in-scope `_RANK_ORDER_DM`; `artifact_max_per_turn=3` and `artifact_max_bytes=262144` (256 KiB) are honest-degrade caps (oversize / per-turn-overflow → skip that one, never raise). v1 mime hardcoded `text/markdown`; binary artifacts keep their existing path (AttachmentStore upload + `POST /api/artifacts` with a pre-uploaded `content_hash`). **NOT built (epic scope):** no artifact UI (AD-797's drawer covers it), no binary tag in v1, no richer work-item binding (AD-927a), no status protocol (AD-928), no unified 3-pane (AD-929). **Tests:** `tests/test_ad927_outputs_folder.py` (+11, BF-287 — real `ArtifactStore` + real `FilesystemAttachmentStore` + real `ChatThreadStore` behind a `MagicMock(spec=ProbOSRuntime)` shell): a Lieutenant+ participant writes a versioned artifact to its room (action + `list_thread_latest` + bytes in `AttachmentStore` under the sha256 + tag stripped); a second `[ARTIFACT name="report"]` increments to v2 (supersedes chained); empty / oversize / per-turn-cap / no-task-room / store-unavailable / malformed all honest-degrade; an Ensign is rank-gated out; the resolver picks the most-recently-active *participating* task room (ignoring a more-recent room the agent is not in). Focused gate green (30 = 11 AD-927 + AD-925 + AD-924); blast-radius `-k "proactive or artifact or thread or chat or group or attachment"` 996 passed (1 pre-existing intermittent `test_ward_room.py::test_browse_threads_sort_recent` timestamp-collision flake, confirmed independent of AD-927 via stash + repeat-run). Files: `src/probos/proactive.py`, `src/probos/config.py`, NEW `tests/test_ad927_outputs_folder.py`. Local-only commit (the Captain reviews the epic before it goes public).

**AD-926 shipped (2026-06-08) — Inputs folder: a read-only Input pane for a task workspace room (2nd AD of the "Task Workspace Rooms" epic AD-925 → AD-929).** AD-925 gives a task fan-out ONE group chat with `chat_threads.task_id` set; AD-926 surfaces that room's **Input folder** — the files the crew use/process. The crux (VERIFIED): a `WorkItem` has NO native attachment slot, only a generic `metadata: dict`, so AD-926 returns an **honest union of two task-scoped sources, de-duplicated by `content_hash`**: (1) the **authoritative additive convention** `WorkItem.metadata["input_attachments"] = [{content_hash, mime, filename}]` (`source="task"`) — purely additive (no schema change, no migration; `to_dict` already serializes `metadata`), with **population deferred** to a future task-seed flow (forward marker **AD-926a**); and (2) the **real-today** AD-916 message attachments `msg.metadata["attachments"] = [{content_hash, mime}]` (`source="message"`), aggregated across the room's messages — so the endpoint returns live data the day it ships. A NEW read-only `GET /api/threads/{thread_id}/inputs` in `routers/threads.py` (private `_collect_task_inputs` helper + route) returns `{thread_id, task_id, inputs:[{content_hash, mime, filename, size, source}]}`; task-level wins on collision (then message arrival order). **Honest-degrade everywhere (Tier-2, never raises into the response):** a non-task thread (`task_id` unset) → `inputs: []`; missing thread → 404; `work_item_store=None` / a failed `get_work_item` → message attachments only; `size` is best-effort from the content-addressable `AttachmentStore` (`FilesystemAttachmentStore.size`, async) with a missing blob or absent store degrading to `size=None`. Bytes reuse the existing `GET /api/chat/attachments/{content_hash}` — **no new blob store, no new content endpoint**. UI: a self-contained NEW `ui/src/components/inputs/{inputsApi.ts, InputsList.tsx}` drop-in mirroring the artifacts pane (stroke-SVG icons, **no emoji** per HXI #3, read-only download rows) — **mounting into the room / 3-pane is AD-929, NOT wired here**.
