Frozen `@dataclass DelegationDecision` carries `spec_id`, `chief_agent_id: str | None`, `worker_agent_id: str | None`, `order_id: str | None`, `delegated: bool`, `reason`. `CrewDelegator.__init__(*, ontology, order_manager, agent_registry)` (keyword-only DI); `delegate(decision) -> DelegationDecision`. **Algorithm:** resolve the worker's billet via `agent_registry.get(id).agent_type` → `ontology.get_post_for_agent`; walk `ontology.get_chain_of_command(worker_post.id)` for the first in-department superior whose `authority_over` contains `worker_post.id` (the **chief**); resolve the chief's live `agent_id` via `ontology.get_agents_for_post` (first non-`None`); then `order_manager.issue_order(from_agent_id=chief, to_post_id=worker_post.id, directive=<capability|spec_id>)` — a non-`None` `Order` → `delegated=True`, `order_id=order.id`, reason `delegated_via_chief`; a `None` (governed `authority_over` rejection) → reason `out_of_chain`, `delegated=False`. **Honest-degrade (always direct, worker keeps the work):** worker is itself a leader with no in-department superior → `self_assigned`; no chief / chief unwired (`agent_id is None`) / `order_manager is None` → `direct_no_chief`; `decision.agent_id is None` upstream → `unresolved` (no order attempt). `worker_agent_id` is always carried (it's what lands in `WorkItem.assigned_to` in AD-867); `chief_agent_id`/`order_id` are provenance. The whole `delegate` body is Tier-2 log-and-degrade (`exc_info=True`) — a delegation failure never propagates into the dispatch path. Verified-against-HEAD shapes baked in: `Post.id` (not `post_id`), `order.id` (not `order_id`), keyword-only `issue_order` returning `None` on out-of-chain, `Assignment.agent_id` nullable. +11 pytest (`tests/test_ad865_crew_delegation.py`) against **real** `VesselOntologyService` (loaded from a copy of `config/ontology`), **real** `OrderManager`, **real** `AgentRegistry` — no MagicMock at the substrate boundary per BF-287. Wiring into the runtime orchestrator (AD-867) intentionally deferred. One commit, no push.

**AD-864 shipped (2026-06-03; Wave 211).** Crew-assignment resolver for the chain-of-command collaboration epic (AD-863 chain; closes [#834](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/834)). New module `src/probos/cognitive/crew_assignment.py` maps a sub-task's `capability`/`department` hints (the fields AD-863 added to `WorkItemSpec`) to a single qualified agent — a **pure decision**, no dispatch, no WorkItem mutation, no LLM call. Frozen `@dataclass AssignmentDecision` carries `spec_id`, `agent_id: str | None`, `department`, `capability`, `score`, `reason`. `CrewAssignmentResolver.__init__(*, capability_registry, ontology, trust_network, agent_registry)` (keyword-only DI); `resolve(spec) -> AssignmentDecision` and `resolve_all(specs) -> list[...]`. **Resolution order:** capability hint → `capability_registry.query(capability, trust_scores=trust_network.all_scores())` (trust-weighted match scores), filter to **alive** agents (`agent_registry.get(id) is not None`); when a department is also set, prefer in-department matches (reason `capability_match`) and fall back to the top alive match when the department has no capable agent (reason `capability_match_dept_unavailable`); department-only hint → highest-trust agent whose `ontology.get_agent_department(agent_type)` matches, deterministic tie-break by `(-trust, agent_id)` (reason `department_only`); neither hint, no candidate, or any collaborator error → `agent_id=None`, `score=0.0`, reason `unresolved_no_candidate`. **Honest-degrade:** the whole `resolve` body is wrapped — any exception logs Tier-2 (`exc_info=True`) and returns the unresolved decision, never raises (the resolver runs inside the dispatch path). +11 pytest (`tests/test_ad864_crew_assignment.py`) against **real** `AgentRegistry`/`VesselOntologyService`/`TrustNetwork`/`CapabilityRegistry` fixtures — no MagicMock at the substrate boundary per BF-287. Wiring into the dispatcher (AD-867) and delegation (AD-865) intentionally deferred. One commit, no push.

**AD-853 shipped (2026-06-03; Wave 210).** First unit of the Crew Self-Unblock epic (AD-854..857 deferred — acquire-vs-build triage, BLOCKED→resume loop driver, AgenticLoop bridge, HXI/chat decision surface). New top-level module `src/probos/capability_request.py` gives the Captain a **single approval queue** spanning all three capability-need kinds (tool grant / extension install / self-mod build) instead of three divergent surfaces. `@dataclass CapabilityRequest` carries `id` (uuid4), `agent_id` (stable AgentID trust key), `kind: Literal["grant","install","build"]`, `target`, `rationale` (truncated to 280 per AD-718a), `work_item_id`, `status: Literal["pending","approved","denied","fulfilled","failed"]`, and the `created_at`/`decided_at`/`decided_by`/`decision_reason` audit fields. `CapabilityRequestStore(EventEmitterMixin)` mirrors the verified `ClearanceGrantStore` DB+cache shape verbatim — cloud-ready `ConnectionFactory` Protocol (falls back to `sqlite_factory.default_factory`, never direct `aiosqlite`), `async start()` with WAL/`busy_timeout`/`synchronous=NORMAL` PRAGMAs + `executescript(_SCHEMA)` (the `capability_requests` table + `idx_caprequests_status`/`idx_caprequests_agent`), and `async file_request` / `decide` / `list_pending` / `get` / `_refresh_cache`. **Learning loop:** `decide()` records the outcome against the per-agent trust ledger via the verified-sync keyword signature `trust.record_outcome(agent_id=…, success=approve, weight=1.0, intent_type="capability_request", source="capability_request")` (not awaited, Tier-2 log-and-degrade) — a denied request is a negative trust signal, an approval is provenance — while `decided_by` captures the Captain approver (dual attribution). **One legitimate divergence** from the silent sibling: the store emits 3 new `EventType` members (`CAPABILITY_REQUEST_FILED`/`DECIDED`/`FULFILLED`) via an `emit_event` callback through `EventEmitterMixin`. **Wiring** routes through the real `CommunicationResult` pattern: constructed in `startup/communication.py` after the `ClearanceGrantStore` block (no config gate, `db_path = data_dir/capability_requests.db`, `emit_event=emit_event_fn`), surfaced as a `capability_request_store` field on `startup/results.py`, and assigned `self.capability_request_store = comm.capability_request_store` in `runtime.py` (4 edits: TYPE_CHECKING import, class annotation, `__init__` None, assignment). `trust_network` is not in startup scope so it is left `None` at startup (late-bound in a future AD; the hook degrades cleanly when absent). Additive only — existing vision/tool/extension proposal paths untouched, migrate onto this store in AD-854. +8 pytest (`tests/test_ad853_capability_request.py`, real store fixture + real `TrustNetwork`, no MagicMock at the storage boundary per BF-287). One commit, no push.

**BF-599 shipped (2026-06-03; Wave 209).** Yeo (YeomanAgent, AD-766, the conversational front-door) stopped confabulating "I can't browse the web" in 1:1 DMs despite the ship running live `web_search` / `page_reader` / `http` pools — a functional capability-grounding gap. Root cause: the conversational DM prompt is composed with `hardcoded_instructions=""`, so Yeo's static role rules never reached the DM turn and the LLM filled the void with a plausible-but-false limitation. **Fix (D1 — capability grounding only):** a new overridable base hook `CognitiveAgent._conversational_capability_block(observation) -> str` (default `""`, so every other agent is byte-unaffected) is appended to the composed conversational instructions for EVERY reply path (1:1, ward room, proactive) in `_decide_via_llm`'s `is_conversation` branch. `YeomanAgent` overrides it to render a POSITIVE block from the **live** registry — for each of pools `web_search` / `page_reader` / `http` that currently has ≥1 registered agent, it lists the delegable intent (`web_search` / `read_page` / `http_fetch`) and tells Yeo to delegate web research/page-reading through the mesh (Science → @Number One) rather than declining. Honest-degrade: no runtime, no `registry`, a registry read that raises, or none of the three pools present → returns `""` (no block, no confabulation-bait). The block text is deliberately authored to contain NONE of the decomposer `_CAPABILITY_GAP_RE` tokens (asserted by test). Web access stays mesh-delegated — no `httpx`/`requests` import added to Yeo (asserted by source-scan test). +6 pytest (`tests/test_bf599_yeo_web_research_delegation.py`, real `AgentRegistry` fixture per the no-MagicMock-at-substrate-boundary rule); `tests/test_yeoman_agent.py` (25) + `test_cognitive_agent.py` / `test_cognitive_agent_dm_game.py` / `test_bf010_conversational_prompt.py` (70) green. **Deferred to BF-599b:** D2 auto-delegation (AD-840) — Yeo auto-routing a web-research request to the right specialist without the Captain re-asking.

**BF-598 + BF-600 shipped (2026-06-02; Wave 208).** Recurring AD-820 boot-refusal fix plus a sweep of the pre-existing test-suite failures. **BF-598** (commit `b3e20866`) made `startup/shutdown.py::shutdown()` idempotent — a new `runtime._shutdown_started` guard at the very top (init `False` in `runtime.py.__init__`, set before the BF-135 session write, getattr-defaulted for transitional procs) early-returns on re-entry so a Phase-2 teardown failure / `__main__` 5s timeout can no longer trigger a second `shutdown()` that overwrites the clean AD-820 marker with a `partial`/`skipped` one; plus a non-regressive marker write at the AD-820 site (only `"skipped"` is non-regressive — nothing torn, HNSW safe — so a pre-existing clean/rebuilt marker is preserved; `partial`/`failed` still block as torn-index risks). +9 tests (`test_bf598_shutdown_idempotency.py`); the guard returned truthy on MagicMock doubles so `test_bf296` got `runtime._shutdown_started = False` added to its 3 doubles. **BF-600** (`episodic_health.py::validate_hnsw_files`) fixed a structural-probe false positive: BF-298's torn-write invariant `length.bin entries == cur_element_count` does NOT hold on chromadb 1.5.x, where a fresh/small `PersistentClient` store leaves `header.cur_element_count=0` while `length.bin` is written at allocation capacity (default 100) — so a healthy 1-element store (and the real `data/node-1` dir) was being hard-rejected with `exit=5` BEFORE chroma even opened. The check is now gated on `cur_element_count > 0`; a genuine torn write (non-zero cur mismatching length, e.g. BF-298's 70779 vs 70966) is still flagged, and a `cur=0` store now flows past the file pre-check into the real chroma open + `peek(1)` + `count()` deep check (strictly safer than the blind reject). +2 regression tests in `test_bf298_hnsw_validation_uses_actual_count.py` (cur=0 passes; non-zero torn still fails). This unblocked the 4 episodic health/backup fixtures (`test_ad822`/`test_ad823`) and, with the 8 stale-MagicMock thread-id doubles (`test_ad732_vision_tier` ×2 + `test_agents_router_game_move` ×6 opting out of AD-791a explicit-thread-id validation via `runtime.chat_thread_store = None`), cleared all 12 pre-existing failures. Re-verify of the 34 reported-failing files from the correct rootdir: **609 passed, 0 failed** (the full-gate's 98 "failures" were a wrong-`rootdir` CWD artifact from launching pytest outside `d:\ProbOS`, not regressions).

**AD-818a-2 shipped (2026-06-02; Wave 207).** Paginated the two remaining read-streaming episodic migrations ([#751](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/751) #2 OOM / #3 cancellability), continuing AD-818a. **AD-605** `migrate_enriched_embedding` went from a SYNC `def` run via `loop.run_in_executor` (which `asyncio.wait_for` can never cancel — the boot timeout fired but the executor thread ran on) to an `async def` that streams via `_iter_collection_pages` with one batched `await asyncio.to_thread(collection.update, …)` per page; the `enriched_embedding_version >= 1` short-circuit reads `collection.metadata` once before the loop, the version marker is set after the loop (idempotent, covers the empty-collection path), the `safe_meta` hnsw-key filter is preserved, and its call site became a direct `await` (the now-unused `_loop` removed). **BF-207** `sweep_hash_integrity` went from a full-collection load + global timestamp sort (O(N) memory) to a bounded size-`max_episodes` (200) **min-heap** keyed by `(timestamp, seq)` (`seq` a monotonic int tiebreaker so comparisons never reach the unorderable dict); on overflow `heappop` evicts the oldest, and the heap is **drained in descending `(timestamp, seq)` order** (REQUIRED — `test_sweep_batches_multiple_mismatches` asserts `update` ids newest-first `["ep-c","ep-b","ep-a"]`) into a single batched `update` — O(200) memory, O(N) time, cancellable between pages, retaining the "heal the 200 most-recent" semantics. BF-207's internal `try/except` + logging were **dropped** so the now-wrapping `_run_one_migration` owns honest-degrade (Architect caught the misreport: a retained internal swallow would let a real sweep failure return `healed=0` and be logged as a clean "0 mismatches" noop); its call site is wrapped WITHOUT `schema_store`/`migration_id`/`version_hash` (BF-207 is unversioned, runs every boot, gated by `config.memory.verify_content_hash`) and stays LAST. Five obsolete-contract tests updated (4 AD-605 sync→async, 1 BF-207 exception now propagates); new `tests/test_ad818a2_paginated_migrations.py` (14 tests, real in-memory chroma, monkeypatched `_MIGRATION_BATCH_SIZE`, deterministic cancellability + heap-newest + descending-order pin). **133 passed** across the new file + equivalence + AD-818a sibling suites. Commit `56d91cc8`. Remaining #751 work: **AD-818a-3** (AD-584 `migrate_embedding_model` temp-collection swap + rename — distinct write-path rearchitecture), **AD-818b** (`probos migrate apply` CLI), **AD-818c** (refuse-to-start version-mismatch gate). #751 stays OPEN. *(Also Wave 206 hygiene **BF-597**: realigned 4 YEO conformance gates after the `prompts/`→`prompts/archive/` move (383dde25) + 2 BF-207 shutdown tests after AD-820 made the dream-cycle timeout configurable; test-only, commit `82931868`.)*

**AD-818a shipped (2026-06-02; Wave 206).** Paginated the three simplest deferred episodic migrations ([#751](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/751) #2/#3). New shared async helper `episodic.py::_iter_collection_pages(collection, include, page_size=None)` streams a ChromaDB collection page-by-page via `await asyncio.to_thread(collection.get, include=, limit=effective, offset=offset)`, terminating when a page yields zero ids or fewer than the page size; `page_size` defaults to module-global `_MIGRATION_BATCH_SIZE` (2000) read at call time so tests can monkeypatch it small to force multi-page. `BF-103` (episode-id mismatch), `AD-570` (anchor index), `AD-570b` (participant index) were converted from full-load loops to `async for page in _iter_collection_pages(...)` with per-page batched writes through `to_thread`, giving genuine page-granular cancellability under the boot `wait_for`. Deferred to AD-818a-2/-3: AD-584, AD-605, BF-207. Commit `4cc722d1`.

**AD-818 v1 shipped (2026-06-02; Wave 205).** Schema-version sidecar + boot-scan short-circuit for episodic-memory migrations ([#751](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/751)). The boot path re-ran every one-shot migration (`BF-103`, `AD-570`, `AD-570b`, `AD-584`, `AD-605`) on *every* start, each scanning the full ChromaDB collection even when nothing changed — O(N) per migration per boot. **New module** `src/probos/cognitive/schema_versions.py`: `MIGRATION_VERSIONS: dict[str, str]` (exactly the 5 versioned ids → `"1"`; `BF-207` hash heal-sweep intentionally ABSENT — it must keep running every boot) with a ⚠️ BUMP CONTRACT comment (bump a migration's value when its output shape changes or stores will skip the corrected migration), and `SchemaVersionStore` — an aiosqlite sidecar (`schema_versions` table: `migration_id` PK, `applied_at` REAL, `episode_count` INTEGER, `version_hash` TEXT) mirroring the AD-570b `ParticipantIndex` Cloud-Ready-Storage pattern (`connection_factory` Protocol, aiosqlite fallback only in `start()`). Methods: `async start/stop`, `async get(id) -> dict | None` (Tier-2 log-and-degrade → None), `async record(id, *, episode_count, version_hash, applied_at=None)` (INSERT-OR-REPLACE, auto-stamps `time.time()`, swallows write faults), `async is_current(id, version_hash) -> bool` (False on no row / hash mismatch). **Short-circuit** in `startup/cognitive_services.py::_run_one_migration` — three optional kwargs (`schema_store`, `migration_id`, `version_hash`); when all set and `is_current` matches, the migration's coro is NEVER constructed (skip the scan, log + return); on **clean success only** it records the version as the FINAL statement INSIDE the `try` (R1 — placing it after the try/except would reference `migrated`, unbound on timeout → `UnboundLocalError` boot crash, and would falsely mark a timed-out migration current). Timeout / exception clauses unchanged (both still honest-degrade, record nothing → retry next boot). A guarded build block (after the `_skip_migrations` guard, before the BF-103 block) constructs the store only when `episodic_memory and not _skip_migrations and config.memory.schema_version_tracking`; a build/start failure leaves `schema_store=None` so every migration runs unversioned exactly as before. The 5 versioned call sites thread `MIGRATION_VERSIONS[id]`; `BF-207` (`sweep_hash_integrity`) deliberately does NOT. Wired through `CognitiveServicesResult.schema_version_store` → `runtime.schema_version_store` → guarded `stop()` teardown in `startup/shutdown.py` (R2 — the store has no owner; left unstopped its WAL connection holds `.db-wal`/`-shm` locks, a Windows test-isolation hazard). **Config:** new `MemoryConfig.schema_version_tracking: bool = False` — opt-in for one release of bake time; a grandchild AD flips it True. Phased: AD-818a (pagination), AD-818b (`probos migrate apply` CLI), AD-818c (refuse-to-start gating) are deferred. +16 pytest (`tests/test_ad818_schema_versions.py` — real in-memory sqlite at the DB boundary, no MagicMock per the Phantom-via-MagicMock rule); no migration BODY changed; no ui/ changes.

**AD-833 v1 shipped (2026-06-02; Wave 204).** Improvement-proposal grounding gate — an advisory, non-blocking verifier that checks a `CapabilityProposal` against the live codebase *before* it reaches crew review, so phantom-symbol proposals surface evidence instead of slipping through. **New module** `src/probos/cognitive/self_improvement/grounding.py`: frozen `GroundingFinding(provider_name, verified: bool | None, score, evidence)` and `ProposalGroundingResult(score, verified, findings, confidence)`; a `@runtime_checkable GroundingProvider` Protocol (`name: str`, `async check(proposal) -> GroundingFinding`); `SymbolExistenceProvider` (extracts symbol-like tokens from `summary + fit_assessment` via a tokenizer that only keeps `_`/`.`/interior-capital tokens, resolves each through `CodebaseIndex.query` → `find_callers` → `get_full_api_surface` in try/except, `verified=None` when no symbols, else `score = resolved/total` and `verified` is False if any token is UNRESOLVED with per-token evidence); and `ProposalGroundingVerifier(providers)` that runs each provider with per-provider try/except log-and-degrade (a raising provider is skipped, never fatal), returns the empty-aggregate default `(score=1.0, verified=True, confidence=0.0)` when no findings, else `score = mean`, `verified = score >= 0.5 AND no finding.verified is False`, `confidence = determined/total`. Mirrors the AD-734 `ObservableStateVerifier` provider pattern. **ProposalStore** (`proposal.py`) gained `attach_grounding(id, result)` (unknown id → `logger.warning` + return, never raises) and `get_grounding(id) -> ProposalGroundingResult | None`; `submit` is byte-unchanged (sync, signature-stable). **ApprovalGate** (`approval_gate.py`) gained an optional ctor `grounding_verifier` param, `list_pending_grounded() -> list[tuple[CapabilityProposal, ProposalGroundingResult | None]]`, and `async enqueue_grounded(proposal)` (submits first, then verify+attach under try/except — grounding failure leaves the submit standing). `list_pending` / `enqueue` unchanged. **Wiring:** `startup/finalize.py` builds the verifier over `runtime.codebase_index` (absent → `providers=[]` log-and-degrade, never crashes finalize) and injects it into `ApprovalGate`; `runtime.proposal_grounding_verifier` exposed. Grounding is **advisory only — it never vetoes a proposal**. v1 is `SymbolExistenceProvider` only; `TrackerCrossRefProvider` (AD-833a) and `BenignTelemetryProvider` (AD-833b) are deferred. +18 pytest (`tests/test_ad833_grounding_gate.py`, real `_FakeCodebaseIndex` stub — no MagicMock at the index boundary). No UI change.

**AD-838c shipped (2026-06-01; Wave 203).** Dynamic dependency install for the runtime task path — Copilot-style ask-before-install. `DependencyResolver` (`src/probos/cognitive/dependency_resolver.py`) gained a `policy` (`"whitelist"` | `"prompt_unlisted"`), an `install_fn` / `approval_fn` callback pair, a `deny_imports` hard block, and an `IMPORT_TO_PACKAGE` map (`bs4`→`beautifulsoup4`, `yaml`→`pyyaml`, `dateutil`→`python-dateutil`); `detect_missing` surfaces unlisted imports only under `prompt_unlisted`, never names in `deny_imports`, and `resolve` verifies each install via `importlib.util.find_spec` post-install (failures land in `failed`, not `installed`). **Config:** new `DependencyConfig` (`dynamic_install_enabled: bool = False`, `dynamic_install_policy: Literal = "prompt_unlisted"`, `dynamic_install_deny: list[str] = []`) wired into `SystemConfig.dependency`; disabled by default, zero-config boot unchanged. **Wiring:** `startup/cognitive_services.py` builds a SINGLE shared `DependencyResolver` (when `self_mod.enabled` *or* `dependency.dynamic_install_enabled`) and passes the same instance to both the self-mod pipeline and the new `CognitiveServicesResult.dependency_resolver` field; `runtime.py` exposes `self.dependency_resolver` and a new `async def ensure_dependency(import_name: str | list[str]) -> DependencyResult` task-path API; the shell wires the interactive `approval_fn` (`user_dep_install_approval`). **Defense in depth:** `ensure_dependency` hard-declines unlisted packages with `success=False` + `declined=[...]` when no approval callback is wired (never silent install), auto-approves the `self_mod.allowed_imports` whitelist tier, and logs every step to the event log (`category="dependency"`: `dependency_check`, `dependency_install_approved`, `dependency_install_success`, `dependency_install_declined`, `dependency_install_failed`). +9 pytest (`tests/test_ad838c_dynamic_install.py`); `tests/test_dependency_resolver.py` + `tests/test_config.py` (38) and `tests/test_self_mod.py` + AD-838 wiring (78) green. No new package-manager backend, no auto un-prompted installs, no CodeValidator/forbidden-pattern changes. Zero new deps.

**AD-838 shipped (2026-06-01; Wave 203).** Office create intents wired end-to-end — the `DocxAgent` / `PptxAgent` / `XlsxAgent` skill agents (`src/probos/skill_framework.py`) now declare `intent_descriptors` and dispatch through `handle_intent`, so the mesh can route document/deck/spreadsheet creation to them instead of the methods being callable only by direct Python call. **Intents + consensus:** `docx_summarize` (`requires_consensus=False`), `docx_create` / `docx_revise` (`True`); `pptx_summarize` (`False`), `pptx_create` (`True`); `xlsx_read_range` (`False`), `xlsx_update` (`True`) — write intents are consensus-gated per the destructive-op rule, reads are not. `handle_intent` validates required params at the boundary (missing `title` / `file_path` / `sheet` / `range` / `instructions` / `updates` → `IntentResult(success=False, error=...)`, never a raw raise) and returns `None` for intents the agent doesn't own (clean self-deselect). **Output-path resolution:** new module helpers `_slugify`, `_parse_json_list`, `_resolve_default_output_path` (pure, no import-time side effects) resolve a default save path from `runtime.config.office_skills.output_dir` when the caller omits `output_path`, slugifying the title (`"My Big Report"` → `my-big-report.docx`); an explicit `output_path` is honored exactly, including nested dirs. `create_docx` / `create_pptx` gained an optional `output_path: str | None = None` param. **NL content synthesis:** `docx_create` / `pptx_create` accept a natural-language brief and synthesize structured content when explicit content isn't supplied — `_synthesize_paragraphs` (docx) and `_synthesize_slides` (pptx, JSON `{"title","bullets"}` array) are await-aware (`hasattr(maybe, "__await__")`) and honest-degrade to a minimal single-paragraph / single-slide fallback when no LLM client is wired. **Config:** `OfficeSkillsConfig.output_dir: str = "~/.probos/output"` + an `_expand_output_dir` field-validator (mirrors `_expand_template_dir`); zero-config boot unchanged (`SystemConfig()` constructs identically). +10 pytest (`tests/test_ad838_office_create_wiring.py` — dispatch, consensus flags, output-path honoring + slugified default, NL synthesis, honest-degrade single-slide deck, missing-title error); `tests/test_office_agents.py` (AD-755, 6) unchanged and green. AD-838 blast-radius gate (18 skill_framework/office test files) 408 passed, 1 pre-existing unrelated failure (`test_captain_invariant_exposure_gate` — missing prompt file on disk, identical at clean HEAD). Zero new deps.

**AD-839 + BF-331 + BF-332 shipped (2026-06-01; Wave 202).** Fixed three live bugs the operator hit testing the AD-834 NL task-dispatch feature: a dispatched task showed "Unassigned" on the Kanban board, the card couldn't be opened, and the assigned agent (Ezri) had no awareness of the task. **BF-331 (backend root cause):** `ProbOSRuntime._register_workforce_resources` ran in the communication startup phase *before* `self.work_item_store` was assigned from `init_communication`'s return, so its `if not self.work_item_store: return` guard fired and **zero** BookableResources registered (`GET /api/resources` → 0). The method now takes an optional `work_item_store` param resolved as `store = work_item_store or self.work_item_store`; `startup/communication.py` passes the freshly-created store explicitly, and zero-argument call sites keep working via the attribute fallback. +3 pytest (`tests/test_bf331_workforce_resource_registration.py`, real ProbOSRuntime instances + fake stores per the no-MagicMock-at-substrate-boundary rule); `tests/test_workforce.py` (114) green. **BF-332 (frontend):** `WorkBoard.tsx` gained a `resolveAssignee` resolver that falls back to the `agents` map (keyed by UUID) when no bookable resource matches — so cards show the callsign instead of "Unassigned" even with an empty resource registry — plus a click-to-open work-item detail modal (`onClick`/`role=button`/`tabIndex`/Enter-Space, click-backdrop close, stroke-SVG `Close` glyph per HXI #3) rendering title/badges/assignee/description/steps/tags from the already-loaded `WorkItemView` (no fetch). +5 vitest (`ui/src/__tests__/WorkBoard.bf332.test.tsx`); `npx vitest run` + `npm run build` both green per the BF-279 dual gate. **AD-839 (agent awareness):** a `work_item_dispatched` IntentMessage targeted at this agent previously matched neither `is_direct` nor `_handled_intents`, hit the self-deselect fast path in `CognitiveAgent.handle_intent`, and returned None — silently dropped. A new branch BEFORE the fast path routes `work_item_dispatched` + `target_agent_id == self.id` to `_handle_work_item_dispatch`, which composes a Captain-style task message, logs it to the agent's DM thread, runs it through the normal direct-message lifecycle for an acknowledgment, appends the reply to the thread, and transitions the item to `in_progress` via `transition_work_item(..., source=self.id)`. All side effects tier-2 log-and-degrade (dispatch never raises into the cognitive queue); direct-assignment-only (broadcast untouched). +5 pytest (`tests/test_ad839_work_item_dispatch.py`). Verify all changes comply with the Engineering Principles in `.github/copilot-instructions.md`.

**AD-746a shipped (2026-05-31; Wave 202f).** Router-side latest-frame mirror for FORCE DESCRIBE — defense-in-depth filed during the BF-323 retrospective (closes [#794](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/794)). `VisionConsumer.force_describe_current_frame` reads `_latest_frame_by_session` / `_latest_frame_global`, which were only populated by `consumer._handle` when the VisionAggregator FORWARDS a frame — so Captain-initiated FORCE DESCRIBE was transitively dependent on the aggregator buffer/timer state machine being healthy (when it deadlocked in BF-323 the cache stayed empty and FORCE DESCRIBE silently returned `None`, no vision LLM call). **Fix:** added a PUBLIC second writer `VisionConsumer.record_uploaded_frame(self, sha, session_id, captured_at) -> None` (`consumer.py`) that mirrors the `_handle` write — `_latest_frame_by_session[session_id] = (sha, captured_at)` when session_id, plus `_latest_frame_global = (sha, captured_at)` — no-op on empty `sha`, idempotent with `_handle` (`_handle` itself unchanged). The upload endpoint `upload_camera_frame` (`routers/perception.py`) calls it via `getattr(runtime, "vision_consumer", None)` immediately BEFORE `intent_bus.broadcast(msg)`, guarded with a Tier-1 swallow + `logger.debug` so a mirror failure can never break frame admission (broadcast call, params dict, and status-code paths all unchanged). This gives FORCE DESCRIBE a direct path independent of the bus/aggregator chain. Router never touches `_latest_frame_*` directly — only the public method (Law of Demeter / Open-Closed). +3 pytest (`tests/test_ad746a_force_describe_mirror.py` — both caches populated; force-describe resolves the mirrored SHA with `_process` stubbed and NO `_handle` call; empty-sha no-op). Scoped force_describe/perception/vision_consumer regression 94 passed.

**AD-836 shipped (2026-05-31; Wave 202).** Offline agent-behavior evaluation harness — ProbOS-Bench v1, decomposer scope (closes [#810](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/810)). Tests/tooling only; **zero production-code changes, no live-model calls**. Motivated by the 2026-05-15 VS Code "Coding Harness" blog: the harness's reliability is attributed to a rigorous offline eval loop (VSC-Bench) that scores every change against a fixed task set *before* it ships — ProbOS had the raw materials (the AD-716 opt-in JSON-scoreline pattern, hand-authored `tests/fixtures/eval/decomposer_cases.json`) but no runner that loads them and emits an aggregate score. **Section 1** — `tests/benchmarks/probos_bench.py`: pure, import-side-effect-free scoring functions with full type annotations — `load_cases(path) -> list[dict]`, `score_case(case, produced_intents) -> {resolved, precision, n_intents}` honoring the three case shapes (exact `expected_intents` subset match; `min_intents` effort floor; conversational `expected_intents == []` → expect no intents), and `aggregate(results, total_tokens) -> {resolution_rate, intent_precision, mean_intents, total_tokens}` (empty list → zeroed rates). **Section 2** — `tests/benchmarks/test_probos_bench_decomposer.py`: opt-in runner copying the AD-716 module-level `skipif` guard exactly, but env-gated on `PROBOS_BENCHMARK=1` (skipped by default). Builds a real `IntentDecomposer(llm_client=MockLLMClient(), working_memory=WorkingMemoryManager())` — deterministic mock, **no live model** — runs every fixture case through `decompose(...)`, scores via `probos_bench`, prints a single `PROBOS_BENCH` json scoreline, and asserts the scorecard is well-formed (so CI exercises the path even when the body is opt-in). `total_tokens` is `0` (mock reports no usage). **Section 3** — `tests/test_ad836_probos_bench.py`: 5 always-on self-tests (normal gate, not under `benchmarks/`) covering exact-match happy path, `min_intents` satisfied/unsatisfied, conversational empty/non-empty produced, empty-produced edge, and `aggregate` over a mixed list (+ empty-list boundary). +5 pytest self-tests (all green); the opt-in runner is skipped by default and prints a directional `resolution_rate=0.83 / intent_precision=0.83 / mean_intents=0.61` over 18 cases when run with `PROBOS_BENCHMARK=1`. Seams: **AD-836b** (full `AgenticLoop` trajectory/tool-call scoring via `score_trajectory(AgenticResult)` mirroring `score_case`), **AD-836c** (wire the harness as a required pre-merge gate). No new dependencies; directional, not publishable (same disclaimer as AD-716 LoCoMo).

**AD-835 shipped (2026-05-31; Wave 202).** Per-tier harness adaptation hook — a config-driven, per-tier `system_prompt_suffix` appended at the single system-message composition point in `_call_openai` (closes [#809](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/809)). Motivated by the 2026-05-15 VS Code "Coding Harness" blog: per-model adaptation (a distinct system prompt per checkpoint) is the harness's highest-leverage differentiator, and ProbOS's tiered client had the seam but no hook. **Config** (`config.py`): four optional flat fields `llm_system_prompt_suffix_{fast,standard,deep,vision}: str | None = None` (default `None` = no-op; zero-config boot byte-identical), surfaced through `tier_config(tier)` as the dict key `"system_prompt_suffix"` via a fast/standard/deep/vision `suffix_map`. **Threading** (`llm_client.py`): `_complete_inner` resolves `effective_system_suffix = tc.get("system_prompt_suffix")` for the **attempt** tier (a fallback to `standard` uses `standard`'s suffix, not the originally-requested tier's) and threads it through `_call_api` → `_call_openai` alongside the existing `effective_*` sampling params — never read from global state inside `_call_openai`. **Apply block** runs after the prompt-synthesis and pre-built-messages branches converge, before payload assembly: appends the suffix to the **system message only** (`str` content → `f"{base}\n\n{suffix}"`; list content → append a `{type:text,text:suffix}` part; no system message → insert one at index 0), replacing `messages[0]` via spread (never mutating the caller's dict). User/tool messages are never touched; empty/`None` suffix → byte-identical to pre-AD-835. The ollama-native path (`_call_ollama_native`) is intentionally out of scope (openai-format only). Seam documented above `_call_openai` for **AD-835b** (per-model tool-format remap — must hook the same `tc`-threaded path, not a new global). No change to `AgenticLoop`, `ToolExecutor`, the fallback chain (`_TIER_ORDER`/`_LLM_TIERS`), `api_format` routing, attachment-ref resolution, or ModelRouter (AD-463). +6 pytest (`tests/test_ad835_tier_adaptation.py`); scoped regression `1028 passed, 2 skipped` (the 2 `test_ad732_vision_tier` failures are pre-existing AD-791a MagicMock-`thread_id` failures, confirmed identical with AD-835 changes stashed — not an AD-835 regression). Forward marker: AD-835b (tool-format remap). CLOSED.

**AD-834 shipped (2026-05-31; Wave 202).** NL task creation + dispatch toggle in the HXI Work Tab — activates the already-wired WorkItem dispatch engine from the frontend (closes [#808](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/808)). Frontend-only: the "Create Task" form in `ProfileWorkTab.tsx` now carries a natural-language instructions `<textarea>` (forwarded as `description`) and a "Dispatch to agent now" checkbox (default ON) that emits `metadata: { dispatchable: true }`; `createWorkItem` in `useStore.ts` widened its type to pass `description?` + `metadata?` straight through to the existing `POST /api/work-items` body. No backend change — `create_work_item(**body)` already accepts both fields and `WorkItemRouter.is_dispatchable()` already gates on `metadata.dispatchable`, routing the task (with its NL description) to the assigned agent. Toggle OFF omits the flag (draft, not dispatched). The Enter-to-submit handler is removed from the title input (the textarea is now the primary input; submit via the Create button). +3 vitest (`ui/src/components/profile/ProfileWorkTab.create.test.tsx` — renders textarea+toggle, dispatch-ON payload carries description + `{dispatchable:true}`, dispatch-OFF omits metadata). Vitest 1038 → 1041 (+3); `npm run build` clean per BF-279 gate; zero new deps.

**AD-837a shipped (2026-05-31; Wave 201).** Ward Room three-pane reading layout — when the panel is opened up wide (maximized, or a floating window resized to ≥ 680px effective width) the channels experience expands from the narrow single-column stack into a Slack/Discord-style three-pane reading surface: channel rail (230px) | thread list (320px) | thread detail (flex), all visible at once. Below the threshold (docked 420px sidebar + narrow floating windows) the body stays byte-identical single-column (regression-safe). Frontend-only (no backend, no API, no store change) — a derived `isWide` flag (`effectiveWidth >= 680`, from `displayMode` + `windowRect.w`) selects the layout. In wide mode the header drops the per-thread back-arrow / `# channel` title (back to "WARD ROOM"), the Channels/DM-Log tabs stay visible (the rail is persistent), and the detail pane shows a stroke-SVG empty-state ("Select a thread to read the full discussion", no emoji per HXI #3) until a thread is selected. Reuses the existing self-contained `WardRoomChannelList` / `WardRoomThreadList` / `WardRoomThreadDetail` children unchanged (DRY) — only the parent body composition changes; DM views fall through to the existing narrow body. +7 vitest (`ui/src/components/wardroom/__tests__/WardRoomPanel.windowmode.test.tsx`, now 15 total — docked single-column, maximized three-pane, wide/narrow floating threshold, empty-state placeholder, rails-persist-on-select, DM-log excluded). Vitest 1031 → 1038 (+7); `npm run build` clean per BF-279 gate; zero new deps. Builds on AD-837. Forward markers: AD-837e (SHIP/SECTIONS channel grouping in the rail), AD-837f (per-pane width persistence / draggable splitters).

**AD-837 shipped (2026-05-31; Wave 201).** Ward Room detachable window mode — the 420px hard-docked left sidebar can now be undocked into a draggable, corner-resizable floating window and maximized to (near) full viewport, then re-docked, all from one-click header controls. Frontend-only (no backend, no API, no server-data-model change). Reuses the AgentProfilePanel drag/resize/persist convention (DRY): header `onMouseDown` captures a `dragOffset`, a `mousemove`/`mouseup` listener pair is added on drag-start and torn down on release (no always-on global listeners); the bottom-right corner handle resizes with the same min/clamp shape (min 360×320, max viewport-40). New store slice in `ui/src/store/useStore.ts` — exported `WardRoomDisplayMode = 'docked' | 'floating' | 'maximized'` + `WardRoomWindowRect {x,y,w,h}`, `loadWardRoomLayout()` (validates persisted mode + rect, falls back to `{x:80,y:80,w:720,h:640}` on malformed JSON / unavailable storage), and `setWardRoomDisplayMode` / `setWardRoomWindowRect` actions that write through to `localStorage` (`probos.wardroom.mode` / `probos.wardroom.rect`) with Tier-2 log-and-degrade. The docked branch is byte-equivalent to the pre-AD-837 sidebar (slide-in transform + `pointerEvents` gating) so the default is regression-safe; floating/maximized are opacity/visibility-gated windows above the docked z-index. Four new stroke-SVG glyphs (`Undock`, `Dock`, `Maximize`, `Restore` in `Glyphs.tsx`, strokeWidth 1.5 / round caps, amber active / dim inactive — no emoji per HXI #3); control glyphs `stopPropagation` on `onMouseDown` so they are excluded from the drag handle; Maximize/Restore is hidden in docked mode. Body components (channel/thread/DM-log) render unchanged in all three modes — only the chrome changes. +8 vitest (`ui/src/components/wardroom/__tests__/WardRoomPanel.windowmode.test.tsx` — docked default, undock geometry, maximize/restore round-trip, dock + control-visibility, mode persistence, `loadWardRoomLayout` rehydrate/fallback, resize clamp, no-emoji glyph guard). Vitest 1023 → 1031 (+8); `npm run build` clean per BF-279 gate; zero new deps. Forward markers: AD-837b (true OS-level detached window via React-portal-into-`window.open` / Tauri), AD-837c (left/right/top edge resize handles beyond the bottom-right corner), AD-837d (snap-to-edge / tiling). Closes [#811](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/811).

**Wave 199 — codebase-review hygiene shipped (2026-05-31; BF-325..328).** Closes the four actionable findings from the 2026-05-30 codebase review (issues [#800](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/800)–[#803](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/803)). **BF-325 (#800, P0 red main gate):** `src/probos/identity.py` referenced `TransferCertificate` (L1113/L1139/L1177) without importing it → `NameError` on the warm-boot identity-transfer path. Added `from probos.mobility import TransferCertificate` next to the existing `CaptainCard` import (mobility.py is stdlib-only — no circular import) and removed a duplicate `import hashlib`. Validated: `test_ad443_mobility.py` 11-failed → 57-passed. **BF-326 (#801, MagicMock working-tree pollution):** tests passing a `MagicMock` runtime into `create_app()` / `finalize_startup()` made production `Path(mock).mkdir()` create stray `./MagicMock/mock.<attr>/` dirs. Enumeration revealed the scope is **~18 API test modules / 100+ tests** across MULTIPLE independent path vectors (static mounts, data-dir, desktop lock-file, avatar/telemetry dirs) — per-file mock neutralization is unbounded whack-a-mole and was proven insufficient. Shipped a vector-agnostic janitor in `tests/conftest.py` instead: an autouse function-scoped fixture removes any repo-root `MagicMock/` dir a test creates and emits a `warning` (offenders stay discoverable, tests stay green), plus a `pytest_sessionfinish` sweep for dirs created during module/session teardown. With the existing `MagicMock/` `.gitignore` rule, stray dirs can never reach the repo. No production code touched (the fix is test-harness-side, no defensive `isinstance`/`hasattr` added). **BF-327 (#802, repo hygiene):** `git rm` of three tracked scratch files (`.tmp_issue160.txt`, `PROGRESS.md.new`, `tmp_test.txt`) + new `.gitignore` rule for `tmp_*.txt`. **BF-328 (#803, stale doc):** prepended a STALE-SNAPSHOT banner to `docs/development/open-ads-report.md` (a 2026-03-31 snapshot misrepresenting current open work) pointing readers to `PROGRESS.md`/`DECISIONS.md`.

**AD-735 shipped (2026-05-29).** Verified self-identity grounding on the DM path (`whoami`) — issue [#795](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/795). Rides the existing AD-725 read-only pre-LLM lookup seam rather than the issue's proposed (non-existent) LLM function-calling/tool loop: the crew-agent DM path is a one-shot instructions-based call with no mid-turn tool loop, so identity grounding arrives as a high-salience fact-shaped block immediately before generation. New `CognitiveAgent.whoami() -> dict[str, str]` assembles facts from authoritative sources ONLY (birth cert via `runtime._identity_registry.get_by_slot(self.id)` + public `self.callsign`/`self.agent_type`/`_resolve_callsign()`), Tier-2 log-and-degrade (never raises): cert present → canonical `callsign`/`department`/`did`/`birth_iso` (guarded `datetime.fromtimestamp(cert.birth_timestamp)` only when float/int)/`certificate_hash[:12]`/`vessel_name`; no cert → public-attr subset + `get_department(agent_type)` fallback, fabricated keys omitted. New `CognitiveAgent.whoami_block() -> str` renders it as a compact verified-fact block, spelling the callsign explicitly (`"-".join(callsign)` → `Callsign: Ezri (spelled E-z-r-i)`) so name-spelling questions are grounded. New `"identity"` `LookupType` in `dm_targeted_lookup.py` (6 `_IDENTITY_PATTERNS` anchored on `your (name|callsign|department|role)` to avoid over-trigger on "spell the name of that function" / "what role do you want me to play"); classifier checks identity FIRST (cheap, highly specific, beats a generic episodic match); read-only dispatch branch resolves the target via `registry.get(agent_id)` and calls `whoami_block()` inside the same AD-725 firewall (one lookup/turn, no intent_bus, hard `timeout_ms`). New `DmTargetedLookupConfig.identity_enabled: bool = True` — default-on WITHIN the AD-725 master gate (`enabled` stays default `False`); grounding rides for free the moment AD-725 is enabled. One-line instruction nudge added to `_build_personality_block` (defer to the injected `--- Targeted Recall (identity) ---` block verbatim; never guess own name spelling/age); the pre-existing identity block is unchanged. Phantom-API corrections vs issue #795: `self.callsign`/`self.agent_type` (public, not `_callsign`/`_agent_type`); cert via `_identity_registry.get_by_slot` (no `_birth_certificate` attr); `cert.birth_timestamp` float (not `created_at_iso`); `cert.certificate_hash` (not `sha`); `registry.get` (not `get_by_id`). +13 pytest in `tests/test_ad735_whoami_identity.py` (whoami happy/degrade/malformed-timestamp, block spelling, classifier positive + negatives + AD-725 ladder no-regression, dispatcher inject/disabled/no-bus/timeout). Zero new deps. **AD-735a (OPEN forward marker):** promote identity-lookup to an always-on independent pre-LLM injection, decoupled from the AD-725 master gate, if AD-725 remains default-off long-term.

**AD-828 shipped (2026-05-29; Wave 198).** Partial-shutdown diagnostic + startup-incomplete classification. Three boot refusals in 7 days (May 22 / 26 / 29 — ~4-day cadence consistent with Windows sleep/wake killing the runtime mid-startup) traced to the silent consolidation gate at `src/probos/startup/shutdown.py:193` — `if runtime.dream_scheduler and runtime.episodic_memory:` had no `else` branch, so when EITHER was None (most likely `dream_scheduler=None` from kill-before-Phase-5-wiring at `runtime.py:1986`), `_consolidation_result` stayed at its `"skipped"` default and the next boot's `check_previous_shutdown()` refused to start. The observed `phase1_elapsed=2.1s` ≈ the BF-296 Phase A `asyncio.sleep(2.0)` grace + nothing — proof the dream_cycle branch never ran. **AD-828a (diagnostic, +8 LOC):** the new `else:` branch logs WHICH component was None and the value of `_startup_complete` — zero behavior change, makes the next recurrence diagnosable in one log read instead of speculation. **AD-828b (classification, +30 LOC across 3 files):** new `runtime._startup_complete: bool` (False in `__init__`, set True as the unconditional final statement of `start()` after the AD-823 episodic-backup-task spawn), new `"startup_incomplete"` member on the `ConsolidationResult` Literal, and a new carve-out in `check_previous_shutdown` that permits boot for `consolidation == "startup_incomplete"` with a WARNING. Safety reasoning: when the kill fires before Phase-5 wiring, the shutdown handler still runs `episodic_memory.stop()` (graceful HNSW close) AND the AD-822/AD-822b subprocess structural HNSW probe runs BEFORE the real ChromaDB open — so allowing `startup_incomplete` to boot is gated by an actual integrity check on the on-disk index, not by trust. A real consolidation failure mid-write still stamps `"failed"` and still blocks. Honest-degrade for transitional processes via `getattr(runtime, "_startup_complete", True)` — pre-AD running processes default True so they keep the pre-fix `"skipped"` classification (BF-291 convention). Commit `1d752ad4`. +10 pytest in `tests/test_ad828_startup_incomplete.py` including the regression guards that `failed` and `skipped` still raise `UncleanShutdownDetected` (carve-out is NOT widened). Targeted regression: 94 pass across 9 shutdown-adjacent files (test_ad820, ad822b, ad824, ad825, bf291, bf296, bf207, ad819, ad828). Two pre-existing unrelated failures in `test_bf207_shutdown_episodic_integrity.py` (assertions on the old hardcoded "2s" timeout text — AD-820 already replaced with the configurable `_shutdown_consolidation_timeout` formatter; needs a one-line text-update BF). Forward markers: AD-829 (periodic HNSW checkpoint — every ~5min tar.gz; eliminates the failure class entirely), AD-830 (startup-timeout marker-skip if shutdown fires <10s after start began), AD-831 (atomic ChromaDB HNSW flush after every `dream_cycle()` — MEDIUM risk pending ChromaDB private-API audit), AD-828c (record which gate component was None into `shutdown_status.json` note so `probos doctor` can summarize trigger frequency without log scraping).

**AD-797 shipped (2026-05-25; Wave 197).** Artifacts pane — agent-output extractor + viewer drawer + project-pin merge + BF-324 race-safety. v2 extension over the substrate that advance-landed in Wave 195 (commit `9b811df6` shipped `src/probos/artifacts/__init__.py` + 5 endpoints in `routers/artifacts.py` + `runtime.py:457` wiring + `api.py:239` registration). Four real gaps + one latent BF closed in a single commit. (1) Extractor — new `src/probos/cognitive/dm/artifact_extractor.py` (`extract_artifacts()` + `replace_with_stubs()` + `ExtractedArtifact` dataclass) runs two-pass scan: explicit `<artifact name="..." mime="...">...</artifact>` tags first (authoritative; name sanitized to `[A-Za-z0-9._-]+` because it lands in a browser `<a download>` later; missing-mime or empty-sanitized-name skipped with WARNING), then fenced code blocks ≥ `CognitiveConfig.artifact_fenced_threshold_lines` (default 40 — new field; Pydantic `Field(default=40, ge=10)`). 12-language mime map covers `python|py | typescript|ts | javascript|js | markdown|md | bash|sh | json | yaml|yml | html | css | sql | rust|rs | go`; default `text/plain` / `.txt`. Filename-comment hint (`# filename: helper.py`, `// filename: ...`, `<!-- filename: ... -->`) on the first line of a fenced block overrides the generated `artifact-{N}.{ext}` name. (2) Pipeline integration — new `step_4f_extract_artifacts` in `DmReplyPipeline.run()` inserted between `step_4b_dm_outbound_parse` and `step_5_episodic_store` so the stored episode + Captain-visible text both see the stubbed body (NOT the raw blocks). Per extraction: `sha256(blob)` → `attachment_store.write(hash, blob, mime, origin="agent_artifact")` (idempotent) → `artifact_store.add_version(thread_id=..., name=..., ...)` → replace source span with stub line. Stub format: `[Artifact: {name} v{version} - {line_count} lines, {mime}]` — ASCII hyphen (NOT em-dash); UI regex matches the literal hyphen. Honest-degrade Tier-2 throughout: any exception leaves `response_text` intact. Uses `self.ctx.chat_thread_id` (NOT `thread_id` — verified field name at `reply_pipeline.py:67`). (3) Content endpoint — new `GET /api/artifacts/{artifact_id}/content` in `routers/artifacts.py` streams raw bytes from AttachmentStore with `Content-Type` from `artifact.mime`. 404 `artifact_not_found` when row missing; 404 `content_missing` when blob orphaned. For `mime='text/uri-list'` the body IS the URL — UI dereferences to `<img src>`. (4) Project-pin merge — extended `list_thread_artifacts` to merge project-pinned artifacts when `thread.project_id` is non-null: native + pinned union, native wins on `content_hash` collision (skipped silently), pinned SHAs with no `Artifact` row (raw upload, not extracted) skipped silently. Per-row `_pinned_from_project: bool` flag added; response shape stays HEAD-compatible `{"thread_id": ..., "artifacts": [...]}`. New `ArtifactStore.find_first_by_hash(content_hash) -> Artifact | None` helper (`SELECT * FROM artifacts WHERE content_hash = ? ORDER BY created_at ASC LIMIT 1`; uses existing `idx_artifacts_hash` index; first-by-time deterministic — AD-797h forward marker for canonical cross-thread identity). (5) BF-324 — `ArtifactStore.add_version()` previously did SELECT MAX + INSERT without a transaction; two concurrent extractions on `(thread_id, name)` would race the UNIQUE constraint. Wrapped in `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` (matches AD-791a / AD-793 convention). Latent in HEAD because extraction wasn't automated; race-safe before Wave 197 turned on automatic extraction. (6) AttachmentStore origin — added `"agent_artifact"` to `ATTACHMENT_ORIGINS` tuple in `attachments/store.py` AND to `_ORIGIN_DURABILITY` in `filesystem_store.py` (positioned above `chat_attachment` since artifact bytes are committed agent output, the most durable tier). Future AD-797k orphan GC can distinguish artifact bytes from upload bytes. UI: new `ui/src/components/artifacts/` directory with 5 files. `ArtifactDrawer.tsx` mounts in CompactApp as third flex child `[ThreadSidebar | ProfileChatTab | ArtifactDrawer]` — 360px expanded / 28px rail collapsed; `localStorage['probos.artifactDrawer.collapsed']` persists state; viewport <1024px defaults to rail (responsive proper → AD-797j). Subscribes to `useStore.activeThreadId` and re-fetches `/api/artifacts/thread/{id}` on change; auto-collapses to rail when the list is empty AND the Captain hasn't manually expanded. `ArtifactList.tsx` per-row: stroke-based SVG mime icon (HXI #3 — markdown/code/image/text glyphs; NO emoji), name (ellipsis-truncated), `v{N}` chip, `PIN` badge when `_pinned_from_project=true`, relative timestamp, amber active-row highlight. `ArtifactViewer.tsx` render branches by mime: `text/markdown` → `react-markdown` (existing dep, package.json:23), `text/x-* | application/{json,sql,yaml}` → `<pre><code>` (no syntax highlight — AD-797d forward), `image/*` → `<img src="/api/artifacts/{id}/content">`, `text/uri-list` → fetch body → parse URL → `<img>`, `text/plain` → `<pre>` wrap. Toolbar: Copy (`navigator.clipboard.writeText`; disabled for images with tooltip), Save (browser `<a download>` with `URL.createObjectURL`/`revokeObjectURL` lifecycle), Pin-to-project (visible only when `thread.project_id` non-null; calls EXISTING `POST /api/projects/{id}/pin` from AD-793 with `content_hash` as `attachment_id`; re-fetches list to pick up the flag). Multi-version dropdown when 2+ versions exist for the same name. `ArtifactCard.tsx` renders inline inside ProfileChatTab message bodies in place of stub lines; matches `(threadId, name, version)` against `useStore.artifactsByThread` to resolve the UUID (stub doesn't carry it); click opens drawer + selects the artifact; renders with-loading-state until drawer state arrives. `artifactApi.ts` centralizes fetch wrappers + exports the shared `ARTIFACT_STUB_RE` / `parseArtifactStub()` so the card + drawer + ProfileChatTab share one regex. Store slice: `artifactsByThread: Map<string, ArtifactView[]>` + `selectedArtifactId` + `artifactDrawerCollapsed` + actions `hydrateArtifacts` / `selectArtifact` / `setArtifactDrawerCollapsed` (the last persists to localStorage). New `ArtifactView` type matches `Artifact.to_dict()` + the `_pinned_from_project` flag. `CompactApp.tsx` adds `<ArtifactDrawer />` as third flex child after the chat column; `ProfileChatTab.tsx` adds `renderMessageBodyWithArtifacts(text, threadId)` helper that splits message body by `\n` and replaces stub-matching lines with `<ArtifactCard>` (threadId-aware — undefined threadId falls back to plain text). NO Monaco / NO Prism in v1 (AD-797a, AD-797d forward markers). +22 pytest in 4 new files (`test_artifact_extractor.py` ×9, `test_artifact_store.py` ×4 including the BF-324 4-way `asyncio.gather` + `run_in_executor` race regression, `test_artifact_api.py` ×6, `test_artifact_pipeline.py` ×3). +7 vitest in 7 new files (`ArtifactDrawer.{empty-state,list-render,active-thread-change,markdown-render,copy-button,version-selector}.test.tsx` + `ArtifactCard.inline.test.tsx`). Targeted regression: 70 pytest pass across all DM-pipeline + artifact + adjacent suites (test_ad726_dm_reply_pipeline + test_ad722a4_auto_correction + test_ad728d_self_image_awareness_skill + test_ad730_3_agent_image_gen + the 4 new artifact files). 62 pytest pass across attachment_reaper + project_pin + project_crud + hxi_chat_integration + distribution. Vitest 1016 → 1023 (+7); `npm run build` clean per BF-279 gate; zero new deps. Forward markers: AD-797a (Monaco/CodeMirror live edit), AD-797b (version diff), AD-797c (Send-to-tool re-injection), AD-797d (syntax highlighting via Prism/Shiki), AD-797e (additional mimes — svg/mermaid/dot/latex), AD-797f (runtime override of fenced threshold), AD-797h (canonical cross-thread artifact identity), AD-797i (drawer-rail Captain-direct artifact creation UI), AD-797j (full HXI responsive integration), AD-797k (AttachmentStore orphan GC for artifact_artifact origin), AD-797m (materialized message_id back-link column). ~AD-797g~ DROPPED — `POST /api/artifacts` exists at HEAD, v1 simply does not surface it. Closes [#721](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/721).

**AD-793 shipped (2026-05-25; Wave 196).** Projects substrate — long-lived context groups owning N chat threads + pinned attachment refs. Backend: new `projects` table additively in `threads/__init__.py` `_SCHEMA` (`CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS` — no PRAGMA migration needed since the table is new), new `Project` dataclass + `ProjectStore` class (CRUD + pin/unpin + touch + `delete_project(cascade=False)` returning `(deleted, affected_threads)` with `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` race-safety matching the AD-791a convention; `last_active_at == created_at` at creation per spec). New `src/probos/routers/projects.py` exposes GET/POST/PATCH/DELETE `/api/projects` + `/api/projects/{id}/pin` + `/api/projects/{id}/unpin`. Single-project endpoints return `Project.to_dict()` DIRECTLY (no wrapper — matches the Wave 195 review correction for `POST /api/threads`); LIST returns `{"projects": [...]}`; DELETE returns `{"deleted, affected_threads, cascade}`. The pin endpoint is `async def` and `await`s `AttachmentStore.exists()` per the architect v2 R2 correction (the protocol method is async at `attachments/store.py:60`); 400 when the SHA is missing (request was syntactically valid but the referenced data is unknown — 404 would imply attachments are sub-resources of projects). Cross-router import uses `from probos.routers.chat import _get_attachment_store` (precedent at `routers/avatars.py:55`). Project-store wired in `runtime.py:~L444` next to `chat_thread_store` (NOT in `startup/finalize.py` per architect v2 R1 correction — `chat_thread_store` doesn't live there). Router registered in `api.py` alongside the threads router. Conceptual contribution: the project `description` is the per-turn Captain-authored framing that injects as a system-message preamble on every chat turn inside contained threads. ~20 LOC inserted in `routers/agents.py` BETWEEN the AD-725 recall prepend (L2073-76) and the AD-733a visual prepend (L2078+) so the canonical on-the-wire `message_text` order is **visual → project → recall → user** (substring-index ordering test #12 guards against regression). Delimiter framing `--- Project: {name} ---` / `--- End Project Context ---` matches AD-733a / BF-294's BF-294 provenance pattern. Type-guard against the MagicMock-auto-attribute pattern (BF-287 phantom-via-MagicMock lesson): preamble only injects when `isinstance(thread.project_id, str)` AND `_project.description` is a real str — otherwise tests using `runtime = MagicMock()` would silently inject garbage preambles in adjacent suites (vision, dm-targeted-lookup). Touch-on-message-append wired at the **router layer** in `routers/threads.py`'s POST `/api/threads/{id}/messages` handler (NOT inside `threads/__init__.py`'s `append_message` per architect v2 R3 correction — keeps the threads substrate module decoupled from the projects layer). Delete defaults to **unparent** (cascade=false; sets `project_id=NULL` on contained threads, deletes the project row); cascade requires `?cascade=true` query param + double-confirmation in the UI ("This will permanently delete N threads"). Episodes / AD-541b anchors preserved in BOTH paths (the store only touches the chat_threads + chat_thread_messages + projects tables). UI: Wave 195's `ThreadSidebar.tsx` placeholder ("Coming with AD-793.") replaced with a real expandable Projects section. New components: `ProjectRow.tsx` (chevron + folder + name + thread count, stroke-based SVG icons per HXI #3; amber `#f0b060` active, dim `#666680` inactive, `strokeWidth: 1.5`), `NewProjectModal.tsx` (name + description inputs; submit disabled when name empty), `ProjectContextMenu.tsx` (Rename / Edit description / Archive / Delete + `ProjectDeleteConfirm` modal with unparent-default radio + cascade double-confirmation + `EditDescriptionModal`), `MoveToProjectMenu.tsx` (submenu under ThreadRow's right-click "Move to project…" item; lists projects + "None (unparent)"). New `projectApi.ts` mirrors `threadApi.ts` shape with verified response contracts. Store slice: `projects: Map<string, ProjectView>` + `hydrateProjects` / `setProject` / `removeProject` actions mirroring the existing `chatThreads` pattern. Recents filter excludes threads with non-null `project_id` (those appear under their project section instead); Pinned section is unaffected — pinning overrides project grouping (matches Claude precedent). ThreadSidebar hydrates `/api/threads` + `/api/projects` in parallel on mount via `Promise.all`. Per-project expansion state persisted to `localStorage['probos.sidebar.projects.expanded']` (JSON dict keyed by project_id). Pinned attachments stored as JSON list of SHAs but NOT auto-injected into chat context — that's AD-793a forward marker (attachment-type-aware decisions about embed vs reference vs render-to-text). Tests: +12 pytest in 4 new files (`test_project_crud.py` ×11, `test_project_pin.py` ×3, `test_project_touch.py` ×2, `test_project_context_injection.py` ×5 — including the substring-index ordering guard) + 11 vitest in 4 new files (`ThreadSidebar.projects-section.test.tsx` ×3, `ThreadSidebar.new-project.test.tsx` ×3, `ThreadSidebar.move-thread-to-project.test.tsx` ×2, `ThreadSidebar.delete-project-modal.test.tsx` ×3) + 1 vitest update (`ThreadSidebar.render.test.tsx:37` placeholder assertion replaced with the real empty-state + "+" button assertions). Targeted regression: 178 tests across all chat/threads/projects/vision/recall files green; 78 api/runtime tests green; vitest 1005 → 1016 (+11). `npm run build` clean per BF-279 gate. Forward markers: AD-793a (auto-inject pinned attachments into chat context, attachment-type aware), AD-793b (many-to-many `project_attachments` junction table for per-pin metadata), AD-793c (project sharing / collaboration / export), AD-793d (per-project LLM tier override inheriting into contained threads), AD-793e (per-project personality override parallel to thread.personality_override), AD-793f (pinned attachments rendering in the sidebar). Closes [#717](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/717).

**AD-792 shipped (2026-05-25; Wave 195).** Thread sidebar for Compact Yeo. Pure UI wave on the AD-791a / Wave 194 substrate — zero backend changes, zero new schema, zero new store slices, zero new deps. New `ui/src/components/sidebar/ThreadSidebar.tsx` (~600 LOC including 5 inline sub-components: `ThreadRow`, `SectionHeader`, `SidebarSearch`-pattern input, `ContextMenuView`, `DeleteConfirm`) renders Pinned → Projects (placeholder for AD-793) → Recents (grouped Today / Yesterday / Earlier via new pure `threadGrouping.timeOfLifeGroup()` helper). New `threadApi.ts` centralizes the response-shape contracts so the React component and tests agree on the three architect v2 phantom corrections: `POST /api/threads` returns `thread.to_dict()` DIRECTLY (verified at `routers/threads.py:117` — NOT wrapped in `{thread: ...}`); `GET /api/threads/search` returns `{query, results}` (read `data.results`, NOT `data.threads`); `GET /api/threads` returns `{threads: [...]}`. Sidebar mounts → fires `GET /api/threads?include_archived=false&limit=100` → pipes through existing `hydrateChatThreads` action; chat handlers continue to add per-turn via the AD-791a round-trip (no double-source-of-truth). Right-click context menu wires Rename (PATCH `{title, title_locked: true}` — Wave 194 contract), Pin/Unpin (optimistic, PATCH `{pinned}`), Archive (PATCH `{archived: true}`, filtered out of sections), Delete (confirm modal → DELETE → drops from store; cascade-to-messages handled by AD-791a; episodes preserved). Search debounces **300ms** matching the precedent at `useStore.ts:549` (architect Recommended fix vs the 250ms in the v1 spec draft) — no client-side filtering; backend owns ranking. Collapse chevron toggles 240px ↔ 56px, persisted to `localStorage['probos.sidebar.collapsed']`; collapsed mode renders icon-only avatar column of pinned threads + new-chat button. HXI design principles honored: #1 sections sorted by salience (Pinned first), #3 all glyphs inline SVG with `strokeWidth: 1.5` / `strokeLinecap: round` / amber `#f0b060` / dim `#666680` (NO emoji), #4 motion communicates state (active row gets 3px amber border-left + glow), #5 progressive disclosure (240 / 56 collapse). `ProfileChatTab.tsx` gains optional `threadId?: string` prop with explicit precedence `props.threadId ?? useStore.getState().threadIdByAgent.get(agentId)` for the request body; response always wins on mismatch — `setThreadForAgent(agentId, data.thread_id)` is still called so the store reflects the latest server-confirmed thread (AD-791a invariant preserved when server-side route reassignment surfaces a different thread). `CompactApp.tsx` wraps `ProfileChatTab` in a flex container with `ThreadSidebar` to the left; `derivedAgentId` resolves from `activeThread.participants[0]` (single-participant 1:1 in v1) and falls back to Yeo when no thread is active (cold-start parity). Full HXI integration explicitly deferred per architect v2 — no toggle, no settings field, no dead code. Honest-degrade throughout: network failures return empty arrays / null and the sidebar keeps its current state instead of throwing (Tier-2 log-and-degrade). +32 vitest across 10 new files (`ThreadSidebar.{render,hydrate,search,recents-grouping,contextMenu,collapse,newChat,activeRow}.test.tsx` + `ProfileChatTab.threadId-prop.test.tsx` + `CompactApp.sidebar-integration.test.tsx`). UI vitest 1005 (was 973; +32). `npm run build` clean per BF-279 gate. Forward markers: AD-792a (unread tracking — `unread_count` field + `last_read_at` per-Captain), AD-792b (per-Captain primary-agent preference for New chat default), AD-792c (Full HXI sidebar layout integration), AD-792d (drag-to-reorder pinned), AD-792e (archived browsing UI), AD-792f (thread sharing / export). Closes [#716](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/716).

**AD-794 + AD-809 shipped (2026-05-25; Wave 194).** First-turn auto-naming and per-thread personality overlay wired into the chat flow on the AD-791a (Wave 193) substrate. `naming.py`'s pre-existing `suggest_title()` and `resolve_personality()` helpers — which had zero consumers — now drive a real first-turn auto-name trigger in `agent_chat` (`routers/agents.py:1660`) and the `/personality <name|list|clear>` slash command. Four new methods on `ChatThreadStore`: `set_personality_override`, `set_title(lock=False|True)`, `is_title_locked`, `maybe_auto_name(force=False|True)` — the last centralizes the auto-name heuristic so the chat handler's first-turn trigger and the existing `POST /api/threads/{id}/auto-name` endpoint share one path (force=False applies single-participant + zero-prior-messages preconditions; force=True preserves the endpoint's pre-AD-794 always-rename behavior; both respect the title-lock flag). Operator-initiated renames send `title_locked: true` on the existing `PATCH /api/threads/{thread_id}` (UpdateThreadRequest extended additively — sibling endpoint NOT added per architect v3 correction), routing through `set_title(lock=True)` so the title + `metadata.title_locked` flag are written atomically (BEGIN IMMEDIATE; race-safe with the AD-791a get_or_create_default_for_agent pattern). Personality consumption rides AD-791a's `intent.thread_id` wire: `CognitiveAgent.perceive()` lifts `intent.thread_id` onto the observation dict; `decide()` resolves and appends the per-thread `personality_override` to the composed system prompt at the convergence point between DM and non-DM branches (`cognitive_agent.py:~L2117`, after the AD-596b skill-instr append, before all three `LLMRequest(system_prompt=composed, ...)` sites at L2180/2192/2198). The overlay is an **identity-preserving register knob, not a replacement** — Section 0 conceptual frame: Ezri stays Ezri, just adopts a different room voice per thread. NEW `personality_registry.py` with 5 entries (concise/formal/socratic/expert/casual). NEW `cognitive/commands/personality_command.py` parser+handler. Per-turn ordering in chat handlers (`agent_chat` and the inline-callsign branch in `routers/chat.py:263`): @-mention strip → `/personality` guard (early-return; appends captain + system messages, no IntentBus dispatch) → `maybe_auto_name` → captain-message append → intent dispatch. Inline-callsign branch returns a plain dict (not a `ChatResponse` model — that BaseModel has no `system` field; architect v3→v4 phantom-field correction). UI: `AgentProfileMessage.role` extended to `'user' | 'agent' | 'system'`; ProfileChatTab renders system replies as dim-italic dashed-border center notes (subtle visual register); chat response carries `title` field so `useStore.chatThreads` map updates without a `/api/threads` round-trip when auto-name fires. Honest-degrade throughout: missing runtime / store / corrupt JSON metadata silently degrades to base behavior — never blocks a turn. +24 pytest (`test_ad809_personality.py` x 11, `test_ad794_auto_name.py` x 13) + 5 vitest (`ProfileChatTab.personality.test.tsx` x 2, `useStore.threadTitle.test.ts` x 3). Vitest 970 → 975 (+5). Targeted regression: 94 adjacent chat/thread/cognitive tests pass + 39 AD-791a/794/809 tests pass. Forward markers: AD-794a (LLM-backed naming), AD-794b (auto-rename on topic drift), AD-809a (operator-extensible registry via config), AD-809b (HXI sidebar personality affordance, AD-792 dependency). Closes [#718](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/718), closes [#733](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/733).

**AD-791g shipped (2026-06-21).** Main-chat thread + Captain↔Ship's-Computer thread — lifts the AD-791a fan-out + Captain↔Computer threading deferral ([#792](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/792)). Two fixed per-instance system threads in `threads/__init__.py`: constants `MAIN_CHAT_THREAD_ID="system-main-chat"` / `COMPUTER_THREAD_ID="system-ships-computer"` + NEW `ChatThreadStore.get_or_create_system_thread(thread_id, *, title)` (fixed-id `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` find-or-create mirroring `get_or_create_default_for_agent`; CALLER-supplied id; `participants=[]` implicit membership — NO roster read-modify-write; `project_id=NULL` per-instance; `metadata.kind="system"` — the column exists since AD-791a, zero migration, no new column). `routers/chat.py` fan-out (`>=2`-mention branch): seeds ONE shared `MAIN_CHAT_THREAD_ID`, stamps every dispatched `IntentMessage.thread_id`, appends the Captain turn + each REAL agent reply (placeholder sentinels skipped). Vision / Captain-only branch: seeds `COMPUTER_THREAD_ID`, appends the Captain turn + LLM reply, resolves the AD-791a `chat_thread_id=""` AnchorFrame literal to `COMPUTER_THREAD_ID`. **THE AD-933a TRAP:** the fan-out episode anchor is set AFTER the `if dream_adapter: build_episode(...) else: Episode(...)` if/else so BOTH branches are reached — and since `AnchorFrame`/`Episode` are BOTH `@dataclass(frozen=True)`, via `dataclasses.replace` (the spec's literal in-place `episode.anchors.chat_thread_id=…` raises `FrozenInstanceError`, which the AD-719 try/except would silently swallow → dropped episode; the deviation realizes the identical documented intent frozen-safely — test 4 caught it). FLAG-LESS honest-degrade (mirror AD-791a): gated ONLY on `runtime.chat_thread_store` presence — store absent ⇒ intents `thread_id=None`, episodes `chat_thread_id=""`, replies still returned (byte-identical to pre-AD-791g). Every side effect tier-2 (try/except → `logger.warning`, never raises into the chat response). NO `config.py`/`system.yaml`/`runtime.py`/`startup/*.py` change (lazy per-turn get-or-create). +8 pytest (`tests/test_ad791g_main_chat_thread.py`; real `ChatThreadStore` + real `CallsignRegistry` per BF-287; test 4 = AD-933a both-branch guard with `dream_adapter=None` AND a fake `build_episode→channel="dag"`; test 8 = byte-identical store-absent across fan-out + vision). Focused gate 25 (8 + AD-791a) passed ×2 (`-n 0`); `-k "thread or chat or fanout or vision or episod"` blast-radius 1248 passed / 1 skipped. Pre-existing sub-number (no new top-level; highest landed AD-1052). Closes [#792](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/792).

**AD-791a (AD-827) shipped (2026-05-25; Wave 193).** Chat-threads substrate wired into the chat flow. New `IntentMessage.thread_id: str | None = None` field on the core dispatch contract; new `AnchorFrame.chat_thread_id: str = ""` field (namespace-separated from the existing Ward-Room `thread_id`). Schema additions to `chat_threads` (`preprompt`, `model`, `metadata` JSON columns) and `chat_thread_messages` (`parent_message_id`, `branch_ordinal`, `score`, `interrupted` for regenerate-as-sibling + thumbs feedback). PRAGMA-pattern column migration in `probos.threads._migrate_v2()`. Three new race-safe methods on `ChatThreadStore` — `find_default_for_agent`, `create_default_for_agent`, `get_or_create_default_for_agent` (BEGIN IMMEDIATE for concurrent-first-turn safety). Conceptual frame: **ProbOS agents are persistent sovereign identities; threads are meeting envelopes, not stateless context substitutes** — `preprompt`/`model` columns are OVERLAYS on agent identity, not replacements; cross-thread continuity is the default (via global episodic memory + AD-640 tiered trust); thread deletion cascades to `chat_thread_messages` only, NOT to episodes (agent memory survives). 1:1 wiring at `routers/agents.py:agent_chat` (line 1660): router builds `DmReplyContext(chat_thread_id=thread.id)` at line 2089; `DmReplyPipeline.run()` then writes both AnchorFrames in `cognitive/dm/reply_pipeline.py` (L658 action-dispatch episode + L757 DM episode) with `chat_thread_id` populated. Parity wiring for `routers/chat.py` inline-callsign (L281) and vision (L424) paths. AD-719 multi-agent fan-out (line 177) and Captain↔Computer thread modeling explicitly DEFERRED to new AD-791g forward marker ([#792](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/792)). UI: additive store slices (`chatThreads`, `threadIdByAgent`, `activeThreadId`) — existing `agentConversations` map untouched, no visible UX change. Five architect-review iterations (v1→v5) converged on phantom-API and layer-discipline corrections; absorbed `huggingface/chat-ui` data shapes (Apache 2.0) for `preprompt`/`model`/`score`/`interrupted`/branching-tree fields. +12 tests (10 pytest + 2 vitest new for thread_id round-trip; 970 vitest + 1 skipped). Forward markers: AD-791b (FTS5 search), AD-791c (archival lifecycle), AD-791d (regenerate UI), AD-791e (thumbs UI), AD-791g (main-chat + Computer threads, #792). Commit `50c6a98a`. Closes #715 (AD-791 umbrella).

**BF-301 shipped (2026-05-23).** Migrated browser STT from abandoned whisper.cpp WASM (#775) to `@huggingface/transformers` v3 + Web Worker + `Xenova/whisper-tiny.en`. The AD-826 architecture is preserved (local-first cascade, AD-705a privacy invariant, AD-826 fallback semantics, BF-290/292/293/294/294b/300 mic UX) — only the local engine swaps. New `ui/src/audio/transformersStt.ts` mirrors the `whisperStt.ts` public surface (`armTransformersStt` / `disarm…` / `onTransformersTranscript` / `onTransformersTranscribing`) plus a new `onTransformersProgress(handler)` channel for first-load model download UX; new `ui/src/audio/transformersWorker.ts` owns the `pipeline('automatic-speech-recognition', 'Xenova/whisper-tiny.en')` lifecycle in a dedicated Web Worker (postMessage init/transcribe/shutdown protocol, OffscreenCanvas-style isolation). All three call sites migrated via import-name swap (`armTransformersStt as armWhisperStt`): `ProfileChatTab.tsx`, `IntentSurface.tsx`, `conversationController.ts`. Backend: `cognitive.primary_stt` Literal extended to `"transformers" | "whisper" | "browser"` (default flipped to `transformers`); `whisper` retained as deprecated alias that resolves to `engine: "transformers"` in `/api/voice/health` so saved operator configs do not break. New `cognitive.transformers_model` field (default `Xenova/whisper-tiny.en`; operators can pin `Xenova/whisper-base.en`). `/api/voice/health` adds `model` field; backend no longer probes the GGML filesystem path (browser owns the fetch via HF CDN + Cache API). `whisper_model_path`, `whisperStt.ts`, `whisperLoader.ts`, and `scripts/whisper-tiny-en-fetch.ps1` retained with deprecation banners for one release cycle; a follow-up hygiene PR will delete them. Vite: new `stt-vendor` `manualChunks` branch keeps the transformers.js + onnxruntime-web runtime out of the main bundle (lazy on PTT arm). `onnxruntime-web` removed from `optionalDependencies` (v3 bundles it). Bundle delta: main `index` chunk unchanged at 48.8 kB (gzip 15.4 kB); new `stt-vendor` chunk at 13.43 kB (gzip 5.34 kB) + lazy `transformersWorker` chunk at 877 kB + ort-wasm at 21.6 MB (gzip 5.1 MB) loaded only when STT arms. Closes #775. +9 backend pytest (`tests/test_bf301_voice_health.py`) + 3 AD-826 backend tests migrated (default flipped, `whisper`-alias, `model` field in browser response). +9 worker-boundary vitest (`ui/src/__tests__/transformersStt.bf301.test.tsx`) + 9 integration vitest (`ui/src/__tests__/ProfileChatTab.bf301.test.tsx`) + 5 AD-826 frontend tests migrated + 7 BF-290/292/293/294/300 + conversationController + conversationWiring tests re-pointed to the new module mock target. UI `npm run build` green per BF-279 gate. Tracker: vitest 924 → 957 (+33).

**BF-300 shipped (2026-05-23).** PTT mic gate + `ttsActive` guard — echo-loop fix (#774). In Chrome PTT mode, the `continuous: true` `SpeechRecognition` session stayed open after a result, captured the agent's TTS reply through the speakers, and auto-sent the agent's own words back as a new user message. Two-layer fix in `ui/src/components/profile/ProfileChatTab.tsx`: (1) all 5 PTT result callbacks (3 browser-SR result paths + 2 whisper-transcript paths) now explicitly terminate capture (`stopListening()` / `disarmWhisperStt()` are already called for whisper) BEFORE the 100 ms `setTimeout(sendText)` fires; (2) new persistent `onSpeechEvent` subscription drives `ttsActiveRef` (synchronous click-handler gate) + `ttsActive` state (drives MicIndicator) so a PTT click during TTS playback is refused with an INFO log. New `MicIndicator` `muted` state (dim violet `#6a5a8a` static dashed ring — HXI #4: absence of motion communicates intentional suppression). Stop-path is intentionally NOT gated — operator must always be able to abort. Conversation-mode path (BF-290 `markAgentReplyComplete`) coexists with the new top-level subscription. Closes #774. +5 vitest in `ui/src/__tests__/ProfileChatTab.bf300.test.tsx`. UI `npm run build` green per BF-279 gate. Zero new deps.

**BF-296 shipped (2026-05-23).** Shutdown Phase A — close `IntentBus` to new dispatches before consolidation (#771). The 2026-05-23 10:39 UTC graceful shutdown produced an AD-820 partial marker (`consolidation_result=failed`) because AD-643a cognitive agents kept receiving `proactive_think` / `ward_room_notification` intents during the consolidation window, racing `dream_cycle`'s ChromaDB writes (torn HNSW). AD-825 shipped Phases B/C/D/E (drain → cancel → mark → close) but missed Phase A: stop accepting new work at the dispatch boundary.
