New `IntentBus.close_to_new_dispatches()` sets a `_closed` flag; `broadcast()` returns `[]`, `send()` returns `None`, `dispatch_async()` no-ops, and the JetStream `_on_dispatch` callback calls `msg.term()` (not `nak()` — no redelivery of pre-shutdown work after consolidation completes). In-flight handlers complete normally. `src/probos/startup/shutdown.py` invokes Phase A before the existing AD-825 `DreamScheduler.stop_gracefully()` quiesce with a 2s grace for already-fanned-out broadcasts; honest-degrades via `getattr`/`hasattr` for transitional processes started before BF-296 shipped. AD-825 success log line added (`DreamScheduler quiesced within N.Ns`) per #771 forensics ask. Closes #771. +10 pytest (`test_bf296_intent_bus_close.py` ×7, `test_bf296_shutdown_phase_ordering.py` ×3). AD-820..AD-826 + BF-291/295 regression suite (92 tests) green. Zero new deps.

**AD-812 shipped (2026-05-23).** Natural-language scheduled actions. New `src/probos/cognitive/schedule_parser.py` (`ScheduleSpec` + `parse_nl_schedule`): regex fast-path for `in N minutes/hours/days` (no LLM call), fast-tier LLM with strict JSON-output prompt for everything else, honest-degrade `ScheduleSpec(kind="error", reason=...)` when LLM unavailable AND regex misses. New `src/probos/experience/commands/commands_schedule.py` (`cmd_remind`, `cmd_schedule list | cancel <id> | <NL>`). New `src/probos/routers/schedule_nl.py` (`POST /api/schedule/nl`). All three surfaces consume the existing AD-418 `PersistentTaskStore.create_task(...)` — no new scheduler, no new background loop, no new persistence. Channel binding is best-effort lowercase-name to `<adapter>:default`. Croniter validates generated cron expressions. Zero new dependencies. +9 pytest in `tests/test_ad812_schedule_parser.py` (regex fast-path ×2, LLM once / cron / cron+channel, honest-degrade no-LLM / non-JSON / invalid-cron, past-execute rejected). Closes #736.

**BF-299 shipped (2026-05-23).** UI build hygiene — eliminated two Vite/Rollup `(!) dynamically imported by ... but also statically imported by ...` warnings for `useSettingsStore` and `useLipSyncCapture`. Both modules were already eagerly loaded by their static-side consumers (`App.tsx`/`CompactApp.tsx`/... for `useSettingsStore`; `CrewVRM.tsx` for `useLipSyncCapture`), so the `await import(...)` indirection in `ui/src/audio/wakeWord.ts:290` and `ui/src/audio/voice.ts:291` paid the syntactic cost of laziness without delivering a chunk split. Converted both to static imports (Option A from #757). The `voice.ts` ↔ `useLipSyncCapture.ts` static cycle is benign — both sides read the imported binding inside function bodies (event-listener callback / hook body), not at module-top-level — and `CrewVRM.tsx` already participates in this same call graph today. No behavior changes; module-load timing unchanged for modules that were already eagerly loaded. Closes #757. Zero new tests (acceptance is build-log + existing vitest suite green): vitest 935 (934 passed + 1 skipped) unchanged; `npm run build` warning-free for the two target warnings (the unrelated 500 kB chunk-size warning remains and is deferred to a follow-up `manualChunks` issue per #757 ¶2). Tracker: vitest 934 → 934.

**AD-826 shipped (2026-05-22).** Whisper-first STT priority for cross-browser voice reliability. New `cognitive.primary_stt: Literal["whisper", "browser"]` (default `whisper`) + `cognitive.fallback_stt_enabled: bool` (default True) in `src/probos/config.py`. New `GET /api/voice/health` endpoint in `src/probos/routers/voice.py` returns `{primary_stt, engine, backend_available, healthy}` — filesystem-only probe (no subprocess; whisper inference is browser-side WASM per AD-705a). `ProfileChatTab.tsx` PTT click handler inverted: whisper-primary path arms `whisperStt` first via `onWhisperTranscript`; new `emptyWhisperCountRef` triggers browser-SR fallback after 2 empty whisper transcripts (mirror of AD-760). Honest-degrade when whisper artifacts absent or `offline_stt_enabled=False` — falls through to browser SR for the press without consuming any counter. Mic button `title` now includes engine cue (`Voice input (whisper)` / `Voice input (browser)`) — no emoji per HXI #3. Section-registry entries hot-reload (under `llm_tiers` SectionDescriptor alongside `cognitive.offline_stt_enabled`). Edge / Firefox / Safari operators get reliable voice without changing the default. Closes #767. +8 pytest in `tests/test_ad826_voice_config.py` (prompt estimated 6; +2 extra coverage); +5 vitest in `ui/src/__tests__/ProfileChatTab.ad826.test.tsx`. UI `npm run build` green per BF-279 gate. Zero new deps. Tracker: vitest 919 → 924.

**BF-294 shipped (2026-05-22).** Three-state mic affordance in `ui/src/components/profile/ProfileChatTab.tsx`. The existing mic button had only two visual states (dim grey / red flash on `listening`), the red looked like an error indicator and gave no feedback during the whisper transcription window. New presentational `MicIndicator` component (`ui/src/components/profile/MicIndicator.tsx`) encapsulates `idle` / `listening` / `processing` — inline SVG glyph (HXI #3, `strokeWidth: 1.5`, `strokeLinecap: round`), trust-spectrum amber `#f0b060` active / `#a08040` dim, CSS keyframes `bf294-mic-listen` (pulsing solid ring) and `bf294-mic-process` (rotating dashed ring) added alongside the existing `pulse-mic` keyframe in `ProfileChatTab`'s inline `<style>` block. `ProfileChatTab` imports `onTranscribing as onWhisperTranscribing` from `whisperStt`, subscribes via a new `useEffect` that flips a `processing` state; mic button replaces inline `<svg>` with `<MicIndicator state={...} />`, derives `processing > listening > idle` priority, retitles/relabels the button per state, switches the background wash from red to amber and the force-stop early-return now also calls `setProcessing(false)`. No `getUserMedia`, no `AudioContext`, no `setInterval` — all CSS-driven. Closes #768. +9 vitest (5 `MicIndicator.test.tsx` + 4 `ProfileChatTab.bf294.test.tsx`); BF-290/292/293/conversationWiring whisperStt mocks extended with `onTranscribing` (regression-fix from new import). UI build green per BF-279 gate. Zero new deps. Tracker: vitest 912 → 919.

**BF-292 shipped (2026-05-22).** PTT auto-send broken by stale `handleSend` closure. Both PTT result paths in `ui/src/components/profile/ProfileChatTab.tsx` (whisperStt fallback line ~798, browser SpeechRecognition line ~816) did `setInput(text); setListening(false); setTimeout(() => handleSend(), 100)`. The captured `handleSend` closed over `input = ''` at scheduling time (React had not re-rendered), so the timer-driven send read `text = ''.trim() === ''`, hit the empty/sending guard, and returned silently — transcript appeared in the textarea but no POST ever reached `/api/agent/{id}/chat`. Factored a new `sendText(textArg)` callback that takes the message body as an argument (NOT from closure; `input` deliberately omitted from deps) and made `handleSend` a thin shim for the Enter-key + send-button paths where `input` is current. Both PTT call sites now `setTimeout(() => { void sendText(text); }, 100)`. Closes #765. +6 vitest in `ui/src/__tests__/ProfileChatTab.bf292.test.tsx` (both PTT paths, empty bail, sending guard, pending attachments, Enter-key regression). UI build green per BF-279 gate. Zero new deps. Tracker: vitest 906 → 912.

**BF-291 shipped (2026-05-22).** Defensive `MemoryConfig` field access in `src/probos/startup/shutdown.py`. The three sites that read `shutdown_consolidation_timeout_s` / `shutdown_drain_timeout_s` via direct attribute access guarded only by truthiness on the parent objects would raise `AttributeError` on Pydantic v2 when the named field was absent — the exact transitional case (process started pre-AD-820/pre-AD-825 shutting down with newer `shutdown.py` on disk) that AD-825 was designed to survive. New module-private `_memory_field(runtime, name, default)` helper uses `getattr(cfg, name, default)` (bypasses Pydantic's strict `__getattr__`). All three call sites collapsed onto the helper; defaults (30.0) and observable behaviour preserved. Closes #764. +5 pytest in `tests/test_bf291_shutdown_field_absence.py`. Zero new deps. Zero schema/config changes.

**BF-290 shipped (2026-05-22).** ProfileChatTab voice input — PTT stuck after whisper fallback (missing `setListening(false)` on the give-up early-return at line 762; missing `disarmWhisperStt()` on the next-press stop branch — whisperStt is a separate subsystem `stopListening()` doesn't reach) and conversation mode silently broken (missing `onAgentReply` / `onStateChange` handlers in the `ArmOptions` passed to `armConversationMode()`; controller called `_opts?.onAgentReply?.(replyText)` on undefined and got stuck in `agent_speaking` forever because `markAgentReplyComplete()` was never reached). Surgical fix in `ui/src/components/profile/ProfileChatTab.tsx`: 2 SEARCH/REPLACE in the PTT click handler + import of `markAgentReplyComplete` / `onSpeechEvent` + full `onAgentReply` wiring (append-to-thread via `addAgentMessage`, `speakResponse` when per-agent TTS enabled with one-shot `onSpeechEvent` subscription that fires `markAgentReplyComplete()` on the matching `'end'` event, or immediate completion when TTS disabled) + cosmetic `onStateChange` debug log. Closes #763. +5 vitest in `ui/src/__tests__/ProfileChatTab.bf290.test.tsx` (2 PTT cleanup, 3 conversation handler wiring). UI build green: bundle compiles per BF-279 gate. Zero new deps. Tracker: 13911 → 13911 pytest (UI-only change); vitest 901 → 906.

**AD-825 shipped (2026-05-22).** Drain-before-cancel shutdown semantics. New `ProbOSRuntime._drain_tasks: set[asyncio.Task]` registry + `_shutdown_event: asyncio.Event` + `_signal_drain_stop()` helper; `_spawn_background(...)` grows kw-only `drain_on_shutdown: bool = False` parameter that routes write-holding loops to the drain registry. New `MemoryConfig.shutdown_drain_timeout_s` (default 30s, range 1.0..300.0). New drain phase in `startup/shutdown.py` BEFORE the AD-824 cancel sweep: signals the event, `asyncio.wait(_drain_tasks, timeout=shutdown_drain_timeout_s)`, logs WARNING for any task that doesn't drain in time (the AD-824 cancel sweep that runs immediately after now also reaps any drain task that didn't exit cleanly — drain is best-effort, cancel is the fallback). `_episodic_backup_task` migrated to `drain_on_shutdown=True`; its loop body replaces bare `asyncio.sleep(N)` with `asyncio.wait_for(self._shutdown_event.wait(), timeout=N)` + early-exit checks before/after each tar so an in-flight snapshot finishes cleanly instead of being cancelled mid-write. New `DreamScheduler.stop_gracefully(timeout)` (uses `asyncio.shield` so the timeout doesn't cancel the in-flight cycle) called from shutdown Phase 1 BEFORE the explicit `dream_cycle()` so the monitor loop's own cycle cannot collide with the explicit one (concurrent-writer hazard on the same Chroma collection was the suspected cause of today's `consolidation_result=failed` events). Closes #760. +7 pytest. Zero new deps.

**AD-822b shipped (2026-05-23).** Boot-time HNSW file validation — pre-open structural probe that runs in the AD-822 subprocess before `chromadb.PersistentClient(...)` opens the collection. New `validate_hnsw_files(data_dir)` in `src/probos/episodic_health.py` parses the 100-byte chroma-hnswlib `header.bin` via `struct.unpack("<IQQQQQQiIQQQdQ", ...)` (layout verified 2026-05-23 against preserved corrupted dir; u32 version prefix + 13 upstream hnswlib `saveIndex` fields) and checks `cur_element_count <= max_elements`, `size_data_per_element` in `[64, 65536]`, `data_level0.bin` size = `size_dpe * max_elements`, `length.bin` entry count = `max_elements`, `link_lists.bin` exists. Wired into `_episodic_probe.py` between the chroma.sqlite3 marker check and the client open; on failure emits `hnsw-validation: <err>` to stderr and exits 5 (new code, reserved alongside AD-822's 0/1/2). `EpisodicHealthResult.file_validation_failed: bool` propagates the distinction; `EpisodicCorruptionDetected` gains kw-only `file_validation_failed` parameter whose True branch leads the operator message with "HNSW index appears truncated or corrupted". Read-only (never opens `data_level0.bin`); <2ms per dir on warm cache. Three-layer defense complete: AD-820 (unclean shutdown gating) + AD-822 (subprocess segfault isolation) + AD-822b (pre-open structural file probe). Closes #755. +7 pytest in `tests/test_ad822b_hnsw_validation.py`. AD-820..826 + BF-295 regression suite (69 tests) remains green. Zero new deps.

**BF-289 shipped (2026-05-22).** Episode-migration upserts now chunk under ChromaDB 1.5.8's per-call batch cap (5461). After today's AD-819 rebuild produced 11,539 episodes, BF-103's sovereign-ID migration tried to upsert 9,514 ids at once and Chroma rejected it; the migration's outer `try/except` logged WARNING and boot continued, but the episodes stayed in legacy slot-ID format. New `_MIGRATION_BATCH_SIZE = 2000` constant in `src/probos/cognitive/episodic.py`; BF-103 (`migrate_episode_agent_ids`) and AD-570 (`migrate_anchor_metadata`) full-collection upserts now chunk through it. Per-chunk failure is logged at WARNING and skipped — surviving chunks persist so a re-run only mops up the failed slice. Returned `migrated` count reflects successful chunks only. AD-584/AD-605/BF-207/live-path writes already safe (audited in prompt). Closes #762. +2 pytest in `tests/test_bf103_episode_id_mismatch.py` (`TestMigrationChunking`). Zero new deps.

**BF-288 shipped (2026-05-22).** `probos rebuild-episodic` now resets the AD-820 shutdown marker on success. After a non-dry-run rebuild with no errors and `episodes_written > 0`, the CLI handler `_cmd_rebuild_episodic` calls `mark_clean_shutdown(data_dir, consolidation_result="rebuilt", note=...)` so the next `probos serve` is not blocked by the `consolidation_result=failed` left over from the crash that forced the rebuild. Dry-run and failed-rebuild paths leave the marker untouched (operator still sees AD-820's warning). Extends `ConsolidationResult` literal with `"rebuilt"`; AD-820's boot gate only blocks on `failed`, so `"rebuilt"` boots cleanly with `status="partial"`. Closes #761. +2 pytest in `tests/test_ad819_rebuild_episodic.py` (`TestShutdownMarkerReset`). Zero new deps.

**AD-824 shipped (2026-05-22).** Shutdown hygiene — centralized background-task cancellation. New `ProbOSRuntime._background_tasks: set[asyncio.Task]` registry + `_spawn_background(coro, name)` helper. Migrated `_episodic_backup_loop` (AD-823) to register through the helper; threaded `background_register` kw-only callable through `startup/infrastructure.py` and `startup/communication.py` so the previously-fire-and-forget `_event_log_prune_loop` and `_journal_prune_loop` are now tracked. Added `except asyncio.CancelledError: ... raise` arms to all three loops. New cancellation sweep in `startup/shutdown.py` immediately before the AD-820 `mark_clean_shutdown` write: `asyncio.wait(timeout=5.0)`, per-abandoned-task WARNING, never raises (so a stuck loop can never block the AD-820 integrity marker). Closes #759. +7 pytest. Zero new deps.

**AD-822 shipped (2026-05-22).** Subprocess-isolated ChromaDB boot-time health probe. New `src/probos/_episodic_probe.py` (read-only probe, exits 0 on healthy/first-boot/no-collection, 1 on any open/peek/count failure) + new `src/probos/episodic_health.py` (`check_episodic_health()` spawns probe via `subprocess.Popen` per shell_command `_run_sync` precedent — NOT `asyncio.create_subprocess_*`; tempfile stderr capture per BF-282; 30s default timeout with hung-process kill; `EpisodicCorruptionDetected` exception carrying operator-readable AD-819 remediation message; `PROBOS_SKIP_EPISODIC_HEALTH_CHECK=1` bypass with WARNING). Wired into `_serve` between AD-820 `check_previous_shutdown` and `_boot_runtime` (SystemExit(4); 2/3 already owned by AD-820). Closes #756. +7 pytest. Zero new deps.

**Wave 186 (2026-05-21) — Yeo native desktop tray app v1.**
- **AD-759** — Electron tray host wrapping the existing HXI at `http://127.0.0.1:8765`. New top-level `desktop/` workspace (MIT deps only: electron, electron-vite, electron-builder, vitest, typescript). Single-instance lock, `probos://` deep-link with strict sanitization (length ≤ 2048, path/query allowlist `[a-zA-Z0-9_-]+`, reject traversal/control/shell-metachars), native notifications with click-through routing, tray menu (8 actionable items), disconnected-state in-line HTML repair surface, preload-only `window.probos` IPC bridge (`contextIsolation: true`, `sandbox: true`, `nodeIntegration: false`). Assumes runtime is already running via AD-751 lifecycle (no Python spawning). Closes [#705](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/705). +24 vitest. Forward markers AD-759a/b/c/d/e + AD-759-1/-2 filed in roadmap for launch-at-login, installer, auto-update, code-signing (commercial), CI release pipeline, macOS/Linux parity.

**Wave 180 in flight (2026-05-19) — live-use triage build (BF-317 + BF-318 + AD-746 + AD-747). Architect-only draft. Continuous-build dispatch authorized (Builder runs immediately after this commit).**
- **BF-317** — Share-screen button discoverability in DM thread. Closes [#681](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/681). UI-only fix to `ui/src/components/wardroom/WardRoomThreadDetail.tsx`: AD-744 share-screen button (verified `data-testid="wardroom-dm-share-screen-button"` at line 397) gains a persistent `Share screen` text label, larger glyph (18×18 vs 14×14 attach), filled monitor base differentiating it from the line-only paperclip attach button, and a 1px divider separator between attach and share-screen affordances. HXI Design Principle #1 ("the system understands the human"). Zero backend changes. +3 vitest. Forward marker BF-317-1 (composer tool palette) when share-class affordances ≥ 3. **Status: drafted (`prompts/bf-317-share-screen-discoverability.md`).**
- **BF-318** — `speechRecognitionArbiter` priority-queue leases. Closes [#683](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/683). NEW module `ui/src/audio/speechRecognitionArbiter.ts` (~120 lines) owns the singleton SpeechRecognition device with `acquire(holder, priority, onAcquired, onPreempted, onReleased)` / `release` / `currentHolder` surface. Priority constants `PRIORITY_PRESS_TO_TALK=100`, `PRIORITY_CONVERSATION=75` (reserved for AD-747), `PRIORITY_WAKE_WORD=50`. `ui/src/audio/speechInput.ts` (`activeRecognition` singleton at line 28) wired through the arbiter; `ui/src/audio/wakeWord.ts:_startContinuousRecognition` (line 322) requests `PRIORITY_WAKE_WORD` and re-arms on `onReleased` after press-to-talk preempt. VAD (`voiceActivity.ts:213` dedicated `getUserMedia` stream) is NOT in scope — separate device path. Zero backend changes. +6 vitest. **Hard prerequisite for AD-747.** **Status: drafted (`prompts/bf-318-mic-arbiter.md`).**
- **AD-746** — Camera + screen source policy (layered fusion + per-agent binding). Closes [#682](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/682). Two layers, both default-ON: **Layer 1** new `src/probos/perception/aggregator.py` `VisionAggregator` inserted BETWEEN source admitters and `VisionConsumer` — when camera + screen frames arrive within `fusion_window_ms=800` (Pipecat `VisionAggregator` pattern, BSD-2-Clause), they are fused into a single multimodal intent message (`params.attachment_refs: list[str]`, `params.sources: list[str]`, `params.fused: True`). Single-source frames pass through unchanged (passthrough preserves legacy contract). AD-733c-6 budget: fused = 1 call. **Layer 2** extends `CrewProfile.perception.camera_device_id` shape (AD-742c) with `bound_sources: list[Literal["camera","screen"]]` field (default both — back-compat). `VisionConsumer._handle` early branch (extending AD-742c `bound_agent_ids` filter at consumer.py:~430) filters frames against active agent's `bound_sources`. Fused frames visible when ANY bound source intersects. Two new `PerceptionConfig` fields (`source_fusion_enabled=True`, `fusion_window_ms=800`, hot-reload). Two new API routes: `GET /api/perception/sources`, `POST /api/perception/sources/binding` (mirror AD-742c shape). HXI extends `PerceptionLivePanel.tsx` CAMERA BINDINGS → SOURCE BINDINGS with per-agent CAMERA/SCREEN check pills (HXI #3 no emoji). AD-541b anchor metadata extends `source: str` → `sources: list[str]` (legacy field preserved one wave; AD-746-5 retires). AD-731 source-scan extends to `aggregator.py`. +14 pytest, +4 vitest. Forward markers AD-746-1 (raw priority knob) / -2 (cross-modal salience scorer) / -3 (audio third source — pairs AD-747) / -4 (browser-side fusion preview) / -5 (legacy `source:str` retirement). **Status: drafted (`prompts/ad-746-camera-screen-source-policy.md`).**
- **AD-747** — ConversationController (always-on natural conversation mode). Closes [#684](https://github.com/seangalliher/ProbOS/issues/684). NEW module `ui/src/audio/conversationController.ts` (~250 lines) owns the duplex when a DM thread is open. State machine: `inactive → listening → transcribing → submitted → agent_speaking → silence_pending → inactive`. Acquires `PRIORITY_CONVERSATION=75` lease through the BF-318 arbiter; subscribes to `whisperStt.onTranscript` (AD-705a, `whisperStt.ts:184`) for STT chunks gated by VAD speech bounds (AD-733c-7, `voiceActivity.ts:195`); auto-submits transcripts to active agent's DM via existing `agent_chat` endpoint with a 750 ms edit window (transcript-preview pill, HXI #5); barge-in via `voice.stopSpeaking()` when VAD speech_start fires during `agent_speaking` (LiveKit `VoicePipelineAgent` pattern, Apache 2.0); 30 s silence timer releases the lease (matches ChatGPT advanced voice mode default per RESEARCH). Press-to-talk preserved but visually demoted when `conversation_mode_enabled=True` (preempts cleanly via arbiter `PRIORITY_PRESS_TO_TALK=100`). Three new `CognitiveConfig` fields (`conversation_mode_enabled=False` default-off transitional gate, `conversation_silence_timeout_ms=30000`, `conversation_barge_in_enabled=True`; all hot-reload). HXI: new CONV badge in `CameraLiveIndicator.tsx` (amber armed, pulse during agent_speaking); transcript-preview pill in `WardRoomThreadDetail.tsx`. AD-733c-7 privacy invariant preserved (audio bytes never leave browser; source-scan asserts no PCM in fetch body). +6 pytest, +14 vitest. Zero new deps. Forward markers AD-747-1 (Hume EVI prosody-gated barge-in) / -2 (cross-agent handoff) / -3 (Vapi interruption sensitivity knob) / -4 (goodbye classifier) / -5 (server-side streaming STT, pairs AD-705a-4) / -6 (multi-Captain voice profile binding) / -7 (audio fused into AD-746, pairs AD-746-3). **Status: drafted (`prompts/ad-747-conversation-controller.md`). Strict build order: ships AFTER BF-318.**

Browser-native `SpeechRecognition` Tier-2 fallback preserved (forward marker AD-705a-7 for fully-offline mode). +5 pytest, +8 vitest. +2 `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md` entries (whisper.cpp MIT + Whisper model weights MIT). Forward markers AD-705a-1 (transformers.js alternative) / -2 (IndexedDB cache) / -3 (lazy CDN) / -4 (streaming decode) / -5 (Moonshine backend) / -6 (multilingual / larger models) / -7 (fully-offline mode) / -8 (transcript audit log). **Status: SHIPPED Wave 179.**
- **AD-705c** — Custom wake-word training pipeline. Closes #557. New `probos wake-word` CLI subcommand (`collect / train / test / status`) in `src/probos/experience/commands/commands_wake_word.py`; new `src/probos/voice/wake_word_trainer.py` `WakeWordTrainer` service module running openWakeWord training via `loop.run_in_executor` (BF-280); new `src/probos/routers/voice.py` (or extension to existing) with three `require_crew_scope` endpoints (`POST /api/voice/wake-word/sample`, `POST /api/voice/wake-word/train`, `GET /api/voice/wake-word/training-status`); training task held in `runtime._wake_word_trainer_tasks: set[asyncio.Task]` per async discipline; new `ui/src/components/wakeword/WakeWordTrainerPanel.tsx` guided recorder UX (countdown → record-4s → upload → next-sample → train-now → polling → activate); modifies `ui/src/audio/wakeWord.ts:_loadOnnxRuntime` (SINGLE BF-274 block edit) to prefer `captain.onnx` over the stock community model. New `WakeWordConfig` Pydantic block with five fields (`wake_word_trainer_enabled`, `custom_model_filename`, `retain_training_samples`, `training_samples_max_count`, `training_audio_max_bytes`); three new `FieldDescriptor`s in the AD-741 Voice section. **No `openwakeword` in `pyproject.toml`** — operator-installed separately via the documented `pip install openwakeword[training]` instruction; CLI honest-degrades when the package is absent. Captain's brief mentions "AD-733c-3's Picovoice path"; the actual codebase uses openWakeWord — surfaced as a clarification, not a blocker. +12 pytest, +4 vitest. +1 `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md` entry (openWakeWord Apache 2.0). Forward markers AD-705c-1 (negatives-fetch script) / -2 (Howl transfer learning) / -3 (EfficientWord-Net few-shot) / -4 (multi-Captain wake words) / -5 (Counselor-suggested retrain on FAR spike). **Status: SHIPPED Wave 179.**

**Wave 175 in flight (2026-05-18):**
- AD-733c-6 — Engaged-mode vision LLM call budget enforcement (closes #677). Extends AD-742e telemetry into enforcement. New `_maybe_enforce_budget(session_id)` method called from `_record_vision_call` after the counter increment: when mode is ENGAGED and total session calls >= cap OR total today calls >= cap, the consumer synchronously calls `controller.transition_to(Mode.AMBIENT, trigger="budget_exhausted")` and logs a once-per-session WARNING. Three new `PerceptionConfig` fields (`engaged_budget_enforcement=True`, `engaged_call_cap_per_session=200`, `engaged_call_cap_per_day=2000`) — all hot-reload per BF-308 (cap dial mid-session, on/off without restart). New `_budget_cap_notified_sessions: set[str]` (cleared on session change so subsequent sessions get their own one-shot WARNING). `get_budget_snapshot()` extended with `cap_per_session` + `cap_per_day` + `enforcement_enabled` + `cap_reached_session` + `cap_reached_day` fields; `session_ceiling_estimate` retained for AD-742e backcompat but now maps to `cap_per_session` (default 200 vs AD-742e heuristic 120). `mode_controller.Transition.trigger` docstring extended to enumerate `"budget_exhausted"`. `VisionBudgetBadge.tsx` reads `cap_per_session` + `cap_reached_session` + `enforcement_enabled` — color scale shifted: green <80%, orange 80-99%, red >=100%; dim grey override when enforcement_enabled=false. Anti-deadlock confirmed: `transition_to` is synchronous; safe to call while holding `_describe_lock` (no async acquisition, no re-entry). `_maybe_enforce_budget` is defense-in-depth — if perception config or controller is missing, silently skips (counter behavior preserved). +9 pytest in `tests/test_ad733c6_engaged_budget_enforcement.py` (under-cap no-transition, session-cap hit drops, day-cap hit drops, enforcement-disabled no-transition, ambient-mode no-transition, WARNING rate-limited per session, session-change resets notification, snapshot exposes caps, hot-reload cap change effective on next call). +3 vitest in `VisionBudgetBadge.test.tsx` (orange via cap_per_session, red via cap_reached_session, dim via enforcement_enabled=false); 6 existing AD-742e color-scheme vitests updated to the new green/orange/red palette. UI bundle: `index-DPxUl-OZ.js`. Zero new pip / npm deps. 0-line diff on all 5 license files. Forward markers filed: AD-733c-6-1 (daily-counter restart persistence), AD-733c-6-2 (WardRoom cap-hit notification).

- AD-742d — Pluggable VisionSupervisor strategies (closes #672). Four new `SupervisorStrategy` implementations alongside `PerceptualHashStrategy`: `MotionStrategy` (per-pixel L1 diff after 32x32 grayscale downscale; catches small motion that aHash blurs), `SceneChangeStrategy` (16-bin HSV histogram chi-square-style distance on 64x64 downscale; catches lighting shifts + new objects entering frame), `NeverDescribeStrategy` (drops every frame — cost-tight deployments, describe only on force/DM-receive paths), `AlwaysAdmitStrategy` (admits every frame — debug + test scaffolding). All four implement the BF-308 setters (`set_min_interval_seconds`, `set_novelty_threshold`, `set_baseline_max_age_seconds`) so hot-reload of cap values works uniformly; null strategies accept-and-ignore. New `STRATEGY_REGISTRY` (name -> class) + `build_strategy()` resolver with honest-degrade-to-aHash on unknown name. New `PerceptionConfig.vision_supervisor_strategy` field (default `"ahash"` — current behavior preserved bit-for-bit) + Pydantic validator restricting to the five names. New non-hot-reload FieldDescriptor (strategy swap orphans previous baseline state — restart required per GATE 1 §3). `VisionConsumer.__init__` accepts `supervisor_strategy_name` kwarg defaulting to `"ahash"`; `startup/finalize.py` threads `vision_supervisor_strategy` through. New shared `_load_pil_image` helper (separate from `_ahash_jpeg_bytes`). Tier-2 honest-degrade: every strategy allows the first frame and then throttles on decode failure; never raises. +12 pytest in `tests/test_ad742d_pluggable_supervisor.py` (registry contents, Protocol conformance, build_strategy resolution, unknown-name fallback with WARNING, motion admits/throttles/diff-detects, scene_change lighting-shift, never/always determinism, config validator rejects unknown, consumer init uses configured strategy). Real PIL JPEG fixtures over BytesIO; no MagicMock at strategy boundary (BF-287). Zero new pip / npm deps. 0-line diff on all 5 license files. Forward markers filed: AD-742d-1 (CLIP-embedding semantic strategy), AD-742d-2 (per-session strategy override).

- AD-742f — Per-agent vision working memory persistence across restart (closes #674). New `src/probos/perception/wm_store.py` ships `WorkingMemoryStore` (stdlib `sqlite3`, module-level write lock, schema with `agent_id` + `timestamp` + `attachment_ref` + `description` + `novelty_score` + `subject_identity` + `session_id` columns — NO BLOB columns per AD-731 invariant). `VisionWorkingMemory.__init__` now accepts optional `store` + `agent_id` kwargs: when both are present, the ring auto-hydrates from disk and every `append` mirrors to SQLite (best-effort, honest-degrades on lock/disk failure with WARNING). `consumer.py` factory gains `set_working_memory_store(store)` module setter + `_WM_STORE` module global; `get_or_create_working_memory` threads the store through; `reset_working_memories_for_tests` clears the handle. `startup/finalize.py` constructs `WorkingMemoryStore(data_dir / "perception_wm.db")` BEFORE `VisionConsumer` construction so observer registration sees a wired store; gated on new `PerceptionConfig.wm_persistence_enabled` (default True, hot-reload — toggles WRITE path only). New `FieldDescriptor("perception.wm_persistence_enabled")` in Perception settings section. Tier-2 honest-degrade: unwritable path → `store.available=False` → WM operates as in-memory ring (legacy behavior bit-for-bit preserved when persistence disabled or store unavailable). AD-541b chroma episodic_memory unchanged (long-term anchored episodes still go through that path; WM persistence is a separate per-agent narrower store). +10 pytest in `tests/test_ad742f_wm_persistence.py` (schema init, append+load roundtrip, capacity cap on load, eviction-beyond-capacity through WM, per-agent isolation on clear, honest-degrade on unwritable path, in-memory-only when store=None creates no .db, AD-731 invariant source-check NO BLOB columns, factory threads store, factory reset clears handle). Zero new pip / npm deps. 0-line diff on all 5 license files. Forward marker filed: AD-742f-1 (federation cross-host WM sync), AD-742f-2 (TTL-based pruning).

- AD-742b — Face-embedding identity recognition (closes #670). Replaces AD-733b's per-session LLM identity prompt with a local face-embedding model. New `src/probos/perception/identity.py` ships `IdentityResolver` (MTCNN face detection + InceptionResnetV1 512-d embedding via facenet-pytorch MIT) + `_cosine_distance` helper. Lazy-loaded models (one process-wide instance); thread offloaded via `loop.run_in_executor` since MTCNN/Resnet are sync + CPU-bound. Two new `PerceptionConfig` fields (`identity_match_threshold=0.6`, `identity_resolver_enabled=True`); `captain_avatar_ref` retained but deprecated. `VisionConsumer._resolve_subject_identity` first tries the resolver path (`is_enrolled` gated); falls back to the AD-733b LLM-prompt path only when `identity_resolver_enabled=False` AND `captain_avatar_ref` is set — preserves the legacy code path for CI/test envs without facenet-pytorch. `startup/finalize.py` constructs the resolver next to `VisionConsumer` when perception is enabled; on import failure, logs WARNING and falls back to legacy path. Three new API endpoints under `require_crew_scope`: `POST /api/perception/identity/enroll` (multipart, 10 MB cap, 422 on no-face-detected, embedding written via atomic .tmp→rename), `DELETE /api/perception/identity`, `GET /api/perception/identity` (returns `{enrolled, resolver_wired, model_id}` — no embedding leaked). **Privacy invariants**: reference photo bytes discarded after enrollment (the 2 KB embedding is the only persistent artifact); `data/captain_identity.json` gitignored (explicit + `data/*` wildcard); embedding stored locally — NOT in AttachmentStore (AD-731 invariant preserved: that's about RPC bus payloads, not local lifecycle artifacts); operator opt-out via DELETE endpoint OR `identity_resolver_enabled=False`. One new pip dep: `facenet-pytorch>=2.5` (MIT verified via PEP 639 classifier; pretrained VGGFace2 weights Apache-2.0). `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md` stamped. +19 pytest in `tests/test_ad742b_face_embedding_identity.py` (enrollment, persistence, revoke, no-face raises, resolve captain/other/unknown, threshold operator-tunable, config defaults, cosine_distance helper edge cases, privacy invariant — reference bytes never leak to disk, VisionConsumer integration — resolver path used when enrolled, fallback to unknown when not enrolled). Tracker: 14119 → 14138.

Three engagement hooks: `note_dm_activity()` step-wise ramp (DORMANT→AMBIENT→ENGAGED, one step per call; ENGAGED only refreshes `last_dm_activity_at`); `note_high_novelty_event()` (AMBIENT→ENGAGED only — observer wired); `note_wake_word()` stub (rewritten by AD-733c-3). Timestamps use `time.time()` throughout (Captain Required override — operator-facing `/api/perception/mode` GET would surface meaningless monotonic values otherwise). `start()`/`stop()` lifecycle holds an `asyncio.create_task` reference, catches CancelledError, re-raises per async-discipline rule; `_run()` body is a 30s tick stub (AD-733c-4 fills with idle drop-back). `routers/perception.py` gains `GET /api/perception/mode` (returns `{mode, since, last_dm_activity, presets, transitions}`) and `POST /api/perception/mode {mode}` (manual override; trigger="manual" bypasses cooldown); both behind `require_crew_scope`. `routers/agents.py:agent_chat` extends the AD-733c-1 force-describe block with a `controller.note_dm_activity()` call so the conversational tempo drives mode transitions. `perception/observer.py` calls `controller.note_high_novelty_event()` after a successful proactive DM dispatch — AMBIENT→ENGAGED on the "you sat down" trigger. `startup/finalize.py` wires the controller next to VisionConsumer with `initial_mode=AMBIENT` + `await controller.start()`; `startup/shutdown.py` mirrors the `recording_reaper` pattern with `await runtime.perception_mode_controller.stop()`. New `ui/src/store/usePerceptionModeStore.ts` Zustand slice exports `PerceptionMode` / `PerceptionTransition` / `PerceptionPreset` types + `refresh()` + `setMode(mode)` actions. `CameraLiveIndicator.tsx` adds a stroke-bordered Mode badge after the CAMERA LIVE span (amber DORMANT/AMBIENT/ENGAGED text, HXI Principle #3 — no emoji). `PerceptionLivePanel.tsx` gets a MODE section above vision-config warnings with three text buttons + last-3-transitions list. AD-731 invariant extended: `test_ad731_invariant_no_inline_base64_in_perception_modules` now scans `mode_controller.py`. AD-541b anchored episodes still written on every force-describe path (no change to `_anchor_episode`). +13 pytest in `tests/test_ad733c2_mode_controller.py` (initial state, preset push via real PerceptualHashStrategy, same-mode no-op, programmatic cooldown blocks, manual override bypasses, three note_* hooks, watchdog start/stop clean, GET endpoint, POST endpoint, AD-731 invariant scan). +6 vitest in `CameraLiveIndicator.modeBadge.test.tsx` (3) + `PerceptionLivePanel.modeSection.test.tsx` (3). - AD-733b — ProactiveVisionObserver + identity hook (closes #666). New `src/probos/perception/observer.py` ships `ProactiveVisionObserver` + `ProactiveBudget`. Two triggers in v1: `scene_introduction` (fires once per camera session on the first WM observation, bypassing the novelty threshold) and `high_novelty` (novelty >= 0.50, bounded by `max_emissions_per_session=3` AND `min_dwell_seconds=30s`). The observer does NOT compose user-facing reply text — it sends a `[SYSTEM-INITIATED: ...]` synthesized user-turn to the agent so the agent's own LLM composes the visible message using its voice profile. **Identity hook** in `VisionConsumer._process` calls the vision LLM with the Captain reference avatar SHA + the live frame on the first frame per session and parses the one-word response (`captain` | `other` | `unknown`), populating `VisionObservation.subject_identity`. AD-731 invariant preserved (identity hook reads BOTH images through `AttachmentStore.read(sha)`; bus message stays ref-only). +10 pytest in `tests/test_ad733b_proactive_observer.py` (scene-intro fires once, low-novelty blocks, dwell blocks, budget exhaustion blocks, session reset clears state, identity resolves captain when configured, identity unknown when no reference, proactive_observer_enabled=False propagates as config-level guard). - AD-733a — Vision consumer + supervisor + working memory + DM context injection (closes #665). Three new modules under `src/probos/perception/`: `supervisor.py` ships pluggable `SupervisorStrategy` Protocol + default `PerceptualHashStrategy` (temporal throttle floor + 64-bit aHash diff, Pillow-only, pure Python); `working_memory.py` per-agent `VisionWorkingMemory` ring buffer (default capacity 8) whose `render_for_prompt` emits a `--- Current Visual Context ---` block (BF-294 confabulation guard: empty buffer renders an explicit "no current visual data" sentinel with "Do NOT describe what you cannot see"); `consumer.py` runtime-owned `VisionConsumer` subscribes to `vision_observation`, runs the supervisor gate, calls the AD-732 vision tier via `build_multimodal_messages` (BF-268 OpenAI-shape resolver), writes to every registered observer's WM, and anchors an `Episode` at `importance=6` with `AnchorFrame(channel="perception", trigger_type="vision_described")`. Five new `PerceptionConfig` fields (`vision_consumer_enabled` / `vision_min_interval_seconds` / `vision_novelty_threshold` / `working_memory_capacity` / `vision_tier`). `startup/finalize.py` wiring iterates `runtime.registry.all()` per BF-287 (NEVER `_agents`); only crew agents with `callsign_registry.get_profile(...).vision_capable=True` are registered as observers; `runtime.vision_consumer` exposed as the public attribute. `routers/agents.py` prepends `render_for_prompt()` ahead of the bus send (mirroring AD-725's `targeted_recall_block` pattern), gated on `runtime.config.perception.enabled` so disabling the subsystem cleanly removes the block. **NeuralCompanion three-tier pattern (Source/Supervisor/Consumer) absorbed under MIT — architecture only, no code copied.** AD-731 invariant rigorously enforced: frames stay as SHA refs end-to-end; new source-scan test `test_ad731_invariant_no_inline_base64_in_perception_modules` asserts ZERO `b64encode` / `base64.b64` / `blob_b64` in any new perception module. **Eight-guard catalog NOT retriggered** — reuses the single AD-732 `vision` tier; AD-742a (#669) splits into `vision_fast`/`vision_deep` later. +19 pytest in `tests/test_ad733a_vision_consumer.py` (6 supervisor, 4 WM, 5 consumer, 3 integration, 1 AD-731 source scan). +1 pytest in `tests/test_wave171_acceptance.py` — Captain's acceptance test (`test_captain_holds_glass_ezri_describes_it`) drives the full end-to-end path: frame → AttachmentStore → bus → supervisor → vision LLM → working memory → DM scene-block injection → importance=6 episode. Zero new pip deps (Pillow already resident), zero new npm deps, zero license diff. Forward markers referenced in DECISIONS.md: AD-742a (#669), AD-742d (#672), AD-742f (#674). Tracker: 14012 → 14032.

- AD-720c — Cloud file picker (OAuth-bound source, Google Drive v1) (closes #551). New `src/probos/cloud_pickers/` module exports `OAuthTokenBundle` + `CsrfStateStore` (tokens.py), `CloudPickerProvider` Protocol + `ProviderError`/`ReauthorizationRequired` (provider.py), `GoogleDriveProvider` v1 working backend, and `OneDriveProvider`/`DropboxProvider` stubs (forward markers AD-720c-1/-2). New `src/probos/routers/cloud_pickers.py` exposes 4 endpoints under `/api/cloud-pickers/{provider}` (POST start, GET callback, GET files, POST attach). OAuth tokens persisted exclusively via AD-706f `CredentialVault` as `OAuthTokenBundle.model_dump_json()` under ref shape `cloud_provider:{provider}:{captain_id}` with `CredentialScope()` empty-frozenset (captain-only per credentials.py:40-58); the AD-706f invariant is preserved (NO new credential store). Google consent URL includes `access_type=offline&prompt=consent` so a refresh_token is issued on every authorization. Refresh-on-401: `_with_refresh_retry` helper attempts a single `grant_type=refresh_token` exchange on 401, preserves the original refresh_token when the response omits one (Google rotates rarely), retries the original request exactly once, and surfaces `ReauthorizationRequired` (→ 401 `reauthorization_required` + vault entry deleted) on refresh failure or second 401. PKCE skipped per OAuth 2.1 §1.5 — ProbOS is a confidential client with server-stored `client_secret`; in-memory CSRF state guard (32-byte url-safe token, single-consume, default 5-min TTL) backs the callback. AD-731 invariant preserved: file bytes flow through the shared `_validate_and_store_attachment` → `AttachmentStore.write(sha, blob, mime)` chain (the same defense-in-depth path used by `POST /api/chat/attachments` and the multipart endpoint); the HTTP response carries only `{attachment_id, mime, size_bytes, filename}` — never raw bytes. Honest-degrade matrix: 503 `feature_disabled` (master switch off), 503 `credential_vault_unavailable` (vault precondition unmet), 503 `provider_disabled` / `provider_not_configured`, 401 `oauth_not_authorized` / `reauthorization_required`, 413 `file_too_large`, 403 `invalid_state_token`. New `CloudPickersConfig` + per-provider `CloudPickerProviderConfig` on `ProbOSConfig` (default-OFF master switch + per-provider default-OFF + BYOC operator-supplied client_id/client_secret/redirect_uri). New `ui/src/components/CloudPicker.tsx` modal: provider selector (only enabled providers render), authorize button opens auth_url in popup + listens for `oauth_complete` postMessage, file list with search + pagination, file click POSTs `/attach` and invokes `onAttached({attachment_id,...})`. HXI Design Principle #3 honored — inline stroke SVG icons, no emoji. +22 pytest in `tests/test_ad720c_provider_protocol.py` (3) + `tests/test_ad720c_google_drive.py` (7, httpx.MockTransport injected via `http_client_factory`) + `tests/test_ad720c_state_store.py` (4, TTL + single-consume + provider-mismatch + purge) + `tests/test_ad720c_endpoints.py` (8, real `SystemConfig()` + real `EncryptedFileCredentialVault` per BF-287; covers feature/vault/provider 503 matrix, /start happy path, /callback CSRF rejection + bundle persistence roundtrip, /files 401 honest-degrade, /attach AD-731 invariant assertion). +7 vitest in `ui/src/components/__tests__/CloudPicker.test.tsx` (provider filter, modal open/close, authorize popup + postMessage flow, attach happy path, 503 banner, 401 reauth banner, close button). UI build: `index-DqmvgWw2.js`. Zero new pip deps, zero new npm deps — `httpx` already resident from Wave 162, `cryptography` already resident from Wave 166; OAuth state uses stdlib `secrets` + `urllib.parse`. Forward markers filed with TECHNICAL triggers per AD-722c-3: AD-720c-1 OneDrive (trigger = operator demand after Google Drive exercised end-to-end ≥1 wave), AD-720c-2 Dropbox (same trigger). **Wave 167 in flight (2026-05-17):**
- AD-721i-2 — VRoid Studio CLI alternative backend evaluation (closes #543; **REJECTED**). Research-only deliverable; zero code, zero deps, zero tests. New `docs/research/vroid-cli-evaluation.md` records the disposition: VRoid Studio is GUI-only with no documented headless / CLI mode, distributed under a Pixiv-controlled proprietary EULA (not OSI-recognized), Windows + macOS only (no Linux), and the GUI export is not byte-deterministic. Any one of those three constraints (no CLI, proprietary, Linux-incompatible) would be sufficient to reject as the OSS-default renderer backend. Together they make VRoid a non-starter. The existing AD-721i Blender + saturday06 VRM-Addon backend stays as v1. Operator-elected VRoid output remains viable as a non-default path via AD-721h upload UI or AD-721g `_baselines/` directory — both consume operator-installed bytes and impose no license claim on the produced VRMs. No forward marker filed; re-evaluation only triggers if Pixiv publishes a CLI or open-source release.
- AD-720b — Chat tool attach: in-chat capability grants for BrowserTool / MCP (closes #550). New `POST /api/chat/tool-grant` endpoint reuses `ToolPermissionStore.issue_grant` (AD-423a/b/c) so a grant issued via chat is indistinguishable on disk from one issued via the `/tool-access grant` shell slash-command — both have `issued_by="captain"`. - AD-721i-1 — License-audited starter asset pack (closes #542). v1 ships **zero asset bytes** — only the audit infrastructure. New `data/avatar-assets/MANIFEST.md` audit ledger with markdown-table schema (name / source_url / license / version / sha256 / attribution / disposition); 4 RESEARCH candidates (Quaternius CC0 humanoid + hair + outfit, Khronos glTF-Sample-Assets CC0, KayKit CC0, Poly Haven CC0) and 4 REJECTED entries with documented reasons (MakeHuman AGPL-3.0 propagates copyleft; Mixamo Adobe TOS; Ready Player Me proprietary; VRoid Studio per-file metadata leak). Zero APPROVED rows — every candidate stays RESEARCH until Captain ruling flips it. New `src/probos/avatars/asset_manifest.py` exposes `AssetManifestEntry` NamedTuple + `AssetManifest.load/approved/by_disposition` parser + `validate_license` whitelist hard-coded to `{CC0, CC0-1.0, MIT, Apache-2.0, Apache 2.0, BSD, BSD-2-Clause, BSD-3-Clause, CC-BY-4.0, CC-BY}`; anything outside (GPL/AGPL/CC-BY-SA/CC-BY-NC/proprietary) returns False. Parser is lenient — skips rows with invalid disposition + logs warning, returns empty manifest on missing file (honest-degrade, never raises). New `scripts/avatar-assets-fetch.ps1` mirrors `piper-voice-fetch.ps1` shape: parses MANIFEST.md, filters to APPROVED, downloads each via `Invoke-WebRequest` to `data/avatar-assets/_<category>/<name>.<ext>`, verifies SHA-256 against the manifest (mismatch → delete + non-zero exit), writes attribution to `ATTRIBUTION.txt` (one line per asset for CC-BY compliance). v1 manifest has zero APPROVED rows so the script is a no-op with an informative "0 assets approved" message. `.gitignore` rules appended: `data/avatar-assets/_base_meshes/`, `_hair/`, `_outfits/`, `_materials/`, `ATTRIBUTION.txt` (manifest itself is tracked — it's the audit ledger). New `docs/development/avatar-assets.md` documents propose / fetch / revoke workflows and the license policy. +6 pytest in `tests/test_ad721i_1_asset_manifest.py`: v1 manifest parses; v1 has zero APPROVED rows (guard against accidental promotion); every row's disposition is valid; whitelist accepts all CC0/MIT/Apache/BSD/CC-BY shapes; whitelist rejects GPL/AGPL/CC-BY-SA/CC-BY-NC/proprietary/empty; synthetic mixed-disposition manifest filters correctly. AD-731 invariant n/a (asset bytes flow through filesystem to Blender, not bus). Full gate: 13869 → 13875 (incl. 1 known dreaming flake outside this wave per dispatch).
