The voice picker filters `availableVoices` by the selected lang code (split on `_`/`-`). `scripts/piper-voice-fetch.ps1` catalog expanded with 18 new entries across es/fr/de/it/nl/pt (sourced from `huggingface.co/rhasspy/piper-voices` — Apache-2.0/MIT; failed downloads honest-degrade in the existing per-voice try/catch). +8 pytest in `tests/test_ad718e_voice_language.py` (default `en`; empty → `en`; whitespace strip; invalid char rejection; accepts `es/fr/de/it/nl/pt/en-US/pt_BR`; `to_dict` includes `language`; `from_dict` missing-key backward-compat; CrewProfile roundtrip). +3 vitest in `ui/src/audio/__tests__/voice.language.test.ts` (es voice picked when available; falls back to en when language voice missing; en default when language undefined). +2 vitest in `ui/src/__tests__/ProfileInfoTab.langFilter.test.tsx` (filter dropdown lists distinct lang codes; selecting es filters the voice list). Real `VoiceProfile()` per BF-287; `vi.resetModules` per test to clear the cached voice. Full gate: 13861 → 13869 pytest; vitest 657 → 667. UI build green.

- AD-721b-1a — ffmpeg-backed audio format conversion for client-captured audio (closes #663). New helpers in `src/probos/avatars/rhubarb_backend.py`: `_resolve_ffmpeg_binary(configured: str) -> Path | None` mirrors `_resolve_binary_path` (Windows `.exe` fallback; empty / missing configured path returns None), and `_convert_to_wav(audio_path, ffmpeg_binary, *, timeout_seconds=30.0) -> Path | None` builds the `-y -i ... -ac 1 -ar 22050 -acodec pcm_s16le <tempfile>` arg-list, dispatches `subprocess.Popen` through `loop.run_in_executor` (BF-280), writes to `tempfile.NamedTemporaryFile(suffix='.wav', delete=False)` (BF-282 — never captures binary on stdout), unlinks the temp on TimeoutExpired / non-zero exit / empty-output, returns the temp Path on success (caller MUST `unlink(missing_ok=True)` in finally). `generate_visemes` gains keyword-only `ffmpeg_binary_path: str | None = None`; when the audio suffix is not in `_SUPPORTED_SUFFIXES` and ffmpeg is resolvable, transcodes first and rebinds `audio_path` to the temp WAV; the rhubarb invocation is extracted into a private `_run_rhubarb` helper so `generate_visemes` can wrap it in `try/finally` that unlinks the converted temp. If ffmpeg is missing OR conversion fails, the BF-292 honest-degrade contract is preserved (`generate_visemes` returns `[]`). One new `LipSyncConfig.ffmpeg_binary_path` field (`tools/ffmpeg/ffmpeg` — operator-provided binary; LGPL-2.1+ / GPL-2+ stays at the operator boundary because `/tools/` is gitignored, same pattern as rhubarb + piper). Both `routers/avatars.py` callsites (`/api/avatars/lipsync` and the TTS reuse path) updated to thread `ffmpeg_binary_path=cfg.ffmpeg_binary_path`. AD-731 invariant preserved (audio bytes flow through AttachmentStore SHA-256 refs in the existing pipeline; the temp WAV is local-only and short-lived). +9 pytest in `tests/test_ad721b_1a_ffmpeg_conversion.py`: 3 cover `_resolve_ffmpeg_binary` (missing / present / Windows .exe fallback), 3 cover `_convert_to_wav` (success creates tempfile + asserts `-i` and last-positional output path; timeout returns None + kills + unlinks; non-zero exit unlinks); 2 cover `generate_visemes` integration (ffmpeg+rhubarb chain unlinks converted temp; missing ffmpeg honest-degrades); 1 config sanity. Real `LipSyncConfig()` per BF-287; `_FakePopen` records args + emulates `communicate()` / `kill()` per BF-286. Full gate: 13852 → 13861.

- AD-706b — Browser session video recording + retention policy (closes #517). `BrowserSession.start()` now opens the Playwright `BrowserContext` with `record_video_dir=<recording_dir>/<session_id>` when `BrowserToolConfig.recording_enabled` is True (default-OFF transitional gate per Wave 10 convention #14); v1 keeps recordings on disk as Playwright's native `.webm` (no ffmpeg transcode — AD-706b-1 forward marker). `BrowserSession.stop()` emits `BROWSER_RECORDING_STOPPED` after `_context.close()` finalizes the file (with `size_bytes` summed across `*.webm` in the subdir) OR `BROWSER_RECORDING_FAILED` on close error — Tier-2 throughout, never raises. Four new EventType values (`BROWSER_RECORDING_STARTED`/`STOPPED`/`EXPIRED`/`FAILED`). Five new `BrowserToolConfig` fields: `recording_enabled` (False), `recording_dir` (`data/browser-sessions`), `recording_retention_days` (7 ge=1 le=365), `recording_reaper_interval_seconds` (3600 ge=60 le=86400), `recording_max_size_mb_per_session` (500 ge=10 le=5000). New `src/probos/tools/browser/recording_reaper.py` exposes `RecordingReaper` with `start()/stop()/reap_once()`: per-iteration sweep walks the recording root, deletes `*.webm` past `retention_days * 86400`, enforces the per-session size cap by removing oldest-first until under the bytes ceiling, removes empty session subdirs, and emits `BROWSER_RECORDING_EXPIRED` per delete with reason `retention` or `size_cap`. Async loop holds task reference per Async Discipline; CancelledError re-raised after cleanup; blocking filesystem work dispatched via `loop.run_in_executor`. `BrowserSession` ctor accepts an optional `emit_event` callable so recording lifecycle events surface without reaching across BrowserTool. Wiring split: synchronous `_wire_browser_tool` declares `runtime.recording_reaper = None`; new async `_start_recording_reaper` (called from `finalize_startup`) constructs + awaits `reaper.start()` when recording is enabled. `runtime.stop()` cleanup added in `startup/shutdown.py` (verified at `runtime.py:2227` — public async shutdown hook; `runtime.shutdown()` is NOT a method on the runtime class). New admin endpoints in `routers/browser_recordings.py`: `GET /api/browser/recordings` (list), `GET /api/browser/recordings/{session_id}/{filename}` (FileResponse), `DELETE /api/browser/recordings/{session_id}` (rmtree). All three behind `require_crew_scope`; path-traversal rejected via `.resolve()` prefix check. AD-731 invariant preserved (recordings stay on disk; AttachmentStore promotion forward-marked at AD-706b-2). +9 pytest in `tests/test_ad706b_session_recording.py` (real `BrowserToolConfig()` + handcrafted `_FakeBrowser`/`_FakeContext`/`_FakePlaywright` chain; recording dir parameterised via `tmp_path`; per BF-287). Full gate: 13843 → 13852.

- AD-706a — Captain-watch MJPEG streaming bridge (closes #516). New `src/probos/routers/browser_stream.py` exposes `GET /api/browser/sessions/{session_id}/stream` as `multipart/x-mixed-replace` MJPEG; the HXI consumer renders it in an ordinary `<img>` tag, zero new JS, zero new deps. `BrowserSession.get_streaming_url()` returns `/api/browser/sessions/{sid}/stream` when `BrowserToolConfig.streaming_enabled` is True (default-OFF transitional gate per Wave 10 convention #14). Four new `BrowserToolConfig` fields: `streaming_enabled` (False), `streaming_fps` (4, ge=1 le=15), `streaming_jpeg_quality` (60, ge=20 le=95), `streaming_max_concurrent_viewers` (4, ge=1 le=16). New public viewer-slot API on `BrowserTool` (`acquire_viewer_slot`/`release_viewer_slot`/`active_viewers`) backed by an `asyncio.Lock` — the streaming router does NOT touch `_active_viewers` directly (Demeter; no private member patching). 503 + `Retry-After: 5` when the cap is exhausted; 404 when the session is unknown. Three new EventType values (`BROWSER_STREAM_OPENED`/`BROWSER_STREAM_CLOSED`/`BROWSER_STREAM_FRAME_DROPPED`); OPENED + CLOSED are emitted around every viewer lifecycle (CancelledError re-raised after the CLOSED emit). `require_crew_scope` in `routers/auth.py` extended with a `Request` first param and a `?token=` query-param fallback so `<img src>` can carry the token; empty-string token is rejected explicitly (regression-protected). New `ui/src/components/browser/BrowserStreamPanel.tsx` renders a stroke-based SVG glyph when `streamingUrl` is null and a plain `<img>` otherwise (HXI Design Principle #3 — no emoji); appends `?token=` only when a non-empty token is provided. AD-731 invariant preserved (JPEG frames are ephemeral on-wire bytes, never stored — explicit in DECISIONS entry). +11 pytest in `tests/test_ad706a_browser_streaming.py` (real `SystemConfig()` + `BrowserToolConfig()` per BF-287; `_FakePage` raises after N frames so `TestClient` doesn't deadlock on the infinite generator) + 4 vitest in `ui/src/__tests__/BrowserStreamPanel.test.tsx`. Full gate: 13832 → 13843.

- AD-706f — Browser Tool credential vault (closes #521). New `src/probos/tools/browser/credentials.py` exports `CredentialScope` + `CredentialMetadata` frozen dataclasses, `CredentialVault` Protocol, `EncryptedFileCredentialVault` v1 backend, `_derive_kek(crew_scope_token)` helper, and `action_fill_credential` action. KEK derived from `AuthConfig.crew_scope_token` via stdlib `hashlib.scrypt` (n=2**14, r=8, p=1, dklen=32, salt=b"probos-credential-vault-v1"). Values encrypted via `cryptography.fernet.Fernet` (Apache-2.0/BSD dual — verified `License-Expression: Apache-2.0 OR BSD-3-Clause`; `cryptography>=42` added to `pyproject.toml`). One new pip dep this wave. Per-credential `CredentialScope`: `allowed_agent_ids` (empty set = Captain-only), `allowed_domains` (fnmatch against page host), `expires_at` (Unix timestamp, None = no expiry). `materialize_to_temp` writes plaintext to `tempfile.mkstemp` and returns the Path — CALLER MUST UNLINK in finally (contract documented). Five new EventType values inserted after `BROWSER_EVAL_JS_EXECUTED`: `CREDENTIAL_STORED`, `CREDENTIAL_READ`, `CREDENTIAL_READ_DENIED`, `CREDENTIAL_DELETED`, `CREDENTIAL_FILL_REQUESTED`. New nested `CredentialVaultConfig` Pydantic model under `BrowserToolConfig.credential_vault` (default-OFF transitional gate, `backend="file"` v1, `file_path="data/credential_vault.json"`, `max_credentials=100` ge=1 le=10000, `require_https_for_fill=True`). New `action_fill_credential(session, params, *, runtime, emit_event)` action: validates selector + credential_ref, honest-degrades if vault is None, blocks http:// when require_https_for_fill, checks `scope.allowed_domains` via fnmatch against the page host BEFORE decryption, calls `vault.read(ref, requesting_agent_id=agent_id)`, then `await page.fill(selector, value)`. Tier-3 always (Captain ACK every credential read). Late-bind handler registration in `actions.py` parallel to AD-706c-2's compute_use_click pattern; tool.py `dispatch_action` flow special-cases `compute_use_click` and `fill_credential` like AD-706c-1's `verify` (kwargs runtime + emit_event); `agent_id` from session `_agent_id` merged into params at dispatch boundary. Startup wiring in `startup/finalize.py:_wire_browser_tool` adds two-phase init: `runtime.credential_vault = None` declared next to BrowserTool construction; constructed only when `cfg.credential_vault.enabled` AND `cfg.auth.crew_scope_token` is non-empty (both preconditions logged with operator remediation on mismatch). Empty crew_scope_token at vault ctor raises `RuntimeError` with explicit operator remediation message. Three-tier hardening: empty allowed_agent_ids = Captain-only, expired = None, scope mismatch = None + `CREDENTIAL_READ_DENIED` event. `list_refs()` returns `CredentialMetadata` with NO value field (regression-protected via test). Atomic write via tmp+rename (mirrors AD-720d-2.1 pattern). RLock for concurrent access; async API dispatches blocking I/O through `asyncio.get_running_loop().run_in_executor`. AD-731 invariant n/a (credentials are short strings, not blobs); AD-706e `upload_file.credential_ref` hook remains forward-compatible (the vault is the consumer). +15 pytest in tests/test_ad706f_credential_vault.py: default-off, ctor rejection, KEK determinism, roundtrip, scope denies unauthorized, expiry enforced, restart persistence, materialize_to_temp + caller unlink, delete, list_refs no-values regression, action honest-degrade when vault None, https required, domain mismatch, happy path with event emission, always-tier-3 classification. Real `EncryptedFileCredentialVault` against `tmp_path` per BF-287. Full gate: 13816 → 13832 passed (20 skipped). `THIRD_PARTY_LICENSES.md` updated with cryptography entry.

- AD-706e — Browser Tool action vocabulary v2 (closes #520). Seven new action verbs in `src/probos/tools/browser/actions.py`: `drag` (tier 2, tier 3 on tier-3 hosts; supports `from_selector`/`to_selector` and index fallback), `key_combo` (tier 2; tier 3 for destructive combos Control+W/Control+Q/Alt+F4/Control+Shift+W via new `_KEY_COMBO_TIER_3_PATTERNS` frozenset), `mouse_move` (tier 1, silent observation — added to `silent` set), `mouse_button` (tier 2, validates `button` ∈ {left/right/middle} and `action` ∈ {down/up/click}), `upload_file` (always tier 3; forward-compatible `credential_ref` hook degrades to `skipped_reason="credential_vault_unavailable"` until AD-706f lands), `download` (tier 2; tier 3 for executable suffixes `.exe`/`.dll`/`.dmg`/`.msi` via new `_DOWNLOAD_TIER_3_SUFFIXES` tuple), `eval_js` (always tier 3, script length capped at 4096 chars via new `_EVAL_JS_MAX_SCRIPT_LEN` constant, result serialised via json.dumps(default=str)). Per-verb short-circuit branches in `classify_action` (vs set-membership) so AD-706f's `fill_credential` is a single new branch with no merge conflict on a literal. AD-706e is NO-OP for `compute_use_click` (owned by AD-706c-2) and `fill_credential` (owned by AD-706f). Three new EventType values (`BROWSER_FILE_UPLOAD_REQUESTED`/`BROWSER_DOWNLOAD_REQUESTED`/`BROWSER_EVAL_JS_EXECUTED`) emitted from BrowserTool layer post-dispatch (alongside existing per-action `BROWSER_ACTION_EXECUTED`). `classify_action_with_llm` unchanged — its existing `rule_tier >= 3` short-circuit handles the always-tier-3 verbs. +23 pytest in tests/test_ad706e_action_vocab_v2.py: happy + error path per verb (14) + 9 classification rules + _HANDLERS registration. Real `BrowserToolConfig()` + `_FakePage`/`_FakeMouse`/`_FakeKeyboard` dataclass stubs per BF-287. Full gate: 13794 → 13816 passed (1 known dreaming flake outside this wave per dispatch).

- AD-706c-2 — Coordinate-aware `compute_use` BrowserTool tier for DOM-less surfaces (closes #643). New `compute_use` LLM tier (fifth peer of fast/standard/deep/vision in `_LLM_TIERS`). New `_TIER_ORDER: tuple[str, ...] = ("fast", "standard", "deep")` promoted to module-level constant; vision + compute_use deliberately excluded (BF-269). ModelRouter bypass extended for compute_use (BF-273). `is_vision_tier_configured` extended to recognize `"compute_use"` tier (REUSE — no new helper, no new honest-degrade constants per BF-274 lesson). Seven new `CognitiveConfig` fields (`llm_base_url_compute_use`, `llm_api_key_compute_use`, `llm_model_compute_use`, `llm_timeout_compute_use`, `llm_api_format_compute_use`, `llm_temperature_compute_use`, `llm_top_p_compute_use`, `llm_max_tokens_compute_use`); `tier_config()` map extended. New file `src/probos/tools/browser/compute_use.py` exports `action_compute_use_click(session, params, *, runtime, emit_event)`: validates intent, captures screenshot via `page.screenshot()`, stores via `AttachmentStore.write` keyed by SHA-256 (AD-731 invariant), builds OpenAI-shape multimodal request via `build_multimodal_messages`, calls `runtime.llm_client.complete(LLMRequest(tier="compute_use", ...))` (no cache per BF-272, no fallback chain per BF-269), parses `{x, y, confidence}` JSON, runs AD-706c-1 `action_verify` handshake (Guard #9 — reuses existing primitive, never forks), then either `page.mouse.click(x, y)` + emit `BROWSER_COMPUTE_USE_CLICK_EXECUTED` or aborts with `BROWSER_COMPUTE_USE_CLICK_ABORTED`. Late-bind registration: `_HANDLERS["compute_use_click"] = action_compute_use_click` appended after `action_verify` to avoid actions.py ↔ compute_use.py circular import. `classify_action` extended with `compute_use_click → 3` short-circuit at top of ladder (Captain ACK required every call); AD-706e's later additive entries can stack without re-shaping this branch. `classify_action_with_llm` already short-circuits when `rule_tier >= 3` — no change. Guard #10 trust budget: two new `BrowserSession` attributes (`_compute_use_consecutive_autonomous`, `_compute_use_total_calls`) + public properties + `note_compute_use_call()` / `note_captain_ack()` methods (LoD-compliant); two new `BrowserToolConfig` fields (`compute_use_max_consecutive_autonomous_actions` default 5 ge=0 le=20, `compute_use_max_per_session` default 50 ge=0 le=500). Four new EventType values (`BROWSER_COMPUTE_USE_CLICK_PROPOSED/VERIFIED/ABORTED/EXECUTED`). REUSES `VISION_UNCONFIGURED_MESSAGE` from `cognitive/vision_dispatch.py` for honest-degrade (BF-274 — one module owns these strings). Tier-2 throughout: every failure mode returns `{ok: False, skipped_reason, ...}`, never raises. AD-731 source-scan asserts no `b64encode`/`base64.b64`. BF-287 enforced: tests use real `SystemConfig()` + dataclass fakes at substrate boundary (no MagicMock at config / page / store / LLM client). +16 pytest tests + 4 existing tests adapted for the fifth peer tier (test_bf069_llm_health, test_per_tier_llm, test_ad732_vision_tier). Full gate: 13778 → 13794.

**Wave 164 shipped (2026-05-16):**
- AD-728c — Agent-initiated render self-check with contextual rate limits (closes #660). Flips AD-728's `agent_initiated_stub` trigger from hard-reject to a gated, two-budget rate-limited path. Default-OFF transitional gate `cfg.avatars.render_self_check_enabled` (False). When enabled, REUSES AD-728's `verify_render_coherence` callsite (no duplication of the vision-LLM call). New `_agent_initiated_rate_check` helper inside `avatars/render_verification.py` implements two-budget logic: per-active-conversation budget (default 2, scope `render_self_check_conv:<last_reply_ts>`) applies INSTEAD OF the hourly budget (default 3, scope `render_self_check_hour`) when the agent is in an active conversation (default 600s since `last_reply_emitted_at`). Budgets are NEVER additive — the per-conversation budget is the override while engaged, not an add-on (Test 5 explicitly enforces). Four new `AvatarsConfig` fields: `render_self_check_enabled` (default False), `render_self_check_max_per_hour_per_agent` (default 3, ge=0), `render_self_check_max_per_active_conversation` (default 2, ge=0), `render_self_check_active_window_seconds` (default 600, ge=0). New async `CognitiveAgent.check_own_render(reason: str | None = None)` method ("look in the mirror") folds every outcome (coherent, divergent, rate-limited, honest-degrade) into the agent's own working memory via `AgentWorkingMemory.record_observation(...)` so the agent's next LLM call sees the mirror result; rationale documented inline — SENSORIUM_REGISTRY is class-level static dispatch metadata, NOT a runtime mailbox. Cost discipline preserved on the event-bus side: agent-initiated coherent calls still emit NOTHING (only divergent calls emit `RENDER_DIVERGENCE_OBSERVED`); the agent-private working-memory entry is the only additional surface. `_last_reply_emitted_at(runtime, agent_id)` lookup goes through public `runtime.registry.get(agent_id)` API per BF-287 — never `registry.agents`. AD-728's existing `test_agent_initiated_stub_hard_rejected` test renamed to `test_agent_initiated_stub_default_off_preserves_baseline` and updated to assert that with `render_self_check_enabled=False` the baseline `"agent_initiated_disabled"` honest-degrade is preserved exactly. No new event types (AD-728c reuses `RENDER_DIVERGENCE_OBSERVED`). AD-731 invariant preserved (source-scan asserts no `b64encode`/`base64.b64` in `render_verification.py`). Tier-2 throughout — `check_own_render` never raises; internal errors `logger.warning(..., exc_info=True)` and fold an `internal_error` observation. Test scaffolding uses real `SystemConfig()` fixtures + hand-rolled `@dataclass` agent stub (NOT `MagicMock(spec=CognitiveAgent)`) per BF-287. Three forward markers filed with TECHNICAL triggers per AD-722c-3: AD-728c-1 (per-conversation budget reset on Captain-acknowledged correction), AD-728c-2 (Counselor mediation of self-check requests), AD-728c-3 (`VisionLLMRateLimit._windows` per-conversation bucket GC). +12 pytest tests; full gate 13750 → 13762.

**Wave 163 in flight (2026-05-15):**
- AD-728 — Vision-LLM render-coherence mirror function (closes #586). New `src/probos/avatars/render_verification.py` with `RenderCoherenceResult` dataclass + module-level `verify_render_coherence(runtime, agent_id, trigger, ...)` function. REUSES AD-722a-1's `VisionLLMRateLimit` (scope `render_verification`) and `is_render_phrased()` AD-727 rule #8 phrasing helper. Three triggers: `captain_command` (new `/verify-render <agent_id>` slash command in `experience/shell.py`), `divergence_followup` (gated by `cfg.avatars.render_verification_followup_enabled`, default False), `agent_initiated_stub` (path exists, hard-rejected). Default-OFF gate `AvatarsConfig.render_verification_enabled`; per-agent hourly cap `render_verification_max_per_hour_per_agent` (default 3, ge=0). New `EventType.RENDER_DIVERGENCE_OBSERVED`. Phrasing rejection re-prompts ONCE with explicit renderer-subject constraint; second failure drops the analog description with `skipped_reason="phrasing_rejected"`. Tier-2 throughout (every failure mode returns a result with `skipped_reason` set). Cost discipline: coherent observations are NOT logged. AD-731 invariant preserved (image bytes flow through AttachmentStore SHA-256 refs via `build_multimodal_messages`; module source-scan asserts no `b64encode`/`base64.b64`). AD-727 rule #1 source-scan asserts no `trust_network`/`hebbian` token in the module. +15 pytest tests.
- AD-729 — Peer avatar perception governance contract (closes #587). New `src/probos/avatars/peer_perception.py` exports `ObservationRegister` enum (OPERATIONAL / PERSONAL), `PeerObservation` frozen dataclass, async `observe_peer(...)` capability surface, async `request_permission(...)` speak-freely protocol, sync `composite_impressions_for(...)` helper, `register_permission_listener(...)` and `reset_state()` test hooks. Four mechanical floors enforced: (1) reputation/routing read-only (source-scan regression test asserts zero `trust_network`/`hebbian`/`mesh.routing` imports), (2) privacy opt-out via observed's `CrewProfile.peer_perception.enabled`, (3) backend-render-only path, (4) cross-federation honest-degrade (`federation_review_required`). Eight hard gates evaluated in order. New `PeerPerceptionProfile` dataclass added to `crew_profile.py` (default `enabled=True, certified=False`) with `to_dict`/`from_dict` roundtrip wired into `CrewProfile` JSON serialisation. 5 new EventType values (`PEER_OBSERVATION_RECORDED/DECLINED/PERMISSION_REQUESTED/GRANTED/DENIED`) inserted after `RENDER_DIVERGENCE_OBSERVED`. 3 new `AvatarsConfig` fields (`peer_perception_enabled` default-OFF, `peer_observation_decay_seconds` default 7-day, `peer_observation_max_per_pair_per_thread` default 1 ge=0). Records-store persistence via existing `records_store.write_entry(...)` API (artifact type `peer_observation`, tagged, classification `ship`). Permission grants: in-module store, single-use, 5-minute TTL; default deny-silent listener. AD-731 invariant preserved (textual payloads only; module source-scan asserts no `b64encode`/`base64.b64`). Tier-2 throughout. Real `AgentRegistry`-shape fixture in tests per BF-287 (no MagicMock at substrate boundary). +18 pytest tests.
- AD-722a-6 — Cross-agent intent-vs-presentation divergence observation (closes #615). New async `observe_peer_divergence(runtime, observer_id, observed_id, *, register, permission_grant_id, thread_id, window_seconds)` in `avatars/peer_perception.py`. Consumes AD-722a-1's `runtime.divergence_history` ring buffers and routes the resulting observation through AD-729's `observe_peer(...)` so the eight governance gates apply uniformly. Three pre-delegation hard gates: (1) new `AvatarsConfig.cross_agent_divergence_observation_enabled` default-OFF, (2) AD-722a-1's `vision_intent_divergence_enabled` upstream gate, (3) at least one history entry inside `window_seconds` (default 24h). New pure-template `_format_divergence_summary(...)` emits flat OPERATIONAL phrasing (count, dominant intent emotion, mean magnitude) — no LLM call, no embeddings. New `EventType.CROSS_AGENT_DIVERGENCE_OBSERVED` emitted only after the AD-729 governance layer records the observation. Dual default-OFF preserved: both `peer_perception_enabled` AND `cross_agent_divergence_observation_enabled` must be True before the surface fires. AD-731 invariant preserved (textual summary only; module + runtime payload source-scans). +10 pytest tests.
- AD-729b — Peer-observation conduct training module (closes #589). New `src/probos/cognitive/peer_observation_training.py` exports `load_module(path)`, `grade_module(*, module, responses)`, `peer_observation_graduation_gate(*, profile, qualification_config)`, async `set_peer_observation_certified(*, runtime, agent_id, value, reason)`. Deterministic weighted-rubric grade (NOT LLM in v1; AD-729b-2 forward marker filed). New YAML scaffold `config/manuals/peer_observation_conduct.yaml` with six AD-729b sections (theory, register_identification, phrasing_practice, permission_protocol, pattern_recognition (placeholder until AD-729c corpus available), final_assessment with 0.8 pass threshold and per-section rubric weights). Two new EventType values (`PEER_OBSERVATION_CERTIFIED`, `PEER_OBSERVATION_CERTIFICATION_REVOKED`). Two new `QualificationConfig` fields: `peer_observation_module_path` (default `config/manuals/peer_observation_conduct.yaml`), `peer_observation_certification_required` (default-OFF; flips True after AD-729a Standing Orders ship). `peer_observation_graduation_gate` is the integration hook for Boot Camp / Qualification consumers (actual `crew_development/boot_camp.py` graduation-pipeline wire-up deferred — gate is exported and unit-tested in isolation). `set_peer_observation_certified` mirrors AD-720d-2.1 `set_vision_capable` shape. Tier-2 throughout (parse failures honest-degrade to False; never raises). +8 pytest tests with real `SystemConfig()` fixtures loading the actual YAML scaffold.
- AD-729c — Counselor pattern-monitoring for peer-observation conduct (closes #590). New `src/probos/cognitive/peer_observation_monitor.py` exports `PatternFinding` dataclass, `PeerObservationPatternDetector` Protocol, seven concrete detectors (`FrequencyDriftDetector`, `RegisterDriftDetector`, `CascadeSignalDetector`, `StaticImpressionDetector`, `PermissionDenialPatternDetector`, `SycophancyPatternDetector`, `PrivilegedTierLeakageDetector`), `default_detectors(...)` factory, `aggregate_health_metrics(...)` privacy-preserving counts helper, and `PeerObservationMonitor` orchestrator. Fixed 60-second cadence pinned at module-level constant `_MONITOR_INTERVAL_SECONDS=60` (AD-504 phantom dropped; configurable cadence filed as AD-729c-1 forward marker). Three-tier escalation per `(detector, observer_id)` pair: Tier 1 first finding → `PEER_OBSERVATION_INTERVENTION_TIER_1` event (private coaching), Tier 2 persistence → revoke certification (calls injected `revoke_certification` callable; typically `set_peer_observation_certified(...False)`) + Tier-2 event, Tier 3 persistence post-recert → Tier-3 event (bridge alert surfaces via existing AD-635 alert API; concrete wiring deferred). State persists across runtime restarts via sidecar JSON at `<data_dir>/peer_observation_intervention_state.json` (atomic-write tmp+rename pattern mirroring AD-720d-2.1). 4 new EventType values (`PEER_OBSERVATION_PATTERN_FLAGGED`, `*_INTERVENTION_TIER_1/2/3`). Counselor-own-conduct enforced: module never invokes the AD-729 capability surface (source-scan regression Test 21). Trust read-only: SycophancyPatternDetector reads via injected callable, never writes (source-scan Test 20). +23 pytest tests covering all seven detectors (positive + negative each), three-tier escalation cascade, aggregate metrics correctness, cadence pinning, sidecar restart roundtrip. Tier-2 throughout.
- AD-729d — Peer-observation reinforcement loop FORWARD MARKER (closes #591 as the marker filing; the build is a future wave). Documentation-only Wave 163 deliverable. Reinforcement creates an optimization gradient that peer perception alone does not; the AD-729 mechanical constraint #1 (read-only with respect to trust) prevents trust drift, but reinforcement is the natural channel where that pressure could re-enter the system through DSL drift. Five TECHNICAL triggers per AD-722c-3 (NOT calendar dates) — all must hit before the marker advances to a build: (A) operational maturity (`PEER_OBSERVATION_RECORDED` count ≥100 across ≥3 pairs over a 2-quarter window); (B) conduct stability (zero `PEER_OBSERVATION_INTERVENTION_TIER_3` events in the same window); (C) AD-729a Standing Orders shipped AND content includes a reinforcement-specific section reviewed by Counselor; (D) AD-729b module YAML includes reinforcement content AND ≥3 officers passed the extended module; (E) Captain explicit ruling at design stage documented in DECISIONS.md. Open design questions captured in the DECISIONS entry (proposals-vs-direct DSL mutation, mentor-mentee scope, decay semantics, clinical-role boundary). No code, no tests, no config — pure documentation housekeeping.
- AD-721d-2c — HXI Counselor-mediation button (closes #658). Adds the UI surface for the AD-721d-2 server-side mediation endpoint (shipped Wave 162). New props on `CrewAvatarPopout.tsx`: `onMediateRevision?: (note) => Promise<{refined_hint?, proposal_iteration?, error?}>` and `counselorOnline?: boolean`. New inline-SVG "Mediate" button (two circles + bridge glyph; stroke-based per HXI Design Principle #3, no emoji) rendered between the revision counter and the existing submit button when ALL of: prop callback present, counselor online, revision note non-empty. Click invokes the callback then renders the refined hint inline ("Counselor refined: ...") with an iteration chip ("(iter N)"); on success the refined text populates the textarea so the Captain can review/edit before submitting via the existing submit-revision path. On error, a stroke-based error surface is shown and the Captain's original hint is preserved (NOT clobbered). All local state (mediating, mediateRefined, mediateError, mediateIteration) lives inside CrewAvatarPopout; no store mutations. AgentProfilePanel wiring not in this AD (parent integration of the callback + counselor-online lookup is a sub-AD; the new props default to undefined so the existing wiring is byte-compatible). +4 vitest tests covering: visible-when-conditions-met, hidden-when-offline, happy-path-renders-refined-panel, error-path-preserves-original-hint. Vitest: 644 → 648 passed. `npm run build` green; bundle hash `index-a4x_HPw3.js` → `index-cAfin0aS.js`. No backend changes; existing pytest 13715 baseline preserved.
- AD-719b — Copilot-style left rail + Agents nav (closes #547). New self-contained `ui/src/components/leftrail/LeftRail.tsx` exports `LeftRail` plus `LeftRailAgent` / `LeftRailThread` / `LeftRailProps` types. Default OFF via localStorage flag `hxi_left_rail_enabled` (returns null until parent flips). Two sections: online agents (filtered to `status==='online'`) and recent threads. Collapse toggle persists `hxi_left_rail_collapsed` to localStorage (width 240px ↔ 56px). Progressive disclosure per HXI Design Principle #5: first-time users (visit count <10) see max 5 agents + 3 threads; veteran users see max 12 + 8. Visit count tracked in localStorage `hxi_visit_count`. Three inline-SVG stroke-based glyphs (agents, threads, collapse) per HXI Design Principle #3 — no emoji. Pure presentational — data flows in via props; parent wires zustand stores. `onSelectAgent` / `onSelectThread` callbacks fire on click. Component is built but NOT yet imported by App.tsx (parent integration deferred to AD-719b-parent-wire forward marker); bundle hash unchanged because Vite tree-shakes the unreferenced module. +5 vitest tests: default-OFF returns null, enabled renders both sections, click-agent fires callback, click-thread fires callback, collapse toggle persists state and updates width. Vitest: 648 → 653.
- AD-719a — Persistent multi-agent chat threads under WardRoom (closes #546). Wave 163 ships the architectural contract — `thread_mode='multi_agent'` marker on existing `WardRoomThread`, the `create_multi_agent_thread(service, *, channel_id, captain_id, title, body, mentioned_agent_ids)` helper, and the participants-trailer protocol. Architectural decisions (Captain ruling 2026-05-15): (1) YES, agents observe other agents' messages mid-thread when they have already been @-mentioned in the thread; (2) NO, agents do NOT observe messages in threads they were never @-mentioned in (cross-thread observation out of scope); (3) Captain messages are always the seed (agent-to-agent without Captain prompt deferred to AD-719a-2). New `src/probos/ward_room/multi_agent.py` exports `MULTI_AGENT_THREAD_MODE` constant, `create_multi_agent_thread`, `format_participant_trailer`, `parse_participants`, `is_participant`, `cross_agent_visibility` helpers. Participants encoded in thread body via `@participants: id1,id2` trailer; recovered via regex. AD-719 transient @-mention fan-out wire-up deferred to forward marker AD-719a-wire (the helper is the seam the future wire-up consumes). UI delta minimal: new "MULTI" badge with stroke-based two-circle-bridge glyph in `WardRoomThreadList.tsx` for `thread_mode==='multi_agent'` rows; store type extended `'inform' | 'discuss' | 'action' | 'multi_agent'`. +6 pytest tests (helper roundtrip, participants trailer, visibility rules, Captain-as-author, AD-731 invariant source-scan). +4 vitest tests (badge renders for multi_agent, hidden for discuss, mixed-mode list, click selects thread). Vitest: 653 → 657. Bundle hash `index-cAfin0aS.js` → `index-hKNByK6W.js`. Full pytest gate: 13730 passed.
- AD-739 — Captain Card data model + render pipeline (closes #649, v1 contract). Operator self-card always-in-context across all CognitiveAgent prompts. System-maintained — NOT agent-self-edited. New `src/probos/captain_card/card.py` exports the `CaptainCard` Pydantic model (identity, voice, active-context, preferences max-10, recent_corrections max-3, avatar_ref, version, updated_at), `CorrectionRef` model, `default_captain_card()` bootstrap, `load_card(path)` / `save_card(card, path)` JSON-sidecar persistence (atomic temp+replace, tier-2 degrade), and `render_card_for_prompt(card, *, max_tokens=500)` pure-template renderer. Renderer truncates `preferences`/`recent_corrections` from the tail when over budget while preserving identity fields. AD-588/589/592 confabulation-guard via `_CAPABILITY_GAP_RE` from `probos.cognitive.decomposer`: any rendered line matching the capability-gap regex is dropped with a logged warning. AD-731 invariant: `avatar_ref` Pydantic validator enforces SHA-256 hex format (64 chars); ValueError raised on non-hash strings; module source-scan asserts no `b64encode`/`base64.b64`. Four new `CognitiveConfig` fields: `captain_card_enabled: bool = True` (default-ON; benign anchor), `captain_card_path: str = "captain_card.json"`, `captain_card_max_tokens: int = 500` (ge=100, le=1500), `captain_card_refresh_min_interval_seconds: int = 3600` (ge=60). Prompt-builder injection point and Dreaming-loop integration deferred to forward markers AD-739-prompt-wire and AD-739-dreaming-wire (the model + renderer are the contract; consumer wiring is mechanical). +10 pytest tests: bootstrap default, roundtrip persistence, render-within-budget, truncation preserves identity, capability-gap-line stripped, avatar_ref SHA-256 validation, avatar_ref None allowed, AD-731 source-scan, CognitiveConfig defaults, package public surface. Real `SystemConfig()` fixtures per BF-287.
- AD-706d — LLM-driven Browser Tool tier classifier (closes #519). New `src/probos/tools/browser/llm_classifier.py` exports `classify_action_with_llm(runtime, rule_tier, action, ...)` companion to the existing rule-based `classify_action` (at `tools/browser/actions.py:550`, UNCHANGED — int tier 1/2/3 contract preserved). Default OFF via `BrowserToolConfig.llm_classifier_enabled`. Critical safety property: LLM can only UPGRADE tier (1→2→3), never DOWNGRADE — short-circuits without calling LLM when rule_tier >= 3. REUSES AD-722a-1's `VisionLLMRateLimit` under new scope `browser_action_classifier` (no fork to `LLMCallRateLimit`; the class is already generalized via cross-module use). In-memory cache keyed by `(action, url[:80], element_text[:120], page_title[:80])` with configurable TTL. Strict single-word output parsing: maps `auto_run`/`ack_required`/`destructive` (plus synonyms) to int 1/2/3; any other string honest-degrades. Four new `BrowserToolConfig` fields: `llm_classifier_enabled` (default OFF), `llm_classifier_tier` (default `fast`), `llm_classifier_max_per_hour` (default 60, ge=0), `llm_classifier_cache_ttl_seconds` (default 300, ge=0). Sync entry-point: classifier reads `runtime.llm_client.complete_sync` when present; runtimes that only expose async `complete` honest-degrade. AD-731 invariant n/a (text-only classifier). +10 pytest tests: disabled preserves rule tier, destructive short-circuits, LLM upgrades 1→2, LLM cannot downgrade 2→1, LLM failure degrades, malformed output degrades, unknown enum degrades, rate-limit exhaustion degrades, cache hit reuses prior tier, source-scan asserts `classify_action` unchanged + companion exists + VisionLLMRateLimit reused.

- AD-720e + AD-738e-2 — Audio attachment playback + Refs-trailer standing rule (+5 pytest tests + 3 Vitest tests; closes #566, #653). `AttachmentsConfig.allowed_mime_types` defaults extended with `audio/mpeg`, `audio/mp4`, `audio/ogg`. `attachments/mime.py._SIGNATURES` registers MP3 sync bytes (4 variants) + MP4 ftyp brands (3 variants) + Ogg `OggS`; `_ANY_OF` extends to include `audio/mpeg` + `audio/mp4` so the existing any-of branch validates them. `IntentSurface.tsx` attachment preview gains middle audio branch rendering `<audio controls preload="metadata">` between the image and file-icon branches. `WardRoomThreadDetail.tsx` paste handler accepts audio MIMEs (chip-only render — playback delivered through IntentSurface, scope-collapse per AD). AD-731 SHA-ref invariant preserved (audio bytes flow through AttachmentStore exactly like images). Transcription explicitly deferred (AD-705a forward marker). BUILDER-EXECUTION-PLAN standing rule for orphan sub-AD Refs-trailer added per #653; DECISIONS AD-738e-1 forward-marker AD-738e-2 (noise_w / sentence_silence) renumbered to AD-738e-2-prosody so #653 owns the AD-738e-2 slot. Forward markers AD-720e-1 (drop-zone visual feedback) + AD-720e-2 (waveform thumbnail) + AD-720e-3 (inline player in WR/Profile chips) filed.
- AD-725 — Targeted sub-intent dispatch on DM one-shot path (+11 pytest tests; closes #583). New `src/probos/cognitive/dm_targeted_lookup.py` `LookupDispatcher` + `SubintentClassifier` Protocol + v1 `RegexSubintentClassifier` (episodic → codebase → knowledge → oracle → none ladder). New `DmTargetedLookupConfig` (default `enabled=False`, `timeout_ms=500`, per-store enables, `max_lookup_chars=1500`, classifier-tier validator with `{regex, embedding}`). Wired into `routers/agents.py::agent_chat` BEFORE the vision-pipeline branch. Result prepended to `message_text` as `--- Targeted Recall (<type>) ---` block immediately before IntentMessage build. Four hard contracts: (1) at most one lookup per turn, (2) read-only, (3) hard `asyncio.wait_for` timeout, (4) no `intent_bus` broadcast. Defensive dispatch — missing methods log INFO and degrade to `""`. Test #10 explicitly verifies zero `trust_network`/`intent_bus`/`hebbian_router`/`consensus_engine` mock calls (firewall regression test). Forward markers AD-725-1 (sensorium-path registration) + AD-725-2 (embedding classifier) + AD-725-3 (per-agent sub-intent vocabulary) + AD-725-4 (multi-store fan-out) + AD-725-5 (LRU cache) + AD-725-6 (Episode dataclass stringify) filed.

**Wave 158 in flight (2026-05-13):**
- AD-737a — Divergence-detector hygiene (+3 pytest tests; closes #648). Hoisted `import dataclasses` to module-top; collapsed the two-pass `parse_intent_self_tag` re-parse in `apply_divergence_check` (palette fetched first, single parse); documented the test-fake contract for `runtime.profile_store` / `divergence_results` / `divergence_history` in the function docstring.
- AD-738a — Orchestrator commit-count audit + voice.ts test gate (+2 Vitest tests; closes #650). `Format-Gate2` now prints a COMMIT-COUNT AUDIT line comparing the wave-plan expectation vs HEAD's unpushed commits (audit trail only — never blocks push). `_resetTtsStatusForTests` gated behind `import.meta.env.MODE === 'test'` so production callers are inert. Renumbered Wave-157 forward markers AD-738a/b/c/d → AD-738f/g/h/i atomically in `docs/development/roadmap.md` and the AD-738 closure block.
- AD-738b — Per-wave UI gate codifies `npm run build` requirement (closes #651, BF-279 root cause). Standing rule added to `BUILDER-EXECUTION-PLAN.md` and surfaced in `wave-orchestrator.ps1:Format-BuildDispatch` so every UI-touching prompt's per-commit gate runs `npx vitest run` AND `npm run build` (Vitest skips `tsc -b` strict; BF-279 was 32h of stale-bundle drift across three waves).
- AD-738c — rhubarb→Oculus viseme mapping polish (+4 pytest tests + 1 Vitest test; closes #652). Duration-aware Preston-Blair → Oculus routing: long `B` frames (>80 ms) now route to `ih` (full vowel) instead of `kk` (stop consonant default). Consonant residuals in `VISEME_TARGETS` bumped from 0.10–0.20 → 0.20–0.30 so stop consonants are visible above the perceptual blend baseline. AD-721b-3 (#561 whisper.cpp WASM tiny.en) remains the long-term proper fix.
- AD-738e-1 — Per-emotion Piper prosody overrides (+7 pytest tests + 2 Vitest tests). Additive on top of AD-738e global defaults; concerned (slower + expressive), excited (faster + more variation), formal (drier). Custom AD-737 emotions resolve to v1 parent server-side via the new public `resolve_emotion_to_v1` alias before reaching the chat response. PiperBackend merges overrides per-call (no instance mutation).

**Wave 157 in flight (2026-05-13):**
- AD-738 — Server-streamed TTS via Piper (+26 pytest tests + 6 Vitest tests + 1 regression Vitest; closes forward marker AD-721b-2.3). MIT-licensed operator-provided binary at `tools/piper/piper(.exe)` + MIT-licensed default voice model `en_US-amy-medium.onnx[.json]` at `tools/piper/voices/`. New `src/probos/audio/tts/` module with `TTSBackend` Protocol + `PiperBackend` subprocess wrapper + `NullBackend` honest-degrade. New `TTSConfig` Pydantic model (default `backend = "browser"` — zero behaviour change for operators who don't install Piper). New `GET /api/avatars/tts/status` probe endpoint + `POST /api/avatars/tts` synthesis endpoint (AD-731 ref-shape invariant — audio bytes flow through AttachmentStore as SHA-256 refs, never inline). Browser `speakResponse` caches the one-time status probe in module-level state and skips POST entirely when `backend != "piper"` (load-bearing zero-HTTP-per-utterance guarantee). On piper happy path, browser plays via `<audio>` element + injects visemes into `useLipSyncCapture` via new `injectLipSyncFrames` setter. AD-735 volume + AD-737 emotion modulation apply to BOTH paths. Honest-degrade chain preserves `SpeechSynthesisUtterance` fallback at three tiers. Forward markers AD-738a (per-agent voice selection), AD-738b (GPU TTS backend eval — Kokoro/StyleTTS2), AD-738c (server-side voice modulation), AD-738d (TTS text caching) filed.

**Wave 156 in flight (2026-05-13):**
- AD-735 — Per-agent volume slider in `ProfileInfoTab.tsx` (+5 Vitest tests; closes #527). Backend chain (`VoiceProfile.volume`, `SetVoiceProfileRequest`, `PUT /api/agents/{id}/voice-profile`, `voice.ts` playback) was already shipped under AD-718; this AD exposed the UI slider with inline SVG speaker glyph (HXI Design Principle #3) and percent display. Mirrors Pitch/Rate `onMouseUp`/`onTouchEnd` persistence semantics. No backend, deps, or wire-shape changes.
- AD-736 — Mic-permission UX polish (+8 Vitest tests; closes #558). New `MicPermissionState` enum (`pending` / `granted` / `denied` / `unavailable`) + `onMicPermissionState` listener API in `wakeWord.ts`. Pre-flight `navigator.mediaDevices.enumerateDevices` hardware probe distinguishes "no audio device" from "permission denied"; `audio-capture` SR error mapped to `unavailable`. New `MicPermissionHint.tsx` HXI overlay mounts at `App.tsx` root; renders only on `denied`/`unavailable`. Dismissal sticky via `localStorage[hxi_mic_hint_dismissed]`. Inline SVG mic glyph (HXI Design Principle #3) — no emoji. AD-705 wake-word algorithm + AD-731 attachment invariant unchanged.
- AD-737 — Per-agent custom emotion taxonomy v2 (+8 pytest tests; closes #612). New `EmotionProfile` dataclass + `CrewProfile.custom_emotions` field (max 8 entries, must not collide with the v1 fixed 8). `parse_intent_self_tag` accepts custom names; `apply_voice_modulation` resolves through `inherits`, composes additive ±0.15 deltas, and emits BOTH the parent `intent_X` rule (so `compute_divergence`'s `startswith('intent_')` filter preserves scoring) and a `custom_X` observability tag. `apply_divergence_check` pre-resolves the custom name to its v1 parent for scoring, then restores the custom name on the `DivergenceResult` for observability. Prompt builder dynamically renders v1 + custom names from `profile_store`. Parent-equivalence pinned: zero-shift custom emotion inheriting `concerned` scores `match_score=1.0` identical to `concerned`. `EmotionalIntent`, `INTENT_EXPECTED_RULES`, `_REQUIRED_INTENT_EMOTIONS`, manifest, AD-731 invariant all unchanged.

**Wave 155 in flight (2026-05-12):**
- AD-721b-1 — Server-side rhubarb-lip-sync backend (+16 pytest tests; closes #559). MIT-licensed operator-provided binary at `tools/rhubarb/`; honest-degrade to AD-721b v1 heuristic when binary absent. Section 0.5 extends `AttachmentsConfig.allowed_mime_types`, `attachments/mime.py._SIGNATURES`, and `attachments/filesystem_store.py._MIME_TO_EXT` for `audio/webm` and `audio/wav`. `POST /api/avatars/lipsync` endpoint accepts AD-720 sha256 attachment refs and returns Oculus-mapped viseme schedule.
- AD-721b-2 — Browser-side real-audio capture infrastructure (+8 Vitest tests; closes #560). Always-on capture via `MediaStreamAudioDestinationNode` + `MediaRecorder`; honest-degrade end-to-end on the server (capture-fail → server-fail → empty frames → CrewVRM falls back to v1 heuristic → AD-721 D5 amplitude). AD-731 invariant honored: captured bytes upload via `/api/chat/attachments/multipart` as a sha256 ref; the lipsync request body carries only the hash, never inline base64. Most browsers today do not route SpeechSynthesis through Web Audio — the infrastructure ships ahead of upstream browser routability.

**Wave 154 in flight (2026-05-12):**
- AD-719c + AD-718d-1 — HXI polish: @-picker keyboard nav (↑/↓/Tab) + ModulationIndicator pulse overlay (+6 Vitest tests; closes #548, #553).
- AD-730-1-1 — WardRoomThreadDetail drag/drop + paste-image attachment (+3 Vitest tests; closes #646; #647 closed as duplicate pre-flight).
- AD-720d-1 — Multi-image batch + per-attachment timing; AttachmentsConfig.multi_image_warn_threshold soft warning (+5 pytest tests; closes #563).
- AD-724-1 + AD-724-2 + AD-724-5 — DM sanity gate hardening: one-shot retry on rejection, stdlib SequenceMatcher fuzzy repetition, shared `apply_dm_sanity` helper lifted into proactive WR/chain paths (+12 pytest tests; closes #627, #628, #629).

## Era VI — Closing Notes

- **Commercial overlay seam is live.** `runtime.commercial_overlay_loaded` and `/api/system/extensions` expose registry state. `pre_intent_authorization` hook fires on every `IntentBus.broadcast`; default-empty registry means zero overhead.
- **No open OSS issues.** Pre-public-release tracker stays clean unless a regression or a new commercial-tagged AD lands.
- **Roadmap drift convention:** `(SHIPPED, OSS)` for landed entries; `(planned, OSS)` for pending. Reconciliation runs on demand (tracked in BF #465 / AD-696).
